  export default function FileActionsMenu({
      onDownload,
      onOpenFile,
      onDuplicate,
      onDelete,
      onClose,
      anchorRef,
+     readOnly = false,
  }) {
      const ref = useClickAway(onClose, anchorRef);
      return (
          <div ref={ref} className="bl-popover bl-popover--up bl-file-menu">
              <button type="button" className="bl-file-menu-item" onClick={() => { onDownload(); onClose(); }}>
                  <Download size={14} /> Download .boundless.json
              </button>
+             {!readOnly && (
+                 <button type="button" className="bl-file-menu-item" onClick={() => { onOpenFile(); onClose(); }}>
+                     <FolderOpen size={14} /> Load from file…
+                 </button>
+             )}
              <button type="button" className="bl-file-menu-item" onClick={() => { onDuplicate(); onClose(); }}>
                  <Copy size={14} /> Duplicate canvas
              </button>
+             {!readOnly && <div className="bl-file-menu-divider" />}
+             {!readOnly && (
+                 <button type="button" className="bl-file-menu-item bl-file-menu-item--danger" onClick={() => { onDelete(); onClose(); }}>
+                     <Trash2 size={14} /> Delete canvas
+                 </button>
+             )}
          </div>
      );
  }
//// src/Components/editor/SyncChip.js  (new file)
+ import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
+ import useClickAway from "./useClickAway";
+ export function syncLabel(sync, signedIn) {
+     if (!sync) return "Opening…";
+     if (sync.mode === "readonly") return sync.why === "tab" ? "Read-only — open in another tab" : `Read-only — editing on ${(sync.holder && sync.holder.name) || "another device"}`;
+     if (sync.mode === "taking") return "Taking over…";
+     if (sync.mode === "handing") return "Handing over…";
+     if (sync.mode === "starting") return "Checking for changes…";
+     if (!signedIn || sync.cloud === "off" || sync.cloud === "local") return "Saved on this device";
+     if (sync.cloud === "saving") return "Saving…";
+     if (sync.cloud === "offline") return "Offline, saved on this device";
+     if (sync.cloud === "error") return "Not synced — saved on this device";
+     if (sync.cloud === "checking") return "Checking for changes…";
+     return "Saved";
+ }
+ export function syncStatus(sync, signedIn) {
+     if (!sync) return { key: "opening", short: "Opening…", icon: "up", tone: "muted" };
+     if (sync.error && !(sync.mode === "editing" && sync.cloud === "offline")) {
+         return { key: "error", short: sync.mode === "editing" ? "Not synced — saved here" : "Sync problem", icon: "alert", tone: "red" };
+     }
+     if (sync.mode === "readonly" || sync.mode === "taking") return { key: "viewing", short: "Viewing", icon: "eye", tone: "muted" };
+     if (sync.mode === "handing") return { key: "saving", short: "Saving…", icon: "up", tone: "muted" };
+     if (sync.mode === "starting") return { key: "checking", short: "Checking…", icon: "up", tone: "muted" };
+     if (!signedIn || sync.cloud === "off" || sync.cloud === "local") return { key: "local", short: "Saved here", icon: "check", tone: "muted" };
+     if (sync.cloud === "saving") return { key: "saving", short: "Saving…", icon: "up", tone: "muted" };
+     if (sync.cloud === "offline") return { key: "offline", short: "Offline — saved here", icon: "off", tone: "amber" };
+     if (sync.cloud === "error") return { key: "error", short: "Not synced — saved here", icon: "alert", tone: "red" };
+     if (sync.cloud === "checking") return { key: "checking", short: "Checking…", icon: "up", tone: "muted" };
+     return { key: "saved", short: "Saved", icon: "cloudCheck", tone: "green" };
+ }
+ export function syncSentence(sync, signedIn) {
+     if (sync && sync.error) return sync.error;
+     const s = syncStatus(sync, signedIn);
+     if (s.key === "saved") return "Saved to your account";
+     if (s.key === "saving" && signedIn && sync.mode === "editing") return "Saving to your account…";
+     return syncLabel(sync, signedIn);
+ }
+ export function syncName(sync, signedIn, compact) {
+     const s = syncStatus(sync, signedIn);
+     const label = syncLabel(sync, signedIn);
+     const name = compact || s.short === label ? label : `${s.short} — ${label}`;
+     return sync && sync.error ? `${name}. ${sync.error}` : name;
+ }
+ const PEN_NOTICES = new Set(["handed", "lost", "stolen"]);
+ export function showsNotice(n) {
+     return !!n && !!n.message && !PEN_NOTICES.has(n.kind);
+ }
+ export function useNarrow(max = 480) {
+     const query = `(max-width: ${max}px)`;
+     const read = () => (typeof window === "undefined" ? false
+         : window.matchMedia ? window.matchMedia(query).matches : window.innerWidth <= max);
+     const [narrow, setNarrow] = useState(read);
+     useEffect(() => {
+         const on = () => setNarrow(read());
+         window.addEventListener("resize", on);
+         on();
+         return () => window.removeEventListener("resize", on);
+     }, [query]);
+     return narrow;
+ }
+ const svg = (size, children, stroke = "currentColor", width = 1.8) => (
+     <svg width={size} height={size} viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke={stroke} strokeWidth={width}
+         strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false">{children}</svg>
+ );
+ const CLOUD = <path d="M7 18a4.5 4.5 0 0 1-.6-8.96A6 6 0 0 1 18 8.5a4 4 0 0 1-.5 9.5Z" />;
+ function StatusIcon({ icon, size }) {
+     switch (icon) {
+         case "cloudCheck": return svg(size, <>{CLOUD}<path d="m9.5 13 2 2 3.5-3.5" /></>);
+         case "up": return svg(size, <>{CLOUD}<path d="M12 16v-5" /><path d="m9.5 13 2.5-2.5 2.5 2.5" /></>);
+         case "off": return svg(size, <><path d="M7 18a4.5 4.5 0 0 1-.6-8.96A6 6 0 0 1 9 5.6" /><path d="M14.5 5.3A6 6 0 0 1 18 8.5a4 4 0 0 1 1.9 7.3" /><path d="M3 3l18 18" /></>);
+         case "alert": return svg(size, <>{CLOUD}<path d="M12 10v3" /><path d="M12 16h.01" /></>);
+         case "eye": return svg(size, <><path d="M2 12s3.6-7 10-7 10 7 10 7-3.6 7-10 7S2 12 2 12Z" /><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="3" /></>);
+         default: return svg(size, <path d="m5 12 4 4 10-10" />);
+     }
+ }
+ const CheckIcon = ({ size = 16 }) => svg(size, <path d="m5 12 4 4 10-10" />, "var(--bl-sync-green)", 2);
+ function SyncDetails({ sync, signedIn, deviceName, onClose, anchorRef }) {
+     const ref = useClickAway(onClose, anchorRef);
+     useEffect(() => {
+         if (ref.current) ref.current.focus();
+         const onKey = (e) => {
+             if (e.key !== "Escape") return;
+             onClose();
+             if (anchorRef.current) anchorRef.current.focus();
+         };
+         document.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
+         return () => document.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
+     }, [onClose, anchorRef, ref]);
+     const s = syncStatus(sync, signedIn);
+     const good = s.key === "saved" && !(sync && sync.error);
+     return (
+         <div ref={ref} className="bl-sync-details" role="dialog" aria-label="Sync details" tabIndex={-1}>
+             <div className="bl-sync-details-head">
+                 {good ? <CheckIcon /> : <span className={`bl-sync-tone--${s.tone}`}><StatusIcon icon={s.icon} size={16} /></span>}
+                 <span>{syncSentence(sync, signedIn)}</span>
+             </div>
+             <p className="bl-sync-details-note">Only one device draws at a time. Others can watch and take over.</p>
+             <div className="bl-sync-details-rule" />
+             <div className="bl-sync-details-device"><span>This device</span><b>{deviceName}</b></div>
+         </div>
+     );
+ }
+ export function SyncStatus({ sync, signedIn, deviceName, compact }) {
+     const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
+     const anchorRef = useRef(null);
+     const close = useCallback(() => setOpen(false), []);
+     const s = syncStatus(sync, signedIn);
+     return (
+         <span className="bl-sync-status-anchor">
+             <button ref={anchorRef} type="button" aria-label={syncName(sync, signedIn, compact)} aria-haspopup="dialog" aria-expanded={open}
+                 data-sync={s.key} className={`bl-sync-status bl-sync-tone--${s.tone}${compact ? " bl-sync-status--icon" : ""}`}
+                 onClick={() => setOpen((o) => !o)}>
+                 <span className="bl-sync-status-face">
+                     <StatusIcon icon={s.icon} size={compact ? 18 : 16} />
+                     {!compact && <span className="bl-sync-status-label">{s.short}</span>}
+                 </span>
+             </button>
+             {open && <SyncDetails sync={sync} signedIn={signedIn} deviceName={deviceName} onClose={close} anchorRef={anchorRef} />}
+         </span>
+     );
+ }
+ export function bannerClearance({ bar, notices, rail, gap = 12 }) {
+     const bottoms = (bar || []).filter(Boolean).map((r) => r.bottom);
+     const noticesTop = Math.round((bottoms.length ? Math.max(...bottoms) : 0) + gap);
+     const spans = (notices && notices.spans) || [];
+     if (!rail || !notices || !(notices.height > 0) || !spans.length) return { noticesTop, railTop: null };
+     const left = Math.min(...spans.map((r) => r.left)), right = Math.max(...spans.map((r) => r.right));
+     if (!(left < rail.right && rail.left < right)) return { noticesTop, railTop: null };
+     return { noticesTop, railTop: Math.round(noticesTop + notices.height + gap) };
+ }
+ export function useBannerClearance(rootRef) {
+     useEffect(() => {
+         const root = rootRef.current;
+         if (!root) return undefined;
+         const q = (sel) => root.querySelector(sel);
+         let at = null;
+         const rect = (el) => {
+             if (!el) return null;
+             const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
+             return { left: r.left - at.left, right: r.right - at.left, top: r.top - at.top, bottom: r.bottom - at.top, height: r.height };
+         };
+         let timer = null;
+         const apply = () => {
+             timer = null;
+             at = root.getBoundingClientRect();
+             const stack = q(".bl-top-notices"), railBox = q(".bl-editor-left");
+             const c = bannerClearance({
+                 bar: [rect(q(".bl-editor-top-left")), rect(q(".bl-editor-top-right-group"))],
+                 notices: stack ? { height: stack.offsetHeight, spans: [...stack.children].map(rect) } : null,
+                 rail: rect(railBox && railBox.firstElementChild),
+             });
+             root.style.setProperty("--bl-notices-top", `${c.noticesTop}px`);
+             if (c.railTop != null) root.style.setProperty("--bl-rail-top", `${c.railTop}px`);
+             if (railBox) railBox.classList.toggle("bl-editor-left--below", c.railTop != null);
+         };
+         const schedule = () => { if (!timer) timer = setTimeout(apply, 0); };
+         const ro = typeof ResizeObserver !== "undefined" ? new ResizeObserver(schedule) : null;
+         if (ro) for (const sel of [".bl-editor-top-left", ".bl-editor-top-right-group", ".bl-top-notices", ".bl-editor-left"]) { const el = q(sel); if (el) ro.observe(el); }
+         window.addEventListener("resize", schedule);
+         apply();
+         return () => { clearTimeout(timer); if (ro) ro.disconnect(); window.removeEventListener("resize", schedule); };
+     }, [rootRef]);
+ }
+ function bannerOf(sync, took) {
+     const mode = sync && sync.mode;
+     if (mode === "readonly") {
+         const sub = sync.why === "tab" ? "This canvas is open in another tab" : `${(sync.holder && sync.holder.name) || "Another device"} has the pen`;
+         return { kind: "viewing", title: "Viewing only", sub, icon: "eye", error: sync.error || null };
+     }
+     if (mode === "taking") return { kind: "moving", title: "Taking over…", sub: null, icon: "up" };
+     if (mode === "handing") return { kind: "moving", title: "Handing over…", sub: null, icon: "up" };
+     if (mode === "editing" && took) return { kind: "pen", title: "You have the pen", sub: `${took} is watching`, icon: "check" };
+     return null;
+ }
+ export function SyncBanner({ sync, onTakeOver, compact, penMs = 4000 }) {
+     const [took, setTook] = useState(null);
+     const seen = useRef({ asked: false, from: null });
+     const mode = sync && sync.mode;
+     useEffect(() => {
+         const s = seen.current;
+         if (mode === "readonly") { s.asked = false; s.from = sync.why === "tab" ? "Your other tab" : (sync.holder && sync.holder.name) || "The other device"; }
+         else if (mode === "taking") s.asked = true;
+         else if (mode === "editing" && s.asked) { s.asked = false; setTook(s.from || "The other device"); }
+         if (mode !== "editing") setTook(null);
+     }, [mode, sync]);
+     useEffect(() => {
+         if (!took) return undefined;
+         const t = setTimeout(() => setTook(null), penMs);
+         return () => clearTimeout(t);
+     }, [took, penMs]);
+     const b = bannerOf(sync, took);
+     if (!b) return null;
+     return (
+         <div className={`bl-sync-banner bl-sync-banner--${b.kind}${compact ? " bl-sync-banner--compact" : ""}`} role="status">
+             <span className="bl-sync-banner-icon">{b.icon === "check" ? <CheckIcon size={compact ? 20 : 18} /> : <StatusIcon icon={b.icon} size={compact ? 20 : 18} />}</span>
+             <span className="bl-sync-banner-text">
+                 <span className="bl-sync-banner-title">{b.title}</span>
+                 {b.sub && <span className="bl-sync-banner-sub">{b.sub}</span>}
+                 {b.error && <span className="bl-sync-banner-error" role="alert">{b.error}</span>}
+             </span>
+             {b.kind === "viewing" && (
+                 <button type="button" className="bl-sync-banner-action" onClick={onTakeOver}>Edit here</button>
+             )}
+             {b.kind === "pen" && (
+                 <button type="button" className="bl-sync-banner-dismiss" aria-label="Dismiss" onClick={() => setTook(null)}>
+                     {svg(18, <><path d="M6 6l12 12" /><path d="M18 6 6 18" /></>)}
+                 </button>
+             )}
+         </div>
+     );
+ }

//// src/cloud/canvasSession.js  (new file)
+ import {
+     CANVAS_COLLECTION, cloudGetCanvasMeta, cloudPushCanvas2, cloudPullCanvas2, cloudLoadCanvas,
+     cloudTakeLease, cloudRenewLease, cloudReleaseLease, cloudAskLease, cloudWatchManifest,
+ } from "./canvasSync";
+ import {
+     getStoreHeader, setSyncState, applyPulledStore, forkCanvas, saveCanvasDoc, stashCanvasStore, claimStore,
+     saveThumbs, upsertIndexEntry, readIndex, statsFromDoc, newCanvasId,
+ } from "../storage/localCanvases";
+ import { decideOpen, headOf, sameHead, leaseState, newEpoch, conflictName, LEASE_BEAT_MS, LEASE_TTL_MS, LEASE_SKEW_MS } from "./syncCore";
+ import { makeStorageLocks } from "./storageLocks";
+ export const SESSION_DEFAULTS = {
+     beatMs: LEASE_BEAT_MS,
+     ttlMs: LEASE_TTL_MS,
+     skewMs: LEASE_SKEW_MS,
+     syncMs: 30000,
+     askMaxMs: 6 * 60000,
+     stealAfterMs: 5000,
+     openTimeoutMs: 4000,
+     openRetryMs: 20000,
+     pushDeadlineMs: 5 * 60000,
+     followMinMs: 2000,
+     followCostFactor: 10,
+ };
+ export function editorFromHook(get) {
+     const h = () => get() || {};
+     return {
+         setReadOnly: (on) => { if (h().setReadOnly) h().setReadOnly(on); },
+         setSaving: (on) => { if (h().setSaving) h().setSaving(on); },
+         setWriter: (t) => { if (h().setWriter) h().setWriter(t); },
+         flush: () => (h().flushSave ? h().flushSave() : Promise.resolve(true)),
+         idle: () => (h().idle ? h().idle() : Promise.resolve(true)),
+         reload: (o) => (h().reloadFromStore ? h().reloadFromStore(o) : Promise.resolve(null)),
+         refreshMeta: () => (h().refreshMetaFromStore ? h().refreshMetaFromStore() : Promise.resolve(false)),
+         hasUnsaved: () => !!(h().hasUnsaved && h().hasUnsaved()),
+         keepUnsaved: (name) => (h().keepUnsaved ? h().keepUnsaved(name) : Promise.resolve(null)),
+         knownSeq: () => (h().knownSeq ? h().knownSeq() : null),
+         pushSource: () => (h().cloudPushSource ? h().cloudPushSource() : Promise.resolve(null)),
+         stats: () => (h().stats ? h().stats() : {}),
+     };
+ }
+ const withTimeout = (p, ms, why) => new Promise((res, rej) => {
+     const t = setTimeout(() => rej(Object.assign(new Error(why), { name: "Timeout" })), ms);
+     Promise.resolve(p).then((v) => { clearTimeout(t); res(v); }, (e) => { clearTimeout(t); rej(e); });
+ });
+ const perfNow = () => (typeof performance !== "undefined" ? performance.now() : Date.now());
+ const who = (s) => ({ device: s.deviceId, tab: s.tabId, name: s.deviceName });
+ const shownName = (entry, local) => {
+     const n = (entry && entry.name) || (local && local.name);
+     return n && n !== "untitled" ? n : "Untitled canvas";
+ };
+ const isNetwork = (err) => !!err && (err.name === "Timeout" || /unavailable|deadline|offline|network|failed to fetch/i.test(`${err.code || ""} ${err.message || ""}`));
+ export class CanvasSession {
+     constructor({ canvasId, uid = null, deviceId, deviceName, tabId = newEpoch(), editor, getEntry = null, env = {}, opts = {}, onState = null, onNotice = null, onPerf = null }) {
+         this.id = canvasId;
+         this.uid = uid;
+         this.deviceId = deviceId;
+         this.deviceName = deviceName;
+         this.tabId = tabId;
+         this.editor = editor;
+         this.getEntry = getEntry || (() => readIndex().find((e) => e.id === canvasId) || null);
+         const webLocks = env.locks !== undefined ? env.locks : (typeof navigator !== "undefined" && navigator.locks) || null;
+         this.env = {
+             locks: webLocks || makeStorageLocks(),
+             Channel: env.Channel !== undefined ? env.Channel : (typeof BroadcastChannel !== "undefined" ? BroadcastChannel : null),
+             now: env.now || (() => Date.now()),
+             visible: env.visible || (() => typeof document === "undefined" || document.visibilityState !== "hidden"),
+         };
+         this.o = { ...SESSION_DEFAULTS, ...opts };
+         this.onState = onState || (() => {});
+         this.onNotice = onNotice || (() => {});
+         this.onPerf = onPerf || (() => {});
+         this.col = CANVAS_COLLECTION;
+         this.lockName = `boundless:canvas:${canvasId}`;
+         this.state = { mode: "starting", why: null, holder: null, cloud: uid ? "checking" : "off", error: null };
+         this.gen = 0;
+         this.token = null;
+         this.hasLock = false;
+         this.epoch = null;
+         this.createLease = null;
+         this.dirty = false;
+         this.busy = null;
+         this.fail = { count: 0, until: 0 };
+         this.needOpen = false;
+         this.openFails = 0;
+         this.openRetryAt = 0;
+         this.lastHead = null;
+         this.lastReloadMs = 0;
+         this.followAt = 0;
+         this._followData = false;
+         this._following = false;
+         this._followNext = null;
+         this.stopped = false;
+         this._timers = new Set();
+         this._unwatch = null;
+         this._channel = null;
+         this._release = null;
+         this._held = null;
+         this._queued = null;
+         this._reqSeq = 0;
+         this._chain = Promise.resolve();
+     }
+     async start() {
+         this._openChannel();
+         const got = await this._requestLock({ wait: false });
+         if (this.stopped) return;
+         if (got) await this._holdDevice({ fromTab: false });
+         else this._tabFollower();
+     }
+     async stop() {
+         if (this.stopped || this._stopping) return;
+         this._stopping = true;
+         if (this.hasLock && (this.state.mode === "editing" || this.state.mode === "handing")) {
+             try {
+                 const ok = await withTimeout(this.editor.flush(), 10000, "flush");
+                 if (!ok) await this._keepUnsaved();
+             } catch (err) {  }
+         }
+         this.stopped = true;
+         for (const t of this._timers) { clearTimeout(t); clearInterval(t); }
+         this._timers.clear();
+         if (this._unwatch) { this._unwatch(); this._unwatch = null; }
+         if (this._queued) { this._queued.ac.abort(); this._queued = null; }
+         const epoch = this.epoch;
+         const last = this.hasLock && this.state.mode === "editing" && this.dirty && !this.discarded ? this._push({ why: "leave", force: true }) : Promise.resolve();
+         if (this.uid && epoch) last.then(() => cloudReleaseLease(this.uid, this.id, epoch)).catch(() => {});
+         try { await withTimeout(this.editor.idle(), 10000, "idle"); } catch (err) {  }
+         this._letGoOfLock();
+         if (this._channel) { try { this._channel.close(); } catch (err) {  } this._channel = null; }
+     }
+     markDirty() { this.dirty = true; }
+     onLocalSaved(seq, { data = true } = {}) {
+         this.dirty = true;
+         this._post({ type: "saved", seq, data: data !== false });
+     }
+     onRefused(kind) {
+         const g = this.gen;
+         (async () => {
+             await this._keepUnsaved();
+             if (this.stopped || this.gen !== g) return;
+             if (kind === "fenced") { if (this.hasLock) this._lockLost("fenced"); return; }
+             this.editor.setSaving(false);
+             this.lastReloadMs = (await this.editor.reload({ keepCamera: true })) || 0;
+             if (this._live(g) && this.state.mode === "editing") this.editor.setSaving(true);
+         })();
+     }
+     onVisibility(visible) {
+         if (visible) {
+             const owed = this._followOwed;
+             this._followOwed = null;
+             if (owed) this._scheduleFollow(owed);
+             this._beat();
+             return;
+         }
+         if (this.state.mode === "editing" && this.dirty) this._push({ why: "hide" });
+     }
+     async pushNow({ thumbs = null } = {}) {
+         if (!this.uid) return { ok: false, local: true };
+         if (this.state.mode !== "editing") return { ok: false, readOnly: true };
+         if (this.busy) return { ok: false, busy: true };
+         this.dirty = true;
+         return this._push({ why: "save", thumbs, force: true });
+     }
+     async takeOver() {
+         if (this.stopped || this.state.mode === "editing" || this.state.mode === "taking" || this.state.mode === "starting") return;
+         const why = this.state.why;
+         this._set({ mode: "taking", error: null });
+         if (why === "tab") {
+             this._post({ type: "want", tab: this.tabId });
+             this._after(this.o.stealAfterMs, () => { if (!this.hasLock && !this.stopped) this._stealLock(); });
+             return;
+         }
+         this.asking = true;
+         try {
+             const r = await cloudAskLease(this.uid, this.id, who(this), { now: this.env.now(), ttl: this.o.ttlMs, skewMs: this.o.skewMs });
+             if (r.ok) { this.asking = false; this.epoch = r.lease ? r.lease.epoch : null; this._serial(() => this._promote()); return; }
+             this._after(this.o.askMaxMs, () => { if (this.asking) this._takeLease(true); });
+             this._watch();
+         } catch (err) {
+             this.asking = false;
+             this._set({ mode: "readonly", error: "Couldn't reach the cloud to take over" });
+         }
+     }
+     _openChannel() {
+         const C = this.env.Channel;
+         if (!C) return;
+         try {
+             this._channel = new C(this.lockName);
+             this._channel.onmessage = (ev) => this._onMessage(ev && ev.data);
+         } catch (err) { this._channel = null; }
+     }
+     _post(msg) {
+         if (!this._channel) return;
+         try { this._channel.postMessage({ ...msg, from: this.tabId }); } catch (err) {  }
+     }
+     _onMessage(msg) {
+         if (!msg || msg.from === this.tabId || this.stopped) return;
+         if (msg.type === "want" && this.hasLock) this._handToTab();
+         else if (msg.type === "saved" && !this.hasLock) {
+             if (msg.data !== false) this._followData = true;
+             this._scheduleFollow(() => this._reloadFollow());
+         }
+     }
+     _requestLock({ wait = false, steal = false }) {
+         const locks = this.env.locks;
+         const id = ++this._reqSeq;
+         return new Promise((resolve) => {
+             let granted = false;
+             const opts = steal ? { steal: true } : wait ? {} : { ifAvailable: true };
+             let q = null;
+             if (wait) {
+                 if (this._queued) this._queued.ac.abort();
+                 let settle;
+                 q = { id, ac: new AbortController(), done: new Promise((r) => { settle = r; }) };
+                 q.settle = settle;
+                 opts.signal = q.ac.signal;
+                 this._queued = q;
+             }
+             const p = locks.request(this.lockName, opts, (lock) => {
+                 if (q) q.settle(lock ? "granted" : "none");
+                 if (!lock || this.stopped || this.hasLock) { resolve(false); return undefined; }
+                 granted = true;
+                 this._held = id;
+                 this.hasLock = true;
+                 if (this._queued === q) this._queued = null;
+                 resolve(true);
+                 return new Promise((release) => { this._release = release; });
+             });
+             p.catch(() => {
+                 if (q) q.settle("aborted");
+                 if (!granted) { resolve(false); return; }
+                 if (this._held === id && this.hasLock && !this.stopped) this._lockLost("stolen");
+             });
+         });
+     }
+     _drop() {
+         this.gen += 1;
+         this.hasLock = false;
+         this.token = null;
+         this._held = null;
+         this.editor.setSaving(false);
+         this.editor.setReadOnly(true);
+         this._stopCloud();
+     }
+     _letGoOfLock() {
+         const r = this._release;
+         this._release = null;
+         if (this.hasLock) this._drop();
+         if (r) r();
+     }
+     _lockLost(why) {
+         const unsaved = this.editor.hasUnsaved();
+         this._drop();
+         this._release = null;
+         this.epoch = null;
+         this.onNotice({ kind: "stolen", message: "Editing moved to another tab" });
+         (async () => {
+             if (unsaved) await this._keepUnsaved();
+             if (!this.stopped) this._tabFollower();
+         })();
+     }
+     async _stealLock() {
+         if (this.hasLock || this.stopped) return;
+         const q = this._queued;
+         if (q) {
+             q.ac.abort();
+             if ((await q.done) === "granted") return;
+         }
+         if (this.hasLock || this.stopped) return;
+         const got = await this._requestLock({ steal: true });
+         if (got && !this.stopped) this._serial(() => this._holdDevice({ fromTab: true }));
+     }
+     _tabFollower() {
+         this.editor.setReadOnly(true);
+         this.editor.setSaving(false);
+         this._set({ mode: "readonly", why: "tab", holder: null, cloud: this.uid ? "follow" : "off" });
+         this._requestLock({ wait: true }).then((got) => {
+             if (got && !this.stopped) this._serial(() => this._holdDevice({ fromTab: true }));
+         });
+     }
+     _live(g) { return !this.stopped && this.hasLock && this.gen === g; }
+     async _holdDevice({ fromTab }) {
+         this.gen += 1;
+         const g = this.gen;
+         this.token = `${this.tabId}.${g}`;
+         this.editor.setSaving(false);
+         this.editor.setReadOnly(true);
+         this.editor.setWriter(this.token);
+         await claimStore(this.id, this.token);
+         if (!this._live(g)) return;
+         const stored = await getStoreHeader(this.id);
+         if (!this._live(g)) return;
+         if (fromTab || (stored && stored.store === "kobin-2" && (stored.seq || 0) !== this.editor.knownSeq())) {
+             this.lastReloadMs = (await this.editor.reload({ keepCamera: true })) || 0;
+             if (!this._live(g)) return;
+             this.onNotice({ kind: "reloaded" });
+         }
+         if (!this.uid) { this._edit(g); return; }
+         this._set({ mode: "starting", why: null, holder: null, cloud: "checking", error: null });
+         this._every(this.o.beatMs, () => this._beat());
+         this._every(this.o.syncMs, () => { if (this.dirty && this.state.mode === "editing") this._push({ why: "sync" }); });
+         this._watch();
+         await this._openCloud(g);
+     }
+     async _handToTab() {
+         if (!this.hasLock || this._handingTab) return;
+         this._handingTab = true;
+         const g = this.gen;
+         this._set({ mode: "handing" });
+         this.editor.setReadOnly(true);
+         try {
+             const ok = await this.editor.flush();
+             if (!ok) await this._keepUnsaved();
+         } finally { this._handingTab = false; }
+         if (this.gen !== g || this.stopped) return;
+         this.epoch = null;
+         this._letGoOfLock();
+         this._post({ type: "released" });
+         this._tabFollower();
+     }
+     _stopCloud() {
+         for (const t of this._timers) { clearTimeout(t); clearInterval(t); }
+         this._timers.clear();
+         this._followPending = false;
+         if (this._unwatch) { this._unwatch(); this._unwatch = null; }
+     }
+     async _openCloud(g) {
+         let manifest;
+         try { manifest = await withTimeout(cloudGetCanvasMeta(this.uid, this.id), this.o.openTimeoutMs, "the cloud did not answer"); }
+         catch (err) {
+             if (!this._live(g)) return;
+             this._offline(err); this.needOpen = true; this._edit(g);
+             return;
+         }
+         if (!this._live(g)) return;
+         let holder = null;
+         try {
+             if (manifest && manifest.format === "kobin-2") {
+                 const r = await cloudTakeLease(this.uid, this.id, who(this), { now: this.env.now(), ttl: this.o.ttlMs, skewMs: this.o.skewMs });
+                 if (!this._live(g)) { if (r.ok && r.lease) cloudReleaseLease(this.uid, this.id, r.lease.epoch).catch(() => {}); return; }
+                 if (r.ok && r.lease) { this.epoch = r.lease.epoch; this.createLease = null; }
+                 else if (!r.ok) holder = r.holder;
+             } else {
+                 this.epoch = null;
+                 this.createLease = { ...who(this), epoch: newEpoch(), until: this.env.now() + this.o.ttlMs };
+             }
+             const res = await this._reconcile(g, { manifest, opening: true });
+             if (!this._live(g) || res === "gone" || res === "blocked") return;
+         } catch (err) {
+             if (!this._live(g)) return;
+             if (isNetwork(err)) { this._offline(err); this.needOpen = true; this._edit(g); return; }
+             this._blocked(`Couldn't open the cloud copy: ${String((err && err.message) || err).slice(0, 80)}`);
+             return;
+         }
+         this.needOpen = false;
+         this.openFails = 0;
+         if (holder) this._readOnlyDevice(holder);
+         else this._edit(g);
+     }
+     _blocked(message) {
+         this.needOpen = true;
+         this.openFails += 1;
+         const wait = Math.min(10 * 60000, this.o.openRetryMs * Math.pow(2, this.openFails - 1));
+         this.openRetryAt = this.env.now() + wait;
+         this.editor.setSaving(false);
+         this.editor.setReadOnly(true);
+         this._set({ mode: this.state.why === "device" ? "readonly" : "starting", cloud: "error", error: `${message}. Retrying.` });
+     }
+     async _reconcile(g, { manifest, opening = false } = {}) {
+         const m = manifest !== undefined ? manifest : await cloudGetCanvasMeta(this.uid, this.id);
+         if (!this._live(g)) return "gone";
+         const local = await getStoreHeader(this.id);
+         if (!this._live(g)) return "gone";
+         const d = decideOpen({ local, cloud: m, deviceId: this.deviceId, col: this.col });
+         if (d.base && local && local.store === "kobin-2" && !sameHead(d.base, local.base || null)) {
+             await setSyncState(this.id, { base: d.base, intent: null }, this.token);
+             if (!this._live(g)) return "gone";
+         }
+         this.lastHead = headOf(m);
+         this.onPerf("syncDecide", perfNow(), { action: d.action, why: d.why });
+         switch (d.action) {
+             case "push": this.dirty = true; return "ok";
+             case "pull": return this._pull(g, { full: d.full, local, keepCamera: !opening });
+             case "stash": case "conflict": {
+                 const kept = await this._keepStore(g, d.action, local);
+                 if (kept !== true) return kept === "gone" ? "gone" : "blocked";
+                 return this._pull(g, { full: true, local: null, keepCamera: !opening });
+             }
+             case "legacy": return this._pullLz1(g, local, m);
+             default: return "ok";
+         }
+     }
+     async _pull(g, { full, local, keepCamera }) {
+         const t0 = perfNow();
+         const have = full || !local ? null : { frameSeq: local.frameSeq || {}, seq: local.seq || 0 };
+         const pulled = await cloudPullCanvas2(this.uid, this.id, { have });
+         if (!this._live(g)) return "gone";
+         if (!pulled) return "ok";
+         const base = { ...pulled.head, col: this.col };
+         const ok = await applyPulledStore(this.id, pulled, base, this.token);
+         if (!this._live(g)) return "gone";
+         if (ok !== true) {
+             if (!full && ok !== "fenced") return this._pull(g, { full: true, local: null, keepCamera });
+             this._blocked("Couldn't write the cloud copy into this browser");
+             return "blocked";
+         }
+         if (pulled.thumbs) await saveThumbs(this.id, pulled.thumbs);
+         const nm = pulled.meta && pulled.meta.name;
+         const prevEntry = this.getEntry();
+         upsertIndexEntry({
+             ...(prevEntry || {}), id: this.id,
+             name: (nm && nm !== "untitled" ? nm : null) || (prevEntry && prevEntry.name) || "Untitled canvas",
+             savedAt: pulled.meta.savedAt || new Date().toISOString(),
+             strokes: pulled.header.strokes || 0, levels: pulled.header.levels || 0,
+         });
+         this.lastHead = pulled.head;
+         this.lastReloadMs = (await this.editor.reload({ keepCamera })) || 0;
+         if (!this._live(g)) return "gone";
+         this._post({ type: "saved", seq: pulled.header.seq });
+         this.onPerf("cloudPull", t0, { full: pulled.full ? 1 : 0, frames: Object.keys(pulled.frames).length, entries: pulled.entries.length, reloadMs: Math.round(this.lastReloadMs) });
+         this.onNotice({ kind: "pulled", name: nm || null });
+         return "ok";
+     }
+     async _keepStore(g, kind, local) {
+         const flushed = await this.editor.flush();
+         if (!this._live(g)) return "gone";
+         if (!flushed) await this._keepUnsaved();
+         const name = shownName(this.getEntry(), local);
+         if (kind === "stash") {
+             const t = await stashCanvasStore(this.id, name);
+             if (!this._live(g)) return "gone";
+             if (!t) { this._blocked("Couldn't keep this browser's copy (is the storage full?), so the newer cloud copy was not loaded"); return false; }
+             this.onNotice({ kind: "stash", message: "The cloud copy was newer; this browser's copy is in “Restore deleted canvases”" });
+             return true;
+         }
+         const cname = conflictName(name, this.deviceName);
+         const entry = await forkCanvas(this.id, cname);
+         if (!this._live(g)) return "gone";
+         if (!entry) { this._blocked("Couldn't keep this device's changes as a new canvas (is the storage full?), so the cloud copy was not loaded"); return false; }
+         this.onNotice({ kind: "conflict", name: cname, id: entry.id, message: `This device's changes were kept as a new canvas, “${cname}”` });
+         return true;
+     }
+     async _pullLz1(g, local, m) {
+         const entry = this.getEntry();
+         const localNewer = !!local && String(m.savedAt || "") <= String((entry && entry.savedAt) || local.savedAt || "");
+         const res = await cloudLoadCanvas(this.uid, this.id);
+         if (!this._live(g)) return "gone";
+         if (!res || !res.json) { if (localNewer) this.dirty = true; return "ok"; }
+         const parsed = JSON.parse(res.json);
+         const nm = res.meta && res.meta.name;
+         if (localNewer) {
+             if (statsFromDoc(parsed).strokes > 0) {
+                 let day = "";
+                 try { day = new Date().toLocaleDateString(undefined, { month: "short", day: "numeric" }); } catch (err) { day = ""; }
+                 const name = `${shownName(entry, local)} — the older cloud copy, ${day}`;
+                 const id = newCanvasId();
+                 if (!(await saveCanvasDoc(id, { ...parsed, meta: { ...(parsed.meta || {}), name } }))) {
+                     this._blocked("Couldn't keep the older cloud copy (is the storage full?)");
+                     return "blocked";
+                 }
+                 if (!this._live(g)) return "gone";
+                 upsertIndexEntry({ id, name, savedAt: m.savedAt || new Date().toISOString(), ...statsFromDoc(parsed) });
+                 this.onNotice({ kind: "kept", id, name, message: `The older cloud copy was kept as “${name}”` });
+             }
+             this.dirty = true;
+             return "ok";
+         }
+         if (local && statsFromDoc(parsed).strokes === 0) return "ok";
+         if (local) {
+             const kept = await this._keepStore(g, "stash", local);
+             if (kept !== true) return kept === "gone" ? "gone" : "blocked";
+         }
+         await saveCanvasDoc(this.id, nm && nm !== "untitled" ? { ...parsed, meta: { ...(parsed.meta || {}), name: nm } } : parsed);
+         if (!this._live(g)) return "gone";
+         if (res.thumbs) await saveThumbs(this.id, res.thumbs);
+         this.lastReloadMs = (await this.editor.reload({ keepCamera: false })) || 0;
+         return this._live(g) ? "ok" : "gone";
+     }
+     async _keepUnsaved() {
+         if (!this.editor.hasUnsaved()) return null;
+         const name = conflictName(shownName(this.getEntry(), null), this.deviceName).replace(" — edits from ", " — unsaved edits from ");
+         const entry = await this.editor.keepUnsaved(name);
+         this.onNotice(entry
+             ? { kind: "kept", id: entry.id, name, message: `Changes that could not be saved here were kept as “${name}”` }
+             : { kind: "unsaved", message: "Changes here could not be saved or copied (is the storage full?) — download them from the ⋮ menu" });
+         return entry;
+     }
+     _edit(g) {
+         if (g !== undefined && !this._live(g)) return;
+         this.editor.setSaving(true);
+         this.editor.setReadOnly(false);
+         const cloud = !this.uid ? "off" : this.needOpen ? "offline" : !this.getEntry() ? "local" : this.dirty ? "saving" : "saved";
+         this._set({ mode: "editing", why: null, holder: null, cloud, error: this.needOpen ? this.state.error : null });
+     }
+     discard() { this.dirty = false; this.discarded = true; }
+     _readOnlyDevice(holder) {
+         this.editor.setReadOnly(true);
+         this.editor.setSaving(false);
+         this.epoch = null;
+         this._set({ mode: "readonly", why: "device", holder: holder ? { device: holder.device, name: holder.name || "another device" } : { name: "another device" }, cloud: "follow" });
+     }
+     _offline(err) {
+         this._set({ cloud: "offline", error: String((err && err.message) || err).slice(0, 120) });
+     }
+     async _beat() {
+         if (this.stopped || !this.hasLock || !this.uid || !this.env.visible()) return;
+         const g = this.gen;
+         const mode = this.state.mode;
+         if (mode === "taking") {
+             if (this.asking) {
+                 try { const m = await cloudGetCanvasMeta(this.uid, this.id); if (m && this._live(g)) this._maybeTake(m.lease || null); } catch (err) {  }
+             }
+             return;
+         }
+         if (this.needOpen) {
+             if (this.env.now() < this.openRetryAt || this._reopening) return;
+             this._reopening = true;
+             try {
+                 if (mode === "editing") {
+                     const ok = await this.editor.flush();
+                     if (!this._live(g)) return;
+                     if (!ok) await this._keepUnsaved();
+                 }
+                 this.editor.setSaving(false);
+                 this.editor.setReadOnly(true);
+                 await this._openCloud(g);
+             } finally { this._reopening = false; }
+             return;
+         }
+         if (mode === "starting") return;
+         if (mode === "readonly") { this._scheduleFollow(() => this._followCloud()); return; }
+         if (!this.epoch) return;
+         if (this.busy && this._renewedAt != null && this.env.now() - this._renewedAt < this.o.ttlMs / 2) return;
+         let r;
+         try { r = await cloudRenewLease(this.uid, this.id, this.epoch, { now: this.env.now(), ttl: this.o.ttlMs }); }
+         catch (err) { this._offline(err); return; }
+         if (!this._live(g)) return;
+         if (r.ok) this._renewedAt = this.env.now();
+         if (!r.ok) {
+             if (r.absent) { this.epoch = null; this.createLease = { ...who(this), epoch: newEpoch(), until: this.env.now() + this.o.ttlMs }; return; }
+             this._serial(() => this._lose(r.holder));
+             return;
+         }
+         if (this.state.cloud === "offline") this._set({ cloud: this.dirty ? "saving" : "saved", error: null });
+         if (r.want && r.want.device !== this.deviceId && this.state.mode === "editing") this._handToDevice(r.want);
+     }
+     _watch() {
+         if (this._unwatch || !this.uid) return;
+         try {
+             this._unwatch = cloudWatchManifest(this.uid, this.id, (m) => this._onManifest(m), () => {});
+         } catch (err) { this._unwatch = null; }
+     }
+     _onManifest(m) {
+         if (!m || this.stopped || !this.hasLock) return;
+         const L = m.lease || null;
+         if (this.state.mode === "editing" && this.epoch) {
+             if (L && String(L.epoch) !== String(this.epoch)) this._serial(() => this._lose(L));
+             else if (L && L.want && L.want.device !== this.deviceId && L.until > 0) this._handToDevice(L.want);
+             return;
+         }
+         if (this.state.why !== "device" || this.state.mode === "handing") return;
+         if (this._maybeTake(L)) return;
+         if (L && L.device && L.device !== this.deviceId && L.until > 0 && (!this.state.holder || this.state.holder.device !== L.device)) {
+             this._set({ holder: { device: L.device, name: L.name || "another device" } });
+         }
+         if (!sameHead(headOf(m), this.lastHead)) this._scheduleFollow(() => this._followCloud());
+     }
+     _maybeTake(L) {
+         if (this.stopped || !this.hasLock || this.epoch || (this.state.mode !== "readonly" && this.state.mode !== "taking") || this.state.why !== "device" || !this.env.visible()) return false;
+         const now = this.env.now();
+         let free = leaseState(L, { device: this.deviceId, now, skewMs: this.o.skewMs }).free;
+         if (!free) {
+             const key = `${L.epoch}|${L.until}|${L.to || ""}`;
+             if (!this._seen || this._seen.key !== key) this._seen = { key, at: now };
+             else free = now - this._seen.at >= this.o.ttlMs;
+         }
+         if (!free) return false;
+         this._takeLease(false, L ? { epoch: L.epoch, until: L.until } : null);
+         return true;
+     }
+     async _lose(holder) {
+         if (this.state.mode !== "editing" && this.state.mode !== "handing") return;
+         const g = this.gen;
+         this.editor.setReadOnly(true);
+         const ok = await this.editor.flush();
+         if (!this._live(g)) return;
+         if (!ok) await this._keepUnsaved();
+         this._readOnlyDevice(holder);
+         this.onNotice({ kind: "lost", message: `Editing moved to ${(holder && holder.name) || "another device"}` });
+         await this._followCloud();
+     }
+     async _handToDevice(want) {
+         if (this.state.mode !== "editing" || !this.epoch || this._handingDev) return;
+         this._handingDev = true;
+         const g = this.gen, epoch = this.epoch;
+         try {
+             this._set({ mode: "handing" });
+             this.editor.setReadOnly(true);
+             const ok = await this.editor.flush();
+             if (!this._live(g)) return;
+             if (!ok) await this._keepUnsaved();
+             this.editor.setSaving(false);
+             if (this.busy) await this.busy;
+             if (this.dirty && this._live(g)) await this._push({ why: "handover", force: true });
+             if (this.busy) await this.busy;
+             if (!this._live(g)) return;
+             try { await cloudReleaseLease(this.uid, this.id, epoch, { to: want.device }); } catch (err) {  }
+             this._readOnlyDevice({ device: want.device, name: want.name });
+             this.onNotice({ kind: "handed", message: `Handed over to ${want.name || "another device"}` });
+         } finally { this._handingDev = false; }
+     }
+     async _takeLease(force, ifLease = null) {
+         if (this._takingLease || this.stopped) return;
+         this._takingLease = true;
+         try {
+             const r = await cloudTakeLease(this.uid, this.id, who(this), { force, ifLease, now: this.env.now(), ttl: this.o.ttlMs, skewMs: this.o.skewMs });
+             if (r.ok) {
+                 this.asking = false;
+                 this.epoch = r.lease ? r.lease.epoch : null;
+                 this._serial(() => this._promote());
+             }
+         } catch (err) { this._offline(err); }
+         finally { this._takingLease = false; }
+     }
+     async _promote() {
+         const g = this.gen;
+         if (!this._live(g)) return;
+         this.editor.setSaving(false);
+         this.editor.setReadOnly(true);
+         let res;
+         try { res = await this._reconcile(g, { opening: false }); }
+         catch (err) {
+             if (!this._live(g)) return;
+             if (isNetwork(err)) { this._offline(err); this.needOpen = true; this._edit(g); }
+             else this._blocked(`Couldn't catch up with the cloud copy: ${String((err && err.message) || err).slice(0, 80)}`);
+             return;
+         }
+         if (!this._live(g) || res !== "ok") return;
+         this._edit(g);
+         this._watch();
+     }
+     async _followCloud() {
+         const g = this.gen;
+         if (!this._live(g) || this.state.why !== "device" || this.state.mode !== "readonly") return;
+         try {
+             const m = await cloudGetCanvasMeta(this.uid, this.id);
+             if (!this._live(g)) return;
+             const res = await this._reconcile(g, { manifest: m, opening: false });
+             if (m && res === "ok") this._maybeTake(m.lease || null);
+         } catch (err) { if (this._live(g)) this._offline(err); }
+     }
+     async _reloadFollow() {
+         if (this.stopped || this.hasLock) return false;
+         if (!this._followData) {
+             await this.editor.refreshMeta();
+             if (!this.stopped && !this.hasLock) this.onNotice({ kind: "reloaded" });
+             return false;
+         }
+         this._followData = false;
+         try { this.lastReloadMs = (await this.editor.reload({ keepCamera: true })) || 0; }
+         catch (err) { this._followData = true; throw err; }
+         if (!this.stopped && !this.hasLock) this.onNotice({ kind: "reloaded" });
+         return true;
+     }
+     _scheduleFollow(fn) {
+         if (!this.env.visible()) { this._followOwed = fn; return; }
+         if (this._following) { this._followNext = fn; return; }
+         if (this._followPending) return;
+         const gap = Math.max(this.o.followMinMs, this.lastReloadMs * this.o.followCostFactor);
+         const wait = Math.max(0, this.followAt + gap - this.env.now());
+         this._followPending = true;
+         this._after(wait, () => {
+             this._followPending = false;
+             this._following = true;
+             this._serial(async () => {
+                 try { if ((await fn()) !== false) this.followAt = this.env.now(); } finally {
+                     this._following = false;
+                     const next = this._followNext;
+                     this._followNext = null;
+                     if (next && !this.stopped) this._scheduleFollow(next);
+                 }
+             });
+         });
+     }
+     _push({ why = "sync", thumbs = null, force = false } = {}) {
+         if (!this.uid || this.busy) return Promise.resolve({ ok: false, busy: !!this.busy });
+         if (this.state.mode !== "editing" && this.state.mode !== "handing") return Promise.resolve({ ok: false, readOnly: true });
+         if (!force && this.env.now() < this.fail.until) return Promise.resolve({ ok: false, backoff: true });
+         let settle;
+         const busy = new Promise((r) => { settle = r; });
+         this.busy = busy;
+         const done = () => { if (this.busy === busy) this.busy = null; settle(); };
+         const run = (async () => {
+             let raw = null;
+             try {
+                 const entry0 = this.discarded ? null : this.getEntry();
+                 if (!entry0) { this._set({ cloud: "local" }); return { ok: false, skipped: "never saved" }; }
+                 const source = await this.editor.pushSource();
+                 if (!source) return { ok: false, skipped: "nothing on disk" };
+                 const t0 = perfNow();
+                 this.dirty = false;
+                 this._set({ cloud: "saving" });
+                 const entry = { ...entry0, savedAt: new Date().toISOString(), ...(this.editor.stats ? this.editor.stats() : {}) };
+                 let started;
+                 const began = new Promise((r) => { started = r; });
+                 const creating = this.epoch ? null : this.createLease;
+                 raw = cloudPushCanvas2(this.uid, entry, source, {
+                     deviceId: this.deviceId, epoch: this.epoch, lease: creating,
+                     thumbs, dropBelow: source.dropBelow || 0, laneMs: this.o.pushDeadlineMs, onStart: started,
+                 });
+                 raw.then(done, done);
+                 const landed = (r) => {
+                     if (creating && !this.epoch && this.createLease === creating) { this.epoch = creating.epoch; this.createLease = null; this._watch(); }
+                     if (r && r.head) this.lastHead = r.head;
+                 };
+                 raw.then(landed, () => {});
+                 try {
+                     const r = await Promise.race([raw, began.then(() => withTimeout(raw, this.o.pushDeadlineMs, "the push did not finish in 5 min"))]);
+                     landed(r);
+                     this.fail = { count: 0, until: 0 };
+                     this._set({ cloud: this.dirty ? "saving" : "saved", error: null });
+                     this.onPerf("cloudSave", t0, { where: why, ...r, head: undefined });
+                     return { ok: true, r };
+                 } catch (err) {
+                     this.dirty = true;
+                     if (err && (err.name === "SyncConflict" || err.name === "LeaseLost")) {
+                         this.onPerf("cloudSaveRefused", t0, { where: why, err: err.name });
+                         this._serial(() => this._lose(err.holder || null));
+                         return { ok: false, err };
+                     }
+                     const f = this.fail;
+                     f.count += 1;
+                     const waitMs = Math.min(30 * 60000, 60000 * Math.pow(2, f.count - 1));
+                     f.until = this.env.now() + waitMs;
+                     const wait = waitMs >= 60000 ? `${Math.round(waitMs / 60000)} min` : `${Math.round(waitMs / 1000)} s`;
+                     const msg = String((err && err.message) || err).slice(0, 80);
+                     this._set({ cloud: "error", error: `Cloud sync failed: ${msg}. Saved in this browser only; next try in ${wait}.` });
+                     this.onPerf("cloudSaveFail", t0, { where: why, tries: f.count, waitMs });
+                     return { ok: false, err };
+                 }
+             } finally {
+                 if (!raw) done();
+             }
+         })();
+         return run;
+     }
+     _set(patch) {
+         this.state = { ...this.state, ...patch };
+         try { this.onState(this.state); } catch (err) {  }
+     }
+     _serial(fn) {
+         const p = this._chain.then(() => (this.stopped ? null : fn()));
+         this._chain = p.catch(() => null);
+         return p;
+     }
+     _after(ms, fn) {
+         const t = setTimeout(() => { this._timers.delete(t); if (!this.stopped) fn(); }, ms);
+         this._timers.add(t);
+         return t;
+     }
+     _every(ms, fn) {
+         const t = setInterval(() => { if (!this.stopped) fn(); }, ms);
+         this._timers.add(t);
+         return t;
+     }
+ }
//// src/cloud/canvasSync.js
//// src/cloud/canvasSync.js:16
  import {
+     collection, doc, getDoc, getDocs, setDoc, deleteDoc, writeBatch, runTransaction, onSnapshot, Bytes,
  } from "firebase/firestore";

+ export function cloudSaveCanvas(uid, entry, json, thumbs = null, { laneMs = LANE_MS } = {}) {
+     return inLane((run) => saveCanvasLz1(uid, entry, json, thumbs, run), { ms: laneMs });

+ async function saveCanvasLz1(uid, entry, json, thumbs, run) {
      const t0 = perfNow();
      const { codec, chunks } = await encodeCanvasPayloadAsync(json);
      const tPack = perfNow();
      let bytes = 0;
      for (const c of chunks) bytes += c.length;
      const prev = await getDoc(canvasDoc(uid, entry.id));
+     if (prev.exists() && prev.data().format === FORMAT2_CLOUD) throw new SyncConflict("a kobin-2 copy exists", headOf(prev.data()));
      const prevThumbs = prev.exists() ? prev.data().thumbs || null : null;
      const mergedThumbs = thumbs || prevThumbs
          ? boundedThumbs({ ...(prevThumbs || {}), ...(thumbs || {}) })
          : null;
      for (let start = 0; start < chunks.length; start += PARTS_PER_COMMIT) {
+         if (run.dead) throw pushDead();
+         run.tick();
          const batch = writeBatch(getDb());
          chunks.slice(start, start + PARTS_PER_COMMIT).forEach((data, j) => {
              batch.set(partDoc(uid, entry.id, start + j), { data: Bytes.fromUint8Array(data) });
          });
          await batch.commit();
      }
+     await runTransaction(getDb(), async (t) => {
+         const snap = await t.get(canvasDoc(uid, entry.id));
+         const cur = snap.exists() ? snap.data() : null;
+         if (cur && cur.format === FORMAT2_CLOUD) throw new SyncConflict("a kobin-2 copy exists", headOf(cur));
+         t.set(canvasDoc(uid, entry.id), {
+             name: entry.name || "Untitled canvas",
+             savedAt: entry.savedAt || new Date().toISOString(),
+             levels: entry.levels || 0,
+             strokes: entry.strokes || 0,
+             size: json.length,
+             parts: chunks.length,
+             codec,
+             ...(mergedThumbs ? { thumbs: mergedThumbs } : {}),
+         });
+         for (let i = chunks.length; i < ((cur && cur.parts) || 0); i++) t.delete(partDoc(uid, entry.id, i));
      });
      return { packMs: +(tPack - t0).toFixed(1), putMs: +(perfNow() - tPack).toFixed(1), bytes, parts: chunks.length };
  }

  export async function cloudLoadCanvas(uid, id) {
      const parent = await getDoc(canvasDoc(uid, id));
      if (!parent.exists()) return null;
      const meta = parent.data();
+     if (meta.format === FORMAT2_CLOUD) {
+         const pulled = await cloudPullCanvas2(uid, id);
+         const doc = pulled && docFromStore(pulled);
+         return doc ? { json: JSON.stringify(doc), thumbs: meta.thumbs || null, meta: { id, ...meta } } : null;
+     }
      const n = meta.parts || 0;
      const reads = [];
      for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) reads.push(getDoc(partDoc(uid, id, i)));
      const snaps = await Promise.all(reads);
      const parts = snaps.map((p) => {
          if (!p.exists()) return "";
          const d = p.data().data;
          return d && typeof d.toUint8Array === "function" ? d.toUint8Array() : d;
      });
      return {
          json: await decodeCanvasPayloadAsync(parts, meta.codec),
          thumbs: meta.thumbs || null,
          meta: { id, ...meta },
      };
  }

  export async function cloudDeleteCanvas(uid, id) {
      const parent = await getDoc(canvasDoc(uid, id));
+     const m = parent.exists() ? parent.data() : null;
+     const refs = [];
+     if (m && m.format === FORMAT2_CLOUD) for (const c of chunksOf(m)) for (let i = 0; i < c.parts; i++) refs.push(partDoc2(uid, id, c.chunk, i));
+     else for (let i = 0; i < ((m && m.parts) || 0); i++) refs.push(partDoc(uid, id, i));
+     await commitAll(refs, DELETES_PER_COMMIT, (b, ref) => b.delete(ref));
+     await deleteDoc(canvasDoc(uid, id));
  }

//// src/cloud/canvasSync.js:293
+ async function commitAll(items, per, fn, before = null) {
      for (let i = 0; i < items.length; i += per) {
+         if (before) await before();
          const batch = writeBatch(getDb());
          for (const it of items.slice(i, i + per)) fn(batch, it);
          await batch.commit();
      }
  }
//// src/cloud/canvasSync.js:307
+ const LANE_MS = 5 * 60000;
+ let lane = Promise.resolve();
+ function inLane(fn, { ms = LANE_MS, onStart = null } = {}) {
+     const run = { dead: false, tick: () => {} };
+     const go = () => {
+         if (onStart) onStart();
+         let t = null, over = false, release;
+         const released = new Promise((r) => { release = r; });
+         run.tick = () => {
+             if (over || run.dead) return;
+             clearTimeout(t);
+             t = setTimeout(() => { run.dead = true; release(); }, ms);
+         };
+         run.tick();
+         const p = Promise.resolve().then(() => fn(run));
+         const end = () => { over = true; clearTimeout(t); release(); };
+         p.then(end, end);
+         return { p, released };
+     };
+     const started = lane.then(go, go);
+     lane = started.then((x) => x.released);
+     return started.then((x) => x.p);
+ }
+ const pushDead = () => Object.assign(new Error("the push passed its deadline and was let go"), { name: "PushDead" });
+ function chunksOf(m) {
+     const out = [];
+     for (const f of Object.values((m && m.frames) || {})) out.push({ chunk: f.chunk, parts: f.parts });
+     for (const c of (m && m.log) || []) out.push({ chunk: c.chunk, parts: c.parts });
+     return out;
+ }
+ const partsOf = (uid, id, chunks) => {
+     const refs = [];
+     for (const c of chunks) for (let i = 0; i < c.parts; i++) refs.push(partDoc2(uid, id, c.chunk, i));
+     return refs;
+ };

+ export function cloudPushCanvas2(uid, entry, source, opts = {}) {
+     return inLane((run) => pushCanvas2(uid, entry, source, opts, run), { ms: opts.laneMs || LANE_MS, onStart: opts.onStart || null });

+ async function pushCanvas2(uid, entry, source, { deviceId = "", epoch = null, lease = null, thumbs = null, dropBelow = 0 } = {}, run = { dead: false }) {
      const t0 = perfNow();
+     const setSync = source.setSync || (async () => true);
+     const col = CANVAS_COLLECTION;
+     const ref = canvasDoc(uid, entry.id);
+     const tag = String(epoch || (lease && lease.epoch) || newEpoch());
+     const nonce = Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 7);
      const codec = compression();
+     const prev = await getDoc(ref);
      const pm = prev.exists() ? prev.data() : null;
+     const mirror = pm && pm.format === FORMAT2_CLOUD ? pm : null;
      const cloudFrames = mirror ? { ...(mirror.frames || {}) } : {};
      const cloudLog = mirror ? [...(mirror.log || [])] : [];
+     const cloudSeq = mirror ? headOf(mirror).seq : 0;
+     const cloudFrameSeq = {};
+     for (const [fid, c] of Object.entries(cloudFrames)) cloudFrameSeq[fid] = c.seq || 0;
+     let header, frames, entries;
+     if (source.read) {
+         const st = await source.read(cloudFrameSeq, cloudSeq);
+         if (!st) throw new Error("nothing in the local store to push");
+         ({ header, frames, entries } = st);
+     } else {
+         header = source.header;
+         const ls = header.frameSeq || {};
+         const need = (header.frameIds || []).filter((fid) => !(fid in cloudFrameSeq) || cloudFrameSeq[fid] !== (ls[fid] || 0));
+         frames = need.length ? await source.readFrames(need) : [];
+         entries = (await source.readLog(cloudSeq)).filter((e) => e.seq <= (header.seq || 0));
+     }
+     const base = intentLanded(header.intent, pm, col) ? { epoch: header.intent.epoch, seq: header.intent.seq || 0, col }
+         : header.base && header.base.col === col ? header.base : null;
+     const check = (cur) => {
+         if (!cur || cur.format !== FORMAT2_CLOUD) return;
+         if (!base || !sameHead(headOf(cur), base)) throw new SyncConflict("the cloud copy moved", headOf(cur));
+         if (!cur.lease || String(cur.lease.epoch) !== String(epoch)) throw new LeaseLost(cur.lease);
+     };
+     check(pm);
      const frameIds = header.frameIds || [];
+     const writes = [], made = [];
      const nextFrames = {};
      let bytes = 0;
+     const sent = new Set(frames.map((f) => f.frameId));
+     for (const fid of frameIds) if (cloudFrames[fid] && !sent.has(fid)) nextFrames[fid] = cloudFrames[fid];
      for (const f of frames) {
          const zipped = await codec.gzip(packFrameChunk(f));
          const parts = splitParts(zipped);
+         const chunkId = `f_${chunkKey(f.frameId)}_${f.seq}_${chunkKey(tag)}_${nonce}`;
          parts.forEach((p, i) => writes.push({ ref: partDoc2(uid, entry.id, chunkId, i), data: { data: Bytes.fromUint8Array(p) } }));
+         made.push({ chunk: chunkId, parts: parts.length });
          nextFrames[f.frameId] = { chunk: chunkId, parts: parts.length, seq: f.seq, bytes: zipped.length };
          bytes += zipped.length;
      }
      let nextLog = cloudLog;
      if (entries.length) {
          const zipped = await codec.gzip(packLogChunk(entries));
          const parts = splitParts(zipped);
+         const chunkId = `l_${entries[0].seq}_${entries[entries.length - 1].seq}_${chunkKey(tag)}_${nonce}`;
          parts.forEach((p, i) => writes.push({ ref: partDoc2(uid, entry.id, chunkId, i), data: { data: Bytes.fromUint8Array(p) } }));
+         made.push({ chunk: chunkId, parts: parts.length });
          nextLog = [...cloudLog, { chunk: chunkId, parts: parts.length, seqFrom: entries[0].seq, seqTo: entries[entries.length - 1].seq, bytes: zipped.length }];
          bytes += zipped.length;
      }
+     if (dropBelow > 0) nextLog = nextLog.filter((c) => c.seqTo >= dropBelow);
+     const seq = header.seq || 0;
+     const next = base && !made.length && seq === (base.seq || 0) ? { epoch: base.epoch, seq, col }
+         : { epoch: base && seq === (base.seq || 0) ? `${tag}.${nonce}` : tag, seq, col };
+     const pending = [...(header.pending || []), ...made];
+     const noted = await setSync({ addPending: made, intent: { ...next, chunks: made.map((c) => c.chunk) } });
+     if (noted === "fenced") throw new LeaseLost(null);
      const tPack = perfNow();
+     const stillOurs = async () => {
+         if (run.dead) throw pushDead();
+         run.tick();
+         if (source.alive && !(await source.alive())) throw new LeaseLost(null);
+     };
+     await commitAll(writes, PARTS_PER_COMMIT, (b, w) => b.set(w.ref, w.data), stillOurs);
+     if (run.dead) throw pushDead();
+     run.tick();
      let total = 0;
      for (const f of Object.values(nextFrames)) total += f.bytes || 0;
      for (const c of nextLog) total += c.bytes || 0;
+     const manifestOf = (cur) => {
+         const prevThumbs = cur ? cur.thumbs || null : null;
+         const mergedThumbs = thumbs || prevThumbs ? boundedThumbs({ ...(prevThumbs || {}), ...(thumbs || {}) }) : null;
+         const keepLease = cur && cur.format === FORMAT2_CLOUD ? cur.lease || null : null;
+         return {
+             format: FORMAT2_CLOUD, codec: codec.name, device: deviceId,
+             version: header.version || 1, meta: header.meta || {}, camera: header.camera || null, crossings: header.crossings || {},
+             frameIds, frames: nextFrames, log: nextLog, seq: next.seq, head: { epoch: next.epoch, seq: next.seq },
+             lease: keepLease || lease || null,
+             name: entry.name || "Untitled canvas",
+             savedAt: entry.savedAt || new Date().toISOString(),
+             levels: entry.levels || 0, strokes: entry.strokes || 0, size: total,
+             ...(mergedThumbs ? { thumbs: mergedThumbs } : {}),
+         };
+     };
+     let replaced = [];
+     let lz1Parts = 0;
+     let manifest = null;
+     try {
+         await runTransaction(getDb(), async (t) => {
+             const snap = await t.get(ref);
+             const cur = snap.exists() ? snap.data() : null;
+             check(cur);
+             if (!cur !== !pm) throw new SyncConflict("the cloud copy appeared or went during the push", headOf(cur));
+             manifest = manifestOf(cur);
+             const keep = new Set(chunksOf(manifest).map((c) => c.chunk));
+             replaced = cur && cur.format === FORMAT2_CLOUD
+                 ? chunksOf(cur).filter((c) => !keep.has(c.chunk))
+                 : [];
+             lz1Parts = cur && cur.format !== FORMAT2_CLOUD ? cur.parts || 0 : 0;
+             t.set(ref, manifest);
+         });
+     } catch (err) {
+         let landed = null;
+         try { const s = await getDoc(ref); landed = s.exists() ? s.data() : null; } catch (e2) {  }
+         if (!(landed && sameHead(headOf(landed), next))) {
+             if (landed) await sweep(uid, entry.id, pending, landed, setSync);
+             throw err;
+         }
+         manifest = landed;
+     }
+     const named = new Set(chunksOf(manifest).map((c) => c.chunk));
+     const stale = [...pending.filter((c) => !named.has(c.chunk)), ...replaced];
+     await setSync({ base: next, intent: null, addPending: replaced });
+     const deletes = partsOf(uid, entry.id, stale);
+     for (let i = 0; i < lz1Parts; i++) deletes.push(partDoc(uid, entry.id, i));
+     if (run.dead) {
+         await setSync({ dropPending: made.map((c) => c.chunk) });
+         return { packMs: +(tPack - t0).toFixed(1), putMs: +(perfNow() - tPack).toFixed(1), bytes, frames: frames.length, entries: entries.length, parts: writes.length, deleted: 0, head: next };
+     }
+     run.tick();
+     await commitAll(deletes, DELETES_PER_COMMIT, (b, r) => b.delete(r), async () => run.tick());
+     await setSync({ dropPending: [...stale.map((c) => c.chunk), ...made.map((c) => c.chunk)] });
+     return { packMs: +(tPack - t0).toFixed(1), putMs: +(perfNow() - tPack).toFixed(1), bytes, frames: frames.length, entries: entries.length, parts: writes.length, deleted: deletes.length, head: next };
+ }
//// src/cloud/canvasSync.js:504
+ async function sweep(uid, id, pending, manifest, setSync) {
+     const named = new Set(chunksOf(manifest).map((c) => c.chunk));
+     const gone = pending.filter((c) => !named.has(c.chunk));
+     try {
+         await commitAll(partsOf(uid, id, gone), DELETES_PER_COMMIT, (b, r) => b.delete(r));
+         await setSync({ dropPending: gone.map((c) => c.chunk) });
+     } catch (err) {  }
  }
//// src/cloud/canvasSync.js:520
+ export async function cloudPullCanvas2(uid, id, { have = null } = {}) {
+     const manifestRef = canvasDoc(uid, id);
+     const partRef = partDoc2;
+     const parent = await getDoc(manifestRef);
      if (!parent.exists()) return null;
      const m = parent.data();
      if (m.format !== FORMAT2_CLOUD) return null;
      const readChunk = async (c) => {
          const reads = [];
+         for (let i = 0; i < c.parts; i++) reads.push(getDoc(partRef(uid, id, c.chunk, i)));
          const snaps = await Promise.all(reads);
+         const parts = snaps.map((p, i) => { if (!p.exists()) throw new Error(`cloud copy is missing part ${c.chunk}~${i}`); return p.data().data.toUint8Array(); });
          return gunzipAs(m.codec, joinParts(parts));
      };
+     const haveSeq = have ? have.frameSeq || {} : null;
      const frames = {};
      for (const [fid, c] of Object.entries(m.frames || {})) {
+         if (haveSeq && fid in haveSeq && (haveSeq[fid] || 0) === (c.seq || 0)) continue;
          const f = unpackFrameChunk(await readChunk(c));
          frames[fid] = { objects: f.objects, geo: f.geo, seq: f.seq };
      }
+     const from = have ? have.seq || 0 : -1;
      let entries = [];
+     for (const c of (m.log || []).slice().sort((a, b) => a.seqFrom - b.seqFrom)) {
+         if (c.seqTo <= from) continue;
+         entries = entries.concat(unpackLogChunk(await readChunk(c)).filter((e) => e.seq > from));
+     }
      const frameSeq = {};
      for (const [fid, c] of Object.entries(m.frames || {})) frameSeq[fid] = c.seq || 0;
      const header = {
          format: "boundless-drawing", version: m.version || 1, meta: m.meta || {}, camera: m.camera || null, crossings: m.crossings || {},
+         name: m.name, seq: m.seq || 0, frameSeq, frameIds: m.frameIds || Object.keys(m.frames || {}), savedAt: m.savedAt,
+         strokes: m.strokes || 0, levels: m.levels || 0,
      };
+     const logFloor = (m.log || []).length ? Math.min(...m.log.map((c) => c.seqFrom)) : (m.seq || 0) + 1;
+     return { header, frames, entries, logFloor, head: headOf(m), full: !have, thumbs: m.thumbs || null, meta: { id, ...m } };
+ }
//// src/cloud/canvasSync.js:569
+ const leaseFor = ({ device, tab, name }, now, ttl) => ({ device, tab: tab || null, name: name || null, epoch: newEpoch(), until: now + ttl });
//// src/cloud/canvasSync.js:576
+ export async function cloudTakeLease(uid, id, who, { force = false, ifLease = null, now = Date.now(), ttl = LEASE_TTL_MS, skewMs = LEASE_SKEW_MS } = {}) {
+     const ref = canvasDoc(uid, id);
+     return runTransaction(getDb(), async (t) => {
+         const snap = await t.get(ref);
+         if (!snap.exists()) return { ok: true, absent: true, lease: null, head: null };
+         const cur = snap.data();
+         if (cur.format !== FORMAT2_CLOUD) return { ok: true, legacy: true, lease: null, head: null };
+         const st = leaseState(cur.lease, { device: who.device, now, skewMs });
+         const watched = ifLease && cur.lease && String(cur.lease.epoch) === String(ifLease.epoch) && cur.lease.until === ifLease.until;
+         if (!st.free && !force && !watched) return { ok: false, holder: st.holder, head: headOf(cur) };
+         const lease = leaseFor(who, now, ttl);
+         t.update(ref, { lease });
+         return { ok: true, lease, head: headOf(cur) };
+     });
+ }
//// src/cloud/canvasSync.js:594
+ export async function cloudRenewLease(uid, id, epoch, { now = Date.now(), ttl = LEASE_TTL_MS } = {}) {
+     const ref = canvasDoc(uid, id);
+     return runTransaction(getDb(), async (t) => {
+         const snap = await t.get(ref);
+         if (!snap.exists()) return { ok: false, absent: true, holder: null };
+         const cur = snap.data();
+         const L = cur.lease;
+         if (!L || String(L.epoch) !== String(epoch) || !(L.until > 0)) return { ok: false, lost: true, holder: L || null, head: headOf(cur) };
+         t.update(ref, { lease: { ...L, until: now + ttl } });
+         return { ok: true, want: L.want || null, head: headOf(cur) };
+     });
+ }
//// src/cloud/canvasSync.js:607
+ export async function cloudReleaseLease(uid, id, epoch, { to = null } = {}) {
+     const ref = canvasDoc(uid, id);
+     return runTransaction(getDb(), async (t) => {
+         const snap = await t.get(ref);
+         if (!snap.exists()) return false;
+         const L = snap.data().lease;
+         if (!L || String(L.epoch) !== String(epoch)) return false;
+         t.update(ref, { lease: { ...L, until: 0, to: to || null } });
+         return true;
+     });
+ }
//// src/cloud/canvasSync.js:619
+ export async function cloudAskLease(uid, id, who, { now = Date.now(), ttl = LEASE_TTL_MS, skewMs = LEASE_SKEW_MS } = {}) {
+     const ref = canvasDoc(uid, id);
+     return runTransaction(getDb(), async (t) => {
+         const snap = await t.get(ref);
+         if (!snap.exists()) return { ok: true, absent: true, lease: null, head: null };
+         const cur = snap.data();
+         const st = leaseState(cur.lease, { device: who.device, now, skewMs });
+         if (st.free) {
+             const lease = leaseFor(who, now, ttl);
+             t.update(ref, { lease });
+             return { ok: true, lease, head: headOf(cur) };
+         }
+         t.update(ref, { lease: { ...cur.lease, want: { device: who.device, tab: who.tab || null, name: who.name || null, at: now } } });
+         return { ok: false, asked: true, holder: cur.lease, head: headOf(cur) };
+     });
+ }
