//// src/cloud/storageLocks.js  (new file)
+ const abortError = () => Object.assign(new Error("The lock request was aborted"), { name: "AbortError" });
+ const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+ export function makeStorageLocks({ storage = typeof localStorage !== "undefined" ? localStorage : null, heartbeatMs = 1000, staleMs = 10000, pollMs = 250, settleMs = 60, now = () => Date.now(), target = typeof window !== "undefined" ? window : null } = {}) {
+     const key = (name) => `boundless.lock.${name}`;
+     const read = (name) => { try { return JSON.parse(storage.getItem(key(name)) || "null"); } catch (err) { return null; } };
+     const write = (name, owner) => { try { storage.setItem(key(name), JSON.stringify({ owner, at: now() })); } catch (err) {  } };
+     const isFree = (v) => !v || !(now() - v.at <= staleMs);
+     const held = new Map();
+     if (target && target.addEventListener) {
+         target.addEventListener("pagehide", () => {
+             for (const [owner, name] of held) { const v = read(name); if (v && v.owner === owner) { try { storage.removeItem(key(name)); } catch (err) {  } } }
+         });
+     }
+     return {
+         request(name, opts, cb) {
+             if (typeof opts === "function") { cb = opts; opts = {}; }
+             opts = opts || {};
+             const owner = Math.random().toString(36).slice(2) + now().toString(36);
+             return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
+                 let beat = null;
+                 let over = false;
+                 const end = (fn, v) => { if (over) return; over = true; clearInterval(beat); held.delete(owner); fn(v); };
+                 const letGo = () => { const v = read(name); if (v && v.owner === owner) { try { storage.removeItem(key(name)); } catch (err) {  } } };
+                 const granted = () => {
+                     held.set(owner, name);
+                     beat = setInterval(() => {
+                         const v = read(name);
+                         if (!v || v.owner !== owner) { end(reject, abortError()); return; }
+                         write(name, owner);
+                     }, heartbeatMs);
+                     Promise.resolve()
+                         .then(() => cb({ name, mode: "exclusive" }))
+                         .then((v) => { if (!over) letGo(); end(resolve, v); }, (e) => { if (!over) letGo(); end(reject, e); });
+                 };
+                 const attempt = async () => {
+                     if (over) return;
+                     if (opts.signal && opts.signal.aborted) { end(reject, abortError()); return; }
+                     if (opts.steal || isFree(read(name))) {
+                         write(name, owner);
+                         await sleep(settleMs);
+                         if (opts.signal && opts.signal.aborted) { letGo(); end(reject, abortError()); return; }
+                         const v = read(name);
+                         if (v && v.owner === owner) { granted(); return; }
+                     }
+                     if (opts.ifAvailable) {
+                         over = true;
+                         Promise.resolve().then(() => cb(null)).then(resolve, reject);
+                         return;
+                     }
+                     setTimeout(attempt, pollMs);
+                 };
+                 if (opts.signal) opts.signal.addEventListener("abort", () => { if (!beat) end(reject, abortError()); });
+                 attempt();
+             });
+         },
+     };
+ }
//// src/cloud/syncCore.js  (new file)
+ export const LEASE_TTL_MS = 90 * 1000;
+ export const LEASE_BEAT_MS = 20 * 1000;
+ export const LEASE_SKEW_MS = 5 * 60 * 1000;
+ export function newEpoch() {
+     return Date.now().toString(36).slice(-5) + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 7);
+ }
+ export function headOf(manifest) {
+     if (!manifest) return null;
+     const h = manifest.head;
+     if (h && h.seq != null) return { epoch: h.epoch == null ? 0 : h.epoch, seq: h.seq || 0 };
+     return { epoch: 0, seq: manifest.seq || 0 };
+ }
+ export function sameHead(a, b) {
+     return !!a && !!b && String(a.epoch) === String(b.epoch) && (a.seq || 0) === (b.seq || 0);
+ }
+ export function leaseState(lease, { device, now = Date.now(), skewMs = LEASE_SKEW_MS }) {
+     if (!lease || !lease.device) return { free: true, mine: false, holder: null };
+     if (!(lease.until > 0)) return lease.to && lease.to !== device ? { free: false, mine: false, holder: lease, reserved: true } : { free: true, mine: lease.device === device, holder: null };
+     if (lease.device === device) return { free: true, mine: true, holder: lease };
+     if (now > lease.until + skewMs) return { free: true, mine: false, holder: null };
+     return { free: false, mine: false, holder: lease };
+ }
+ function chunkNames(m) {
+     const out = new Set();
+     for (const f of Object.values((m && m.frames) || {})) out.add(f.chunk);
+     for (const c of (m && m.log) || []) out.add(c.chunk);
+     return out;
+ }
+ export function intentLanded(intent, manifest, col) {
+     if (!intent || intent.col !== col || !manifest) return false;
+     if (sameHead(intent, headOf(manifest))) return true;
+     const named = chunkNames(manifest);
+     return (intent.chunks || []).some((c) => named.has(c));
+ }
+ export function decideOpen({ local, cloud, deviceId, col }) {
+     if (!cloud) return { action: "none", base: local && local.base && local.base.col === col ? local.base : null, why: "no cloud copy" };
+     if (cloud.format !== "kobin-2") return { action: "legacy", base: null, why: "an lz1 cloud copy" };
+     const head = { ...headOf(cloud), col };
+     if (!local) return { action: "pull", full: true, base: null, why: "no local copy" };
+     const kobin2 = local.store === "kobin-2";
+     let base = kobin2 && local.base && local.base.col === col ? local.base : null;
+     if (kobin2 && intentLanded(local.intent, cloud, col)) base = { epoch: local.intent.epoch, seq: local.intent.seq || 0, col };
+     const seq = kobin2 ? local.seq || 0 : 0;
+     if (!base) {
+         if (!(local.strokes > 0)) return { action: "pull", full: true, base: null, why: "the local copy is empty" };
+         if (!cloud.head && cloud.device && cloud.device === deviceId) base = { epoch: 0, seq: cloud.seq || 0, col };
+         else if (kobin2 && sameFrames(local, cloud) && seq === head.seq) base = head;
+         else {
+             const cloudAt = String(cloud.savedAt || ""), localAt = String(local.savedAt || "");
+             if (cloudAt > localAt || !kobin2) return { action: "stash", full: true, base: null, why: "unknown ancestry, the cloud copy is newer" };
+             return { action: "conflict", base: null, why: "unknown ancestry, this copy is newer" };
+         }
+     }
+     const ahead = seq > (base.seq || 0);
+     const moved = !sameHead(head, base);
+     if (!ahead && !moved) return { action: "sync", base, why: "in step" };
+     if (ahead && !moved) return { action: "push", base, why: "this device is ahead" };
+     if (!ahead && moved) return { action: "pull", full: false, base, why: "the cloud moved" };
+     return { action: "conflict", base, why: "both moved" };
+ }
+ function sameFrames(local, cloud) {
+     const lf = local.frameSeq || {}, cf = cloud.frames || {};
+     const lk = Object.keys(lf), ck = Object.keys(cf);
+     if (lk.length !== ck.length) return false;
+     for (const k of lk) if (!cf[k] || (cf[k].seq || 0) !== (lf[k] || 0)) return false;
+     return true;
+ }
+ export function conflictName(name, deviceName, at = new Date()) {
+     let day = "";
+     try { day = at.toLocaleDateString(undefined, { month: "short", day: "numeric" }); } catch (err) { day = at.toISOString().slice(0, 10); }
+     return `${name || "Untitled canvas"} — edits from ${deviceName || "another device"}, ${day}`;
+ }
+ export function defaultDeviceName(ua = typeof navigator !== "undefined" ? navigator.userAgent : "") {
+     const s = String(ua || "");
+     const browser = /Edg\//.test(s) ? "Edge" : /OPR\//.test(s) ? "Opera" : /Firefox\//.test(s) ? "Firefox"
+         : /CriOS|Chrome\//.test(s) ? "Chrome" : /Safari\//.test(s) ? "Safari" : "Browser";
+     const os = /iPhone/.test(s) ? "iPhone" : /iPad/.test(s) ? "iPad" : /Android/.test(s) ? "Android"
+         : /Windows/.test(s) ? "Windows" : /Mac OS X|Macintosh/.test(s) ? "Mac" : /CrOS/.test(s) ? "ChromeOS" : /Linux/.test(s) ? "Linux" : "";
+     return os ? `${browser} on ${os}` : browser;
+ }

+ export function opFrames(op, out = new Set()) {
+     const add = (k) => { if (k != null) out.add(String(k)); };
+     if (!op) return out;
+     switch (op.op) {
+         case "absent": case "erase": add(op.level); break;
+         case "move": add(op.from); add(op.to); break;
+         case "moveMany": for (const m of op.moves) { add(m.from); add(m.to); } break;
+         case "eraseMany": case "removeMany": for (const r of op.records) add(r.level); break;
+         case "removeFrames": case "eraseFrames": for (const r of op.records) add(r.frame); break;
+         case "deleteMany": for (const p of op.parts) opFrames(p, out); break;
+         case "moveFrames":
+             for (const e of op.frames) {
+                 add(e.id); if (e.from) add(e.from.parent); if (e.to) add(e.to.parent);
+                 for (const m of e.merged || []) { add(m.id); add(m.into); add(m.parent); for (const kd of m.kids || []) add(kd.shadowOf); }
+                 if (e.settled) for (const c of e.settled.carries || []) add(c[1]);
+             }
+             for (const m of op.moves || []) { add(m.from); add(m.to); }
+             break;
+         case "eraseCommit": case "eraseRevert":
+             if (op.strokeRec) add(op.strokeRec.level);
+             for (const st of op.baked || []) {
+                 if (st.removed) add(st.removed.level);
+                 for (const p of st.pieces || []) add(p.level);
+                 for (const r of st.frames || []) add(r.frame);
+             }
+             break;
+         default: break;
+     }
+     return out;
+ }

      _invert(op) {
          switch (op.op) {
              case "add": {
                  const rec = this.removeById(op.id);
                  return rec ? { op: "absent", obj: rec.obj, level: rec.level, index: rec.index } : op;
              }
              case "erase": {
                  this.insertAt(op.obj, op.level, op.index);
                  return { op: "present", id: op.obj.id };
              }
              case "absent": {
                  this.insertAt(op.obj, op.level, op.index);
                  return { op: "add", id: op.obj.id };
              }
              case "present": {
                  const rec = this.removeById(op.id);
                  return rec ? { op: "erase", obj: rec.obj, level: rec.level, index: rec.index } : op;
              }
              case "move": return this._undoMove([op]) && { op: "move", ...this._invertMove(op) };
              case "moveMany": {
                  this._undoMove(op.moves);
                  return { op: "moveMany", moves: op.moves.map((m) => this._invertMove(m)) };
              }
              case "eraseMany": {
                  for (let i = op.records.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
                      const rec = op.records[i];
                      if (!this.getById(rec.obj.id)) this.insertAt(rec.obj, rec.level, rec.index);
                  }
                  return { op: "removeMany", records: op.records };
              }
              case "removeMany": {
                  for (const rec of op.records) this.removeById(rec.obj.id);
                  return { op: "eraseMany", records: op.records };
              }
              case "moveFrames": {
                  for (const e of op.frames) {
+                     const pf = this.lm.frame(e.id);
+                     if (pf && pf.shift && !sameShift(pf.shift, e.shiftAfter)) this._settleIfPending(e.id);
                      if (e.merged) { for (let i = e.merged.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) this.unmerge(e.merged[i]); e.merged = null; }
                      if (e.settled) { this.unsettle(e.id, e.settled); e.settled = null; }
                  }
                  const was = new Map(op.frames.map((e) => { const f = this.lm.frame(e.id); return [e.id, f ? { parent: f.parent, i: f.cell.i, j: f.cell.j } : null]; }));
                  this.lm.readdressMany(op.frames.map((e) => ({ id: e.id, depth: e.depth, parent: e.from.parent, i: e.from.i, j: e.from.j })),
                      { empty: (id) => this.count(id) === 0 });
                  const inv = {
                      op: "moveFrames",
                      frames: op.frames.map((e) => ({ ...e, from: e.to, to: e.from, shiftBefore: e.shiftAfter, shiftAfter: e.shiftBefore, settled: null, merged: null })),
                      moves: (op.moves || []).map((m) => this._invertMove(m)),
                  };
                  for (const e of inv.frames) {
                      this.setFrameShift(e.id, e.shiftAfter);
                      this.noteFrameOwner(e.id, e);
                      const w = was.get(e.id), f = this.lm.frame(e.id);
                      if (w && f) this._emit({ kind: "refile", frame: e.id, from: w, to: { parent: f.parent, i: f.cell.i, j: f.cell.j } });
                  }
                  if (op.moves && op.moves.length) this._undoMove(op.moves);
                  return inv;
              }
              case "removeFrames": {
                  this.insertFrameSubtree(op.records);
                  return { op: "eraseFrames", records: op.records };
              }
              case "eraseFrames": {
+                 for (const r of op.records) this.detachFrame(r.frame, r);
                  return { op: "removeFrames", records: op.records };
              }
              case "deleteMany": {
                  const inverted = [];
                  for (let i = op.parts.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) inverted.push(this._invert(op.parts[i]));
                  return { op: "deleteMany", parts: inverted.reverse() };
              }
              case "eraseCommit": {
+                 this._stepsUndo(op);
                  op.strokeRec = this.removeById(op.strokeId);
                  op.op = "eraseRevert";
                  return op;
              }
              case "eraseRevert": {
                  if (op.strokeRec && !this.getById(op.strokeRec.obj.id)) {
                      this.insertAt(op.strokeRec.obj, op.strokeRec.level, op.strokeRec.index);
                  }
+                 this._stepsRedo(op);
                  op.op = "eraseCommit";
                  return op;
              }
              case "clear": {
                  const curNatives = this.nativesByLevel;
                  const curExternal = op.onExternal ? op.onExternal() : undefined;
                  this._replace(op.natives);
                  if (op.restoreExternal) op.restoreExternal(op.external);
                  return { ...op, natives: curNatives, external: curExternal };
              }
              default: return op;
          }
      }
//// src/engine/Document.js:1427
+     _stepIndex(extra) {
+         const consumed = new Map(), minted = new Map(), opOf = new Map(), steps = [], applied = new Set();
+         const add = (op, on) => {
+             if (op.op !== "eraseCommit" && op.op !== "eraseRevert") return;
+             if (on) applied.add(op);
+             for (const st of op.baked || []) {
+                 if (st._seq == null) st._seq = ++this._stepSeq;
+                 opOf.set(st, op); steps.push(st);
+                 if (!st.removed || !st.pieces) continue;
+                 consumed.set(st.removed.obj.id, st);
+                 for (const pc of st.pieces) minted.set(pc.obj.id, st);
+             }
+         };
+         for (const op of this._undo) add(op, true);
+         for (const op of this._redo) add(op, false);
+         if (extra && !opOf.has((extra.baked || [])[0])) add(extra, extra.op === "eraseCommit");
+         for (const st of steps.filter((x) => x._on === undefined).sort((x, y) => x._seq - y._seq)) {
+             if (!applied.has(opOf.get(st))) { st._on = false; continue; }
+             if (!st.removed || !st.pieces) { st._on = true; continue; }
+             const m = minted.get(st.removed.obj.id);
+             st._on = !this.getById(st.removed.obj.id) && (!m || m._on !== false);
+         }
+         return { consumed, opOf, steps, applied };
+     }
+     _revertStep(st) {
+         st._on = false;
+         if (st.frames) { this.insertFrameSubtree(st.frames); return; }
+         if (st.rekey) { for (const r of st.rekey) { const o = this.getById(r.id); if (o) Object.assign(o.obj, r.before); } this.keysChanged(); return; }
+         let took = st.pieces.length === 0;
+         for (const pc of st.pieces) if (this.removeById(pc.obj.id)) took = true;
+         if (took && !this.getById(st.removed.obj.id)) this.insertAt(st.removed.obj, st.removed.level, st.removed.index);
+     }
//// src/engine/Document.js:1464
+     _applyStep(st) {
+         if (st.frames) { for (const r of st.frames) this.detachFrame(r.frame, r); st._on = true; return true; }
+         if (st.rekey) { for (const r of st.rekey) { const o = this.getById(r.id); if (o) Object.assign(o.obj, r.after); } this.keysChanged(); st._on = true; return true; }
+         const r = this.removeById(st.removed.obj.id);
+         if (!r) return false;
+         st.removed = r;
+         for (const pc of st.pieces) if (!this.getById(pc.obj.id)) this.insertAt(pc.obj, pc.level, 1e9);
+         st._on = true;
+         return true;
+     }
//// src/engine/Document.js:1475
+     _stepsUndo(op) {
+         const ix = this._stepIndex(op);
+         const S = new Set(op.baked.filter((st) => st._on !== false));
+         const stack = [...S];
+         while (stack.length) {
+             const t = stack.pop();
+             for (const pc of t.pieces || []) {
+                 const c = ix.consumed.get(pc.obj.id);
+                 if (c && c._on !== false && !S.has(c)) { S.add(c); stack.push(c); }
+             }
+         }
+         for (const st of [...S].sort((a, b) => b._seq - a._seq)) this._revertStep(st);
+     }
//// src/engine/Document.js:1489
+     _stepsRedo(op) {
+         const ix = this._stepIndex(op);
+         for (const st of [...op.baked].sort((a, b) => a._seq - b._seq)) this._applyStep(st);
+         const waiting = ix.steps.filter((st) => st._on === false && ix.opOf.get(st) !== op && ix.applied.has(ix.opOf.get(st))).sort((x, y) => x._seq - y._seq);
+         for (let changed = true; changed;) {
+             changed = false;
+             for (const st of waiting) if (st._on === false && this._applyStep(st)) changed = true;
+         }
+     }

      _perf(op, t0, always, extra) {

//// src/engine/KobinEngine.js:704
+     _drainPlates() {
+         const q = this._plateEvents;
+         if (!q || !this.plates) return 0;
+         let n = 0;
+         for (let k = 0; k < q.length; k++) {
+             const ev = q[k];
+             if (this._holdMove && this._heldByMove(ev)) continue;
+             if (ev.neutral) {
+                 if (ev.obj && ev.id != null && this._footBelow) this._footBelow.set(ev.id, ev.obj.below || null);
+                 this.plates.s.neutralEvents++;
+                 n++;
+                 continue;
+             }
+             if (ev.kind === "refile" && ev.frame != null && ev.from && ev.to) { this._refilePlates(ev); n++; continue; }
+             if ((ev.kind === "mergeFrame" || ev.kind === "splitFrame") && ev.frame != null) {
+                 if (ev.kind === "mergeFrame") this.plates.dropFrames((G) => G === ev.frame);
+                 n++;
+                 continue;
+             }
+             if ((ev.kind === "removeFrame" || ev.kind === "addFrame") && ev.frame != null && this._frameListPlates(ev)) { n++; continue; }
+             const prev = ev.id != null && this._footBelow ? this._footBelow.get(ev.id) : undefined;
+             this.plates.onDocEvent(ev, (frameId) => this._drawnRectIn(ev, prev, frameId));
+             if (ev.obj && ev.id != null && this._footBelow) this._footBelow.set(ev.id, ev.obj.below || null);
+             n++;
+         }
+         q.length = 0;
+         if (this._footBelow && this._footBelow.size > 1024) this._footBelow.clear();
+         this.plates.noteDrain(n);
+         return n;
+     }

+             if (m) out.push(m);

+     _framesFootIn(frames) {

+     _releaseMove() {

+     _movedRectsIn(h, frameId) {
+         const F = String(frameId);
+         const out = [];
+         let union = null;
+         const add = (rect, H, below) => {
+             const m = this.lm.mapRectObj(rect, H, F, below, H, false);
+             if (!m) return;
+             if (h.ids.size <= MOVE_RECTS_MAX) out.push(m);

+         };

+             if (!rec) continue;

+             if (L0 !== rec.level || b0 !== (o.below || null)) add(r, L0, b0);
+         }

+         return out.length ? out : null;

//// src/engine/KobinEngine.js:956
+     _drawnRectIn(ev, prev, frameId) {
+         const o = ev.obj;
+         if (!o || ev.level == null) return null;
+         const H = String(ev.level), F = String(frameId);
+         const b = bboxOf(o, this._drawing);
+         const lw = o.type === "fill" ? 0 : (o.lwFrame || 0);

+         const box = (bb) => ({ left: bb.x0 - half, top: bb.y0 - half, right: bb.x1 + half, bottom: bb.y1 + half });
+         const r = box(b);
+         const movedTable = prev !== undefined && prev !== (o.below || null);
+         const ob = ev.oldBbox;
+         const movedGeom = !!ob && (ob.x0 !== b.x0 || ob.y0 !== b.y0 || ob.x1 !== b.x1 || ob.y1 !== b.y1);
+         const out = [];
+         const add = (rect, below) => { const m = this.lm.mapRectObj(rect, H, F, below, H, false); if (m) out.push(m); };
+         add(r, o.below);
+         if (movedGeom) add(box(ob), o.below);
+         if (movedTable) add(r, prev);
+         if (movedTable && movedGeom) add(box(ob), prev);
+         if (!out.length) return null;
+         return out.length === 1 ? out[0] : out;
+     }

//// src/engine/KobinEngine.js:1135
+     _buildLadder() {

+         const steps = LADDER_FACTORS.slice(0, n).map((factor) => ({ factor, cross: false }));

+         this._ladderSteps = steps;

//// src/engine/KobinEngine.js:1159
+     _ladderCrossings(maxFactor) {
+         const out = [], base = this.cfg.base, exit = this.cfg.exit;
+         let f = this.lm.frame(this.cam.frame), s = this.cam.inScale, total = 1;
+         while (f && f.parent != null && f.edge) {
+             total *= s / exit;
+             if (total * 1.02 > maxFactor) break;
+             out.push(total * 1.02);
+             s = (f.edge.s / base) * exit;
+             f = this.lm.frame(f.parent);
+         }
+         return out;
+     }
+     _ladderKey() { const c = this.cam; return c.frame + "|" + c.inScale + "|" + c.inPanX + "|" + c.inPanY + "|" + this.width + "x" + this.height; }
//// src/engine/KobinEngine.js:1173
+     _ladderRung(k, key) {
+         const steps = this._ladderSteps || [];
+         const rungs = steps.length;
+         this._ladderBusy = false;
+         if (this._destroyed || k >= rungs || key !== this._ladderKey() || this._drawing || this._dragSel || this._lasso) return 0;
+         if (this._ladderCap && this._ladderCap.key === key && k > this._ladderCap.k) return 0;
+         if (this._reportBusy) {
+             this._ladderBusy = true;
+             setTimeout(() => this._ladderRung(k, key), LADDER_REPORT_WAIT_MS);
+             return 0;
+         }
+         const step = steps[k];
+         const aim = this._zoomedOut(step.factor);
+         if (!aim) return 0;
+         const t = { id: aim.frame, inScale: aim.inScale, first: k === 0 };
+         let posted = 0;
+         if (this.renderer.ladderMisses(t, aim.win) > 0 || step.cross) {
+             const tL = perfNow();
+             const budget = this.ladderRungBudgetMs != null ? this.ladderRungBudgetMs : (this.plates.phone ? LADDER_RUNG_BUDGET_PHONE_MS : LADDER_RUNG_BUDGET_MS);
+             this.store.warmReads = true;
+             let list;
+             const slice = this.ladderSliceMs != null ? this.ladderSliceMs : (this.plates.phone ? LADDER_SLICE_PHONE_MS : LADDER_SLICE_MS);
+             try { list = this.listFor(aim.frame, this.renderer.plateCover(t, aim.win, LADDER_DQ), false, slice); } finally { this.store.warmReads = false; }
+             this._perf("ladder", tL, false, { frame: aim.frame, k, n: list ? list.length : -1, cross: step.cross });
+             if (!list) {
+                 const tries = (this._ladderTries && this._ladderTries.key === key && this._ladderTries.k === k) ? this._ladderTries.n + 1 : 1;
+                 this._ladderTries = { key, k, n: tries };
+                 if (tries >= LADDER_RUNG_TRIES) { this._ladderCap = { key, k: k - 1 }; return 0; }
+                 this._ladderBusy = true;
+                 ladderIdle(() => this._ladderRung(k, key));
+                 return 0;
+             }
+             if (perfNow() - tL > budget) this._ladderCap = { key, k };
+             const tP = perfNow();
+             posted = this.renderer.ladderPlates(t, aim.win, list);
+             this._perf("ladderPost", tP, false, { k, n: list.length, posted });
+         }
+         if (k + 1 < rungs && !(this._ladderCap && this._ladderCap.key === key)) {
+             this._ladderBusy = true;
+             ladderIdle(() => this._ladderRung(k + 1, key));
+         }
+         return posted;
+     }
//// src/engine/KobinEngine.js:1228
+     _zoomedOut(factor) {
+         const base = this.cfg.base, exit = this.cfg.exit;
+         let f = this.lm.frame(this.cam.frame);
+         if (!f) return null;
+         const W2 = this.width / 2, H2 = this.height / 2;
+         let s = this.cam.inScale;
+         let p = [(W2 - this.cam.inPanX) / s, (H2 - this.cam.inPanY) / s];
+         s /= factor;
+         while (s < exit) {
+             if (f.parent == null || !f.edge) return null;
+             const q = this.lm.mapPointF(p, f.id, f.parent);
+             if (!q) return null;
+             s = (f.edge.s / base) * s;
+             p = q;
+             f = this.lm.frame(f.parent);
+             if (!f) return null;
+         }
+         const hw = (W2 / s) * LADDER_MARGIN, hh = (H2 / s) * LADDER_MARGIN;
+         return { frame: f.id, inScale: s, win: { left: p[0] - hw, top: p[1] - hh, right: p[0] + hw, bottom: p[1] + hh } };
+     }
      _maybePrebake() {
          if (this._prebakeQueued) return;
+         const aim = this._crossTarget();
+         if (!aim) return;
          this._prebakeQueued = true;
          const idle = typeof requestIdleCallback === "function" ? requestIdleCallback : (fn) => setTimeout(fn, 30);
          idle(() => {
              this._prebakeQueued = false;
              if (this._destroyed) return;
+             const t = this._crossTarget();
+             if (!t || t.id !== aim.id || !this.lm.frame(t.id)) return;
+             const win = this.lm.mapRectF(this.cam.frameWindow(0), this.cam.frame, t.id);
+             if (!win) return;
              const tP = perfNow();
+             this.store.warmReads = true;
+             let list;
+             try { list = this.listFor(t.id, this.raster && this.renderer.plateCover ? this.renderer.plateCover(t, win, PREVIEW_QUANTA) : win); } finally { this.store.warmReads = false; }
+             this._perf("prebake", tP, false, { frame: t.id, dir: t.dir, n: list.length });
+             if (this.raster && this.plates && this.renderer.prebakePlates) {

      _queueIdleFits() {

      _pointerMove(sx, sy) {

                  }

              }

                  return;

          }

                  o._pen.addSample(p);
              }
              this.renderer.setLiveArcs(o._pen.gaps);
              this.renderer.update();
          }
      }

                  });

+ function cedeVirtual(vdoc, id, level, regions, holeInParent, attachRect, kidTile, kidBelow, boolOpts, dustBar) {

              color: src.color, opacity: src.opacity, editId: editKey,
          };

  export function descentJob(world, input) {

  export function wireDescent(world, input) {
      const transfer = [];
      const msg = {
+         lattice: world.lm.serialize(), retired: world.lm._retired ? [...world.lm._retired] : null,
+         width: world.width, height: world.lm.height, cfg: world.cfg, opacityGroups: world.opacityGroups,
          freezeR: input.freezeR, HE: input.HE,
          target: { level: String(input.target.level), obj: objOut(input.target.obj, transfer) },
          E: { id: input.E.id, w: input.E.w, bakePx: input.E.bakePx, loops: input.E.loops.map(own) },
      };
      for (const x of msg.E.loops) transfer.push(x.buf.buffer, x.idx.buffer);

  export function cutJob(j) {
      const opts = { freezeR: j.freezeR };
      const eb = loopsBBox(j.clip);
      const rE = eb ? Math.max(eb.x1 - eb.x0, eb.y1 - eb.y0) / 2 : 0;
+     const both = shapeBoolean(j.subject, j.clip, "both", opts);
+     const res = both.difference;
      const stats = res.stats || null;
+     const removed = Math.abs(loopsArea(both.intersection.loops));
      let graze = 1e-4 * rE * rE, grazed;
      if (j.graze === "cede") grazed = removed < graze;
      else { graze = Math.min(graze, 1e-6 * Math.max(loopsArea(j.subject), 0)); grazed = removed <= graze; }
      if (grazed) return { kind: "grazed", stats, removed, dust: 0 };
      const groups = shapeComponents(res.loops);
      const keep = dropDust(groups, j.w, j.dustBar);
      const dust = groups.length - keep.length;
      if (!keep.length) return { kind: "removed", stats, removed, dust };
      return { kind: "cut", regions: keep.map((g) => g.map((l) => Loop.from(l))), stats, removed, dust };
  }

      _familyComponents(key) {
          const members = this._familyMembers(key);
          const G = new Groups(members.length);
          for (let i = 0; i < members.length; i++) {
              const kid = members[i], R = kid.obj.attachRect;
              if (!R) continue;
              const kidDepth = this.lm.depthOf(kid.level);
              if (kidDepth == null) continue;
              const kidArcs = contactArcs(this._inkOutline(kid.obj, rectTol(R)), R, rectTol(R));
              if (!kidArcs.length) continue;
              for (let j = 0; j < members.length; j++) {
                  const up = members[j];
                  if (j === i) continue;
                  if (up.obj.type !== "shape" && up.obj.type !== "fill") continue;
                  const upDepth = this.lm.depthOf(up.level);
                  if (upDepth === kidDepth) {
                      if (!up.obj.attachRect) continue;
+                     const d = this._cellOffset(kid.level, up.level);
+                     if (!d) continue;
+                     const r = up.obj.attachRect, Rj = { x0: r.x0 + d[0], y0: r.y0 + d[1], x1: r.x1 + d[0], y1: r.y1 + d[1] };

+                     let rings = this._inkOutline(up.obj, rectTol(R));
+                     if (d[0] || d[1]) rings = rings.map((ring) => ring.map((p) => [p[0] + d[0], p[1] + d[1]]));
+                     if (arcsTouch(kidArcs, contactArcs(rings, R, rectTol(R)), rectTol(R) / rectSpan(R))) G.union(i, j);
                      continue;
                  }
                  if (upDepth !== kidDepth - 1) continue;
                  const Rk = { left: R.x0, top: R.y0, right: R.x1, bottom: R.y1 };
                  const upF0 = up.obj.below ? this.lm.objShift(up.obj.below, up.level, kid.level) : null;

                  if (!Rp) continue;

+     _cellOffset(A, B) {
+         if (String(A) === String(B)) return [0, 0];

+                 if (n && String(n.id) === String(B)) return [di * FRAME_W, dj * FRAME_W];

      _resplitFamily(op, key) {

              }

      _settleSelectionErases() {

+             if (ev.kind === "add" && ev.obj && !ev.obj.erase) added.push(ev.id);
+             else if (ev.kind === "remove" && !ev.rehome) removed.push(ev.id);
+         });

+         try {

+                 added.length = 0; removed.length = 0;

+                 for (const id of [...live]) {
+                     if (this.doc.getById(id) && this._flushErasesFor(id, marks)) { baked = true; break; }
+                 }
+                 if (!baked) break;
+                 for (const id of added) live.add(id);
+                 for (const id of removed) live.delete(id);
+                 replaced = true; changed = true;
+                 marks = this._eraseStrokes();
              }
+         } finally { unsub(); }

              changed = true;
          }

          }

//// src/engine/frameGc.js  (new file)
+ import { opFrames } from "./Document";
+ export const FRAME_GC_ENABLED = true;
+ export const CAM_TRAIL = 4;
+ export function initFrameGc(E) {
+     E._gcCand = new Set();
+     E._camTrail = [];
+     E._gcCamAt = null;
+     E._gcStats = { runs: 0, forgotten: 0 };
+     E.lm.minted = E._gcCand;
+     E.doc.onOpsDropped = (ops) => { for (const k of namedBy(E, ops)) E._gcCand.add(k); };
+ }
+ function namedBy(E, ops) {
+     const out = new Set();
+     for (const op of ops) opFrames(op, out);
+     return out;
+ }
+ export function noteFrameEvent(E, ev) {
+     const c = E._gcCand;
+     if (!c) return;
+     if (ev.kind === "remove" && ev.level != null) c.add(String(ev.level));
+     else if (ev.kind === "refile") { if (ev.frame != null) c.add(String(ev.frame)); if (ev.from && ev.from.parent != null) c.add(String(ev.from.parent)); }
+     else if (ev.kind === "removeFrame" && ev.frame != null) c.add(String(ev.frame));
+     else if (ev.kind === "reset") { E._gcCamAt = null; E._camTrail.length = 0; }
+ }
+ export function noteCameraFrame(E) {
+     const at = E.cam.frame;
+     if (!E._gcCand || E._gcCamAt === at) return false;
+     const was = E._gcCamAt;
+     E._gcCamAt = at;
+     if (was == null) return false;
+     const t = E._camTrail, k = t.indexOf(was);
+     if (k >= 0) t.splice(k, 1);
+     t.push(was);
+     const here = t.indexOf(at);
+     if (here >= 0) t.splice(here, 1);
+     while (t.length > CAM_TRAIL) E._gcCand.add(t.shift());
+     return true;
+ }
+ export function collectFrames(E, inRender = false) {
+     const cand = E._gcCand;
+     if (!FRAME_GC_ENABLED) { if (cand) cand.clear(); return 0; }
+     if (!cand || !cand.size) return 0;
+     if (E._dragSel || E._drawing || E._lasso || E._cutInflight || E.doc.holdMerges) return 0;
+     const lm = E.lm, doc = E.doc;
+     const keep = new Set(E._camTrail);
+     for (let f = lm.frame(E.cam.frame); f; f = f.parent != null ? lm.frame(f.parent) : null) keep.add(f.id);
+     const todo = [...cand];
+     cand.clear();
+     let named = null;
+     const gone = [];
+     while (todo.length) {
+         const id = String(todo.pop());
+         const f = lm.frame(id);
+         if (!f || f.parent == null || keep.has(id)) continue;
+         if (doc.count(id) || (f._kids && f._kids.size) || (f._shadows && f._shadows.size)) continue;
+         if (!named) named = doc.namedFrames();
+         if (named.has(id)) continue;
+         const up = f.shadowOf != null ? f.shadowOf : f.parent;
+         if (!lm.forget(id)) continue;
+         doc.forgetFrame(id);
+         if (E.store && E.store.forgetFrame) E.store.forgetFrame(id);
+         gone.push(id);
+         todo.push(up);
+     }
+     if (gone.length && E.plates) { const g = new Set(gone); E.plates.dropFrames((G) => g.has(String(G))); }
+     E._gcStats.runs++; E._gcStats.forgotten += gone.length;
+     doc.noteForgotten(gone);
+     const s = E.selection;
+     if (gone.length && s && s.frames.some((fr) => !lm.frame(fr))) {
+         const frames = s.frames.filter((fr) => lm.frame(fr));
+         if (!inRender) E._setSelection(s.ids, frames);
+         else if (s.ids.length || frames.length) E.selection = { ...s, frames };
+         else E.selection = null;
+     }
+     return gone.length;
+ }
+ export function sweepFrames(E) {
+     if (!E._gcCand) return 0;
+     for (const id of E.lm.frames.keys()) E._gcCand.add(id);
+     return collectFrames(E);
+ }

//// src/engine/geometry/arcPerimeter.js:54
+ function sameCircle(p, q, tol) {
+     return !p.line && !q.line && Math.abs(p.r - q.r) <= tol
+         && Math.abs(p.C[0] - q.C[0]) <= tol && Math.abs(p.C[1] - q.C[1]) <= tol;
+ }
//// src/engine/geometry/arcPerimeter.js:59
+ function samePiece(p, q, tol) {
+     if (p.line || q.line) return p.line && q.line;
+     return sameCircle(p, q, tol) && Math.abs(p.sweep - q.sweep) <= 1e-6 * Math.max(1, Math.abs(p.sweep));
+ }
//// src/engine/geometry/arcPerimeter.js:619
+     verdict(x, y, r, margin, src) {
+         const eff = r - margin, reach = r + margin;
+         if (eff <= 0) return 0;
+         let out = 0;
+         const p = [x, y];
+         const e2 = eff * eff, t2 = reach * reach;
+         this.grid.near([x - reach, y - reach, x + reach, y + reach], (i) => {
+             if (out === 2) return;
+             const b = this.bbs[i];
+             const dx = x < b[0] ? b[0] - x : (x > b[2] ? x - b[2] : 0);
+             const dy = y < b[1] ? b[1] - y : (y > b[3] ? y - b[3] : 0);
+             const q = dx * dx + dy * dy;
+             if (q >= t2) return;
+             const tieable = src >= 0 && (i < src - 1 || i > src + 1);
+             if (q >= e2 && !tieable) return;
+             const d = arcDist(this.arcs[i], p);
+             if (d < eff) out = 2;
+             else if (tieable && d <= reach) out = 1;
+         });
+         return out;
+     }
//// src/engine/geometry/arcPerimeter.js:820
      _centerline(deadline) {
          if (!this.pen) { this.pen = new BiarcPen({ tol: this.tol }); this.i = 0; }
          const pts = this.pts;

+     _pruneTies() {

  function rayCross(flat, p, dx, dy) {
      let w = 0;
      const pm = Math.abs(p[0]) + Math.abs(p[1]);
      for (const q of flat) {
          if (q.line) {
              const ex = q.B[0] - q.A[0], ey = q.B[1] - q.A[1];
              const elen = Math.hypot(ex, ey);
              if (!(elen > 0)) continue;
              const eps = (pm + Math.abs(q.A[0]) + Math.abs(q.A[1]) + elen) * GRAZE;
              const den = dx * ey - dy * ex;
              const rx = q.A[0] - p[0], ry = q.A[1] - p[1];
              if (Math.abs(den) <= GRAZE * elen) {
                  if (Math.abs(rx * dy - ry * dx) <= eps) return null;
                  continue;
              }
              const t = (rx * ey - ry * ex) / den;
              const u = (rx * dy - ry * dx) / den;
              if (u * elen < -eps || (u - 1) * elen > eps) continue;
              if (t < -eps) continue;
              if (Math.abs(t) <= eps) return null;
              if (u * elen <= eps || (1 - u) * elen <= eps) return null;
              w += den > 0 ? 1 : -1;
          } else {
              const arcLen = Math.abs(q.r * q.sweep);
              const cf = Math.abs(q.sweep) < Math.PI ? chordFrame(q) : null;
+             if (cf && cf.r >= cf.L && Math.pow(cf.L, 4) / (64 * Math.pow(cf.r, 3)) <= 1e-15 * (pm + Math.abs(cf.Mx) + Math.abs(cf.My) + cf.L)) {
                  const eps = (pm + Math.abs(q.A[0]) + Math.abs(q.A[1]) + cf.L) * GRAZE;
                  const resTol = 1e-12 * (pm + Math.abs(cf.Mx) + Math.abs(cf.My) + cf.L);
                  for (const { a, res } of chordLineRoots(cf, -dy, dx, -dy * p[0] + dx * p[1], 200)) {
                      if (!(res <= resTol)) continue;
                      const h = chordHit(cf, a);
                      const t = (h.x - p[0]) * dx + (h.y - p[1]) * dy;
                      if (t < -eps) continue;
                      if (h.s < 0 || h.s > 1) {
                          if ((h.s < 0 ? -h.s : h.s - 1) * arcLen <= eps) return null;
                          continue;
                      }
                      if (Math.abs(t) <= eps) return null;
                      if (h.s * arcLen <= eps || (1 - h.s) * arcLen <= eps) return null;
                      const tl = Math.hypot(h.tx, h.ty);
                      const cross = (-h.tx * dy + h.ty * dx) / tl;
                      if (Math.abs(cross) <= 1e-12) return null;
                      w += cross > 0 ? 1 : -1;
                  }
                  continue;
              }
+             const Cx = cf ? cf.Mx - cf.d * cf.nx : q.C[0], Cy = cf ? cf.My - cf.d * cf.ny : q.C[1];
+             const qa0 = cf ? Math.atan2(q.A[1] - Cy, q.A[0] - Cx) : q.a0;
+             const qsw = cf ? (q.sweep > 0 ? 2 : -2) * Math.asin(Math.min(1, cf.half / cf.r)) : q.sweep;
+             {
+                 const ve = (pm + Math.abs(q.A[0]) + Math.abs(q.A[1]) + Math.abs(q.B[0]) + Math.abs(q.B[1])) * GRAZE;
+                 for (const V of [q.A, q.B]) {
+                     const vx = V[0] - p[0], vy = V[1] - p[1];
+                     if (Math.abs(vx * dy - vy * dx) <= ve && vx * dx + vy * dy >= -ve) return null;
+                 }
+             }
+             const fx = p[0] - Cx, fy = p[1] - Cy;
              const fd = fx * dx + fy * dy;
              const disc = fd * fd - (fx * fx + fy * fy - q.r * q.r);
              if (disc < 0) continue;
              const sq = Math.sqrt(disc);
+             const eps = (pm + Math.abs(Cx) + Math.abs(Cy) + q.r) * GRAZE;
              for (const t of [-fd - sq, -fd + sq]) {
                  if (t < -eps) continue;
                  const qx = p[0] + t * dx, qy = p[1] + t * dy;
+                 const th = Math.atan2(qy - Cy, qx - Cx);
+                 const off = wrap(th - (qa0 + qsw / 2) + Math.PI) - Math.PI;
+                 const s = qsw === 0 ? 0 : 0.5 + off / qsw;
                  if (s < 0 || s > 1) {
                      if ((s < 0 ? -s : s - 1) * arcLen <= eps) return null;
                      continue;
                  }
                  if (Math.abs(t) <= eps) return null;
                  if (sq <= eps) return null;
                  if (s * arcLen <= eps || (1 - s) * arcLen <= eps) return null;
                  const g = q.sweep > 0 ? 1 : -1;
                  const tx = -Math.sin(th) * g, ty = Math.cos(th) * g;
                  const cross = -tx * dy + ty * dx;
                  if (Math.abs(cross) <= 1e-12) return null;
                  w += cross > 0 ? 1 : -1;
              }
          }
      }
      return w;
  }

//// src/engine/geometry/chordMath.js  (new file)
+ export function bulgeK(sweep) {
+     return Math.tan(sweep / 4) / 2;
+ }
+ export function chordMidK(ax, ay, bx, by, k, out) {
+     out[0] = (ax + bx) / 2 + (by - ay) * k;
+     out[1] = (ay + by) / 2 - (bx - ax) * k;
+     return out;
+ }
+ export function chordMidXY(ax, ay, bx, by, sweep, out) {
+     return chordMidK(ax, ay, bx, by, bulgeK(sweep), out);
+ }
+ export function sagittaOf(ax, ay, bx, by, sweep) {
+     const a = Math.abs(sweep);
+     if (!(a > 0)) return 0;
+     if (a > Math.PI) return Infinity;
+     const dx = bx - ax, dy = by - ay;
+     return (Math.hypot(dx, dy) / 2) * Math.abs(Math.tan(a / 4));
+ }
+ function segRatio(theta) {
+     if (Math.abs(theta) <= 1e-2) {
+         const t2 = theta * theta;
+         return (theta / 3) * (1 + t2 / 30 + (t2 * t2) / 840);
+     }
+     const s = Math.sin(theta / 2);
+     return (theta - Math.sin(theta)) / (2 * s * s);
+ }
+ export function arcAreaTerm(ax, ay, bx, by, sweep) {
+     if (!(Math.abs(sweep) > 0)) return 0;
+     const dx = bx - ax, dy = by - ay;
+     const c2 = dx * dx + dy * dy;
+     if (!(c2 > 0)) return 0;
+     return (c2 / 2) * segRatio(sweep);
+ }

  export function loopTester(poly) {
      const n = poly ? poly.length : 0;
      if (n < 3) return { inside: () => false, ringInside: () => false };
      let l = Infinity, t = Infinity, r = -Infinity, b = -Infinity;
      for (const p of poly) {
          if (p[0] < l) l = p[0];
          if (p[0] > r) r = p[0];
          if (p[1] < t) t = p[1];
          if (p[1] > b) b = p[1];
      }
      const G = 32;
      const w = Math.max(r - l, 1e-9), h = Math.max(b - t, 1e-9);
      const cx = (x) => Math.min(G - 1, Math.max(0, Math.floor(((x - l) / w) * G)));
      const cy = (y) => Math.min(G - 1, Math.max(0, Math.floor(((y - t) / h) * G)));
      const cells = new Array(G * G);
      for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
          const a = poly[i], c = poly[(i + 1) % n];
          const x0 = cx(Math.min(a[0], c[0])), x1 = cx(Math.max(a[0], c[0]));
          const y0 = cy(Math.min(a[1], c[1])), y1 = cy(Math.max(a[1], c[1]));
          for (let y = y0; y <= y1; y++) for (let x = x0; x <= x1; x++) {
              const k = y * G + x;
              (cells[k] || (cells[k] = [])).push(i);
          }
      }
+     const rows = new Array(G);
+     for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
+         const a = poly[i], c = poly[(i + 1) % n];
+         const y0 = cy(Math.min(a[1], c[1])), y1 = cy(Math.max(a[1], c[1]));
+         for (let y = y0; y <= y1; y++) (rows[y] || (rows[y] = [])).push(i);
+     }
+     const insideRow = (p) => {
+         if (!(p[1] >= t && p[1] <= b)) return false;
+         const list = rows[cy(p[1])];
+         let inside = false;
+         if (list) for (const k of list) {
+             const a = poly[(k + 1) % n], c = poly[k];
+             if ((a[1] > p[1]) !== (c[1] > p[1]) && p[0] < ((c[0] - a[0]) * (p[1] - a[1])) / (c[1] - a[1]) + a[0]) inside = !inside;
+         }
+         return inside;
+     };
      const stamp = new Int32Array(n);
      let tick = 0;
      const segmentCrosses = (p, q) => {
          if (Math.max(p[0], q[0]) < l || Math.min(p[0], q[0]) > r ||
              Math.max(p[1], q[1]) < t || Math.min(p[1], q[1]) > b) return false;
          tick++;
          const x0 = cx(Math.min(p[0], q[0])), x1 = cx(Math.max(p[0], q[0]));
          const y0 = cy(Math.min(p[1], q[1])), y1 = cy(Math.max(p[1], q[1]));
          for (let y = y0; y <= y1; y++) for (let x = x0; x <= x1; x++) {
              const list = cells[y * G + x];
              if (!list) continue;
              for (const i of list) {
                  if (stamp[i] === tick) continue;
                  stamp[i] = tick;
                  if (segsCross(poly[i], poly[(i + 1) % n], p, q)) return true;
              }
          }
          return false;
      };
      return {
+         inside: insideRow,
          ringInside: (ring) => {
              const m = ring ? ring.length : 0;
              if (m < 1) return false;
+             if (!insideRow(ring[0])) return false;
              for (let k = 0; k < m; k++) {
                  if (segmentCrosses(ring[k], ring[(k + 1) % m])) return false;
              }
              return true;
          },
      };

  export function strokeStripNear(pts, width, rect, opts = {}) {
+     if (opts.joins === "round") return strokeStripRound(pts, width, opts);
      const half = width / 2;
      const out = [];
      const diagR = Math.hypot(rect.right - rect.left, rect.bottom - rect.top);
      const rcx = (rect.left + rect.right) / 2, rcy = (rect.top + rect.bottom) / 2;
      const capPiece = (p) => {
          const dx = rcx - p[0], dy = rcy - p[1];
          const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
          if (dist - diagR > half) return;
          const phi = Math.atan2(dy, dx);
          const delta = Math.min(Math.PI, 4 * Math.asin(Math.min(1, diagR / Math.max(dist, diagR))));
          const K = 96, ring = [[p[0], p[1]]];
          for (let k = 0; k <= K; k++) {
              const a = phi - delta + (2 * delta * k) / K;
              ring.push([p[0] + Math.cos(a) * half, p[1] + Math.sin(a) * half]);
          }
          out.push(ring);
      };
      if (pts.length === 1) { capPiece(pts[0]); }
      else {
          const n = pts.length, nx = [], ny = [];
          for (let i = 0; i < n - 1; i++) {
              const dx = pts[i + 1][0] - pts[i][0], dy = pts[i + 1][1] - pts[i][1];
              const len = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
              nx.push(-dy / len); ny.push(dx / len);
          }
          const L = [], R = [];
          for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
              const a = Math.max(0, i - 1), b = Math.min(n - 2, i);
              let mx = (nx[a] + nx[b]) / 2, my = (ny[a] + ny[b]) / 2;
              const ml = Math.hypot(mx, my);
              if (ml < 1e-12) { mx = nx[b]; my = ny[b]; } else { mx /= ml; my /= ml; }
              const dot = mx * nx[b] + my * ny[b];
              const sc = half / Math.max(0.5, dot);
              L.push([pts[i][0] + mx * sc, pts[i][1] + my * sc]);
              R.push([pts[i][0] - mx * sc, pts[i][1] - my * sc]);
          }
          out.push(L.concat(R.reverse()));
          for (let i = 0; i < n - 1; i++) {
              const ox = nx[i] * half, oy = ny[i] * half;
              out.push([
                  [pts[i][0] + ox, pts[i][1] + oy], [pts[i + 1][0] + ox, pts[i + 1][1] + oy],
                  [pts[i + 1][0] - ox, pts[i + 1][1] - oy], [pts[i][0] - ox, pts[i][1] - oy],
              ]);
          }
          if (opts.startCap) capPiece(pts[0]);
          if (opts.endCap) capPiece(pts[n - 1]);
      }
      for (const ring of out) {
          let area = 0;
          for (let i = 0, m = ring.length; i < m; i++) { const a = ring[i], b = ring[(i + 1) % m]; area += a[0] * b[1] - b[0] * a[1]; }
          if (area > 0) ring.reverse();
      }
      return out;
  }
//// src/engine/geometry/polyline.js:420
+ export function strokeStripRound(pts, width, opts = {}) {
+     const half = width / 2;
+     const tol = opts.tol > 0 ? opts.tol : half / 64;
+     const step = half > 0 ? 2 * Math.acos(Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, 1 - tol / half))) : Math.PI;
+     const w = new RingWriter(opts.out);
+     const p = [];
+     for (const q of pts) {
+         const n = p.length;
+         if (!n || q[0] !== p[n - 1][0] || q[1] !== p[n - 1][1]) p.push(q);
+     }
+     if (!p.length || !(half > 0)) return w.done();
+     if (p.length === 1) { disc(w, p[0][0], p[0][1], half, step); return w.done(); }
+     const n = p.length, dx = [], dy = [];
+     for (let i = 0; i < n - 1; i++) {
+         const ex = p[i + 1][0] - p[i][0], ey = p[i + 1][1] - p[i][1];
+         const len = Math.hypot(ex, ey);
+         dx.push(ex / len); dy.push(ey / len);
+         const ox = (-ey / len) * half, oy = (ex / len) * half;
+         w.startRing();
+         w.pt(p[i][0] + ox, p[i][1] + oy); w.pt(p[i + 1][0] + ox, p[i + 1][1] + oy);
+         w.pt(p[i + 1][0] - ox, p[i + 1][1] - oy); w.pt(p[i][0] - ox, p[i][1] - oy);
+         w.close();
+     }
+     for (let i = 1; i < n - 1; i++) {
+         const cross = dx[i - 1] * dy[i] - dy[i - 1] * dx[i];
+         const dot = dx[i - 1] * dx[i] + dy[i - 1] * dy[i];
+         const turn = Math.atan2(cross, dot);
+         if (turn === 0) continue;
+         if (Math.abs(Math.abs(turn) - Math.PI) < 1e-12) { disc(w, p[i][0], p[i][1], half, step); continue; }
+         const s = cross > 0 ? -1 : 1;
+         const a0 = Math.atan2(s * dx[i - 1], -s * dy[i - 1]);
+         fanAt(w, p[i][0], p[i][1], half, a0, turn, step);
+     }
+     fanAt(w, p[0][0], p[0][1], half, Math.atan2(dx[0], -dy[0]), Math.PI, step);
+     const L = n - 2;
+     fanAt(w, p[n - 1][0], p[n - 1][1], half, Math.atan2(-dx[L], dy[L]), Math.PI, step);
+     return w.done();
+ }
//// src/engine/geometry/polyline.js:470
+ function fanAt(w, cx, cy, r, a0, delta, step) {
+     w.startRing();
+     w.pt(cx, cy);
+     const k = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(Math.abs(delta) / step));
+     for (let i = 0; i <= k; i++) {
+         const a = a0 + (delta * i) / k;
+         w.pt(cx + Math.cos(a) * r, cy + Math.sin(a) * r);
+     }
+     w.close();
+ }
//// src/engine/geometry/polyline.js:483
+ function disc(w, cx, cy, r, step) {
+     w.startRing();
+     const k = Math.max(3, Math.ceil((2 * Math.PI) / step));
+     for (let i = 0; i < k; i++) {
+         const a = (2 * Math.PI * i) / k;
+         w.pt(cx + Math.cos(a) * r, cy + Math.sin(a) * r);
+     }
+     w.close();
+ }
//// src/engine/geometry/polyline.js:499
+ class RingWriter {
+     constructor(out) {
+         this.xy = (out && out.xy) || new Float64Array(256);
+         this.ring = (out && out.ring) || new Uint32Array(16);
+         this.n = 0; this.r = 0; this.start = 0; this.area = 0;
+     }
+     startRing() {
+         if (this.r === this.ring.length) { const g = new Uint32Array(this.r * 2); g.set(this.ring); this.ring = g; }
+         this.ring[this.r++] = this.n;
+         this.start = this.n;
+     }
+     pt(x, y) {
+         if (this.n * 2 + 2 > this.xy.length) { const g = new Float64Array(this.xy.length * 2); g.set(this.xy); this.xy = g; }
+         this.xy[this.n * 2] = x; this.xy[this.n * 2 + 1] = y; this.n++;
+     }
+     close() {
+         let area = 0;
+         for (let i = this.start; i < this.n; i++) {
+             const j = i + 1 < this.n ? i + 1 : this.start;
+             area += this.xy[i * 2] * this.xy[j * 2 + 1] - this.xy[j * 2] * this.xy[i * 2 + 1];
+         }
+         this.area += (area < 0 ? -area : area) / 2;
+         if (area < 0) {
+             for (let a = this.start, b = this.n - 1; a < b; a++, b--) {
+                 const x = this.xy[a * 2], y = this.xy[a * 2 + 1];
+                 this.xy[a * 2] = this.xy[b * 2]; this.xy[a * 2 + 1] = this.xy[b * 2 + 1];
+                 this.xy[b * 2] = x; this.xy[b * 2 + 1] = y;
+             }
+         }
+     }
+     done() { return { xy: this.xy, ring: this.ring, rings: this.r, pts: this.n, area: this.area }; }
+ }

      drain() {
          const entries = [];

          for (const it of this.queue) {

+                 const aside = !cur && !this.removed.has(it.id) ? this.doc._byId.get(it.id) : null;
+                 const src = cur ? { obj: cur.obj, level: lvl(cur.level) }

              } else if (it.k === "move") {
                  enc = encodeEntry({ k: "move", many: it.many, moves: it.moves.map((m) => this._moveFile(m)) });
              } else if (it.k === "moveFrames") {
                  enc = encodeEntry({ k: "moveFrames", frames: it.frames.map(frameFile), moves: it.moves.map((m) => this._moveFile(m)) });
              } else if (it.k === "removeFrames") {

                  const records = [], frames = [];
                  for (const p of it.parts) {
                      if (p.op === "eraseMany" || p.op === "removeMany") for (const r of p.records) records.push({ level: lvl(r.level), index: r.index, obj: r.obj });
                      else if (p.op === "removeFrames" || p.op === "eraseFrames") frames.push(...frameRecs(p.records));
                  }
                  enc = encodeEntry({ k: "deleteMany", records, frames });
              } else if (it.k === "settle") {
                  enc = { op: { k: "settle", frame: it.frame, of: it.of, ...settleFile(it.rec) }, geo: EMPTY };
              } else if (it.k === "mergeFrame") {
                  enc = { op: { k: "mergeFrame", of: it.of, rec: mergeFile(it.rec) }, geo: EMPTY };
              }
              if (!enc) continue;
              entries.push({ seq: it.seq, t: Date.now(), op: enc.op, geo: enc.geo, touches: it.touches });
          }
          const out = { entries, forced: [...this.forced], full: this.needFull };
          this.queue = []; this.queued = new Map(); this.removed = new Map(); this.forced = new Set(); this.needFull = false;
          return out;
      }

      _selSeamRects(piece, table, F, cache) {

  export function encodeDrawing({ camera, crossings, natives, meta = {} }) {
      const now = new Date().toISOString();
      const decoded = decodeMeta(meta, { lenient: true });
      const outMeta = {
          name: decoded.name,
          createdAt: decoded.createdAt,
          modifiedAt: now,
          app: "bound.less",
      };
      if (decoded.scaleDef) outMeta.scaleDef = decoded.scaleDef;
      if (decoded.scenes) outMeta.scenes = decoded.scenes;
+     if (decoded.sceneIndex) outMeta.sceneIndex = decoded.sceneIndex;
      let version = 1;
      for (const l of Object.keys(natives || {})) {
          for (const o of natives[l] || []) {
              if (o && o.type === "shape" && encodedLoopsNeedV2(o.loops)) { version = VERSION; break; }
          }
          if (version === VERSION) break;
      }
      return {
          format: FORMAT,
          version,
          meta: outMeta,
          camera: {
              activeLevel: camera.activeLevel, inScale: camera.inScale,
              inPanX: camera.inPanX, inPanY: camera.inPanY,
              ...(camera.frame != null ? { frame: camera.frame } : {}),
          },
          crossings: crossings || {},
          natives: natives || {},
      };
  }

  function decodeMeta(m, { lenient = false } = {}) {
      const now = new Date().toISOString();
      const out = {
          name: m && typeof m.name === "string" && m.name.trim() ? m.name.trim().slice(0, 200) : "untitled",
          createdAt: m && typeof m.createdAt === "string" ? m.createdAt : now,
          modifiedAt: m && typeof m.modifiedAt === "string" ? m.modifiedAt : now,
      };
      if (m && m.scaleDef != null) {
          const sd = decodeScaleDef(m.scaleDef, { lenient });
          if (sd) out.scaleDef = sd;
      }
      if (m && Array.isArray(m.scenes)) {
          const scenes = m.scenes.filter(validScene).map(cloneScene);
          if (scenes.length) out.scenes = scenes;
      }
      if (m && Number.isInteger(m.sceneSeq) && m.sceneSeq > 0) out.sceneSeq = m.sceneSeq;
+     const si = decodeSceneIndex(m && m.sceneIndex);
+     if (si) out.sceneIndex = si;
+     return out;
+ }
//// src/engine/persist.js:162
+ const MEMBERS = /^[0-9a-z.,]*$/;
+ const finite = (v) => typeof v === "number" && Number.isFinite(v);
+ function decodeSceneIndex(si) {
+     if (!si || typeof si !== "object" || si.v !== 3 || !Array.isArray(si.scenes)) return null;
+     const idn = (s) => typeof s.id === "string" && s.id && s.id.length <= 40 && typeof s.name === "string" && isFrameKey(s.anchor);
+     const named = (p) => p && typeof p.id === "string" && p.id && p.id.length <= 40 && (p.name == null || typeof p.name === "string");
+     const nameOf = (p) => (typeof p.name === "string" ? p.name.slice(0, 120) : null);
+     const scenes = si.scenes.filter((s) => s && idn(s) && typeof s.m === "string" && MEMBERS.test(s.m)).map((s) => ({
+         id: s.id, seq: Number.isInteger(s.seq) ? s.seq : 0, name: s.name.slice(0, 120), named: !!s.named, anchor: s.anchor,
+         ox: finite(s.ox) ? s.ox : 0, oy: finite(s.oy) ? s.oy : 0, version: Number.isInteger(s.version) ? s.version : 0,
+         ...(typeof s.vtag === "string" && /^[0-9a-z]{1,12}$/.test(s.vtag) ? { vtag: s.vtag } : {}),
+         Lmin: finite(s.Lmin) ? s.Lmin : 0, Lmax: finite(s.Lmax) ? s.Lmax : 0,
+         ...(validRect(s.rect) ? { rect: cloneRect(s.rect) } : {}), m: s.m,

+     }));

//// src/engine/persist.js:296
  function decodeNatives(n) {
      if (n == null) return { 0: [] };
      if (typeof n !== "object" || Array.isArray(n)) throw new Error("bad natives: expected an object of frames");
      const seen = new Set();
      for (const l of Object.keys(n)) {
+         if (!FRAME_ID.test(l)) throw new Error(`bad natives: frame "${l}" is not a valid frame id`);
          if (!Array.isArray(n[l])) throw new Error(`bad natives: frame ${l} is not an array`);
          const survivors = [];
          for (const o of n[l]) {
              if (!o || typeof o !== "object") throw new Error(`bad object at level ${l}: not an object`);
              if (!Number.isInteger(o.id) || o.id < 1) throw new Error(`bad object at level ${l}: id must be a positive integer`);
              if (seen.has(o.id)) throw new Error(`bad natives: duplicate id ${o.id}`);
              seen.add(o.id);
              if (o.z != null && !isFiniteNum(o.z)) throw new Error(`bad object ${o.id}: z must be a number`);
              if (o.editId != null && (!Number.isInteger(o.editId) || o.editId < 1)) throw new Error(`bad object ${o.id}: editId must be a positive integer`);
              if (o.attachRect != null && !validOwnedRect(o.attachRect)) throw new Error(`bad object ${o.id}: attachRect is malformed`);
              if (o.below != null) {
                  if (!Array.isArray(o.below) || !o.below.every((t) => Array.isArray(t) && t.length === 3
                      && Number.isInteger(t[0]) && t[0] >= 0 && isFiniteNum(t[1]) && isFiniteNum(t[2]))) {
                      throw new Error(`bad object ${o.id}: below (offsets under the home) is malformed`);
                  }
              }
              if (o.tile != null) {
                  if (!(Array.isArray(o.tile) && o.tile.length === 2
                      && isFiniteNum(o.tile[0]) && isFiniteNum(o.tile[1]))) {
                      throw new Error(`bad object ${o.id}: tile phase is malformed`);
                  }
                  o.tile = [tilePhase(o.tile[0]), tilePhase(o.tile[1])];
              }
              if (o.type === "stroke") {
                  if (!validPts(o.pts) || o.pts.length < 1) throw new Error(`bad stroke ${o.id}: pts must be a non-empty array of finite [x,y]`);
                  if (!isFiniteNum(o.lwFrame) || o.lwFrame <= 0) throw new Error(`bad stroke ${o.id}: lwFrame must be a positive number`);
              } else if (o.type === "fill") {
                  if (!Array.isArray(o.polys) || !o.polys.length || !o.polys.every(validPts)) {
                      throw new Error(`bad fill ${o.id}: polys must be arrays of finite [x,y] rings`);
                  }
              } else if (o.type === "shape") {
                  if (!encodedLoopsWellFormed(o.loops)) {
                      throw new Error(`bad shape ${o.id}: loops must be arrays of finite numbers`);
                  }
                  if (!validEncodedLoops(o.loops)) {
                      const fixed = repairLoops(decodeLoops(o.loops), o.w);
                      if (!fixed.length) continue;
                      o.loops = encodeLoops(fixed);
                  }
              } else {
                  throw new Error(`bad object ${o.id}: unknown type "${o && o.type}"`);
              }
              survivors.push(o);
          }
          n[l] = survivors;
      }
      return n;
  }
//// src/engine/raster/MovePlane.js  (new file)
+ import { PLATE_PX, PREVIEW_QUANTA, QUANTA_PER_OCTAVE, plateKey, zqOf } from "./plate";
+ import { paintFamilies } from "./rasterPack";
+ export const MOVE_PLANE_LAYERS = 5;
+ export const MOVE_PLANE_PLATES = 40;
+ export const MOVE_PLANE_PLATES_PHONE = 24;
+ export const PLANE_COARSEN_MAX = 3;
+ export const PLANE_QUICK_OCTAVES = 2;
+ const PLANE_QUICK_MIN = 4;
+ const PLANE_SCALED_MAX = 16;
+ export const MOVE_HOLD_MS = 3000;
+ export const planeStats = {
+     builds: 0, merged: 0, coarse: 0, overCap: 0, requests: 0, drags: 0, notReady: 0, lost: 0,
+     quickSteps: 0, partialSteps: 0, stopgaps: 0, stopgapMsMax: 0,
+     lands: 0, landTimeouts: 0, landMsMax: 0,
+     composes: 0, underBlits: 0, inkBlits: 0, overBlits: 0, clipComposes: 0, maxLayers: 0,
+     farListFallbacks: 0,
+ };
+ function idsHash(ids) {
+     let a = 0, b = 0;
+     for (const id of ids) {
+         let h = Math.imul(Number(id) ^ 0x9e3779b9, 0x85ebca6b);
+         h ^= h >>> 13; h = Math.imul(h, 0xc2b2ae35); h ^= h >>> 16;
+         a = (a + h) | 0; b ^= h;
+     }
+     return (a >>> 0).toString(36) + "." + (b >>> 0).toString(36) + "." + ids.size;
+ }
+ function rangeOf(cells) {
+     if (!cells.length) return null;
+     let i0 = Infinity, i1 = -Infinity, j0 = Infinity, j1 = -Infinity;
+     for (const [i, j] of cells) { if (i < i0) i0 = i; if (i > i1) i1 = i; if (j < j0) j0 = j; if (j > j1) j1 = j; }
+     return { i0, i1, j0, j1 };
+ }
+ export default class MovePlane {
+     constructor() {
+         this.stats = planeStats;
+         this.gen = 0;
+         this._reset();
+     }
+     _reset() {
+         this.sel = null;
+         this.ids = null;
+         this.list = null;
+         this.frameId = null; this.q = null; this.Zq = 1;
+         this.main = null;
+         this.quick = null;
+         this.cur = null;
+         this.partial = false;
+         this.stop = null;
+         this.layers = [];
+         this.selIds = null;
+         this.max = 0;
+         this.tags = [];
+         this.r = null;
+         this.selR = null;
+         this.keys = [];
+         this.state = "idle";
+         this.ready = false;
+         this.off = [0, 0];
+         this.landUntil = 0;
+         this.landAt = 0;
+         this.refused = false;
+         this.gen = (this.gen || 0) + 1;
+     }
+     get showing() { return this.state === "drag" || this.state === "land"; }
+     aim(st, list, view, cfg, max, dpr = 1, live = null) {
+         if (this.state === "drag" || this.state === "land") return true;
+         const same = st && this.sel === st.key && this.frameId === view.frameId && this.q === view.q && this.Zq === view.Zq;
+         if (!same) {
+             this._reset();
+             if (!st || !st.ids || !st.ids.size) return false;
+             this.sel = st.key; this.frameId = view.frameId; this.q = view.q; this.Zq = view.Zq;
+             planeStats.builds++;
+         }
+         if (this.list !== list || this.ids !== st.ids || this.max !== max) {
+             this.list = list; this.ids = st.ids; this.max = max;
+             this._cut(list, st.ids, view, cfg, max, dpr, live);
+         }
+         return !this.refused;
+     }
+     _cut(list, ids, view, cfg, max, dpr, live) {
+         this.main = null; this.quick = null; this.cur = null; this.stop = null; this.ready = false; this.partial = false;
+         this.layers = []; this.keys = []; this.tags = []; this.r = null; this.selR = null; this.selIds = null; this.refused = true;
+         const s = view.Zq / PLATE_PX;
+         const runs = [];
+         for (const f of paintFamilies(list, view.q, view.Zq, dpr, cfg, live)) {
+             if (f.all.length && f.all.every((d) => d.o.erase)) continue;
+             let sel = false, on = false;
+             for (const d of f.all) {
+                 if (ids.has(d.o.id)) sel = true;
+                 const b = d.box;
+                 if (Math.floor(b[2] * s) >= view.i0 && Math.floor(b[0] * s) <= view.i1 && Math.floor(b[3] * s) >= view.j0 && Math.floor(b[1] * s) <= view.j1) on = true;
+             }
+             if (!on) continue;
+             let run = runs[runs.length - 1];
+             if (!run || run.sel !== sel) runs.push(run = { sel, ids: new Set(), boxes: [] });
+             for (const d of f.all) { run.ids.add(d.o.id); run.boxes.push(d.box); }
+         }
+         if (!runs.some((r) => r.sel)) { this.refused = true; return; }
+         if (!runs[0].sel) runs.shift();
+         const nLayers = runs.length + 1;
+         if (nLayers > planeStats.maxLayers) planeStats.maxLayers = nLayers;
+         while (runs.length + 1 > MOVE_PLANE_LAYERS) {
+             let m = -1;
+             for (let k = 1; k < runs.length - 1; k++) if (!runs[k].sel && (m < 0 || runs[k].boxes.length < runs[m].boxes.length)) m = k;
+             if (m < 0) break;
+             const a = runs[m - 1], b = runs[m + 1];
+             for (const id of b.ids) a.ids.add(id);
+             for (const x of b.boxes) a.boxes.push(x);
+             runs.splice(m, 2);
+             planeStats.merged++;
+         }
+         this.selIds = new Set();
+         for (const r of runs) if (r.sel) for (const id of r.ids) this.selIds.add(id);
+         const capAt = (k) => (k || view.s !== 1 ? Math.min(max, PLANE_SCALED_MAX) : max);
+         let main = null;
+         for (let k = 0; k <= PLANE_COARSEN_MAX && !main; k++) {
+             const L = this._level(runs, view, k);
+             if (L.plates <= capAt(k)) main = L;
+             else if (k === PLANE_COARSEN_MAX) { main = L; planeStats.overCap++; }
+         }
+         if (main.k) planeStats.coarse++;
+         if (main.plates > PLANE_QUICK_MIN) {
+             const Q = this._level(runs, view, main.k + PLANE_QUICK_OCTAVES);
+             if (Q.plates < main.plates && main.plates + Q.plates <= max && Q.plates <= PLANE_SCALED_MAX) this.quick = Q;
+         }
+         this.main = main;
+         this.layers = main.layers;
+         this.r = main.r; this.selR = main.selR;
+         for (const L of [main, this.quick]) if (L) { for (const t of L.tags) this.tags.push(t); for (const key of L.keys) this.keys.push(key); }
+         this.refused = false;
+     }
+     _level(runs, view, k) {
+         let pq = view.q, pZq = view.Zq;
+         if (k) {
+             const q0 = typeof view.q === "number" ? view.q : Math.floor(Math.log2(view.Zq) * QUANTA_PER_OCTAVE + 1e-9);
+             pq = q0 - k * QUANTA_PER_OCTAVE; pZq = zqOf(pq);
+         }
+         const f = pZq / view.Zq, s = pZq / PLATE_PX;
+         const vi0 = Math.floor(view.i0 * f), vi1 = Math.floor((view.i1 + 1) * f - 1e-9);
+         const vj0 = Math.floor(view.j0 * f), vj1 = Math.floor((view.j1 + 1) * f - 1e-9);
+         const cellsOf = (boxes) => {
+             const out = new Set();
+             for (const b of boxes) {
+                 const i0 = Math.max(Math.floor(b[0] * s), vi0), i1 = Math.min(Math.floor(b[2] * s), vi1);
+                 const j0 = Math.max(Math.floor(b[1] * s), vj0), j1 = Math.min(Math.floor(b[3] * s), vj1);
+                 for (let j = j0; j <= j1; j++) for (let i = i0; i <= i1; i++) out.add(i + "," + j);
+             }
+             return out;
+         };
+         const toList = (set) => [...set].map((c) => c.split(",").map(Number));
+         const below = new Set(), omit = new Set(), per = [];
+         for (const r of runs) {
+             const c = cellsOf(r.boxes);
+             per.push(c);
+             for (const x of c) below.add(x);
+             for (const id of r.ids) omit.add(id);
+         }
+         const layers = [{ kind: "below", cells: toList(below), omit }];
+         runs.forEach((r, n) => layers.push({ kind: r.sel ? "sel" : "over", cells: toList(per[n]), ids: r.ids }));
+         const tags = [], keys = [];
+         let plates = 0;
+         layers.forEach((L, n) => {
+             L.tag = "z" + n + "_" + pq + "_" + idsHash(L.kind === "below" ? L.omit : L.ids);
+             tags.push(L.tag);
+             plates += L.cells.length;
+             for (const [i, j] of L.cells) keys.push(plateKey(this.frameId, L.tag, i, j));
+         });
+         const selCells = [].concat(...layers.filter((L) => L.kind === "sel").map((L) => L.cells));
+         return { k, pq, pZq, layers, tags, keys, plates, r: rangeOf(layers[0].cells), selR: rangeOf(selCells), below };
+     }
+     farLevels() {
+         return [this.quick, this.main].filter((lvl) => lvl && lvl.list === undefined && !lvl.listPending && lvl.k * QUANTA_PER_OCTAVE > PREVIEW_QUANTA);
+     }
+     coverOf(lvl) {
+         const r = lvl.r, u = PLATE_PX / lvl.pZq;
+         return r ? { left: r.i0 * u, top: r.j0 * u, right: (r.i1 + 1) * u, bottom: (r.j1 + 1) * u } : null;
+     }
+     requests(list) {
+         if (this.refused || !this.main || this.state === "land") return null;
+         const out = [];
+         for (const lvl of [this.quick, this.main]) {
+             if (!lvl || lvl.listPending) continue;
+             for (const L of lvl.layers) {
+                 const base = { frameId: this.frameId, Zq: lvl.pZq, q: L.tag, list: lvl.list || list, offCam: true, prio: 2, mode: "plane", cells: L.cells };
+                 out.push(L.kind === "below" ? { ...base, omit: L.omit } : { ...base, only: L.ids, paper: [0, 0, 0, 0] });
+             }
+         }
+         return out;
+     }
+     _whole(lvl, store, selOnly = false) {
+         if (!lvl) return false;
+         for (const L of lvl.layers) {
+             if (selOnly && L.kind !== "sel") continue;
+             for (const [i, j] of L.cells) { const e = store.get(plateKey(this.frameId, L.tag, i, j)); if (!e || e.dead) return false; }
+         }
+         return true;
+     }
+     checkReady(store) {
+         if (!this.ready && this._whole(this.main, store)) this.ready = true;
+         return this.ready;
+     }
+     pick(store) {
+         this.partial = false;
+         if (this.checkReady(store)) return (this.cur = this.main);
+         if (this._whole(this.quick, store)) return (this.cur = this.quick);
+         for (const lvl of [this.main, this.quick]) if (this._whole(lvl, store, true)) { this.partial = true; return (this.cur = lvl); }
+         this.cur = null;
+         return null;
+     }
+     dragTo(dxCss, dyCss, dpr) {
+         if (this.state !== "drag") planeStats.drags++;
+         this.state = "drag";
+         this.off[0] = Math.round(dxCss * dpr);
+         this.off[1] = Math.round(dyCss * dpr);
+     }
+     land(now) {
+         if (this.state !== "drag") return false;
+         this.state = "land";
+         this.landAt = now;
+         this.landUntil = now + MOVE_HOLD_MS;
+         planeStats.lands++;
+         return true;
+     }
+     landCells(v) {
+         const lvl = this.cur || this.main;
+         const r = lvl && lvl.r, sr = lvl && lvl.selR;
+         if (!r || !sr) return null;
+         const f = v.Zq / lvl.pZq;
+         const ex = this.off[0] / (PLATE_PX * v.s), ey = this.off[1] / (PLATE_PX * v.s);
+         const i0 = Math.min(Math.floor(r.i0 * f), Math.floor(sr.i0 * f + ex));
+         const i1 = Math.max(Math.ceil((r.i1 + 1) * f) - 1, Math.ceil((sr.i1 + 1) * f + ex) - 1);
+         const j0 = Math.min(Math.floor(r.j0 * f), Math.floor(sr.j0 * f + ey));
+         const j1 = Math.max(Math.ceil((r.j1 + 1) * f) - 1, Math.ceil((sr.j1 + 1) * f + ey) - 1);
+         return { i0: Math.max(i0, v.i0), i1: Math.min(i1, v.i1), j0: Math.max(j0, v.j0), j1: Math.min(j1, v.j1) };
+     }
+ }

+     }
+     get planeW() { return snapPx(this.cssW * this.dpr); }
+     get planeH() { return snapPx(this.cssH * this.dpr); }
+     _style(c, cssW, cssH, z) {
+         if (!c.style) return;
+         c.style.position = "absolute";
+         c.style.left = "0"; c.style.top = "0";
+         c.style.transformOrigin = "0 0";
+         c.style.pointerEvents = "none";
+         c.style.zIndex = String(z);
+         c.style.width = cssW + "px";
+         c.style.height = cssH + "px";
+     }
+     _mount() {
+         if (this._canvas) return this._canvas;
+         const w = this.planeW, h = this.planeH;
+         const c = this._mk(w, h);
+         if (!c) return null;
+         c.width = w; c.height = h;
+         this._style(c, this.cssW, this.cssH, 2);
+         if (c.style) c.style.willChange = "transform";
+         if (c.setAttribute) { c.setAttribute("class", "bl-overlay"); c.setAttribute("aria-hidden", "true"); }
+         this._ctx = c.getContext ? c.getContext("2d") : null;
+         this._canvas = c;
+         return c;
+     }
+     _mountStrips() {
+         const dpr = this.dpr, t = Math.max(2, Math.ceil(EDGE_STRIP_CSS * dpr));
+         const W = this.planeW, H = this.planeH;
+         let bytes = 0;
+         for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
+             const side = SIDES[i];
+             const w = (side === "top" || side === "bottom") ? W : t;
+             const h = (side === "top" || side === "bottom") ? t : H;
+             let s = this._strips[i];
+             if (s && s.w === w && s.h === h && s.buf) { bytes += s.buf.bytes; continue; }
+             if (!s) {
+                 const c = this._mk(w, h);
+                 if (!c) continue;
+                 if (c.setAttribute) { c.setAttribute("class", "bl-overlay-edge"); c.setAttribute("aria-hidden", "true"); }
+                 s = { side, canvas: c, ctx: c.getContext ? c.getContext("2d") : null, buf: null, img: null, w: 0, h: 0 };
+                 this._strips[i] = s;
+             }
+             s.w = w; s.h = h;
+             s.canvas.width = w; s.canvas.height = h;
+             this._style(s.canvas, w / dpr, h / dpr, 2);
+             if (s.canvas.style) {
+                 if (side === "right") s.canvas.style.left = (W - w) / dpr + "px";
+                 if (side === "bottom") s.canvas.style.top = (H - h) / dpr + "px";
+             }
+             if (s.buf) s.buf.resize(w, h); else s.buf = new OverlayBuffer(w, h);
+             s.img = this._mkImg(s.buf.data, w, h);
+             if (!s.img) this._rep.noImageData++;
+             this._rep.allocs++;
+             bytes += s.buf.bytes;
+         }
+         this._rep.stripBytes = bytes;
+     }
+     _ensurePlane() {
+         const w = this.planeW, h = this.planeH;
+         if (!this.buf) {
+             this.buf = new OverlayBuffer(w, h);
+             this._img = this._mkImg(this.buf.data, w, h);
+             if (!this._img) this._rep.noImageData++;
+             this._rep.allocs++;
+             this._planeDirty = true;
+         } else if (this.buf.resize(w, h)) {
+             this._img = this._mkImg(this.buf.data, w, h);
+             if (!this._img) this._rep.noImageData++;
+             this._rep.allocs++;
+             this._planeDirty = true;
+         }
+         this._mountStrips();
+         return this.buf;
+     }
+     _attach() {
+         if (this._shown || !this.host || !this.host.appendChild) return;
+         if (this._canvas) this.host.appendChild(this._canvas);
+         for (const s of this._strips) if (s && s.canvas) this.host.appendChild(s.canvas);
+         this._shown = true;
+     }
+     _detach() {
+         const els = [this._canvas].concat(this._strips.map((s) => s && s.canvas));
+         for (const c of els) if (c && c.parentNode && c.parentNode.removeChild) c.parentNode.removeChild(c);
+         this._shown = false;
+     }
+     setTransform(s, dx, dy) {
+         const t = this._t;
+         t.s = s; t.dx = dx; t.dy = dy;
+         this._rep.transforms++;
+         const c = this._canvas;
+         if (c && c.style) c.style.transform = "matrix(" + s + ", 0, 0, " + s + ", " + dx + ", " + dy + ")";
+     }
+     _applyTransform() {
+         const p = this._paintAt, v = this._view;
+         const s = p.k > 0 && isFinite(p.k) ? v.k / p.k : 1;
+         this.setTransform(s, (v.tx - p.tx * s) / this.dpr, (v.ty - p.ty * s) / this.dpr);
+     }
+     _readView(view, dec) {
+         const v = this._view;
+         v.k = view.k; v.tx = view.tx; v.ty = view.ty;
+         const off = dec && dec.off;
+         if (off && off.length === 2) {
+             v.tx += Math.round(off[0] * this.dpr);
+             v.ty += Math.round(off[1] * this.dpr);
+         }
+         return v;
+     }
+     _paint(phase, repaint) {
+         const buf = this._ensurePlane();
+         if (!buf || !this._dec) return;
+         const t0 = this._now();
+         const r = paintPhase(buf, this._dec, this._view, phase, this._css);
+         const t1 = this._now();
+         const p = this._paintAt, v = this._view;
+         p.k = v.k; p.tx = v.tx; p.ty = v.ty;
+         this.setTransform(1, 0, 0);
+         this.blit();
+         const rep = this._rep;
+         rep.paintMs = t1 - t0; rep.lastPhaseMs = t1 - t0;
+         rep.dashes = r.dashes; rep.stamps = r.stamps; rep.pixels = r.pixels;
+         rep.refused += r.refused; rep.nonFinite += r.nonFinite;
+         rep.phases++;
+         this._planeDirty = false;
+         this._lastRepaintAt = this._now();
+         if (repaint) {
+             rep.repaints++;
+             if (this._frozen) rep.repaintsDuringGesture++;
+         }
+         rep.uncoveredPx = 0;
+     }
+     blit(rect) {
+         const buf = this.buf, ctx = this._ctx, img = this._img;
+         this._rep.blitMs = 0; this._rep.blitPx = 0;
+         if (!buf || !ctx || !img) return 0;
+         const r = rect || buf.dirty;
+         const x0 = Math.max(0, r.x0 | 0), y0 = Math.max(0, r.y0 | 0);
+         const x1 = Math.min(buf.width, r.x1 | 0), y1 = Math.min(buf.height, r.y1 | 0);
+         if (x1 <= x0 || y1 <= y0) return 0;
+         const t0 = this._now();
+         ctx.putImageData(img, 0, 0, x0, y0, x1 - x0, y1 - y0);
+         this._rep.blitMs = this._now() - t0;
+         this._rep.blitPx = (x1 - x0) * (y1 - y0);
+         this._rep.blits++;
+         return this._rep.blitPx;
+     }
+     _paintEdges(dec, shift) {
