+         const edges = (dec && dec.edges) || [];
+         const dpr = this.dpr, kt = this._kt;
+         const inset = (ANT_LOOK.W * kt / 2) * dpr;
+         const sx = shift ? shift[0] * dpr : 0, sy = shift ? shift[1] * dpr : 0;
+         this._edgeDec.inkPx = dec ? dec.inkPx : null;
+         let painted = 0;
+         for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
+             const s = this._strips[i];
+             if (!s || !s.buf) continue;
+             const side = SIDES[i];
+             const runs = this._edgeDec.runs;
+             runs.length = 0;
+             for (let j = 0; j < edges.length; j++) {
+                 const e = edges[j];
+                 if (e.side !== side) continue;
+                 const run = this._edgeRun(runs.length);
+                 const p = run.pieces[0];
+                 if (side === "left" || side === "right") {
+                     const x = side === "left" ? inset : s.w - inset;
+                     p.A[0] = x; p.B[0] = x;
+                     p.A[1] = e.from * dpr + sy; p.B[1] = e.to * dpr + sy;
+                 } else {
+                     const y = side === "top" ? inset : s.h - inset;
+                     p.A[1] = y; p.B[1] = y;
+                     p.A[0] = e.from * dpr + sx; p.B[0] = e.to * dpr + sx;
+                 }
+                 runs.push(run);
+             }
+             const r = paintPhase(s.buf, this._edgeDec, this._edgeView, this._phase, this._css);
+             painted += r.dashes;
+             const d = s.buf.dirty;
+             if (s.ctx && s.img && d.x1 > d.x0 && d.y1 > d.y0) {
+                 s.ctx.putImageData(s.img, 0, 0, d.x0, d.y0, d.x1 - d.x0, d.y1 - d.y0);
+             }
+         }
+         this._rep.edgeDashes = painted;
+     }
+     _edgeRun(i) {
+         let r = this._edgeRuns[i];
+         if (!r) {
+             r = { pieces: [{ line: true, A: [0, 0], B: [0, 0] }], closed: false, len: 0 };
+             this._edgeRuns[i] = r;
+             this._rep.allocs++;
+         }
+         return r;
+     }
+     _arm() {
+         if (this._timer != null || !this._shown || this._reduced || !this._dec) return;
+         this._timer = this._setTimer(() => { this._timer = null; this.step(); this._arm(); }, ANT_STEP_MS);
+     }
+     _disarm() {
+         if (this._timer != null) { this._clearTimer(this._timer); this._timer = null; }
+         if (this._resume != null) { this._clearTimer(this._resume); this._resume = null; }
+     }
+     freeze() {
+         this._frozen = true;
+         if (this._resume != null) { this._clearTimer(this._resume); this._resume = null; }
+         this._resume = this._setTimer(() => { this._resume = null; this.resume(); }, SEL_CRAWL_RESUME_MS);
+     }
+     resume() { this._frozen = false; }
+     step() {
+         if (!this._dec || !this._shown || this._reduced) return;
+         if (this._frozen) { this._maybeUncovered(); this._note(); return; }
+         this._phase = (this._phase + 1) % ANT_PHASES;
+         this._paint(this._phase, this._planeDirty);
+         this._note();
+     }
+     _note() {
+         if (this.perf && this.perf.noteCrawl) this.perf.noteCrawl(this.report());
+     }
+     _uncoveredPx() {
+         const W = this.planeW, H = this.planeH;
+         if (!(W > 0) || !(H > 0)) return 0;
+         const t = this._t, dpr = this.dpr;
+         const x0 = t.dx * dpr, y0 = t.dy * dpr;
+         const x1 = x0 + W * t.s, y1 = y0 + H * t.s;
+         const w = Math.min(W, x1) - Math.max(0, x0), h = Math.min(H, y1) - Math.max(0, y0);
+         const cov = (w > 0 ? w : 0) * (h > 0 ? h : 0);
+         const un = W * H - cov;
+         return un > 0 ? Math.round(un) : 0;
+     }
+     _maybeUncovered() {
+         const un = this._uncoveredPx();
+         const rep = this._rep;
+         rep.uncoveredPx = un;
+         if (un <= 0) return;
+         rep.uncoveredFrames++;
+         const f = un / (this.planeW * this.planeH);
+         if (f > rep.uncoveredWorst) rep.uncoveredWorst = f;
+         if (this._now() - this._lastRepaintAt >= OVERLAY_UNCOVERED_MS) this._paint(this._phase, true);
+     }
+     show(dec, view, shift) {
+         const runs = (dec && dec.runs) || [], marks = (dec && dec.marks) || [], edges = (dec && dec.edges) || [];
+         if (!dec || !view || !(runs.length || marks.length || edges.length)) { this.hide(); return; }
+         if (!this._mount()) return;
+         this._ensurePlane();
+         this._attach();
+         this._dec = dec;
+         const kt = selAntScale(dec.inkPx);
+         this._readView(view, dec);
+         const moved = view.q !== this._q || view.Zq !== this._Zq || view.mode !== this._mode;
+         const changed = dec.key !== this._key || kt !== this._kt;
+         const repaint = moved || changed || this._planeDirty;
+         this._key = dec.key; this._kt = kt;
+         this._q = view.q; this._Zq = view.Zq; this._mode = view.mode;
+         if (repaint) this._paint(this._phase, true);
+         else this._applyTransform();
+         this.freeze();
+         this._arm();
+         this._paintEdges(dec, shift);
+         if (!repaint) this._maybeUncovered();
+     }
+     hide() {
+         if (!this._shown && !this._dec) return;
+         this._disarm();
+         this._detach();
+         this._dec = null; this._key = null;
+         this._frozen = false;
+         this._rep.uncoveredPx = 0;
+     }
+     setSize(cssW, cssH, dpr) {
+         const d = dpr > 0 ? dpr : this.dpr;
+         if (cssW === this.cssW && cssH === this.cssH && d === this.dpr) return false;
+         this.cssW = cssW; this.cssH = cssH; this.dpr = d; this._css.dpr = d;
+         const c = this._canvas;
+         if (c) {
+             c.width = this.planeW; c.height = this.planeH;
+             this._style(c, cssW, cssH, 2);
+             if (c.style) c.style.willChange = "transform";
+         }
+         this._ensurePlane();
+         if (this._dec && this._shown) this._paint(this._phase, true);
+         return true;
+     }
+     onStoreRelease(reason) {
+         this.buf = null; this._img = null;
+         for (const s of this._strips) if (s) { s.buf = null; s.img = null; s.w = 0; s.h = 0; }
+         this._rep.stripBytes = 0;
+         this._planeDirty = true;
+         return reason;
+     }
+     destroy() {
+         this._disarm();
+         this._detach();
+         this._canvas = null; this._ctx = null;
+         this.buf = null; this._img = null;
+         this._strips = [null, null, null, null];
+         this._dec = null; this._key = null;
+         this._rep.stripBytes = 0;
+         this._planeDirty = true;
+     }
+     setReducedMotion(v) {
+         const was = this._reduced;
+         this._reduced = !!v;
+         if (this._reduced) { this._disarm(); this._phase = 0; if (this._dec && this._shown) this._paint(0, true); }
+         else if (was) this._arm();
+     }
+     noteDecide(ms) { this._rep.decideMs = ms > 0 ? ms : 0; }
+     report() {
+         const r = this._rep;
+         r.bytes = this.buf ? this.buf.bytes : 0;
+         return r;
+     }
+ }
//// src/engine/raster/PictureLayer.js  (new file)
+ import { PLATE_PX, QUANTA_PER_OCTAVE, QUANTUM_HYSTERESIS, PREVIEW_QUANTA, quantumFor, zqOf, plateRange, plateKey, plateOriginPx } from "./plate";
+ const S_MIN = Math.pow(2, -QUANTUM_HYSTERESIS / QUANTA_PER_OCTAVE);
+ export const S_MAX_HOLD = 2;
+ export const S_MIN_HOLD = 0.5;
+ const PREVIEW_STICKY = 2;
+ const PREVIEW_FAST = 12;
+ const FAST_ZOOM_MS = 150;
+ const SCREEN_CELLS_MAX = 4096;
+ export const COMPOSE_IMAGES = 30;
+ const FB_DRAW_MIN = 4;
+ const CARRY_MAX_SC = 65536;
+ export const SETTLE_MS = 150;
+ export const SYNC_CHORD_BUDGET = 5000;
+ export const SYNC_MAX_MS = 8;
+ const DEFAULT_PAPER = "#ffffff";
+ const STALE_LOG = 8;
+ export const pictureStats = {
+     composes: 0, layerRecomposes: 0, blits: 0, paperFills: 0, plateBlits: 0,
+     arrivals: 0, arrivalComposes: 0, arrivalsOutside: 0,
+     staleDrops: 0, staleUnwanted: 0, staleNewer: 0, reposts: 0,
+     settles: 0, settlePublishes: 0, settleDrops: 0,
+     quantumChanges: 0, syncFlips: 0,
+     syncCostExits: 0,
+     previews: 0, previewHits: 0, previewKept: 0,
+     holds: 0, holdsPartial: 0, holdSkips: 0, holdSwitches: 0, quantumAbandons: 0,
+     heldComposes: 0, holdMaxS: 0, holdMinS: 0, holdBreaks: 0,
+     holdShrinks: 0,
+     paperComposes: 0, paperCellsMax: 0, coverRefused: 0,
+     crossFrameBlits: 0,
+     carryComposes: 0, carryShifted: 0, carryDropped: 0,
+     switchRefusedLow: 0, switchRefusedHigh: 0,
+     staleComposes: 0, stalePlateBlits: 0,
+     fallbackComposes: 0, fallbackBlits: 0, fallbackScans: 0, fallbackRefused: 0, fallbackMaxR: 0,
+     fallbackCulled: 0, fallbackOverBudget: 0,
+     canvasBytes: 0, layerBytes: 0, canvasAllocs: 0,
+     noContext: 0, noBitmap: 0, zqMismatch: 0, sizeMismatch: 0, badZoom: 0, layerOverflow: 0,
+     deadRefused: 0,
+     syncRefused: 0,
+     allocs: 0,
+ };
+ function snapPx(x) { return Math.round(x); }
+ function stretchOf(Z, Zq) { return Zq > 0 ? Z / Zq : 1; }
+ function mkCanvas(w, h) {
+     if (typeof document === "undefined" || !document.createElement) return null;
+     const c = document.createElement("canvas");
+     c.width = w; c.height = h;
+     return c;
+ }
+ export default class PictureLayer {
+     constructor(host, camera, opts = {}) {
+         this.host = host || null;
+         this.cam = camera || null;
+         this.store = opts.store || null;
+         this.paper = opts.paper || DEFAULT_PAPER;
+         this.settleMs = opts.settleMs != null ? opts.settleMs : SETTLE_MS;
+         this.syncBudget = opts.syncBudget != null ? opts.syncBudget : SYNC_CHORD_BUDGET;
+         this.syncMaxMs = opts.syncMaxMs != null ? opts.syncMaxMs : SYNC_MAX_MS;
+         this._syncMs = 0;
+         this._syncSlowAt = -1;
+         this._mk = opts.makeCanvas || mkCanvas;
+         this._onPlates = opts.onPlates || null;
+         this._onRepost = opts.onRepost || null;
+         this._now = opts.now || (() => Date.now());
+         this._setTimer = opts.setTimer || ((fn, ms) => setTimeout(fn, ms));
+         this._clearTimer = opts.clearTimer || ((t) => clearTimeout(t));
+         this.dpr = opts.dpr > 0 ? opts.dpr
+             : (typeof window !== "undefined" && window.devicePixelRatio > 0 ? window.devicePixelRatio : 1);
+         this.cssW = opts.width > 0 ? opts.width : (camera && camera.lm ? camera.lm.width : 0);
+         this.cssH = opts.height > 0 ? opts.height : (camera && camera.lm ? camera.lm.height : 0);
+         this._v = {
+             rev: 0, mode: "plates", frameId: null, q: 0, Zq: 1, Z: 1, s: 1,
+             ox: 0, oy: 0, dx: 0, dy: 0, dpr: this.dpr, cssW: this.cssW, cssH: this.cssH,
+             k: 1, tx: 0, ty: 0, i0: 0, i1: -1, j0: 0, j1: -1,
+         };
+         this._req = { mode: "settle", frameId: null, q: "e", Zq: 1, i0: 0, i1: -1, j0: 0, j1: -1, prio: 2 };
+         this._pend = { mode: "quantum", frameId: null, q: 0, Zq: 1, i0: 0, i1: -1, j0: 0, j1: -1, prio: 1 };
+         this._prev = { mode: "preview", frameId: null, q: 0, Zq: 1, i0: 0, i1: -1, j0: 0, j1: -1, prio: 0 };
+         this._prevLive = false;
+         this._qChangeAt = null;
+         this._hold = null;
+         this._pendLive = false;
+         this._holdWhy = "";
+         this._rHold = { i0: 0, i1: -1, j0: 0, j1: -1 };
+         this._rFull = { i0: 0, i1: -1, j0: 0, j1: -1 };
+         this._covered = false;
+         this._screenCovered = false;
+         this._layerDrawn = 0;
+         this._hits = [];
+         this._nHits = 0;
+         this._fb = [];
+         this._nFb = 0;
+         this._fbB = [];
+         this._uncovered = 0;
+         this._mf = new Map();
+         this._mfF = null;
+         this._mfE = -1;
+         this._mapFrameFn = (G) => this._mapFrame(G);
+         this._plane = null;
+         this._carry = null;
+         this._cyB = [];
+         this._nCy = 0;
+         this._layerDirty = true;
+         this._qPrev = null;
+         this._sync = false;
+         this._timer = null;
+         this._settlePending = false;
+         this._settleDone = false;
+         this._eLive = false;
+         this._cam0 = { frameId: null, inScale: NaN, panX: NaN, panY: NaN, sized: true };
+         this._stale = [];
+         this._staleAt = 0;
+         this._arr = { compose: false, req: null, miss: null };
+         this._fire = () => this._settleNow();
+         this._canvas = null; this._ctx = null;
+         this._layer = null; this._lctx = null;
+         this._mount();
+     }
+     _mount() {
+         const w = snapPx(this.cssW * this.dpr), h = snapPx(this.cssH * this.dpr);
+         const c = this._mk(w, h);
+         if (!c) return;
+         this._canvas = c;
+         c.width = w; c.height = h;
+         if (c.style) {
+             c.style.position = "absolute";
+             c.style.left = "0"; c.style.top = "0";
+             c.style.transformOrigin = "0 0";
+             c.style.zIndex = "0";
+             c.style.width = this.cssW + "px";
+             c.style.height = this.cssH + "px";
+         }
+         if (c.setAttribute) c.setAttribute("class", "bl-ink");
+         if (c.setAttribute) c.setAttribute("aria-hidden", "true");
+         this._ctx = c.getContext ? c.getContext("2d", { alpha: false }) : null;
+         if (!this._ctx) pictureStats.noContext++;
+         pictureStats.canvasAllocs++;
+         pictureStats.canvasBytes = w * h * 4;
+         if (this.host && this.host.appendChild) this.host.appendChild(c);
+     }
+     setSize(cssW, cssH, dpr) {
+         const d = dpr > 0 ? dpr : this.dpr;
+         if (cssW === this.cssW && cssH === this.cssH && d === this.dpr) return false;
+         this.cssW = cssW; this.cssH = cssH; this.dpr = d;
+         const c = this._canvas;
+         if (c) {
+             c.width = snapPx(cssW * d); c.height = snapPx(cssH * d);
+             if (c.style) { c.style.width = cssW + "px"; c.style.height = cssH + "px"; }
+             pictureStats.canvasBytes = c.width * c.height * 4;
+         }
+         this._dropLayer();
+         this._carry = null;
+         this._layerDirty = true;
+         this._cam0.sized = true;
+         if (this.store) this.store.noteDpr(d);
+         return true;
+     }
+     onStoreRelease(reason) {
+         this._dropLayer();
+         this._nHits = 0;
+         this._layerDirty = true;
+         this._endHold("release");
+         return reason;
+     }
+     destroy() {
+         this._clear();
+         this._endHold("destroy");
+         this._dropLayer();
+         const c = this._canvas;
+         if (c && c.parentNode && c.parentNode.removeChild) c.parentNode.removeChild(c);
+         this._canvas = null; this._ctx = null;
+         this._nHits = 0;
+         pictureStats.canvasBytes = 0;
+     }
+     view() { return this._v; }
+     status() {
+         const s = this._st || (this._st = { q: 0, pendQ: "", heldQ: "", prevQ: "", settlePending: false });
+         s.q = this._v.q;
+         s.pendQ = this._pendLive ? this._pend.q : "";
+         s.prevQ = this._prevLive ? this._prev.q : "";
+         s.heldQ = this._hold ? this._hold.q : "";
+         s.settlePending = this._settlePending;
+         return s;
+     }
+     syncSurface() {
+         const L = this._ensureLayer();
+         this._layerDirty = true;
+         return L;
+     }
+     frameFor(cam, chords) {
+         const c = cam || this.cam;
+         const v = this._frame(c, chords, 0);
+         if (this._prevLive) this._askPreview(c);
+         if (this._hold && this._pendLive) this._askQuantum(c);
+         return v;
+     }
+     _frame(cam, chords, stage) {
+         const v = this._v;
+         if (!cam) return v;
+         const dpr = this.dpr;
+         const frameId = cam.frame;
+         let Z = cam.inScale * dpr;
+         if (!(Z > 0) || !isFinite(Z)) { if (stage === 0) pictureStats.badZoom++; Z = 1; }
+         let moved = false;
+         if (stage === 0) {
+             moved = this._moved(cam, frameId);
+             if (!moved) { if (this._settleDone) return v; } else this._onMove();
+         }
+         let q, Zq, s = 1, mode, qLive = 0, ZqLive = 1, quantised = false;
+         if (stage === 3 || stage === 5) {
+             const p = stage === 3 ? this._pend : this._prev;
+             q = p.q; Zq = p.Zq; s = 1; mode = "plates";
+         } else if (stage === 4) {
+             q = this._pend.q; Zq = this._pend.Zq; mode = "plates";
+             quantised = true;
+         } else if (stage) {
+             q = "e"; Zq = Z; s = 1; mode = "settle";
+         } else if (this._syncSwitch(chords)) {
+             if (this._hold) this._endHold("sync");
+             q = "s"; Zq = Z; s = 1; mode = "sync";
+         } else {
+             const qf = quantumFor(cam.inScale, dpr, this._qPrev);
+             if (this._qPrev != null && qf.q !== this._qPrev) {
+                 pictureStats.quantumChanges++;
+                 this._onQuantumChange(qf, frameId);
+             }
+             this._qPrev = qf.q;
+             if (this._hold && this._hold.frameId !== frameId) this._endHold("frame");
+             if (this._v.frameId != null && this._v.frameId !== frameId) this._aimPreview(qf.q, frameId);
+             qLive = qf.q; ZqLive = qf.Zq;
+             q = this._hold ? this._hold.q : qf.q;
+             Zq = this._hold ? this._hold.Zq : qf.Zq;
+             mode = "plates";
+             quantised = true;
+         }
+         const win = cam.frameWindow(0);
+         if (stage === 0 && cam.lm
+             && (Math.abs(cam.lm.width - this.cssW) > 0.5 || Math.abs(cam.lm.height - this.cssH) > 0.5)) {
+             pictureStats.sizeMismatch++;
+         }
+         let r = plateRange(win, Zq);
+         if (stage === 1 || stage === 3 || stage === 5) {
+             const q1 = stage === 1 ? this._req : stage === 3 ? this._pend : this._prev;
+             q1.frameId = frameId; q1.Zq = Zq;
+             if (stage === 1) q1.q = "e";
+             q1.i0 = r.i0; q1.i1 = r.i1; q1.j0 = r.j0; q1.j1 = r.j1;
+             return null;
+         }
+         if (quantised) s = stretchOf(Z, Zq);
+         let full = r;
+         if (this._hold) {
+             const h = this._hold, g = this._rHold;
+             if (s > S_MAX_HOLD || s < S_MIN_HOLD) {
+                 if (s > S_MAX_HOLD) { this._endHold("stretch"); pictureStats.holdBreaks++; }
+                 else { this._endHold("shrink"); pictureStats.holdShrinks++; }
+                 q = qLive; Zq = ZqLive;
+                 r = full = plateRange(win, Zq);
+                 s = stretchOf(Z, Zq);
+             } else {
+                 g.i0 = Math.max(r.i0, h.i0); g.i1 = Math.min(r.i1, h.i1);
+                 g.j0 = Math.max(r.j0, h.j0); g.j1 = Math.min(r.j1, h.j1);
+                 if (g.i0 > g.i1 || g.j0 > g.j1) {
+                     this._endHold("offscreen");
+                     q = qLive; Zq = ZqLive;
+                     r = full = plateRange(win, Zq);
+                     s = stretchOf(Z, Zq);
+                 } else r = g;
+             }
+         }
+         const rf = this._rFull;
+         rf.i0 = full.i0; rf.i1 = full.i1; rf.j0 = full.j0; rf.j1 = full.j1;
+         const ox = plateOriginPx(r.i0), oy = plateOriginPx(r.j0);
+         const dx = snapPx(cam.inPanX * dpr + ox * s);
+         const dy = snapPx(cam.inPanY * dpr + oy * s);
+         const setMoved = v.frameId !== frameId || v.q !== q || v.i0 !== r.i0 || v.i1 !== r.i1
+             || v.j0 !== r.j0 || v.j1 !== r.j1 || v.ox !== ox || v.oy !== oy;
+         const changed = setMoved || v.mode !== mode || v.Zq !== Zq || v.Z !== Z || v.s !== s
+             || v.dx !== dx || v.dy !== dy || v.dpr !== dpr || v.cssW !== this.cssW || v.cssH !== this.cssH;
+         if (!changed) { if (moved) this._maybeArm(mode, s); return v; }
+         if (setMoved || v.dpr !== dpr || v.cssW !== this.cssW || v.cssH !== this.cssH) this._layerDirty = true;
+         if ((v.mode === "sync") !== (mode === "sync")) pictureStats.syncFlips++;
+         v.mode = mode; v.frameId = frameId; v.q = q; v.Zq = Zq; v.Z = Z; v.s = s;
+         v.ox = ox; v.oy = oy; v.dx = dx; v.dy = dy;
+         v.dpr = dpr; v.cssW = this.cssW; v.cssH = this.cssH;
+         v.i0 = r.i0; v.i1 = r.i1; v.j0 = r.j0; v.j1 = r.j1;
+         v.k = Zq * s; v.tx = dx - ox * s; v.ty = dy - oy * s;
+         v.rev++;
+         if (moved) this._maybeArm(mode, s);
+         return v;
+     }
+     _maybeArm(mode, s) {
+         this._clear();
+         if (this._hold) return false;
+         if (mode !== "plates" || s === 1 || !this.store) return false;
+         this._timer = this._setTimer(this._fire, this.settleMs);
+         return true;
+     }
+     _moved(cam, frameId) {
+         const c = this._cam0;
+         const moved = c.sized || c.frameId !== frameId || c.inScale !== cam.inScale
+             || c.panX !== cam.inPanX || c.panY !== cam.inPanY;
+         c.sized = false; c.frameId = frameId; c.inScale = cam.inScale;
+         c.panX = cam.inPanX; c.panY = cam.inPanY;
+         return moved;
+     }
+     _onMove() {
+         if (this._eLive && this.store) { this.store.dropQuantum("e"); this.store.unpinAdded(); pictureStats.settleDrops++; }
+         if (this._settleDone) this._nHits = 0;
+         this._eLive = false;
+         this._settlePending = false;
+         this._settleDone = false;
+         this._clear();
+     }
+     _syncSwitch(chords) {
+         const n = chords;
+         if (n == null || !isFinite(n) || n < 0) return false;
+         if (this._sync && this._syncMs > this.syncMaxMs) {
+             this._sync = false; this._syncSlowAt = n; this._syncMs = 0;
+             pictureStats.syncCostExits++;
+             return false;
+         }
+         if (!this._sync && this._syncSlowAt >= 0 && n >= this._syncSlowAt / 2) return false;
+         this._sync = this._sync ? n <= 2 * this.syncBudget : n < this.syncBudget;
+         if (this._sync) this._syncSlowAt = -1;
+         return this._sync;
+     }
+     noteSyncCost(ms) { this._syncMs = ms > 0 ? ms : 0; }
+     compose(plates) {
+         if (plates) this._setHits(plates);
+         const v = this._v, ctx = this._ctx, c = this._canvas;
+         pictureStats.composes++;
+         this._covered = false;
+         this._screenCovered = false;
+         this._nFb = 0;
+         this._fbDrawn = 0;
+         this._fbRan = false;
+         if (this._hold) {
+             pictureStats.heldComposes++;
+             if (v.s > pictureStats.holdMaxS) pictureStats.holdMaxS = v.s;
+             if (pictureStats.holdMinS === 0 || v.s < pictureStats.holdMinS) pictureStats.holdMinS = v.s;
+         }
+         if (!ctx || !c) { pictureStats.noContext++; return 0; }
+         ctx.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);
+         const S = this._scr || (this._scr = [0, 0, 0, 0]);
+         S[0] = ((0 - v.dx) / v.s + v.ox) / PLATE_PX; S[1] = ((0 - v.dy) / v.s + v.oy) / PLATE_PX;
+         S[2] = ((c.width - v.dx) / v.s + v.ox) / PLATE_PX; S[3] = ((c.height - v.dy) / v.s + v.oy) / PLATE_PX;
+         this._nCy = 0;
+         this._nPR = 0;
+         const sync = v.mode === "sync";
+         if (!sync) this._planFallbacks(v);
+         let carried = false;
+         if (!sync && this._carry && (this._fbRan || !this._rangeCovered(v)) && !this._screenCoverOf(v, false)) carried = this._drawCarry(ctx, c, v);
+         if (!carried) {
+             ctx.fillStyle = this.paper;
+             ctx.fillRect(0, 0, c.width, c.height);
+             pictureStats.paperFills++;
+         }
+         let n = this._drawFallbacks(ctx, v);
+         const under = carried || this._fbDrawn > 0;
+         if (v.s === 1) {
+             ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
+             let inR = 0, stale = 0;
+             const pb = this._planeBelow(v);
+             for (let i = 0; i < this._nHits; i++) {
+                 const p = this._hits[i];
+                 const u = this._usable(p, v);
+                 if (!u || u === 3) continue;
+                 if (pb && pb.cells.has(Math.floor(p.i / pb.d) + "," + Math.floor(p.j / pb.d))) {
+                     if (this._inRange(p, v)) inR++;
+                     continue;
+                 }
+                 if (u === 1) {
+                     ctx.drawImage(p.bitmap, plateOriginPx(p.i) - v.ox + v.dx, plateOriginPx(p.j) - v.oy + v.dy);
+                     n++;
+                     if (this._isStale(p)) stale++;
+                 } else if (under) {
+                     ctx.fillStyle = this.paper;
+                     ctx.fillRect(plateOriginPx(p.i) - v.ox + v.dx, plateOriginPx(p.j) - v.oy + v.dy, PLATE_PX, PLATE_PX);
+                 }
+                 if (this._inRange(p, v)) inR++;
+             }
+             pictureStats.plateBlits += n - this._fbDrawn;
+             pictureStats.stalePlateBlits += stale;
+             if (stale) pictureStats.staleComposes++;
+             this._covered = this._rangeCount(v) > 0 && inR >= this._rangeCount(v);
+         } else {
+             const L = this._ensureLayer();
+             if (!L || !this._lctx) { pictureStats.blits += n; return n; }
+             if (this._layerDirty) { this._layerDrawn = this._recomposeLayer(L, v); }
+             this._covered = this._rangeCount(v) > 0 && this._layerDrawn >= this._rangeCount(v);
+             ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = true;
+             const rw = Math.min(L.width, (v.i1 - v.i0 + 1) * PLATE_PX);
+             const rh = Math.min(L.height, (v.j1 - v.j0 + 1) * PLATE_PX);
+             if (rw > 0 && rh > 0) {
+                 ctx.drawImage(L, 0, 0, rw, rh, v.dx, v.dy, rw * v.s, rh * v.s);
+                 n++;
+             }
+         }
+         n += this._composePlane(ctx, v);
+         this._uncovered = 0;
+         this._screenCovered = sync || (this._fbRan || carried || !this._covered ? this._screenCoverOf(v, carried) : this._covered);
+         if (this._uncovered > 0) {
+             pictureStats.paperComposes++;
+             if (this._uncovered > pictureStats.paperCellsMax) pictureStats.paperCellsMax = this._uncovered;
+         }
+         this._keepCarry(v, c);
+         pictureStats.blits += n;
+         return n;
+     }
+     _rangeCovered(v) {
+         const cells = this._rangeCount(v);
+         let inR = 0;
+         for (let i = 0; i < this._nHits; i++) {
+             const p = this._hits[i];
+             const u = this._drawable(p, v);
+             if ((u === 1 || u === 2) && this._inRange(p, v)) inR++;
+         }
+         return cells > 0 && inR >= cells;
+     }
+     _carryXf(v) {
+         const C = this._carry;
+         if (!C || !(C.k > 0) || !(v.k > 0)) return null;
+         let a = 1, bx = 0, by = 0;
+         if (C.frameId !== v.frameId) {
+             const m = this._mapFrame(C.frameId);
+             if (!m) return null;
+             a = m.a; bx = m.bx; by = m.by;
+         }
+         const sc = (a * v.k) / C.k;
+         if (!(sc > 0) || sc > CARRY_MAX_SC || sc < 1 / CARRY_MAX_SC) return null;
+         const ox = (-(C.tx / C.k) * a + bx) * v.k + v.tx, oy = (-(C.ty / C.k) * a + by) * v.k + v.ty;
+         return isFinite(ox) && isFinite(oy) ? { sc, ox, oy } : null;
+     }
+     _drawCarry(ctx, c, v) {
+         const t = this._carryXf(v);
+         if (!t) return false;
+         const B = this._carry.boxes, Y = this._cyB, u = (X, dx, o) => ((X - dx) / v.s + o) / PLATE_PX;
+         let n = 0;
+         for (let k = 0; k < B.length; k += 4) {
+             Y[4 * n] = u(B[k] * t.sc + t.ox, v.dx, v.ox); Y[4 * n + 1] = u(B[k + 1] * t.sc + t.oy, v.dy, v.oy);
+             Y[4 * n + 2] = u(B[k + 2] * t.sc + t.ox, v.dx, v.ox); Y[4 * n + 3] = u(B[k + 3] * t.sc + t.oy, v.dy, v.oy);
+             n++;
+         }
+         this._nCy = n;
+         if (!n) return false;
+         const W = c.width, H = c.height, Dt = this._carry.dirty;
+         if (Math.abs(t.sc - 1) > 1e-9 || Math.abs(t.ox) > 1e-6 || Math.abs(t.oy) > 1e-6) {
+             ctx.save();
+             ctx.globalCompositeOperation = "copy";
+             ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = true;
+             ctx.setTransform(t.sc, 0, 0, t.sc, t.ox, t.oy);
+             ctx.drawImage(c, 0, 0);
+             ctx.restore();
+             ctx.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);
+             ctx.fillStyle = this.paper;
+             const x0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor(t.ox)), y0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor(t.oy));
+             const x1 = Math.min(W, Math.ceil(t.ox + W * t.sc)), y1 = Math.min(H, Math.ceil(t.oy + H * t.sc));
+             if (x0 >= x1 || y0 >= y1) ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
+             else {
+                 if (y0 > 0) ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, y0);
+                 if (y1 < H) ctx.fillRect(0, y1, W, H - y1);
+                 if (x0 > 0) ctx.fillRect(0, y0, x0, y1 - y0);
+                 if (x1 < W) ctx.fillRect(x1, y0, W - x1, y1 - y0);
+             }
+             pictureStats.carryShifted++;
+         }
+         if (Dt) {
+             ctx.fillStyle = this.paper;
+             for (let k = 0; k < Dt.length; k += 4) {
+                 const x0 = Dt[k] * t.sc + t.ox, y0 = Dt[k + 1] * t.sc + t.oy;
+                 ctx.fillRect(x0, y0, (Dt[k + 2] - Dt[k]) * t.sc, (Dt[k + 3] - Dt[k + 1]) * t.sc);
+             }
+         }
+         pictureStats.carryComposes++;
+         return true;
+     }
+     _keepCarry(v, c) {
+         const rf = this._rFull, P = this._pR, np = this._nPR;
+         if (!(v.k > 0) || rf.i0 > rf.i1 || rf.j0 > rf.j1) { this._carry = null; return; }
+         const boxes = [];
+         const onPlane = (x0, y0, x1, y1) => {
+             for (let k = 0; k < 4 * np; k += 4) if (P[k] < x1 && P[k + 2] > x0 && P[k + 1] < y1 && P[k + 3] > y0) return true;
+             return false;
+         };
+         if (this._uncovered === 0 && !np) boxes.push(0, 0, c.width, c.height);
+         else if ((rf.i1 - rf.i0 + 1) * (rf.j1 - rf.j0 + 1) <= SCREEN_CELLS_MAX) {
+             const w = PLATE_PX * v.s;
+             for (let j = rf.j0; j <= rf.j1; j++) {
+                 for (let i = rf.i0; i <= rf.i1; i++) {
+                     if (!this._cellCovered(i, j, v, this._nCy > 0)) continue;
+                     const x = (plateOriginPx(i) - v.ox) * v.s + v.dx, y = (plateOriginPx(j) - v.oy) * v.s + v.dy;
+                     if (np && onPlane(x, y, x + w, y + w)) continue;
+                     boxes.push(x, y, x + w, y + w);
+                 }
+             }
+         }
+         this._carry = boxes.length ? { frameId: v.frameId, k: v.k, tx: v.tx, ty: v.ty, boxes, dirty: np ? P.slice(0, 4 * np) : null } : null;
+     }
+     dropCarry() { if (this._carry) pictureStats.carryDropped++; this._carry = null; this._nCy = 0; }
+     _planFallbacks(v) {
+         this._fbRan = false;
+         this._nFb = 0;
+         if ((v.mode !== "plates" && v.mode !== "settle") || !this.store) return 0;
+         const rf = this._rFull;
+         const clipped = rf.i0 !== v.i0 || rf.i1 !== v.i1 || rf.j0 !== v.j0 || rf.j1 !== v.j1;
+         if (!clipped && this._rangeCovered(v)) return 0;
+         this._fbRan = true;
+         rf.frameId = v.frameId; rf.q = v.q; rf.Zq = v.Zq;
+         const nf = this.store.fallbackCover(rf, this._fb, this._mapFrameFn);
+         pictureStats.fallbackScans++;
+         let n = 0;
+         const B = this._fbB, D = this._fbD || (this._fbD = []);
+         for (let k = 0; k < nf; k++) {
+             const p = this._fb[k];
+             const empty = !p.bitmap;
+             if (empty ? !this._emptyCover(p, v) : this._drawable(p, v) !== 3) { pictureStats.fallbackRefused++; continue; }
+             const m = p.frameId === v.frameId ? null : this._mapFrame(p.frameId);
+             const a = m ? m.a : 1;
+             const t = (PLATE_PX / p.Zq) * a;
+             const fx = p.i * t + (m ? m.bx : 0), fy = p.j * t + (m ? m.by : 0);
+             const r = (t * v.Zq) / PLATE_PX;
+             const X0 = (fx * v.Zq) / PLATE_PX, Y0 = (fy * v.Zq) / PLATE_PX;
+             B[4 * n] = X0; B[4 * n + 1] = Y0; B[4 * n + 2] = X0 + r; B[4 * n + 3] = Y0 + r;
+             D[3 * n] = fx; D[3 * n + 1] = fy; D[3 * n + 2] = r;
+             this._fb[n++] = p;
+         }
+         if (n > FB_DRAW_MIN) n = this._fbBudget(v, n);
+         this._fb.length = n;
+         this._nFb = n;
+         return n;
+     }
+     _fbBudget(v, n) {
+         const S = this._scr, rf = this._rFull, B = this._fbB, D = this._fbD;
+         const W = rf.i1 - rf.i0 + 1, H = rf.j1 - rf.j0 + 1;
+         if (!S || W <= 0 || H <= 0 || W * H > SCREEN_CELLS_MAX) return n;
+         let other = 1;
+         if (v.s === 1) { for (let k = 0; k < this._nHits; k++) if (this._hits[k] && this._hits[k].bitmap) other++; }
+         else other++;
+         const pl = this._plane, lv = pl && pl.showing ? (pl.cur || pl.stop) : null;
+         if (lv) for (const L of lv.layers) other += L.cells.length;
+         const cap = Math.max(FB_DRAW_MIN, COMPOSE_IMAGES - other);
+         const need = this._fbNeed || (this._fbNeed = []);
+         need.length = W * H;
+         for (let j = 0; j < H; j++) {
+             for (let i = 0; i < W; i++) {
+                 const on = Math.max(rf.i0 + i, S[0]) < Math.min(rf.i0 + i + 1, S[2]) && Math.max(rf.j0 + j, S[1]) < Math.min(rf.j0 + j + 1, S[3]);
+                 need[j * W + i] = on ? 1 : 0;
+             }
+         }
+         for (let k = 0; k < this._nHits; k++) {
+             const p = this._hits[k];
+             if (!p || p.i < rf.i0 || p.i > rf.i1 || p.j < rf.j0 || p.j > rf.j1) continue;
+             const u = this._drawable(p, v);
+             if (u === 1 || u === 2) need[(p.j - rf.j0) * W + (p.i - rf.i0)] = 0;
+         }
+         const keep = this._fbKeep || (this._fbKeep = []), A = this._fbA || (this._fbA = []);
+         keep.length = n; A.length = n;
+         let m = 0, imgs = 0;
+         for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) {
+             keep[k] = 0;
+             const x0 = Math.max(B[4 * k], S[0]), x1 = Math.min(B[4 * k + 2], S[2]);
+             const y0 = Math.max(B[4 * k + 1], S[1]), y1 = Math.min(B[4 * k + 3], S[3]);
+             if (x0 >= x1 || y0 >= y1) continue;
+             A[k] = (x1 - x0) * (y1 - y0);
+             const i0 = Math.max(rf.i0, Math.floor(x0)), i1 = Math.min(rf.i1, Math.ceil(x1) - 1);
+             const j0 = Math.max(rf.j0, Math.floor(y0)), j1 = Math.min(rf.j1, Math.ceil(y1) - 1);
+             for (let j = j0; j <= j1 && !keep[k]; j++) {
+                 for (let i = i0; i <= i1; i++) if (need[(j - rf.j0) * W + (i - rf.i0)]) { keep[k] = 1; break; }
+             }
+             if (keep[k]) { m++; if (this._fb[k].bitmap) imgs++; }
+         }
+         pictureStats.fallbackCulled += n - m;
+         if (imgs > cap) {
+             let c = 0;
+             while (c < cap) {
+                 let best = -1, bestN = 0;
+                 for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) {
+                     if (keep[k] !== 1) continue;
+                     const cn = this._fbCovers(k, need, W, false);
+                     if (cn > bestN || (cn > 0 && cn === bestN && Math.abs(Math.log2(D[3 * k + 2])) < Math.abs(Math.log2(D[3 * best + 2])))) { best = k; bestN = cn; }
+                 }
+                 if (best < 0) break;
+                 keep[best] = 2;
+                 if (this._fb[best].bitmap) c++;
+                 this._fbCovers(best, need, W, true);
+             }
+             const ord = this._fbOrd2 || (this._fbOrd2 = []);
+             ord.length = 0;
+             for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) if (keep[k] === 1) ord.push(k);
+             const dq = (k) => Math.floor(Math.abs(Math.log2(D[3 * k + 2])) * QUANTA_PER_OCTAVE + 1e-6);
+             ord.sort((a, b) => (dq(a) - dq(b)) || (A[b] - A[a]) || (a - b));
+             for (const k of ord) {
+                 if (!this._fb[k].bitmap) continue;
+                 if (c < cap) c++; else { keep[k] = 0; pictureStats.fallbackOverBudget++; }
+             }
+         }
+         let w = 0;
+         for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) {
+             if (!keep[k]) continue;
+             if (w !== k) {
+                 this._fb[w] = this._fb[k];
+                 for (let t = 0; t < 4; t++) B[4 * w + t] = B[4 * k + t];
+                 for (let t = 0; t < 3; t++) D[3 * w + t] = D[3 * k + t];
+             }
+             w++;
+         }
+         return w;
+     }
+     _fbCovers(k, need, W, mark) {
+         const B = this._fbB, S = this._scr, rf = this._rFull, eps = 1e-9;
+         const bx0 = B[4 * k], by0 = B[4 * k + 1], bx1 = B[4 * k + 2], by1 = B[4 * k + 3];
+         const i0 = Math.max(rf.i0, Math.floor(bx0)), i1 = Math.min(rf.i1, Math.ceil(bx1) - 1);
+         const j0 = Math.max(rf.j0, Math.floor(by0)), j1 = Math.min(rf.j1, Math.ceil(by1) - 1);
+         let n = 0;
+         for (let j = j0; j <= j1; j++) {
+             for (let i = i0; i <= i1; i++) {
+                 const c = (j - rf.j0) * W + (i - rf.i0);
+                 if (!need[c]) continue;
+                 const x0 = Math.max(i, S[0]), x1 = Math.min(i + 1, S[2]), y0 = Math.max(j, S[1]), y1 = Math.min(j + 1, S[3]);
+                 if (bx0 <= x0 + eps && bx1 >= x1 - eps && by0 <= y0 + eps && by1 >= y1 - eps) { n++; if (mark) need[c] = 0; }
+             }
+         }
+         return n;
+     }
+     _drawFallbacks(ctx, v) {
+         const n = this._nFb, D = this._fbD;
+         if (!n) { this._fbDrawn = 0; return 0; }
+         ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = true;
+         let drawn = 0;
+         for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) {
+             const p = this._fb[k];
+             if (!p.bitmap) continue;
+             const fx = D[3 * k], fy = D[3 * k + 1], r = D[3 * k + 2], w = PLATE_PX * r * v.s;
+             ctx.drawImage(p.bitmap, (fx * v.Zq - v.ox) * v.s + v.dx, (fy * v.Zq - v.oy) * v.s + v.dy, w, w);
+             if (r > pictureStats.fallbackMaxR) pictureStats.fallbackMaxR = r;
+             if (p.frameId !== v.frameId) pictureStats.crossFrameBlits++;
+             drawn++;
+         }
+         this._fbDrawn = drawn;
+         pictureStats.fallbackBlits += drawn;
+         if (drawn) pictureStats.fallbackComposes++;
+         return drawn;
+     }
+     settleComing() { return this._timer != null || this._settlePending; }
+     holding() { return !!this._hold; }
+     _planeBelow(v) {
+         const pl = this._plane, lv = pl && pl.cur;
+         if (!lv || pl.state !== "drag" || pl.partial || !lv.below || pl.frameId !== v.frameId || !this.store) return null;
+         if (v.mode !== "plates" && v.mode !== "settle") return null;
+         if (lv.pZq === v.Zq) return { cells: lv.below, d: 1 };
+         const dq = typeof v.q === "number" && typeof lv.pq === "number" ? v.q - lv.pq : -1;
+         return dq > 0 && dq % QUANTA_PER_OCTAVE === 0 ? { cells: lv.below, d: Math.pow(2, dq / QUANTA_PER_OCTAVE) } : null;
+     }
+     setPlane(p) { this._plane = p || null; }
+     cellFresh(v, i, j) {
+         if (!this.store) return false;
+         const k = plateKey(v.frameId, v.q, i, j);
+         const e = this.store.get(k);
+         return !!e && !e.dead && e.epoch === this.store.epochOf(k);
+     }
+     _composePlane(ctx, v) {
+         const pl = this._plane;
+         if (!pl || !pl.showing || pl.frameId !== v.frameId || (v.mode !== "plates" && v.mode !== "settle") || !this.store) return 0;
+         const lvl = pl.cur || pl.stop;
+         if (!lvl || !lvl.layers.length) return 0;
+         const f = v.Zq / lvl.pZq, w = PLATE_PX * f * v.s;
+         let clipped = false;
+         if (pl.state === "land") {
+             const c = pl.landCells(v);
+             if (!c) return 0;
+             ctx.save();
+             ctx.beginPath();
+             let open = 0;
+             for (let j = c.j0; j <= c.j1; j++) {
+                 for (let i = c.i0; i <= c.i1; i++) {
+                     if (this.cellFresh(v, i, j)) continue;
+                     ctx.rect((plateOriginPx(i) - v.ox) * v.s + v.dx, (plateOriginPx(j) - v.oy) * v.s + v.dy, PLATE_PX * v.s, PLATE_PX * v.s);
+                     open++;
+                 }
+             }
+             if (!open) { ctx.restore(); return 0; }
+             ctx.clip();
+             clipped = true;
+             pl.stats.clipComposes++;
+         }
+         pl.stats.composes++;
+         ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = f !== 1 || v.s !== 1;
+         let n = 0;
+         for (const L of lvl.layers) {
+             if (pl.partial && L.kind !== "sel") continue;
+             const moved = L.kind === "sel", dx = moved ? pl.off[0] : 0, dy = moved ? pl.off[1] : 0;
+             for (const [i, j] of L.cells) {
+                 const e = L.bms ? L.bms.get(i + "," + j) : this.store.get(plateKey(pl.frameId, L.tag, i, j));
+                 if (!e || e.dead) continue;
+                 const x = (i * PLATE_PX * f - v.ox) * v.s + v.dx + dx, y = (j * PLATE_PX * f - v.oy) * v.s + v.dy + dy;
+                 if (e.bitmap) {
+                     ctx.drawImage(e.bitmap, x, y, w, w); n++;
+                     if (moved) pl.stats.inkBlits++; else if (L.kind === "over") pl.stats.overBlits++; else pl.stats.underBlits++;
+                 } else if (L.kind === "below" && !e.pixels && e.bytes === 0) {
+                     ctx.fillStyle = this.paper;
+                     ctx.fillRect(x, y, w, w);
+                 } else continue;
+                 const R = this._pR || (this._pR = []);
+                 R[4 * this._nPR] = x; R[4 * this._nPR + 1] = y; R[4 * this._nPR + 2] = x + w; R[4 * this._nPR + 3] = y + w;
+                 this._nPR++;
+             }
+         }
+         if (clipped) ctx.restore();
+         return n;
+     }
+     _emptyCover(p, v) {
+         if (!p || p.dead || p.bitmap || p.pixels || p.bytes !== 0 || !Number.isFinite(p.q)) return false;
+         if (!(p.Zq > 0) || !isFinite(p.Zq)) return false;
+         return p.frameId === undefined || p.frameId === v.frameId || !!this._mapFrame(p.frameId);
+     }
+     _screenCoverOf(v, withCarry = false) {
+         const rf = this._rFull;
+         if (rf.i0 > rf.i1 || rf.j0 > rf.j1) return false;
+         const cells = (rf.i1 - rf.i0 + 1) * (rf.j1 - rf.j0 + 1);
+         if (cells > SCREEN_CELLS_MAX) { pictureStats.coverRefused++; this._uncovered = cells; return false; }
+         let paper = 0;
+         for (let j = rf.j0; j <= rf.j1; j++) {
+             for (let i = rf.i0; i <= rf.i1; i++) if (!this._cellCovered(i, j, v, withCarry)) paper++;
+         }
+         this._uncovered = paper;
+         return paper === 0;
+     }
+     _cellCovered(i, j, v, withCarry = false) {
+         for (let k = 0; k < this._nHits; k++) {
+             const p = this._hits[k];
+             if (p && p.i === i && p.j === j) { const u = this._drawable(p, v); if (u === 1 || u === 2) return true; }
+         }
+         const S = this._scr;
+         const x0 = S ? Math.max(i, S[0]) : i, y0 = S ? Math.max(j, S[1]) : j;
+         const x1 = S ? Math.min(i + 1, S[2]) : i + 1, y1 = S ? Math.min(j + 1, S[3]) : j + 1;
+         if (x0 >= x1 || y0 >= y1) return true;
+         const B = this._fbB, eps = 1e-9;
+         for (let k = 0; k < this._nFb; k++) {
+             if (B[4 * k] <= x0 + eps && B[4 * k + 2] >= x1 - eps && B[4 * k + 1] <= y0 + eps && B[4 * k + 3] >= y1 - eps) return true;
+         }
+         if (withCarry) {
+             const Y = this._cyB, e2 = 1e-6;
+             for (let k = 0; k < this._nCy; k++) {
+                 if (Y[4 * k] <= x0 + e2 && Y[4 * k + 2] >= x1 - e2 && Y[4 * k + 1] <= y0 + e2 && Y[4 * k + 3] >= y1 - e2) return true;
+             }
+         }
+         return false;
+     }
+     _mapFrame(G) {
+         const F = this._v.frameId, lm = this.cam && this.cam.lm;
+         if (!lm || F == null || typeof lm.frameFactor !== "function" || typeof lm.mapPointF !== "function") return null;
+         const epoch = lm.addrEpoch || 0;
+         if (this._mfF !== F || this._mfE !== epoch) { this._mf.clear(); this._mfF = F; this._mfE = epoch; }
+         if (this._mf.has(G)) return this._mf.get(G);
+         let m = null;
+         const a = typeof lm.frame !== "function" || lm.frame(G) ? lm.frameFactor(G, F) : null;
+         const b = a > 0 && isFinite(a) ? lm.mapPointF([0, 0], G, F) : null;
+         if (b && isFinite(b[0]) && isFinite(b[1])) m = { a, bx: b[0], by: b[1] };
+         this._mf.set(G, m);
+         return m;
+     }
+     _isStale(p) { return !!this.store && p.epoch !== this.store.epochOf(p.key); }
+     _rangeCount(v) {
+         const w = v.i1 - v.i0 + 1, h = v.j1 - v.j0 + 1;
+         return w > 0 && h > 0 ? w * h : 0;
+     }
+     _recomposeLayer(L, v) {
+         const lc = this._lctx;
+         lc.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);
+         lc.clearRect(0, 0, L.width, L.height);
+         lc.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
+         let n = 0, stale = 0;
+         for (let i = 0; i < this._nHits; i++) {
+             const p = this._hits[i];
+             const u = this._usable(p, v);
+             if (!u || u === 3) continue;
+             const x = plateOriginPx(p.i) - v.ox, y = plateOriginPx(p.j) - v.oy;
+             if (x < 0 || y < 0 || x + PLATE_PX > L.width || y + PLATE_PX > L.height) { pictureStats.layerOverflow++; continue; }
+             if (u === 1) {
+                 lc.drawImage(p.bitmap, x, y);
+                 if (this._isStale(p)) stale++;
+             } else {
+                 lc.fillStyle = this.paper;
+                 lc.fillRect(x, y, PLATE_PX, PLATE_PX);
+             }
+             if (this._inRange(p, v)) n++;
+         }
+         this._layerDirty = false;
+         pictureStats.layerRecomposes++;
+         pictureStats.stalePlateBlits += stale;
+         if (stale) pictureStats.staleComposes++;
+         return n;
+     }
+     _inRange(p, v) { return p.i >= v.i0 && p.i <= v.i1 && p.j >= v.j0 && p.j <= v.j1; }
+     _drawable(p, v) {
+         if (!p || p.dead) return 0;
+         if (p.frameId !== undefined && p.frameId !== v.frameId) {
+             return p.bitmap && Number.isFinite(p.q) && p.Zq > 0 && isFinite(p.Zq) && this._mapFrame(p.frameId) ? 3 : 0;
+         }
+         if (!p.bitmap) {
+             if (p.pixels || p.bytes !== 0) return 0;
+             return p.Zq === v.Zq ? 2 : 0;
+         }
+         if (p.Zq !== v.Zq) return Number.isFinite(p.q) && p.Zq > 0 && isFinite(p.Zq) ? 3 : 0;
+         return 1;
+     }
+     _usable(p, v) {
+         const u = this._drawable(p, v);
+         if (u === 3) pictureStats.zqMismatch++;
+         if (u) return u;
+         if (!p) return 0;
+         if (p.dead) { pictureStats.deadRefused++; return 0; }
+         if (!p.bitmap && (p.pixels || p.bytes !== 0)) { pictureStats.noBitmap++; return 0; }
+         pictureStats.zqMismatch++;
+         return 0;
+     }
+     onPlateBatch(recs) {
+         const b = this._arr, out = [];
+         b.compose = false; b.req = null; b.miss = null;
+         if (recs) for (let i = 0; i < recs.length; i++) out.push(this._take(recs[i], b));
+         this._flush(b);
+         return out;
+     }
+     _take(rec, b) {
+         pictureStats.arrivals++;
+         if (!rec || !this.store) return "stale";
+         const key = rec.key || plateKey(rec.frameId, rec.q, rec.i, rec.j);
+         if (String(rec.q) === "s") {
+             pictureStats.syncRefused++;
+             this._noteStale(key, rec, "sync");
+             if (rec.bitmap && rec.bitmap.close) rec.bitmap.close();
+             return "stale";
+         }
+         const st = this.store.put(rec);
+         const wanted = this._wanted(rec);
+         if (st === "stale") {
+             pictureStats.staleDrops++;
+             this._noteStale(key, rec, wanted ? "epoch" : "unwanted");
+             if (!wanted) { pictureStats.staleUnwanted++; return st; }
+             if (this._onRepost) this._addMiss(b, this._reqFor(rec), { key, i: rec.i, j: rec.j, epoch: this.store.epochOf(key) });
+             return st;
+         }
+         if (st === "newer") {
+             pictureStats.staleNewer++;
+             if (!wanted) return st;
+             const req = this._reqFor(rec);
+             if (this._onRepost) this._addMiss(b, req, { key, i: rec.i, j: rec.j, epoch: this.store.epochOf(key) });
+             if (req === this._v) { this._addHit(this.store.get(key)); this._layerDirty = true; b.compose = true; }
+             return st;
+         }
+         if (this._settlePending && String(rec.q) === "e") {
+             if (this._settleArrived()) pictureStats.arrivalComposes++;
+             return st;
+         }
+         if (this._pendLive && String(rec.q) === String(this._pend.q)) {
+             if (this._quantumArrived()) pictureStats.arrivalComposes++;
+             return st;
+         }
+         if (this._prevLive && String(rec.q) === String(this._prev.q)) { b.compose = true; return st; }
+         if (wanted) {
+             this._addHit(this.store.get(key));
+             this._layerDirty = true;
+             b.compose = true;
+         } else {
+             pictureStats.arrivalsOutside++;
+             if (Number.isFinite(Number(rec.q)) && String(rec.q) !== String(this._v.q)) b.compose = true;
+         }
+         return st;
+     }
+     _flush(b) {
+         this._flushMisses(b);
+         if (b.compose) {
+             b.compose = false;
+             this.compose();
+             pictureStats.arrivalComposes++;
+         }
+     }
+     _addMiss(b, req, m) {
+         if (b.req && b.req !== req) this._flushMisses(b);
+         b.req = req;
+         if (!b.miss) b.miss = [];
+         b.miss.push(m);
+     }
+     _flushMisses(b) {
+         if (b.miss && b.miss.length && this._onRepost) {
+             this._onRepost(b.req, b.miss);
+             pictureStats.reposts += b.miss.length;
+         }
+         b.req = null; b.miss = null;
+     }
+     _reqFor(rec) {
+         if (this._settlePending && String(rec.q) === "e") return this._req;
+         if (this._pendLive && String(rec.q) === String(this._pend.q)) return this._pend;
+         if (this._prevLive && String(rec.q) === String(this._prev.q)) return this._prev;
+         return this._v;
+     }
+     _wanted(rec) {
+         const r = this._reqFor(rec);
+         if (rec.frameId !== r.frameId || String(rec.q) !== String(r.q)) return false;
+         return rec.i >= r.i0 && rec.i <= r.i1 && rec.j >= r.j0 && rec.j <= r.j1;
+     }
+     _noteStale(key, rec, why) {
+         const e = { key, why, epoch: rec.epoch, q: rec.q, frameId: rec.frameId, when: "arrival", t: this._now() };
+         if (this._stale.length < STALE_LOG) this._stale.push(e);
+         else this._stale[this._staleAt % STALE_LOG] = e;
+         this._staleAt++;
+     }
+     _clear() { if (this._timer != null) { this._clearTimer(this._timer); this._timer = null; } }
+     _settleNow() {
+         this._timer = null;
+         const v = this._v;
+         if (!this.cam || !this.store || v.mode !== "plates" || v.s === 1) return false;
+         if (this._hold) return false;
+         pictureStats.settles++;
+         this._settlePending = true;
+         this._eLive = true;
+         return this._askSettle();
+     }
+     _askSettle() {
+         this._frame(this.cam, 0, 1);
+         const got = this.store.want(this._req, { pin: "add" });
+         if (!got.misses.length && !got.inFlight) { this._publishSettle(got.hits); return true; }
+         if (got.misses.length && this._onPlates) this._onPlates(this._req, got.misses);
+         return false;
+     }
+     _settleArrived() {
+         if (!this._settlePending) return false;
+         this._frame(this.cam, 0, 1);
+         const got = this.store.want(this._req, { pin: "add" });
+         if (got.misses.length || got.inFlight) return false;
+         this._publishSettle(got.hits);
+         return true;
+     }
+     _publishSettle(hits) {
+         this._settlePending = false;
+         this._settleDone = true;
+         this._frame(this.cam, 0, 2);
+         this._layerDirty = true;
+         this.compose(hits);
+         pictureStats.settlePublishes++;
+     }
+     _onQuantumChange(qf, frameId) {
+         if (!this.store) return false;
+         if (this._pendLive) {
+             this.store.abandonQuantum(this._pend.q);
+             pictureStats.quantumAbandons++;
+             this._pendLive = false;
+         }
+         if (this._qPrev != null && qf.q < this._qPrev) {
+             const p = this._prev, now = this._now();
+             const fast = this._qChangeAt != null && now - this._qChangeAt < FAST_ZOOM_MS;
+             this._qChangeAt = now;
+             const ahead = this._prevLive && p.frameId === frameId && p.q <= qf.q - PREVIEW_STICKY;
+             if (!ahead) this._aimPreview(qf.q, frameId, fast ? PREVIEW_FAST : PREVIEW_QUANTA);
+             else pictureStats.previewKept++;
+         } else this._qChangeAt = null;
+         const v = this._v;
+         if (!this._hold) {
+             if (v.mode !== "plates" || this._nHits === 0) { pictureStats.holdSkips++; return false; }
+             this._hold = { frameId: v.frameId, q: v.q, Zq: v.Zq, i0: v.i0, i1: v.i1, j0: v.j0, j1: v.j1 };
+             this._holdWhy = "";
+             pictureStats.holds++;
+             if (!this._covered) pictureStats.holdsPartial++;
+         }
+         if (String(qf.q) === String(this._hold.q)) { this._endHold("return"); return false; }
+         const p = this._pend;
+         p.frameId = frameId; p.q = qf.q; p.Zq = qf.Zq;
+         p.i0 = 0; p.i1 = -1; p.j0 = 0; p.j1 = -1;
+         this._pendLive = true;
+         return true;
+     }
+     _aimPreview(qLive, frameId, dq = PREVIEW_QUANTA) {
+         if (!this.store) return false;
+         const p = this._prev, q = qLive - dq;
+         p.q = q; p.Zq = zqOf(q); p.frameId = frameId;
+         p.i0 = 0; p.i1 = -1; p.j0 = 0; p.j1 = -1;
+         this._prevLive = true;
+         pictureStats.previews++;
+         return true;
+     }
+     _askPreview(cam) {
+         if (!this._prevLive || !this.store || !cam) return false;
+         this._frame(cam, 0, 5);
+         const got = this.store.want(this._prev, { pin: "none" });
+         pictureStats.previewHits += got.hits.length;
+         if (!got.misses.length && !got.inFlight) { this._prevLive = false; return true; }
+         if (got.misses.length && this._onPlates) this._onPlates(this._prev, got.misses);
+         return false;
+     }
+     _askQuantum(cam) {
+         if (!this._hold || !this._pendLive || !this.store || !cam) return false;
+         this._frame(cam, 0, 3);
+         const got = this.store.want(this._pend, { pin: "add" });
+         if (!got.misses.length && !got.inFlight) return this._switchQuantum(cam);
+         if (got.misses.length && this._onPlates) this._onPlates(this._pend, got.misses);
+         return false;
+     }
+     _quantumArrived() {
+         if (!this._hold || !this._pendLive || !this.cam) return false;
+         this._frame(this.cam, 0, 3);
+         const got = this.store.want(this._pend, { pin: "add" });
+         if (got.misses.length || got.inFlight) return false;
+         return this._switchQuantum(this.cam);
+     }
+     _switchQuantum(cam) {
+         const sw = stretchOf(cam.inScale * this.dpr, this._pend.Zq);
+         if (!(sw >= S_MIN)) { pictureStats.switchRefusedLow++; return false; }
+         if (sw > S_MAX_HOLD) { pictureStats.switchRefusedHigh++; return false; }
+         this._pendLive = false;
+         this._prevLive = false;
+         this._endHold("switch");
+         const got = this.store.want(this._pend, { pin: "replace" });
+         this._frame(cam, null, 4);
+         this._layerDirty = true;
+         this.compose(got.hits);
+         this._maybeArm(this._v.mode, this._v.s);
+         pictureStats.holdSwitches++;
+         return true;
+     }
+     _endHold(why) {
+         if (!this._hold) return false;
+         this._hold = null;
+         this._holdWhy = why;
+         if (this._pendLive && (why === "return" || why === "release")) {
+             if (this.store) this.store.abandonQuantum(this._pend.q);
+             pictureStats.quantumAbandons++;
+         }
+         if (this.store) this.store.unpinAdded();
+         this._pendLive = false;
+         return true;
+     }
+     _setHits(list) {
+         const a = this._hits;
+         let same = list.length === this._nHits;
+         for (let i = 0; i < list.length; i++) {
+             if (i >= a.length) { a.push(list[i]); pictureStats.allocs++; same = false; continue; }
+             if (a[i] !== list[i]) same = false;
+             a[i] = list[i];
+         }
+         for (let i = list.length; i < a.length; i++) a[i] = null;
+         this._nHits = list.length;
+         if (!same) this._layerDirty = true;
+     }
+     _addHit(e) {
+         if (!e) return false;
+         const a = this._hits;
+         for (let i = 0; i < this._nHits; i++) if (a[i] && a[i].key === e.key) { a[i] = e; return true; }
+         if (this._nHits >= a.length) { a.push(e); pictureStats.allocs++; } else a[this._nHits] = e;
+         this._nHits++;
+         return true;
+     }
+     _ensureLayer() {
+         if (this._layer) return this._layer;
+         const c = this._canvas;
+         const W = c ? c.width : snapPx(this.cssW * this.dpr);
+         const H = c ? c.height : snapPx(this.cssH * this.dpr);
+         const lw = (Math.floor(W / (PLATE_PX * S_MIN)) + 2) * PLATE_PX;
+         const lh = (Math.floor(H / (PLATE_PX * S_MIN)) + 2) * PLATE_PX;
+         const L = this._mk(lw, lh);
+         if (!L) return null;
+         L.width = lw; L.height = lh;
+         this._layer = L;
+         this._lctx = L.getContext ? L.getContext("2d", { alpha: true }) : null;
+         if (!this._lctx) pictureStats.noContext++;
+         this._layerDirty = true;
+         pictureStats.canvasAllocs++;
+         pictureStats.layerBytes = lw * lh * 4;
+         return L;
+     }
+     _dropLayer() {
+         this._layer = null; this._lctx = null;
+         this._layerDirty = true;
+         pictureStats.layerBytes = 0;
+     }
+ }
//// src/engine/raster/PlateStore.js  (new file)
+ import { PLATE_PX, QUANTA_PER_OCTAVE, plateKey } from "./plate";
+ export const PLATE_BYTES = PLATE_PX * PLATE_PX * 4;
+ export const DESKTOP_CACHE_BYTES = 96 * 1024 * 1024;
+ export const PHONE_CACHE_BYTES = 56 * 1024 * 1024;
+ const EDGE_PAD_PX = 1;
+ export const HIDDEN_RELEASE_MS = 5 * 60 * 1000;
+ const EPOCH_MAX = 4096;
+ export const FB_Q_LO = -64;
+ export const FB_Q_HI = 8;
+ export const FB_MAX = 48;
+ export function isPhoneBudget(env) {
+     const nav = (env && "deviceMemory" in env) ? env : (typeof navigator !== "undefined" ? navigator : {});
+     const scr = (env && env.screenW != null) ? { width: env.screenW, height: env.screenH || 0 }
+         : (typeof window !== "undefined" && window.screen ? window.screen : null);
+     if (nav && typeof nav.deviceMemory === "number" && nav.deviceMemory <= 4) return true;
+     if (scr && Math.max(scr.width || 0, scr.height || 0) < 1200) return true;
+     return false;
+ }
+ export function defaultCacheBytes(env) {
+     return isPhoneBudget(env) ? PHONE_CACHE_BYTES : DESKTOP_CACHE_BYTES;
+ }
+ function ascending(ids) {
+     for (let k = 1; k < ids.length; k++) if (ids[k] <= ids[k - 1]) return false;
+     return true;
+ }
+ function anyId(ids, want) {
+     if (want.size <= ids.length) { for (const id of want) if (holdsId(ids, id)) return true; return false; }
+     for (let k = 0; k < ids.length; k++) if (want.has(ids[k])) return true;
+     return false;
+ }
+ export function holdsId(ids, id) {
+     let lo = 0, hi = ids.length - 1;
+     while (lo <= hi) {
+         const m = (lo + hi) >> 1, v = ids[m];
+         if (v === id) return true;
+         if (v < id) lo = m + 1; else hi = m - 1;
+     }
+     return false;
+ }
+ function boxOf(i0, i1, j0, j1, Zq, pad) {
+     if (!(Zq > 0) || !isFinite(Zq)) return null;
+     const s = PLATE_PX / Zq, p = pad / Zq;
+     return { left: i0 * s - p, top: j0 * s - p, right: (i1 + 1) * s + p, bottom: (j1 + 1) * s + p };
+ }
+ function rectOf(p) { return boxOf(p.i, p.i, p.j, p.j, p.Zq, EDGE_PAD_PX); }
+ function meets(r, b) {
+     if (!r) return true;
+     if (!b) return false;
+     return !(b.right < r.left || b.left > r.right || b.bottom < r.top || b.top > r.bottom);
+ }
+ export default class PlateStore {
+     constructor(opts = {}) {
+         this.maxBytes = opts.maxBytes != null ? opts.maxBytes : defaultCacheBytes(opts.env);
+         this.phone = this.maxBytes === PHONE_CACHE_BYTES;
+         this.cache = new Map();
+         this.epochs = new Map();
+         this.inflight = new Map();
+         this.jobs = new Map();
+         this.bytes = 0;
+         this._pins = new Set();
+         this._held = new Set();
+         this._ladder = new Set();
+         this._plane = new Set();
+         this._base = new Set();
+         this._addTag = "";
+         this._clock = 0;
+         this.onRelease = opts.onRelease || null;
+         this.s = {
+             wants: 0, hits: 0, misses: 0, inFlightSkips: 0,
+             puts: 0, stalePuts: 0, replaced: 0,
+             evictions: 0, overCapPinned: 0,
+             dropsById: 0, dropsByRect: 0, dropsByQ: 0, dropsAll: 0, dropsFrames: 0, ladderEvictions: 0,
+             noIds: 0, noZq: 0, noEpoch: 0, badIds: 0,
+             epochBumps: 0, epochPrunes: 0,
+             addCallerMismatch: 0,
+             staleKept: 0, staleHits: 0, staleReplaced: 0, staleEvictions: 0, staleNewer: 0,
+             fallbackScans: 0, fallbackCandidates: 0, fallbackDropped: 0,
+             abandons: 0,
+             bitmapsOpen: 0, bitmapsClosed: 0,
+             drains: 0, drainedEvents: 0, releases: 0,
+             neutralEvents: 0,
+             dpr: 0,
+         };
+         this._doc = opts.doc !== undefined ? opts.doc : (typeof document !== "undefined" ? document : null);
+         this._onVis = null;
+         this.hiddenReleaseMs = HIDDEN_RELEASE_MS;
+         this._hideTimer = null;
+         this._releasedHidden = false;
+         if (this._doc && this._doc.addEventListener) {
+             this._onVis = () => {
+                 if (this._doc.visibilityState === "hidden") {
+                     if (this.phone) { this.release("hidden"); this._releasedHidden = true; }
+                     else if (this._hideTimer == null) {
+                         this._hideTimer = setTimeout(() => { this._hideTimer = null; this.release("hidden"); this._releasedHidden = true; }, this.hiddenReleaseMs);
+                     }
+                     return;
+                 }
+                 if (this._hideTimer != null) { clearTimeout(this._hideTimer); this._hideTimer = null; }
+                 if (this._releasedHidden) { this._releasedHidden = false; if (this.onShow) this.onShow(); }
+             };
+             this._doc.addEventListener("visibilitychange", this._onVis);
+         }
+     }
+     want(view, opts = {}) {
+         const pin = opts.pin || "replace";
+         const { frameId, q } = view;
+         const hits = [], misses = [];
+         const add = pin === "add";
+         const pins = new Set();
+         let inFlight = 0;
+         for (let j = view.j0; j <= view.j1; j++) {
+             for (let i = view.i0; i <= view.i1; i++) {
+                 const key = plateKey(frameId, q, i, j);
+                 if (pin !== "none") pins.add(key);
+                 const e = this.cache.get(key);
+                 if (e) {
+                     e.lru = ++this._clock; hits.push(e);
+                     if (!this._isStale(e)) continue;
+                     this.s.staleHits++;
+                 }
+                 if (this.inflight.has(key)) { inFlight++; continue; }
+                 misses.push({ key, i, j, epoch: this.epochOf(key) });
+             }
+         }
+         if (add) {
+             const tag = view.mode || "";
+             if (this._held.size && tag !== this._addTag) this.s.addCallerMismatch++;
+             this._addTag = tag;
+             this._held = pins;
+         } else if (pin !== "none") this._pins = pins;
+         this.s.wants++;
+         this.s.hits += hits.length;
+         this.s.misses += misses.length;
+         this.s.inFlightSkips += inFlight;
+         this._evict();
+         return { hits, misses, inFlight };
+     }
+     unpinAdded() { const n = this._held.size; this._held.clear(); return n; }
+     markLadder(keys, fresh = false) {
+         if (fresh) {
+             this._ladder.clear();
+             this._base = new Set(keys);
+         }
+         for (const k of keys) this._ladder.add(k);
+         return this._ladder.size;
+     }
+     setPlanePins(keys) {
+         if (!keys && !this._plane.size) return 0;
+         this._plane = new Set(keys || []);
+         return this._plane.size;
+     }
+     fallbackCover(req, out, mapFrame = null) {
+         this.s.fallbackScans++;
+         const box = boxOf(req.i0, req.i1, req.j0, req.j1, req.Zq, 0);
+         if (!box) return 0;
+         const qs = String(req.q), qn = Number(req.q), num = Number.isFinite(qn);
+         const qe = this._fbQe || (this._fbQe = []);
+         let n = 0;
+         for (const e of this.cache.values()) {
+             if (e.dead || (!e.bitmap && (e.pixels || e.bytes !== 0))) continue;
+             if (!Number.isFinite(e.q)) continue;
+             const Zq = e.Zq;
+             if (!(Zq > 0) || !isFinite(Zq)) continue;
+             let a = 1, bx = 0, by = 0, qk = e.q;
+             if (e.frameId !== req.frameId) {
+                 const m = mapFrame ? mapFrame(e.frameId) : null;
+                 if (!m) continue;
+                 a = m.a; bx = m.bx; by = m.by;
+                 qk = Math.log2(Zq / a) * QUANTA_PER_OCTAVE;
+             } else if (num ? e.q === qn : String(e.q) === qs) continue;
+             if (num && (qk < qn + FB_Q_LO || qk > qn + FB_Q_HI)) continue;
+             const t = (PLATE_PX / Zq) * a, pad = (EDGE_PAD_PX / Zq) * a;
+             const x0 = e.i * t + bx, y0 = e.j * t + by;
+             if (x0 + t + pad < box.left || x0 - pad > box.right
+                 || y0 + t + pad < box.top || y0 - pad > box.bottom) continue;
+             qe[n] = qk;
+             out[n++] = e;
+         }
+         this.s.fallbackCandidates += n;
+         out.length = n;
+         if (n > 1) {
+             const ord = this._fbOrd || (this._fbOrd = []), tmp = this._fbTmp || (this._fbTmp = []);
+             ord.length = n;
+             for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) ord[k] = k;
+             ord.sort((x, y) => (qe[x] - qe[y]) || (out[x].j - out[y].j) || (out[x].i - out[y].i));
+             for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) tmp[k] = out[ord[k]];
+             for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) out[k] = tmp[k];
+             tmp.length = 0;
+         }
+         if (n > FB_MAX) { this.s.fallbackDropped += n - FB_MAX; out.length = n = FB_MAX; }
+         return n;
+     }
+     get(key) { return this.cache.get(key); }
+     size() { return this.cache.size; }
+     plateBytes() { return this.bytes; }
+     put(rec) {
+         const key = rec.key || plateKey(rec.frameId, rec.q, rec.i, rec.j);
+         this._endKey(key, rec.jobId);
+         this.s.puts++;
+         if (rec.bitmap && typeof rec.bitmap.close === "function") this.s.bitmapsOpen++;
+         let newer = false;
+         if (rec.epoch == null) this.s.noEpoch++;
+         else if (rec.epoch !== this.epochOf(key)) {
+             const cur = this.cache.get(key);
+             if (!cur || cur.dead || !this._isStale(cur) || !(rec.epoch > cur.epoch)) {
+                 this.s.stalePuts++;
+                 closeOf(rec, this.s);
+                 return "stale";
+             }
+             this.s.staleNewer++;
+             newer = true;
+         }
+         const old = this.cache.get(key);
+         const inBase = this._base.has(key), inLadder = this._ladder.has(key);
+         if (old) { if (this._isStale(old)) this.s.staleReplaced++; this._remove(key); this.s.replaced++; }
+         const bytes = rec.bytes != null ? rec.bytes : PLATE_BYTES;
+         let ids = rec.ids || null;
+         if (ids && ids.length > 1 && !ascending(ids)) { this.s.badIds++; ids = Int32Array.from(ids).sort(); }
+         const entry = {
+             key, frameId: rec.frameId, q: rec.q, Zq: rec.Zq, i: rec.i, j: rec.j,
+             bitmap: rec.bitmap || null, pixels: rec.pixels || null,
+             bytes, ids, epoch: newer ? rec.epoch : this.epochOf(key), lru: ++this._clock,
+         };
+         if (!rectOf(entry)) this.s.noZq++;
+         this.cache.set(key, entry);
+         if (inBase) this._base.add(key);
+         if (inLadder) this._ladder.add(key);
+         this.bytes += bytes;
+         this._evict();
+         return newer ? "newer" : "cached";
+     }
+     epochOf(key) { const e = this.epochs.get(key); return e === undefined ? 0 : e; }
+     beginJob(jobId, view, plates) {
+         const out = new Int32Array(plates.length);
+         const keys = [];
+         for (let n = 0; n < plates.length; n++) {
+             const p = plates[n];
+             const key = p.key || plateKey(view.frameId, view.q, p.i, p.j);
+             const epoch = this.epochOf(key);
+             out[n] = epoch;
+             keys.push(key);
+             this.inflight.set(key, { jobId, key, frameId: view.frameId, q: view.q, Zq: view.Zq, i: p.i, j: p.j, epoch });
+         }
+         this.jobs.set(jobId, keys);
+         return out;
+     }
+     endJob(jobId) {
+         const keys = this.jobs.get(jobId);
+         if (!keys) return 0;
+         this.jobs.delete(jobId);
+         let n = 0;
+         for (const key of keys) { const f = this.inflight.get(key); if (f && f.jobId === jobId) { this.inflight.delete(key); n++; } }
+         return n;
+     }
+     dropById(id) {
+         const many = id instanceof Set;
+         if (many && !id.size) return 0;
+         let n = 0;
+         for (const e of [...this.cache.values()]) {
+             if (e.ids) {
+                 if (many) { if (!anyId(e.ids, id)) continue; } else if (!holdsId(e.ids, id)) continue;
+             } else this.s.noIds++;
+             this._drop(e.key); n++;
+         }
+         this.s.dropsById += n;
+         return n;
+     }
+     dropRectPerFrame(foot) {
+         if (!foot) return 0;
+         const memo = new Map(), bumped = new Set();
+         const rectsFor = (frameId) => {
+             if (memo.has(frameId)) return memo.get(frameId);
+             const r = foot(frameId);
+             const arr = !r ? [] : (Array.isArray(r) ? r : [r]);
+             memo.set(frameId, arr);
+             return arr;
+         };
+         const hit = (p) => {
+             const rects = rectsFor(p.frameId);
+             if (!rects.length) return false;
+             const box = rectOf(p);
+             for (const r of rects) if (meets(box, r)) return true;
+             return false;
+         };
+         let n = 0;
+         for (const e of [...this.cache.values()]) {
+             if (!hit(e)) continue;
+             this._drop(e.key); bumped.add(e.key); n++;
+         }
+         for (const f of [...this.inflight.values()]) {
+             if (bumped.has(f.key) || !hit(f)) continue;
+             this._bump(f.key); bumped.add(f.key); n++;
+         }
+         this.s.dropsByRect += n;
+         return n;
+     }
+     dropQuantum(q) {
+         const qs = String(q);
+         const bumped = new Set();
+         let n = 0;
+         for (const e of [...this.cache.values()]) {
+             if (String(e.q) !== qs) continue;
+             this._remove(e.key); this._bump(e.key);
+             this._pins.delete(e.key); this._held.delete(e.key); bumped.add(e.key); n++;
+         }
+         for (const f of [...this.inflight.values()]) {
+             if (String(f.q) !== qs || bumped.has(f.key)) continue;
+             this._bump(f.key); bumped.add(f.key); n++;
+         }
+         this.s.dropsByQ += n;
+         return n;
+     }
+     abandonQuantum(q) {
+         const qs = String(q);
+         let n = 0;
+         for (const e of this.cache.values()) {
+             if (String(e.q) !== qs) continue;
+             this._pins.delete(e.key); this._held.delete(e.key); n++;
+         }
+         for (const f of [...this.inflight.values()]) {
+             if (String(f.q) !== qs) continue;
+             this._pins.delete(f.key); this._held.delete(f.key);
+             this._bump(f.key); n++;
+         }
+         this.s.abandons++;
+         return n;
+     }
+     dropAll(keepStandIns = true) {
+         const bumped = new Set();
+         let n = 0;
+         if (!keepStandIns) { this._plane = new Set(); this._base = new Set(); }
+         for (const e of [...this.cache.values()]) {
+             if (!this._plane.has(e.key) && !this._base.has(e.key)) this._remove(e.key);
+             this._bump(e.key); bumped.add(e.key); n++;
+         }
+         for (const f of [...this.inflight.values()]) { if (bumped.has(f.key)) continue; this._bump(f.key); bumped.add(f.key); n++; }
+         this._pins = new Set();
+         this._held.clear();
+         this.s.dropsAll++;
+         return n;
+     }
+     dropFrames(pred) {
+         const bumped = new Set();
+         let n = 0;
+         for (const e of [...this.cache.values()]) {
+             if (this._plane.has(e.key) || !pred(e.frameId)) continue;
+             this._remove(e.key); this._bump(e.key);
+             this._pins.delete(e.key); this._held.delete(e.key); bumped.add(e.key); n++;
+         }
+         for (const f of [...this.inflight.values()]) {
+             if (bumped.has(f.key) || !pred(f.frameId)) continue;
+             this._bump(f.key); n++;
+         }
+         this.s.dropsFrames += n;
+         return n;
+     }
+     release(reason = "hidden") {
+         this._plane = new Set();
+         const n = this.dropAll();
+         this.s.releases++;
+         if (this.onRelease) this.onRelease(reason);
+         return n;
+     }
+     onDocEvent(ev, foot) {
+         if (!ev) return 0;
+         const k = ev.kind;
+         if (k === "reset") return this.dropAll(false);
+         if (k === "refile" || k === "removeFrame" || k === "addFrame" || k === "mergeFrame" || k === "splitFrame") {
+             return this.dropAll();
+         }
+         if (k === "add" && ev.live) return 0;
+         if (!ev.obj && k !== "remove") return 0;
+         let n = 0;
+         if (ev.id != null) n += this.dropById(ev.id);
+         n += this.dropRectPerFrame(foot);
+         return n;
+     }
+     noteDrain(n) { this.s.drains++; this.s.drainedEvents += n; }
+     noteDpr(dpr) { this.s.dpr = dpr; return this.cache.size; }
+     destroy() {
+         this.dropAll(false);
+         if (this._onVis && this._doc) this._doc.removeEventListener("visibilitychange", this._onVis);
+         this._onVis = null;
+         if (this._hideTimer != null) { clearTimeout(this._hideTimer); this._hideTimer = null; }
+         this.inflight.clear();
+         this.jobs.clear();
+         this.epochs.clear();
+     }
+     _evict() {
+         if (this.bytes <= this.maxBytes) return;
+         const evictable = [];
+         for (const e of this.cache.values()) {
+             if (this._pins.has(e.key) || this._held.has(e.key) || this._plane.has(e.key) || this._base.has(e.key)) continue;
+             e.st = this._isStale(e) ? 0 : this._ladder.has(e.key) ? 2 : 1;
+             evictable.push(e);
+         }
+         evictable.sort((a, b) => (a.st - b.st) || (a.lru - b.lru));
+         for (const e of evictable) {
+             if (this.bytes <= this.maxBytes) break;
+             if (e.st === 0) this.s.staleEvictions++;
+             else if (e.st === 2) this.s.ladderEvictions++;
+             this._remove(e.key);
+             this.s.evictions++;
+         }
+         if (this.bytes > this.maxBytes) this.s.overCapPinned++;
+     }
+     _remove(key) {
+         const e = this.cache.get(key);
+         if (!e) return false;
+         this.cache.delete(key);
+         this._ladder.delete(key);
+         this._base.delete(key);
+         this.bytes -= e.bytes;
+         e.dead = true;
+         closeOf(e, this.s);
+         return true;
+     }
+     _drop(key) {
+         this._bump(key);
+         if (this.cache.has(key)) this.s.staleKept++;
+     }
+     _isStale(e) { return e.epoch !== this.epochOf(e.key); }
+     _bump(key) {
+         this.epochs.set(key, this.epochOf(key) + 1);
+         this.s.epochBumps++;
+         if (this.epochs.size > EPOCH_MAX) this._pruneEpochs();
+     }
+     _pruneEpochs() {
+         for (const key of [...this.epochs.keys()]) {
+             if (this.cache.has(key) || this.inflight.has(key)) continue;
+             this.epochs.delete(key);
+             this.s.epochPrunes++;
+         }
+     }
+     _endKey(key, jobId) {
+         const f = this.inflight.get(key);
+         if (!f || (jobId != null && f.jobId !== jobId)) return;
+         this.inflight.delete(key);
+         const keys = this.jobs.get(f.jobId);
+         if (keys) {
+             const at = keys.indexOf(key);
+             if (at >= 0) keys.splice(at, 1);
+             if (!keys.length) this.jobs.delete(f.jobId);
+         }
+     }
+ }
+ function closeOf(holder, s) {
+     const b = holder && holder.bitmap;
+     if (b && typeof b.close === "function") { b.close(); s.bitmapsClosed++; }
+ }
//// src/engine/raster/RasterRenderer.js  (new file)
+ import Two from "two.js";
+ import Renderer from "../Renderer";
+ import { perfNow } from "../now";
+ import PictureLayer from "./PictureLayer";
+ import { holdsId } from "./PlateStore";
+ import { PLATE_PX, PREVIEW_QUANTA, plateKey, plateOriginPx, plateRange, quantumFor, zqOf } from "./plate";
+ import { packJob, parseColour, visibleChords, packStats, COORD_BOUND } from "./rasterPack";
+ import { renderPlate, coreStats, TW, TH } from "./rasterCore";
+ import { COUNTS_PER_PLATE, TIMINGS_PER_PLATE } from "./rasterJob";
+ import { antStats, selAntScale } from "./antPaint";
+ import OverlayLayer from "./OverlayLayer";
+ import MovePlane, { MOVE_PLANE_PLATES, MOVE_PLANE_PLATES_PHONE, MOVE_HOLD_MS, planeStats } from "./MovePlane";
+ import * as rasterPerf from "./rasterPerf";
+ import {
+     submitPlates, dropQueued, abandonInFlight, ensureRasterPool, destroyRasterPool,
+     plateBitmap, plateIds, stripSizeFor, rasterWorkerReport,
+ } from "./rasterWorkerClient";
+ export { PREVIEW_QUANTA };
+ export const LADDER_DQ = 12;
+ const PLANE_QUIET_MS = 150;
+ const LADDER_MAX_PLATES = 24;
+ export const LIVE_HOLD_MS = 3000;
+ const LIVE_HOLD_MAX_MS = 15000;
+ let liveClipSeq = 0;
+ const STOPGAP_OBJECTS = 500;
+ const STOPGAP_PLATES = 8;
+ const FAR_LIST_BUDGET_MS = 40;
+ const FAR_LIST_TRIES = 40;
+ const planeIdle = (fn) => (typeof requestIdleCallback === "function" ? requestIdleCallback(fn, { timeout: 1000 }) : setTimeout(fn, 30));
+ const CHORD_CAP = (budget) => 2 * budget + 1;
+ export const SLOT = {
+     list: "eng.list", settle: "eng.settle", cull: "eng.cull", band: "eng.band", pack: "eng.pack",
+     view: "eng.view", lookup: "eng.lookup", tail: "ovl.tail",
+     clip: "eng.clip", edges: "ras.edges", union: "ras.union", accum: "ras.accum",
+     xferOut: "xfer.out", bitmap: "br.bitmap", blit: "br.blit",
+ };
+ export const rasterStats = {
+     frames: 0, cameraFrames: 0, posts: 0, postedPlates: 0, strips: 0,
+     dupPostQ: 0,
+     jobsEnded: 0, dropsHandled: 0, staleArrivals: 0, refusedAtDispatch: 0,
+     quantumDrops: 0, settleDrops: 0,
+     syncFrames: 0, syncPlates: 0, syncNoImageData: 0, syncBytes: 0,
+     batches: 0, batchPlates: 0, bitmapWaits: 0,
+     prebakePosts: 0, prebakePlates: 0,
+     liveHolds: 0, liveHandoffs: 0, liveTimeouts: 0, liveHoldMsMax: 0, liveExtends: 0,
+     liveClips: 0,
+     ladderPosts: 0, ladderPlates: 0, ladderRefused: 0,
+     snapshots: 0, snapshotPlates: 0, snapshotAborts: 0, snapshotFails: 0, snapshotOverBound: 0,
+ };
+ function sortCentreOut(misses, r) {
+     const ci = (r.i0 + r.i1) / 2, cj = (r.j0 + r.j1) / 2;
+     misses.sort((a, b) => ((a.i - ci) ** 2 + (a.j - cj) ** 2) - ((b.i - ci) ** 2 + (b.j - cj) ** 2));
