+             acc += ACC[base + x];
+             if ((acc < 0 ? -acc : acc) < COVER_MIN) return false;
+         }
+         if (xb < TW && (acc < 0 ? -acc : acc) < COVER_MIN) return false;
+     }
+     return true;
+ }
+ function keepAt(bits, i) {
+     let keep = 1;
+     for (let k = 0; bits; k++, bits >>>= 1) if (bits & 1) keep *= 1 - MASKS[k][i];
+     return keep;
+ }
+ function resolveMask(k, y0, y1) {
+     let M = MASKS[k];
+     if (!M) M = MASKS[k] = new Float32Array(TW * TH);
+     if (!(maskLive & (1 << k))) { M.fill(0); maskLive |= 1 << k; }
+     for (let y = y0; y < y1; y++) {
+         const xa = ROWMIN[y];
+         if (xa > TW - 1) continue;
+         const base = y * ACC_STRIDE, row = y * TW, rm = ROWMAX[y];
+         const xb = rm + 1 < TW ? rm + 1 : TW;
+         let acc = 0;
+         for (let x = xa; x < xb; x++) {
+             acc += ACC[base + x];
+             let c = acc < 0 ? -acc : acc;
+             if (c > 1) c = 1;
+             if (c > M[row + x]) M[row + x] = c;
+         }
+         if (acc > TAIL_EPS || acc < -TAIL_EPS) {
+             let c = acc < 0 ? -acc : acc;
+             if (c > 1) c = 1;
+             for (let x = xb; x < TW; x++) if (c > M[row + x]) M[row + x] = c;
+         }
+     }
+ }
+ export function clearFamily(y0, y1) {
+     for (let y = y0; y < y1; y++) {
+         const base = y * ACC_STRIDE;
+         const hi = ROWMAX[y] + 2;
+         for (let x = ROWMIN[y]; x <= hi; x++) ACC[base + x] = 0;
+     }
+     if (needFullClear) { ACC.fill(0); needFullClear = false; coreStats.fullClears++; }
+ }
+ function rowsTouched(y0, y1) {
+     let n = 0;
+     for (let y = y0; y < y1; y++) if (ROWMIN[y] <= TW - 1) n++;
+     return n;
+ }
+ let uCount = 0, uSign = 1;
+ let uSkip = 0, uSeen = 0;
+ function pushUnionEdge(ax, ay, bx, by, yb0, yb1) {
+     if (!(isFinite(ax) && isFinite(ay) && isFinite(bx) && isFinite(by))) { coreStats.nonFinite++; needFullClear = true; return true; }
+     if (ay === by) return true;
+     let dir = 1;
+     if (ay > by) { const tx = ax; ax = bx; bx = tx; const ty = ay; ay = by; by = ty; dir = -1; }
+     if (by <= yb0 || ay >= yb1) return true;
+     const dxdy = (bx - ax) / (by - ay);
+     let yTop = ay, xTop = ax;
+     if (yTop < yb0) { xTop = ax + (yb0 - ay) * dxdy; yTop = yb0; }
+     const yBot = by < yb1 ? by : yb1;
+     if (!(yBot > yTop)) return true;
+     if (uSeen++ < uSkip) return true;
+     if (uCount >= MAX_UEDGE) return false;
+     const o = uCount * 4;
+     UEDGE[o] = yTop; UEDGE[o + 1] = yBot; UEDGE[o + 2] = xTop; UEDGE[o + 3] = dxdy;
+     UDIR[uCount] = dir;
+     uCount++;
+     return true;
+ }
+ function sortCrossings(n) {
+     if (n > 32) { mergeCrossings(n); return; }
+     for (let i = 1; i < n; i++) {
+         const x = XS[2 * i], d = XS[2 * i + 1];
+         let j = i - 1;
+         while (j >= 0 && XS[2 * j] > x) { XS[2 * j + 2] = XS[2 * j]; XS[2 * j + 3] = XS[2 * j + 1]; j--; }
+         XS[2 * j + 2] = x; XS[2 * j + 3] = d;
+     }
+ }
+ let XT = new Float64Array(2 * 2048);
+ export const sortStats = { mergeSorts: 0 };
+ function mergeCrossings(n) {
+     if (XT.length < XS.length) XT = new Float64Array(XS.length);
+     sortStats.mergeSorts++;
+     let a = XS, b = XT;
+     for (let w = 1; w < n; w *= 2) {
+         for (let lo = 0; lo < n; lo += 2 * w) {
+             const mid = Math.min(lo + w, n), hi = Math.min(lo + 2 * w, n);
+             let i = lo, j = mid, k = lo;
+             while (i < mid && j < hi) {
+                 if (a[2 * j] < a[2 * i]) { b[2 * k] = a[2 * j]; b[2 * k + 1] = a[2 * j + 1]; j++; }
+                 else { b[2 * k] = a[2 * i]; b[2 * k + 1] = a[2 * i + 1]; i++; }
+                 k++;
+             }
+             while (i < mid) { b[2 * k] = a[2 * i]; b[2 * k + 1] = a[2 * i + 1]; i++; k++; }
+             while (j < hi) { b[2 * k] = a[2 * j]; b[2 * k + 1] = a[2 * j + 1]; j++; k++; }
+         }
+         const t = a; a = b; b = t;
+     }
+     if (a !== XS) XS.set(a.subarray(0, 2 * n));
+ }
+ function growXS() {
+     const bigger = new Float64Array(XS.length * 2);
+     bigger.set(XS);
+     XS = bigger;
+     coreStats.xsGrown++;
+ }
+ function gatherCrossings(ys, n, nAct) {
+     for (let a = 0; a < nAct; a++) {
+         const e = UACT[a], o = e * 4;
+         if (UEDGE[o] > ys || UEDGE[o + 1] <= ys) continue;
+         if (2 * n + 2 > XS.length) growXS();
+         XS[2 * n] = UEDGE[o + 2] + (ys - UEDGE[o]) * UEDGE[o + 3];
+         XS[2 * n + 1] = UDIR[e];
+         n++;
+     }
+     return n;
+ }
+ function emitRuns(y, n, w) {
+     if (n < 2) return;
+     sortCrossings(n);
+     let wind = 0, xa = 0;
+     for (let c = 0; c < n; c++) {
+         const was = wind;
+         wind += XS[2 * c + 1];
+         if (was === 0 && wind !== 0) xa = XS[2 * c];
+         else if (was !== 0 && wind === 0) {
+             let a = xa, b = XS[2 * c];
+             if (a < 0) a = 0; else if (a > TW) a = TW;
+             if (b < 0) b = 0; else if (b > TW) b = TW;
+             if (a === b) continue;
+             depositRun(y, a, a, w);
+             depositRun(y, b, b, -w);
+         }
+     }
+ }
+ function walkUnion(yb0, yb1) {
+     for (let y = yb0; y < yb1; y++) UHEAD[y] = -1;
+     for (let e = 0; e < uCount; e++) {
+         let row = Math.floor(UEDGE[e * 4]) | 0;
+         if (row < yb0) row = yb0; else if (row >= yb1) row = yb1 - 1;
+         UNEXT[e] = UHEAD[row]; UHEAD[row] = e;
+     }
+     const step = 1 / UNION_K, half = step / 2, w = step * uSign;
+     let nAct = 0;
+     for (let y = yb0; y < yb1; y++) {
+         for (let e = UHEAD[y]; e !== -1; e = UNEXT[e]) UACT[nAct++] = e;
+         for (let k = 0; k < UNION_K; k++) emitRuns(y, gatherCrossings(y + half + k * step, 0, nAct), w);
+         let keep = 0;
+         const yNext = y + 1;
+         for (let a = 0; a < nAct; a++) { const e = UACT[a]; if (UEDGE[e * 4 + 1] > yNext) UACT[keep++] = e; }
+         nAct = keep;
+     }
+ }
+ export function fillPaper(out, r, g, b, a = 255) {
+     if ((out.byteOffset & 3) === 0 && out.length === TW * TH * 4) {
+         const u32 = new Uint32Array(out.buffer, out.byteOffset, TW * TH);
+         out[0] = r; out[1] = g; out[2] = b; out[3] = a;
+         u32.fill(u32[0]);
+         return;
+     }
+     for (let p = 0; p < out.length; p += 4) { out[p] = r; out[p + 1] = g; out[p + 2] = b; out[p + 3] = a; }
+ }
+ export function unpremultiply(out) {
+     for (let p = 0; p < out.length; p += 4) {
+         const A = out[p + 3];
+         if (A === 0 || A === 255) continue;
+         const k = 255 / A;
+         out[p] = out[p] * k; out[p + 1] = out[p + 1] * k; out[p + 2] = out[p + 2] * k;
+     }
+ }
+ const RECORD_WIDTH = { 0: 3, 1: 8, 2: 9, 3: 16 };
+ const widthOf = (code) => RECORD_WIDTH[code] || 8;
+ export const ARC_TOL = 1 / 16;
+ const MAXD = 24;
+ const TAU = Math.PI * 2;
+ const WHOLE_TURN = TAU - 1e-9;
+ const MAX_SWEEP = TAU + 1e-9;
+ const HALF_PI = Math.PI / 2;
+ const STACK = new Float64Array(6 * (MAXD + 2));
+ const KTAB = new Float64Array(MAXD + 1);
+ const SUBPT = new Float64Array(2 * 9);
+ const MID = new Float64Array(2);
+ const ARC_EDGE = 0, ARC_UNION = 1;
+ let arcChords = 0;
+ export function arcForced(sweep) {
+     let a = Math.abs(sweep), f = 0;
+     while (a > HALF_PI && f < 3) { a *= 0.5; f++; }
+     return f;
+ }
+ export function arcDepth(ax, ay, bx, by, sweep) {
+     if (sagittaOf(ax, ay, bx, by, sweep) <= ARC_TOL) return 0;
+     const forced = arcForced(sweep);
+     const n = 1 << forced;
+     SUBPT[0] = ax; SUBPT[1] = ay; SUBPT[2 * n] = bx; SUBPT[2 * n + 1] = by;
+     let s = sweep;
+     for (let d = 0; d < forced; d++) {
+         const k = bulgeK(s), span = n >> d, half = span >> 1;
+         for (let i = 0; i < n; i += span) {
+             chordMidK(SUBPT[2 * i], SUBPT[2 * i + 1], SUBPT[2 * (i + span)], SUBPT[2 * (i + span) + 1], k, MID);
+             SUBPT[2 * (i + half)] = MID[0]; SUBPT[2 * (i + half) + 1] = MID[1];
+         }
+         s *= 0.5;
+     }
+     const th = Math.abs(s);
+     let deepest = 0;
+     for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
+         const dx = SUBPT[2 * (i + 1)] - SUBPT[2 * i], dy = SUBPT[2 * (i + 1) + 1] - SUBPT[2 * i + 1];
+         const L = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
+         if (!(L > 0)) continue;
+         let u = 1 - (2 * ARC_TOL * Math.sin(th / 2)) / L;
+         if (u < -1) u = -1; else if (u > 1) u = 1;
+         const ac = Math.acos(u);
+         const cnt = ac > 0 ? th / (2 * ac) : Infinity;
+         const di = cnt > 1 ? Math.ceil(Math.log2(cnt)) : 0;
+         if (di > deepest) deepest = di;
+     }
+     let D = forced + deepest;
+     if (!(D <= MAXD)) { D = MAXD; coreStats.arcClamped++; }
+     return D < forced ? forced : D;
+ }
+ function chordOf(ax, ay, bx, by, ox, oy, mode, yb0, yb1) {
+     if (mode === ARC_EDGE) return addEdge(ax - ox, ay - oy, bx - ox, by - oy);
+     return pushUnionEdge(ax - ox, ay - oy, bx - ox, by - oy, yb0, yb1) ? 0 : -1;
+ }
+ function emitArc(ax, ay, bx, by, sweep, cx, cy, ox, oy, mode, yb0, yb1) {
+     if (!(isFinite(ax) && isFinite(ay) && isFinite(bx) && isFinite(by))) {
+         coreStats.nonFinite++; needFullClear = true; return 0;
+     }
+     if (!isFinite(sweep)) {
+         coreStats.nonFinite++;
+         return chordOf(ax, ay, bx, by, ox, oy, mode, yb0, yb1);
+     }
+     if (Math.abs(sweep) > MAX_SWEEP) {
+         coreStats.arcOverTurn++;
+         if (DEBUG) throw new Error("rasterCore: an arc sweep of " + sweep + " is past a whole turn, which §4.4 bounds at 2π");
+         return chordOf(ax, ay, bx, by, ox, oy, mode, yb0, yb1);
+     }
+     if (Math.abs(sweep) >= WHOLE_TURN) {
+         if (!(isFinite(cx) && isFinite(cy))) {
+             coreStats.nonFinite++;
+             return chordOf(ax, ay, bx, by, ox, oy, mode, yb0, yb1);
+         }
+         const mx = cx + (cx - ax), my = cy + (cy - ay), h = sweep / 2;
+         const a = emitArc(ax, ay, mx, my, h, cx, cy, ox, oy, mode, yb0, yb1);
+         if (a < 0) return -1;
+         const b = emitArc(mx, my, bx, by, h, cx, cy, ox, oy, mode, yb0, yb1);
+         return b < 0 ? -1 : a + b;
+     }
+     const D = arcDepth(ax, ay, bx, by, sweep), f = arcForced(sweep);
+     const forced = f < D ? f : D;
+     arcChords += 1 << D;
+     let s = sweep;
+     for (let d = 0; d <= D; d++) { KTAB[d] = bulgeK(s); s *= 0.5; }
+     const px1 = ox + TW, py0 = oy, py1 = oy + TH;
+     let edges = 0, sp = 6;
+     STACK[0] = ax; STACK[1] = ay; STACK[2] = bx; STACK[3] = by; STACK[4] = sweep; STACK[5] = 0;
+     while (sp > 0) {
+         sp -= 6;
+         const nax = STACK[sp], nay = STACK[sp + 1], nbx = STACK[sp + 2], nby = STACK[sp + 3];
+         const nsw = STACK[sp + 4], d = STACK[sp + 5], k = KTAB[d];
+         if (d >= forced) {
+             const gx = Math.abs((nby - nay) * k), gy = Math.abs((nbx - nax) * k);
+             const ylo = (nay < nby ? nay : nby) - gy, yhi = (nay < nby ? nby : nay) + gy;
+             if (yhi < py0 || ylo > py1) continue;
+             const xlo = (nax < nbx ? nax : nbx) - gx, xhi = (nax < nbx ? nbx : nax) + gx;
+             if (xlo > px1) {
+                 if (mode === ARC_EDGE) continue;
+                 if (!pushUnionEdge(TW, nay - oy, TW, nby - oy, yb0, yb1)) return -1;
+                 continue;
+             }
+             if (xhi < ox) {
+                 if (mode === ARC_EDGE) edges += addEdge(0, nay - oy, 0, nby - oy);
+                 else if (!pushUnionEdge(0, nay - oy, 0, nby - oy, yb0, yb1)) return -1;
+                 continue;
+             }
+             if (d === D) {
+                 if (mode === ARC_EDGE) edges += addEdge(nax - ox, nay - oy, nbx - ox, nby - oy);
+                 else if (!pushUnionEdge(nax - ox, nay - oy, nbx - ox, nby - oy, yb0, yb1)) return -1;
+                 continue;
+             }
+         }
+         chordMidK(nax, nay, nbx, nby, k, MID);
+         const mx = MID[0], my = MID[1], hs = nsw * 0.5, nd = d + 1;
+         STACK[sp] = mx; STACK[sp + 1] = my; STACK[sp + 2] = nbx; STACK[sp + 3] = nby;
+         STACK[sp + 4] = hs; STACK[sp + 5] = nd;
+         STACK[sp + 6] = nax; STACK[sp + 7] = nay; STACK[sp + 8] = mx; STACK[sp + 9] = my;
+         STACK[sp + 10] = hs; STACK[sp + 11] = nd;
+         sp += 12;
+     }
+     return edges;
+ }
+ function emitItem(job, it, rx, ry, ox, oy, Zq) {
+     const meta = job.meta, geom = job.geom, subs = job.subs;
+     const kind = meta[it * 8], gs = meta[it * 8 + 2], gl = meta[it * 8 + 3];
+     const ss = meta[it * 8 + 4], sc = meta[it * 8 + 5];
+     let edges = 0;
+     if (kind === 0) {
+         for (let k = 0; k < sc; k++) {
+             const lo = gs + subs[ss + k];
+             const hi = k + 1 < sc ? gs + subs[ss + k + 1] : gs + gl;
+             if (hi - lo < 5) continue;
+             let ax = (geom[lo] + rx) * Zq, ay = (geom[lo + 1] + ry) * Zq;
+             const fx = ax, fy = ay;
+             for (let o = lo + 2; o < hi; o += widthOf(geom[o])) {
+                 const code = geom[o];
+                 let bx, by, sweep = 0;
+                 if (code === 0) { bx = geom[o + 1]; by = geom[o + 2]; }
+                 else if (code === 2) { bx = geom[o + 7]; by = geom[o + 8]; }
+                 else if (code === 1) { bx = geom[o + 6]; by = geom[o + 7]; sweep = geom[o + 5]; }
+                 else { bx = geom[o + 14]; by = geom[o + 15]; sweep = geom[o + 5]; }
+                 bx = (bx + rx) * Zq; by = (by + ry) * Zq;
+                 if (sweep !== 0) {
+                     edges += emitArc(ax, ay, bx, by, sweep, (geom[o + 1] + rx) * Zq, (geom[o + 2] + ry) * Zq,
+                         ox, oy, ARC_EDGE, 0, TH);
+                 } else {
+                     edges += addEdge(ax - ox, ay - oy, bx - ox, by - oy);
+                 }
+                 ax = bx; ay = by;
+             }
+             edges += addEdge(ax - ox, ay - oy, fx - ox, fy - oy);
+         }
+     } else {
+         for (let k = 0; k < sc; k++) {
+             const lo = gs + 2 * subs[ss + k];
+             const hi = k + 1 < sc ? gs + 2 * subs[ss + k + 1] : gs + gl;
+             if (hi - lo < 4) continue;
+             const fx = (geom[lo] + rx) * Zq - ox, fy = (geom[lo + 1] + ry) * Zq - oy;
+             let ax = fx, ay = fy;
+             for (let o = lo + 2; o < hi; o += 2) {
+                 const bx = (geom[o] + rx) * Zq - ox, by = (geom[o + 1] + ry) * Zq - oy;
+                 edges += addEdge(ax, ay, bx, by);
+                 ax = bx; ay = by;
+             }
+             edges += addEdge(ax, ay, fx, fy);
+         }
+     }
+     return edges;
+ }
+ function itemShoelace(job, it, rx, ry, ox, oy, Zq) {
+     const meta = job.meta, geom = job.geom, subs = job.subs;
+     const kind = meta[it * 8], gs = meta[it * 8 + 2], gl = meta[it * 8 + 3];
+     const ss = meta[it * 8 + 4], sc = meta[it * 8 + 5];
+     let sum = 0;
+     for (let k = 0; k < sc; k++) {
+         const lo = kind === 0 ? gs + subs[ss + k] : gs + 2 * subs[ss + k];
+         const hi = k + 1 < sc ? (kind === 0 ? gs + subs[ss + k + 1] : gs + 2 * subs[ss + k + 1]) : gs + gl;
+         if (hi - lo < 4) continue;
+         const fx = (geom[lo] + rx) * Zq - ox, fy = (geom[lo + 1] + ry) * Zq - oy;
+         let ax = fx, ay = fy;
+         if (kind === 0) {
+             for (let o = lo + 2; o < hi; o += widthOf(geom[o])) {
+                 const code = geom[o];
+                 let bx, by, sweep = 0;
+                 if (code === 0) { bx = geom[o + 1]; by = geom[o + 2]; }
+                 else if (code === 2) { bx = geom[o + 7]; by = geom[o + 8]; }
+                 else if (code === 1) { bx = geom[o + 6]; by = geom[o + 7]; sweep = geom[o + 5]; }
+                 else { bx = geom[o + 14]; by = geom[o + 15]; sweep = geom[o + 5]; }
+                 bx = (bx + rx) * Zq - ox; by = (by + ry) * Zq - oy;
+                 sum += ax * by - bx * ay;
+                 const arc = sweep !== 0 && isFinite(sweep) && Math.abs(sweep) <= MAX_SWEEP;
+                 if (arc && Math.abs(sweep) >= WHOLE_TURN) {
+                     const ccx = (geom[o + 1] + rx) * Zq - ox, ccy = (geom[o + 2] + ry) * Zq - oy;
+                     const rr = Math.hypot(ax - ccx, ay - ccy);
+                     if (rr > 0 && isFinite(rr)) sum += (sweep > 0 ? TAU : -TAU) * rr * rr;
+                 } else if (arc) {
+                     sum += arcAreaTerm(ax, ay, bx, by, sweep);
+                 }
+                 ax = bx; ay = by;
+             }
+         } else {
+             for (let o = lo + 2; o < hi; o += 2) {
+                 const bx = (geom[o] + rx) * Zq - ox, by = (geom[o + 1] + ry) * Zq - oy;
+                 sum += ax * by - bx * ay; ax = bx; ay = by;
+             }
+         }
+         sum += ax * fy - fx * ay;
+     }
+     return sum;
+ }
+ function loadUnionItem(job, it, rx, ry, ox, oy, Zq, yb0, yb1) {
+     const meta = job.meta, geom = job.geom, subs = job.subs;
+     const kind = meta[it * 8], gs = meta[it * 8 + 2], gl = meta[it * 8 + 3];
+     const ss = meta[it * 8 + 4], sc = meta[it * 8 + 5];
+     for (let k = 0; k < sc; k++) {
+         const lo = kind === 0 ? gs + subs[ss + k] : gs + 2 * subs[ss + k];
+         const hi = k + 1 < sc ? (kind === 0 ? gs + subs[ss + k + 1] : gs + 2 * subs[ss + k + 1]) : gs + gl;
+         if (hi - lo < 4) continue;
+         if (kind === 0) {
+             let ax = (geom[lo] + rx) * Zq, ay = (geom[lo + 1] + ry) * Zq;
+             const gfx = ax, gfy = ay;
+             for (let o = lo + 2; o < hi; o += widthOf(geom[o])) {
+                 const code = geom[o];
+                 let bx, by, sweep = 0;
+                 if (code === 0) { bx = geom[o + 1]; by = geom[o + 2]; }
+                 else if (code === 2) { bx = geom[o + 7]; by = geom[o + 8]; }
+                 else if (code === 1) { bx = geom[o + 6]; by = geom[o + 7]; sweep = geom[o + 5]; }
+                 else { bx = geom[o + 14]; by = geom[o + 15]; sweep = geom[o + 5]; }
+                 bx = (bx + rx) * Zq; by = (by + ry) * Zq;
+                 if (sweep !== 0) {
+                     if (emitArc(ax, ay, bx, by, sweep, (geom[o + 1] + rx) * Zq, (geom[o + 2] + ry) * Zq,
+                         ox, oy, ARC_UNION, yb0, yb1) < 0) return false;
+                 } else if (!pushUnionEdge(ax - ox, ay - oy, bx - ox, by - oy, yb0, yb1)) {
+                     return false;
+                 }
+                 ax = bx; ay = by;
+             }
+             if (!pushUnionEdge(ax - ox, ay - oy, gfx - ox, gfy - oy, yb0, yb1)) return false;
+             continue;
+         }
+         const fx = (geom[lo] + rx) * Zq - ox, fy = (geom[lo + 1] + ry) * Zq - oy;
+         let ax = fx, ay = fy;
+         for (let o = lo + 2; o < hi; o += 2) {
+             const bx = (geom[o] + rx) * Zq - ox, by = (geom[o + 1] + ry) * Zq - oy;
+             if (!pushUnionEdge(ax, ay, bx, by, yb0, yb1)) return false;
+             ax = bx; ay = by;
+         }
+         if (!pushUnionEdge(ax, ay, fx, fy, yb0, yb1)) return false;
+     }
+     return true;
+ }
+ function loadUnionBand(job, meta, attr, first, last, ox, oy, Zq, yb0, yb1) {
+     uCount = 0; uSeen = 0;
+     for (let it = first; it <= last; it++) {
+         if (!DRAWN[it] || (meta[it * 8 + 6] & 4) === 0) continue;
+         if (!loadUnionItem(job, it, attr[it * 9 + 4], attr[it * 9 + 5], ox, oy, Zq, yb0, yb1)) return false;
+     }
+     return true;
+ }
+ let gOver = 0, gNonFinite = 0, gClamped = 0, gHave = false;
+ function loadBandCounted(job, meta, attr, first, last, ox, oy, Zq, yb0, yb1) {
+     const o0 = coreStats.arcOverTurn, f0 = coreStats.nonFinite, c0 = coreStats.arcClamped;
+     const fits = loadUnionBand(job, meta, attr, first, last, ox, oy, Zq, yb0, yb1);
+     if (fits && !gHave) {
+         gOver = coreStats.arcOverTurn - o0;
+         gNonFinite = coreStats.nonFinite - f0;
+         gClamped = coreStats.arcClamped - c0;
+         gHave = true;
+     }
+     return fits;
+ }
+ function walkRowChunked(job, meta, attr, first, last, ox, oy, Zq, y) {
+     const step = 1 / UNION_K, half = step / 2, w = step * uSign;
+     const chords0 = arcChords;
+     let rowChords = 0, refills = 0;
+     for (let k = 0; k < UNION_K; k++) {
+         const ys = y + half + k * step;
+         let n = 0, skip = 0, fits = false;
+         while (!fits) {
+             const c0 = arcChords;
+             uSkip = skip;
+             fits = loadBandCounted(job, meta, attr, first, last, ox, oy, Zq, y, y + 1);
+             if (fits && k === 0) rowChords = arcChords - c0;
+             for (let e = 0; e < uCount; e++) UACT[e] = e;
+             n = gatherCrossings(ys, n, uCount);
+             skip += uCount;
+             if (!fits && k === 0) refills++;
+         }
+         emitRuns(y, n, w);
+     }
+     uSkip = 0;
+     arcChords = chords0 + rowChords;
+     coreStats.unionRefills += refills;
+ }
+ function unionPass(job, first, last, ox, oy, Zq, y0, y1) {
+     const meta = job.meta, attr = job.attr;
+     uSkip = 0;
+     const over0 = coreStats.arcOverTurn, nonFin0 = coreStats.nonFinite, clamp0 = coreStats.arcClamped;
+     gOver = 0; gNonFinite = 0; gClamped = 0; gHave = false;
+     let yb0 = y0;
+     while (yb0 < y1) {
+         let yb1 = y1;
+         let fits = loadBandCounted(job, meta, attr, first, last, ox, oy, Zq, yb0, yb1);
+         while (!fits && yb1 - yb0 > 1) {
+             yb1 = yb0 + ((yb1 - yb0) >> 1);
+             coreStats.unionSplits++;
+             fits = loadBandCounted(job, meta, attr, first, last, ox, oy, Zq, yb0, yb1);
+         }
+         if (fits) walkUnion(yb0, yb1);
+         else walkRowChunked(job, meta, attr, first, last, ox, oy, Zq, yb0);
+         yb0 = yb1;
+     }
+     coreStats.arcOverTurn = over0 + gOver;
+     coreStats.nonFinite = nonFin0 + gNonFinite;
+     coreStats.arcClamped = clamp0 + gClamped;
+ }
+ export function renderPlate(job, plateIndex, out, counts, timings) {
+     const clock = timings ? (typeof performance !== "undefined" ? performance : Date) : null;
+     const meta = job.meta, attr = job.attr, rgb = job.rgb;
+     const n = job.nItems | 0, Zq = job.Zq;
+     const ox = job.plates[plateIndex * 2] * TW, oy = job.plates[plateIndex * 2 + 1] * TH;
+     ALPHA_OUT = job.paper.length > 3 && job.paper[3] === 0;
+     fillPaper(out, job.paper[0], job.paper[1], job.paper[2], ALPHA_OUT ? 0 : 255);
+     if (DRAWN.length < n) DRAWN = new Uint8Array(n + 256);
+     if (!CORE_KEYS) { CORE_KEYS = Object.keys(coreStats); CORE_SNAP = new Float64Array(CORE_KEYS.length); }
+     let nFam = 0, nUnion = 0, nDrawn = 0, nEdges = 0, nRows = 0, nCovered = 0;
+     let tClip = 0, tEdges = 0, tUnion = 0, tAccum = 0;
+     arcChords = 0;
+     let t0 = clock ? clock.now() : 0;
+     let hasMask = false;
+     for (let it = 0; it < n; it++) {
+         const a = it * 9;
+         const x0 = attr[a] * Zq - ox, x1 = attr[a + 2] * Zq - ox;
+         const y0 = attr[a + 1] * Zq - oy, y1 = attr[a + 3] * Zq - oy;
+         DRAWN[it] = (x1 >= 0 && x0 <= TW && y1 >= 0 && y0 <= TH) ? 1 : 0;
+         nDrawn += DRAWN[it];
+         if (DRAWN[it] && (meta[it * 8 + 6] & FLAG_MASK)) hasMask = true;
+     }
+     if (clock) tClip = clock.now() - t0;
+     maskLive = 0;
+     let nF = 0;
+     for (let it = 0; it < n;) {
+         const fam = meta[it * 8 + 1];
+         let last = it;
+         while (last + 1 < n && meta[(last + 1) * 8 + 1] === fam) last++;
+         if (last + 1 < n && meta[(last + 1) * 8 + 1] < fam) {
+             throw new Error("rasterCore: family indices must ascend in paint order (§3.3)");
+         }
+         if (FAMS.length < 2 * (nF + 1)) { const g = new Int32Array(FAMS.length * 2 + 512); g.set(FAMS); FAMS = g; }
+         FAMS[2 * nF] = it; FAMS[2 * nF + 1] = last; nF++;
+         it = last + 1;
+     }
+     for (let pass = hasMask ? 0 : 1; pass < 2; pass++) {
+         let from = 0, covered = -1;
+         if (pass === 1 && OCCLUSION) {
+             t0 = clock ? clock.now() : 0;
+             for (let f = nF - 1; f >= 0; f--) {
+                 const it = FAMS[2 * f], last = FAMS[2 * f + 1];
+                 if (!coverCandidate(job, it, last, ox, oy, Zq)) continue;
+                 const chords = arcChords;
+                 for (let k = 0; k < CORE_KEYS.length; k++) CORE_SNAP[k] = coreStats[CORE_KEYS[k]];
+                 const edges = depositFamily(job, it, last, ox, oy, Zq, 0, TH);
+                 if (fillsPlate()) { nEdges += edges; from = f; covered = f; occStats.occluded += f; break; }
+                 arcChords = chords;
+                 for (let k = 0; k < CORE_KEYS.length; k++) coreStats[CORE_KEYS[k]] = CORE_SNAP[k];
+                 occStats.coverMisses++;
+                 clearFamily(0, TH);
+             }
+             if (clock) tEdges += clock.now() - t0;
+         }
+         for (let f = from; f < nF; f++) {
+             const it = FAMS[2 * f], last = FAMS[2 * f + 1];
+             const isMask = (meta[it * 8 + 6] & FLAG_MASK) !== 0;
+             if (isMask !== (pass === 0)) continue;
+             let by0 = Infinity, by1 = -Infinity, first = -1;
+             for (let k = it; k <= last; k++) {
+                 if (!DRAWN[k]) continue;
+                 const a = k * 9;
+                 const t = attr[a + 1] * Zq - oy, b = attr[a + 3] * Zq - oy;
+                 if (t < by0) by0 = t;
+                 if (b > by1) by1 = b;
+                 if (first < 0) first = k;
+                 if (meta[k * 8 + 6] & 4) nUnion++;
+             }
+             if (first < 0) continue;
+             let y0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor(by0 - BOX_SLACK)), y1 = Math.min(TH, Math.ceil(by1 + BOX_SLACK));
+             if (f === covered) { y0 = 0; y1 = TH; }
+             if (y1 <= y0) continue;
+             nFam++;
+             t0 = clock ? clock.now() : 0;
+             if (f !== covered) nEdges += depositFamily(job, it, last, ox, oy, Zq, y0, y1);
+             if (clock) { const t = clock.now(); tEdges += t - t0; t0 = t; }
+             const c = first * 3;
+             nRows += rowsTouched(y0, y1);
+             if (isMask) resolveMask(meta[first * 8 + 7], y0, y1);
+             else {
+                 let bits = 0;
+                 if (maskLive) for (let k = it; k <= last; k++) bits |= meta[k * 8 + 7];
+                 nCovered += resolveFamily(out, rgb[c], rgb[c + 1], rgb[c + 2], attr[first * 9 + 7], y0, y1, bits & maskLive);
+             }
+             clearFamily(y0, y1);
+             if (clock) tAccum += clock.now() - t0;
+         }
+     }
+     if (ALPHA_OUT) { unpremultiply(out); ALPHA_OUT = false; }
+     if (counts) {
+         counts[0] = n; counts[1] = nDrawn; counts[2] = nFam; counts[3] = nUnion;
+         counts[4] = nEdges; counts[5] = arcChords; counts[6] = nRows; counts[7] = nCovered;
+     }
+     if (timings) { timings[2] = tClip; timings[3] = 0; timings[4] = tEdges; timings[5] = tUnion; timings[6] = tAccum; }
+     return counts;
+ }
//// src/engine/raster/rasterJob.js  (new file)
+ import { PLATE_PX } from "./plate";
+ export const META_WORDS = 8;
+ export const ATTR_DOUBLES = 9;
+ export const RGB_BYTES = 3;
+ export const KIND_LOOPS = 0;
+ export const KIND_RINGS = 1;
+ export const FLAG_COVERS = 1;
+ export const FLAG_ERASE = 2;
+ export const FLAG_UNION = 4;
+ export const FLAG_MASK = 8;
+ export const MASKS_MAX = 8;
+ export const TIMINGS_PER_PLATE = 7;
+ export const COUNTS_PER_PLATE = 8;
+ export const GUARDS_PER_JOB = 7;
+ export const COUNT_NAMES = ["items", "itemsDrawn", "families", "unionItems", "edges", "chords", "rowsTouched", "coveredPx"];
+ export const GUARD_NAMES = ["xsGrown", "unionSplits", "unionRefills", "fullClears", "nonFinite", "arcClamped", "arcOverTurn"];
+ const JOB_ARRAYS = ["epochs", "plates", "paper", "meta", "attr", "rgb", "subs", "geom", "ids"];
+ const BYTES = (a) => (a ? a.byteLength : 0);
+ export class BufferPool {
+     constructor(maxBytes = 8 << 20) {
+         this.maxBytes = maxBytes;
+         this.bytes = 0;
+         this.takes = 0; this.hits = 0; this.allocBytes = 0;
+         this._free = new Map();
+     }
+     take(ctor, len) {
+         const want = classOf(len * ctor.BYTES_PER_ELEMENT);
+         this.takes++;
+         const list = this._free.get(want);
+         if (list && list.length) {
+             const buf = list.pop();
+             this.bytes -= buf.byteLength;
+             this.hits++;
+             return new ctor(buf, 0, len);
+         }
+         this.allocBytes += want;
+         return new ctor(new ArrayBuffer(want), 0, len);
+     }
+     give(b) {
+         const buf = b && b.buffer ? b.buffer : b;
+         if (!buf || !buf.byteLength) return;
+         if (this.bytes + buf.byteLength > this.maxBytes) return;
+         let list = this._free.get(buf.byteLength);
+         if (!list) { list = []; this._free.set(buf.byteLength, list); }
+         list.push(buf);
+         this.bytes += buf.byteLength;
+     }
+     clear() { this._free.clear(); this.bytes = 0; }
+ }
+ function classOf(n) {
+     let c = 4096;
+     while (c < n) c *= 2;
+     return c;
+ }
+ export function wireJob(job) {
+     validateJob(job);
+     const msg = {
+         kind: "raster-job",
+         jobId: job.jobId, frameSeq: job.frameSeq, postedAt: job.postedAt,
+         q: job.q, Zq: job.Zq, plateW: job.plateW != null ? job.plateW : PLATE_PX,
+         nItems: job.nItems,
+     };
+     const transfer = [];
+     for (const k of JOB_ARRAYS) {
+         const a = job[k];
+         msg[k] = a;
+         if (a && a.buffer && transfer.indexOf(a.buffer) < 0) transfer.push(a.buffer);
+     }
+     return { msg, transfer };
+ }
+ export function unwireJob(msg) {
+     const job = {
+         jobId: msg.jobId, frameSeq: msg.frameSeq, postedAt: msg.postedAt,
+         q: msg.q, Zq: msg.Zq, plateW: msg.plateW, nItems: msg.nItems,
+     };
+     for (const k of JOB_ARRAYS) job[k] = msg[k];
+     validateJob(job);
+     return job;
+ }
+ export function validateJob(job) {
+     const n = job.nItems | 0;
+     if (!(n >= 0)) throw new Error("rasterJob: nItems is not a count");
+     if (job.meta.length < n * META_WORDS) throw new Error("rasterJob: meta is short");
+     if (job.attr.length < n * ATTR_DOUBLES) throw new Error("rasterJob: attr is short");
+     if (job.rgb.length < n * RGB_BYTES) throw new Error("rasterJob: rgb is short");
+     if (job.ids && job.ids.length < n) throw new Error("rasterJob: ids is short");
+     if (job.plates.length % 2) throw new Error("rasterJob: plates is not [i, j, …]");
+     if (job.epochs && job.epochs.length * 2 !== job.plates.length) {
+         throw new Error("rasterJob: one epoch per plate (§5.4)");
+     }
+     let fam = -1;
+     for (let it = 0; it < n; it++) {
+         const m = it * META_WORDS;
+         const kind = job.meta[m], gs = job.meta[m + 2], gl = job.meta[m + 3];
+         const ss = job.meta[m + 4], sc = job.meta[m + 5];
+         if (kind !== KIND_LOOPS && kind !== KIND_RINGS) throw new Error("rasterJob: item " + it + " has kind " + kind);
+         if (gs + gl > job.geom.length) throw new Error("rasterJob: item " + it + " runs past geom");
+         if (ss + sc > job.subs.length) throw new Error("rasterJob: item " + it + " runs past subs");
+         if (sc < 1) throw new Error("rasterJob: item " + it + " has no sub-chain");
+         if (job.meta[m + 1] < fam) throw new Error("rasterJob: family indices must ascend in paint order");
+         fam = job.meta[m + 1];
+         const scale = kind === KIND_LOOPS ? 1 : 2;
+         let prev = -1;
+         for (let k = 0; k < sc; k++) {
+             const off = job.subs[ss + k] * scale;
+             if (off <= prev) throw new Error("rasterJob: item " + it + " sub " + k + " does not ascend (D36)");
+             if (off >= gl) throw new Error("rasterJob: item " + it + " sub " + k + " starts past its geom span");
+             prev = off;
+         }
+     }
+     return job;
+ }
+ export function wireResult(res) {
+     const msg = {
+         kind: "raster-result",
+         jobId: res.jobId, frameSeq: res.frameSeq,
+         plates: res.plates, epochs: res.epochs,
+         bitmaps: res.bitmaps || null, pixels: res.pixels || null,
+         idOff: res.idOff, idSet: res.idSet,
+         timings: res.timings, counts: res.counts, guards: res.guards,
+         recycled: res.recycled || [],
+         serialiseMs: res.serialiseMs || 0,
+     };
+     const transfer = [];
+     const push = (x) => { if (x && transfer.indexOf(x) < 0) transfer.push(x); };
+     for (const k of ["plates", "epochs", "idOff", "idSet", "timings", "counts", "guards"]) {
+         if (msg[k] && msg[k].buffer) push(msg[k].buffer);
+     }
+     if (msg.bitmaps) for (const b of msg.bitmaps) if (b) push(b);
+     if (msg.pixels) for (const p of msg.pixels) if (p) push(p.buffer || p);
+     for (const r of msg.recycled) push(r.buffer || r);
+     return { msg, transfer };
+ }
+ export function unwireResult(msg) {
+     return {
+         jobId: msg.jobId, frameSeq: msg.frameSeq,
+         plates: msg.plates, epochs: msg.epochs,
+         bitmaps: msg.bitmaps, pixels: msg.pixels,
+         idOff: msg.idOff, idSet: msg.idSet,
+         timings: msg.timings, counts: msg.counts, guards: msg.guards,
+         recycled: msg.recycled || [],
+         serialiseMs: msg.serialiseMs || 0,
+     };
+ }
+ export function resultStale(res, epochOf) {
+     const stale = [];
+     for (let p = 0; p * 2 < res.plates.length; p++) {
+         const i = res.plates[p * 2], j = res.plates[p * 2 + 1];
+         if (res.epochs[p] !== epochOf(i, j)) stale.push(p);
+     }
+     return stale.length ? { stale, why: "epoch" } : null;
+ }
+ export function copyPlate(rgba) {
+     return new Uint8ClampedArray(rgba);
+ }
+ export function jobBytes(job) {
+     let n = 0;
+     for (const k of JOB_ARRAYS) n += BYTES(job[k]);
+     return n;
+ }
+ export function resultBytes(res) {
+     let n = BYTES(res.idOff) + BYTES(res.idSet) + BYTES(res.timings) + BYTES(res.counts) + BYTES(res.guards)
+         + BYTES(res.plates) + BYTES(res.epochs);
+     const px = PLATE_PX * PLATE_PX * 4;
+     if (res.pixels) for (const p of res.pixels) { if (p) n += p.byteLength != null ? p.byteLength : px; }
+     else if (res.bitmaps) for (const b of res.bitmaps) if (b) n += px;
+     return n;
+ }
//// src/engine/raster/rasterPack.js  (new file)
+ import { PLATE_PX, QUANTA_PER_OCTAVE } from "./plate";
+ import {
+     META_WORDS, ATTR_DOUBLES, RGB_BYTES, KIND_LOOPS, KIND_RINGS,
+     FLAG_COVERS, FLAG_ERASE, FLAG_UNION, FLAG_MASK, MASKS_MAX,
+ } from "./rasterJob";
+ import { loopBBox, loopsBBox, loopArea } from "../geometry/arcShape";
+ import { strokeStripNear, ringSignedArea, flattenCurve, controlsFor } from "../geometry/polyline";
+ import { flattenLoops, cubicLoopsBBox } from "../geometry/curveOutline";
+ import { sagittaOf } from "../geometry/chordMath";
+ export const ARC_TOL = 1 / 16;
+ export const COORD_BOUND = 2.6e5;
+ const DEFAULT_BUDGET = 10 << 20;
+ export const packStats = {
+     items: 0, packed: 0, culled: 0, faded: 0, rejected: 0,
+     nonFinite: 0, overBound: 0, mixedFamily: 0, mixedWinding: 0,
+     coordsCopied: 0, unionItems: 0, familyUnion: 0, overlapCapped: 0,
+     bandsBuilt: 0, bandHits: 0, boxWalks: 0, rebuilt: 0,
+     prepasses: 0, prepassHits: 0, famRefills: 0, famReused: 0,
+     masked: 0,
+     memoBytes: 0, memoEntries: 0, evictions: 0, logs: 0,
+ };
+ const LOGGED = new Set();
+ function logOnce(what, detail) {
+     packStats.logs++;
+     if (LOGGED.has(what)) return;
+     LOGGED.add(what);
+     console.warn("rasterPack: " + what + " — " + detail);
+ }
+ const MEMO = new Map();
+ let memoBytes = 0;
+ let budget = DEFAULT_BUDGET;
+ function touch(item, e) { MEMO.delete(item); MEMO.set(item, e); }
+ function evict() {
+     while (memoBytes > budget && MEMO.size > 1) {
+         const first = MEMO.keys().next().value;
+         const e = MEMO.get(first);
+         MEMO.delete(first);
+         memoBytes -= e.bytes;
+         e.dropped = true;
+         packStats.evictions++;
+     }
+     packStats.memoBytes = memoBytes;
+     packStats.memoEntries = MEMO.size;
+ }
+ function charge(e, bytes) {
+     if (e.dropped) return;
+     e.bytes += bytes;
+     memoBytes += bytes;
+     evict();
+ }
+ const geomRef = (item) => (item.type === "shape" ? item.loops : item.type === "fill" ? item.polys : item.pts);
+ function build(item, cfg) {
+     const e = {
+         kind: item.type === "shape" ? KIND_LOOPS : KIND_RINGS,
+         box: null, subBox: null, subSign: null, geom: null, subs: null, geomLen: 0,
+         bands: null, union: false, bad: false, maxAbs: 0, area: 0, bytes: 0,
+         ver: item._ver, src: geomRef(item), outline: item._outline, dropped: false,
+         chordQ: null, chordN: 0,
+     };
+     packStats.boxWalks++;
+     if (item.type === "shape") buildShape(item, e);
+     else if (item.type === "fill") buildFill(item, e);
+     else buildStroke(item, e, cfg);
+     if (!e.bad && e.subBox) {
+         e.union = ringsNeedUnion(e.subBox, e.subSign, e.subBox.length / 4);
+         for (let k = 0; k < e.subSign.length; k++) e.area += e.subSign[k];
+     }
+     return e;
+ }
+ function scanNums(nums) {
+     for (let i = 0; i < nums.length; i++) if (!isFinite(nums[i])) return false;
+     return true;
+ }
+ function buildShape(item, e) {
+     const loops = item.loops || [];
+     const n = loops.length;
+     if (!n) { e.bad = true; return; }
+     e.subBox = new Float64Array(4 * n);
+     e.subSign = new Float64Array(n);
+     let len = 0;
+     for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) {
+         const b = loopBBox(loops[k]);
+         if (!b) { e.bad = true; return; }
+         e.subBox[k * 4] = b.x0; e.subBox[k * 4 + 1] = b.y0;
+         e.subBox[k * 4 + 2] = b.x1; e.subBox[k * 4 + 3] = b.y1;
+         e.subSign[k] = loopArea(loops[k]);
+         const nums = numbersOf(loops[k]);
+         if (!scanNums(nums)) { e.bad = true; return; }
+         len += nums.length;
+     }
+     const bb = loopsBBox(loops);
+     if (!bb) { e.bad = true; return; }
+     e.box = Float64Array.from([bb.x0, bb.y0, bb.x1, bb.y1]);
+     e.geomLen = len;
+     finishEntry(e);
+ }
+ function numbersOf(loop) {
+     if (loop && loop.buf) return loop.buf;
+     if (loop instanceof Float64Array) return loop;
+     throw new Error("rasterPack: a shape's loop is neither a Loop nor a Float64Array");
+ }
+ function buildFill(item, e) {
+     const rings = item.polys || [];
+     const n = rings.length;
+     if (!n) { e.bad = true; return; }
+     e.subBox = new Float64Array(4 * n);
+     e.subSign = new Float64Array(n);
+     let pts = 0;
+     let x0 = Infinity, y0 = Infinity, x1 = -Infinity, y1 = -Infinity;
+     for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) {
+         const ring = rings[k];
+         let rx0 = Infinity, ry0 = Infinity, rx1 = -Infinity, ry1 = -Infinity;
+         for (const p of ring) {
+             if (!isFinite(p[0]) || !isFinite(p[1])) { e.bad = true; return; }
+             if (p[0] < rx0) rx0 = p[0];
+             if (p[0] > rx1) rx1 = p[0];
+             if (p[1] < ry0) ry0 = p[1];
+             if (p[1] > ry1) ry1 = p[1];
+         }
+         pts += ring.length;
+         e.subBox[k * 4] = rx0; e.subBox[k * 4 + 1] = ry0;
+         e.subBox[k * 4 + 2] = rx1; e.subBox[k * 4 + 3] = ry1;
+         e.subSign[k] = ringSignedArea(ring);
+         if (rx0 < x0) x0 = rx0;
+         if (ry0 < y0) y0 = ry0;
+         if (rx1 > x1) x1 = rx1;
+         if (ry1 > y1) y1 = ry1;
+     }
+     e.box = Float64Array.from([x0, y0, x1, y1]);
+     e.geomLen = pts * 2;
+     finishEntry(e);
+ }
+ function buildStroke(item, e, cfg) {
+     const pts = item.pts || [];
+     if (!pts.length) { e.bad = true; return; }
+     let x0 = Infinity, y0 = Infinity, x1 = -Infinity, y1 = -Infinity;
+     const take = (p) => {
+         if (p[0] < x0) x0 = p[0];
+         if (p[0] > x1) x1 = p[0];
+         if (p[1] < y0) y0 = p[1];
+         if (p[1] > y1) y1 = p[1];
+     };
+     for (const p of pts) take(p);
+     if (strokeCurved(item)) {
+         const n = pts.length;
+         for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
+             const c = controlsFor(pts[Math.max(i - 1, 0)], pts[i], pts[Math.min(i + 1, n - 1)]);
+             take(c.left); take(c.right);
+         }
+     }
+     const half = (item.lwFrame || 0) / 2;
+     const fit = cfg && cfg.arcTolerancePx && cfg.enter ? cfg.arcTolerancePx / cfg.enter : 0;
+     const pad = half + fit;
+     if (!isFinite(pad)) { e.bad = true; return; }
+     x0 -= pad; y0 -= pad; x1 += pad; y1 += pad;
+     if (item._outline) {
+         const h = cubicLoopsBBox(item._outline);
+         if (h.x0 < x0) x0 = h.x0;
+         if (h.y0 < y0) y0 = h.y0;
+         if (h.x1 > x1) x1 = h.x1;
+         if (h.y1 > y1) y1 = h.y1;
+     }
+     e.box = Float64Array.from([x0, y0, x1, y1]);
+     e.subBox = null; e.subSign = null;
+     e.bands = new Map();
+     e.union = true;
+     for (const p of pts) {
+         if (!isFinite(p[0]) || !isFinite(p[1])) { e.bad = true; return; }
+         const m = Math.max(Math.abs(p[0]), Math.abs(p[1]));
+         if (m > e.maxAbs) e.maxAbs = m;
+     }
+     charge(e, 32 + pts.length * 16);
+ }
+ function finishEntry(e) {
+     const b = e.box;
+     for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
+         if (!isFinite(b[i])) { e.bad = true; return; }
+         const m = Math.abs(b[i]);
+         if (m > e.maxAbs) e.maxAbs = m;
+     }
+     charge(e, (e.subBox ? e.subBox.byteLength : 0) + (e.subSign ? e.subSign.byteLength : 0) + 64);
+ }
+ function flattenGeom(e, item) {
+     if (e.geom) return;
+     const geom = new Float64Array(e.geomLen);
+     let subs, at = 0;
+     if (e.kind === KIND_LOOPS) {
+         const loops = item.loops || [];
+         subs = new Uint32Array(loops.length);
+         for (let k = 0; k < loops.length; k++) {
+             const nums = numbersOf(loops[k]);
+             subs[k] = at;
+             geom.set(nums, at);
+             at += nums.length;
+         }
+     } else {
+         const rings = item.polys || [];
+         subs = new Uint32Array(rings.length);
+         for (let k = 0; k < rings.length; k++) {
+             subs[k] = at;
+             for (const p of rings[k]) { geom[at * 2] = p[0]; geom[at * 2 + 1] = p[1]; at++; }
+         }
+     }
+     e.geom = geom; e.subs = subs;
+     packStats.coordsCopied += geom.length;
+     charge(e, geom.byteLength + subs.byteLength);
+ }
+ export function ringsNeedUnion(boxes, signs, n) {
+     if (n < 2) return false;
+     let tests = 0;
+     for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
+         for (let j = i + 1; j < n; j++) {
+             if (++tests > 20000) { packStats.overlapCapped++; return true; }
+             if (pairNeedsUnion(boxes, i, signs[i], boxes, j, signs[j])) return true;
+         }
+     }
+     return false;
+ }
+ function boxesOverlap(a, i, b, j) {
+     return a[i * 4] <= b[j * 4 + 2] && b[j * 4] <= a[i * 4 + 2]
+         && a[i * 4 + 1] <= b[j * 4 + 3] && b[j * 4 + 1] <= a[i * 4 + 3];
+ }
+ const boxContains = (a, i, b, j) => a[i * 4] <= b[j * 4] && a[i * 4 + 1] <= b[j * 4 + 1]
+     && a[i * 4 + 2] >= b[j * 4 + 2] && a[i * 4 + 3] >= b[j * 4 + 3];
+ function pairNeedsUnion(ba, i, sa, bb, j, sb) {
+     if (!boxesOverlap(ba, i, bb, j)) return false;
+     if (sa === 0 || sb === 0 || (sa > 0) === (sb > 0)) return true;
+     return !(boxContains(ba, i, bb, j) || boxContains(bb, j, ba, i));
+ }
+ export function entryFor(item, cfg) {
+     let e = MEMO.get(item);
+     if (e && (e.ver !== item._ver || e.src !== geomRef(item) || e.outline !== item._outline)) {
+         MEMO.delete(item);
+         memoBytes -= e.bytes;
+         e.dropped = true;
+         packStats.rebuilt++;
+         e = null;
+     }
+     if (e) { touch(item, e); return e; }
+     e = build(item, cfg);
+     MEMO.set(item, e);
+     packStats.memoEntries = MEMO.size;
+     evict();
+     return e;
+ }
+ export const STROKE_CHORD_MULT = 12;
+ const CURVE_CHORD_MULT = 8;
+ function arcChords(ax, ay, bx, by, sweep, tol) {
+     const th = Math.abs(sweep);
+     if (!(th > 0) || !isFinite(th)) return 1;
+     if (sagittaOf(ax, ay, bx, by, sweep) <= tol) return 1;
+     const forced = th <= Math.PI / 2 ? 0 : th <= Math.PI ? 1 : 2;
+     const dx = bx - ax, dy = by - ay;
+     const L = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
+     let d = forced;
+     if (L > 0) {
+         const u = 1 - (2 * tol * Math.sin(Math.min(th, Math.PI) / 2)) / L;
+         const n = u > -1 && u < 1 ? th / (2 * Math.acos(u)) : 1;
+         if (isFinite(n) && n > 1) d = Math.max(forced, Math.ceil(Math.log2(n)));
+     }
+     return Math.pow(2, Math.min(d, 24));
+ }
+ function itemChords(item, Z, cfg, e) {
+     if (e.bad) return 0;
+     const qz = Math.round(Math.log2(Z) * QUANTA_PER_OCTAVE);
+     if (e.chordQ === qz) return e.chordN;
+     let n = 0;
+     if (e.bands) {
+         if (!item._outline && strokeCurved(item)) n = flattenCurve(item.pts, ARC_TOL / Z).length * CURVE_CHORD_MULT;
+         else n = (item.pts ? item.pts.length : 0) * STROKE_CHORD_MULT;
+         n *= Math.max(1, ((item.lwFrame || 0) * Z) / 32);
+     } else if (e.kind === KIND_LOOPS) {
+         const tol = ARC_TOL / Z;
+         for (const loop of item.loops || []) {
+             for (const p of loop) n += p.line ? 1 : arcChords(p.A[0], p.A[1], p.B[0], p.B[1], p.sweep, tol);
+         }
+     } else {
+         for (const ring of item.polys || []) n += ring.length;
+     }
+     e.chordQ = qz; e.chordN = n;
+     return n;
+ }
+ export function visibleChords(list, win, Z, cfg, cap, live = null) {
+     if (!(Z > 0) || !isFinite(Z)) return cap + 1;
+     let n = 0;
+     for (let k = 0; k < list.length; k++) {
+         const o = list[k];
+         if (o === live) continue;
+         const e = entryFor(o, cfg);
+         if (e.bad) continue;
+         const b = e.box, r = o.res;
+         const x0 = r ? b[0] + r[0] : b[0], y0 = r ? b[1] + r[1] : b[1];
+         const x1 = r ? b[2] + r[0] : b[2], y1 = r ? b[3] + r[1] : b[3];
+         if (x1 < win.left || x0 > win.right || y1 < win.top || y0 > win.bottom) continue;
+         n += itemChords(o, Z, cfg, e);
+         if (n > cap) return cap + 1;
+     }
+     return n;
+ }
+ export function bandFor(item, q, Zq, cfg) {
+     const e = entryFor(item, cfg);
+     if (e.bad) return null;
+     if (!e.bands) return null;
+     const key = Math.round(Math.log2(Zq) * QUANTA_PER_OCTAVE);
+     const had = e.bands.get(key);
+     if (had) { packStats.bandHits++; return had; }
+     for (const [k, band] of e.bands) {
+         if (k > key) { packStats.bandHits++; return band; }
+     }
+     const tol = ARC_TOL / Zq;
+     let band;
+     if (item._outline) {
+         const polys = flattenLoops(item._outline, tol);
+         band = flatten(polys);
+     } else {
+         const line = strokeCurved(item) ? flattenCurve(item.pts, tol) : item.pts;
+         const r = strokeStripNear(line, item.lwFrame, null, { joins: "round", tol });
+         band = fromWriter(r);
+     }
+     if (!band) return null;
+     packStats.bandsBuilt++;
+     if (e.bands.size >= 2) {
+         const oldest = e.bands.keys().next().value;
+         const drop = e.bands.get(oldest);
+         e.bands.delete(oldest);
+         charge(e, -(drop.geom.byteLength + drop.subs.byteLength));
+     }
+     e.bands.set(key, band);
+     charge(e, band.geom.byteLength + band.subs.byteLength);
+     return band;
+ }
+ function strokeCurved(item) { return item.origin === "native" && item.pts.length > 2; }
+ function flatten(polys) {
+     let n = 0;
+     for (const p of polys) n += p.length;
+     if (!n) return null;
+     const geom = new Float64Array(n * 2), subs = new Uint32Array(polys.length);
+     let at = 0, area = 0, x0 = Infinity, y0 = Infinity, x1 = -Infinity, y1 = -Infinity;
+     for (let k = 0; k < polys.length; k++) {
+         subs[k] = at;
+         const ring = polys[k], a = ringSignedArea(ring), back = a < 0;
+         area += back ? -a : a;
+         for (let i = 0; i < ring.length; i++) {
+             const p = ring[back ? ring.length - 1 - i : i];
+             geom[at * 2] = p[0]; geom[at * 2 + 1] = p[1]; at++;
+             if (p[0] < x0) x0 = p[0];
+             if (p[0] > x1) x1 = p[0];
+             if (p[1] < y0) y0 = p[1];
+             if (p[1] > y1) y1 = p[1];
+         }
+     }
+     return { geom, subs, box: Float64Array.from([x0, y0, x1, y1]), area };
+ }
+ function fromWriter(r) {
+     if (!r.pts || !r.rings) return null;
+     const geom = r.xy.slice(0, r.pts * 2), subs = r.ring.slice(0, r.rings);
+     let x0 = Infinity, y0 = Infinity, x1 = -Infinity, y1 = -Infinity;
+     for (let i = 0; i < r.pts; i++) {
+         const x = geom[i * 2], y = geom[i * 2 + 1];
+         if (x < x0) x0 = x;
+         if (x > x1) x1 = x;
+         if (y < y0) y0 = y;
+         if (y > y1) y1 = y;
+     }
+     return { geom, subs, box: Float64Array.from([x0, y0, x1, y1]), area: r.area };
+ }
+ const COLOURS = new Map();
+ const NAMED = { black: [0, 0, 0], white: [255, 255, 255], red: [255, 0, 0], green: [0, 128, 0], blue: [0, 0, 255], transparent: [0, 0, 0] };
+ export function parseColour(css) {
+     if (css == null) return [0, 0, 0];
+     const had = COLOURS.get(css);
+     if (had) return had;
+     let out = null;
+     const s = String(css).trim().toLowerCase();
+     if (s[0] === "#") {
+         const h = s.slice(1);
+         if (h.length === 3 || h.length === 4) {
+             out = [parseInt(h[0] + h[0], 16), parseInt(h[1] + h[1], 16), parseInt(h[2] + h[2], 16)];
+         } else if (h.length === 6 || h.length === 8) {
+             out = [parseInt(h.slice(0, 2), 16), parseInt(h.slice(2, 4), 16), parseInt(h.slice(4, 6), 16)];
+         }
+     } else if (s.startsWith("rgb")) {
+         const m = s.match(/-?[\d.]+/g);
+         if (m && m.length >= 3) out = [clamp255(+m[0]), clamp255(+m[1]), clamp255(+m[2])];
+     } else if (NAMED[s]) {
+         out = NAMED[s].slice();
+     }
+     if (!out || out.some((v) => !isFinite(v))) {
+         logOnce("unparseable colour", JSON.stringify(css) + " painted black");
+         out = [0, 0, 0];
+     }
+     COLOURS.set(css, out);
+     return out;
+ }
+ const clamp255 = (v) => Math.max(0, Math.min(255, Math.round(v)));
+ export function fadeQ(item, Zq, dpr, cfg) {
+     if (item.fadeTag == null) return 1;
+     const px = (item.fadeTag * (Zq / dpr)) / cfg.enter;
+     if (!isFinite(px)) return 1;
+     const lo = cfg.fadeLoPx != null ? cfg.fadeLoPx : 0.15;
+     const hi = cfg.cullPx != null ? cfg.cullPx : 0.3;
+     return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (px - lo) / (hi - lo)));
+ }
+ export function renderIdOf(item, fade) {
+     return item.editId != null && fade >= 1 ? item.editId : item.id;
+ }
+ export class PlateBinner {
+     constructor(plates, Zq) {
+         this.Zq = Zq;
+         this.cells = new Map();
+         this.i0 = Infinity; this.i1 = -Infinity; this.j0 = Infinity; this.j1 = -Infinity;
+         for (let p = 0; p * 2 < plates.length; p++) {
+             const i = plates[p * 2], j = plates[p * 2 + 1];
+             this.cells.set(i + "," + j, []);
+             if (i < this.i0) this.i0 = i;
+             if (i > this.i1) this.i1 = i;
+             if (j < this.j0) this.j0 = j;
+             if (j > this.j1) this.j1 = j;
+         }
+     }
+     add(index, box) {
+         const s = this.Zq / PLATE_PX;
+         let i0 = Math.floor(box[0] * s), i1 = Math.floor(box[2] * s);
+         let j0 = Math.floor(box[1] * s), j1 = Math.floor(box[3] * s);
+         if (i1 < this.i0 || i0 > this.i1 || j1 < this.j0 || j0 > this.j1) return false;
+         i0 = Math.max(i0, this.i0); i1 = Math.min(i1, this.i1);
+         j0 = Math.max(j0, this.j0); j1 = Math.min(j1, this.j1);
+         let hit = false;
+         for (let j = j0; j <= j1; j++) {
+             for (let i = i0; i <= i1; i++) {
+                 const c = this.cells.get(i + "," + j);
+                 if (c) { c.push(index); hit = true; }
+             }
+         }
+         return hit;
+     }
+ }
+ const alloc = (pool, ctor, len) => (pool ? pool.take(ctor, len) : new ctor(len));
+ let PREPASS = new WeakMap();
+ const PREPASS_KEYS = 4;
+ let FAMS = new Map();
+ const FAMS_KEYS = 8;
+ const IDS = new WeakMap();
+ let idSeq = 0;
+ const idOf = (x) => { if (x == null) return 0; let v = IDS.get(x); if (!v) IDS.set(x, (v = ++idSeq)); return v; };
+ function prepassFor(list, q, Zq, dpr, cfg, live, only, omit, offset) {
+     const key = q + "|" + Zq + "|" + dpr + "|" + idOf(cfg) + "|" + idOf(live) + "|" + idOf(only) + "|" + idOf(omit)
+         + "|" + (offset ? offset[0] + "," + offset[1] : "");
+     let m = PREPASS.get(list);
+     if (!m) PREPASS.set(list, (m = new Map()));
+     const hit = m.get(key);
+     if (hit) { packStats.prepassHits++; return hit; }
+     packStats.prepasses++;
+     if (m.size >= PREPASS_KEYS) m.delete(m.keys().next().value);
+     const fams = new Map();
+     let faded = 0;
+     for (let k = 0; k < list.length; k++) {
+         const o = list[k];
+         if (o === live) continue;
+         if (only && !only.has(o.id) && !o.erase) continue;
+         if (omit && omit.has(o.id) && !o.erase) continue;
+         const fade = fadeQ(o, Zq, dpr, cfg);
+         if (fade <= 0) { faded++; continue; }
+         const rid = renderIdOf(o, fade);
+         let f = fams.get(rid);
+         if (!f) { f = { rid, z: o.z != null ? o.z : rid, items: [], all: null, isFam: o.editId != null && rid === o.editId, union: null }; fams.set(rid, f); }
+         f.items.push({ o, fade, idx: k, e: null, res: null });
+     }
+     const order = Array.from(fams.values()).sort((a, b) => (a.z - b.z) || (a.rid - b.rid));
+     const last = FAMS.get(key);
+     for (const f of order) {
+         const g = last && last.get(f.rid);
+         if (g && sameFamily(g, f)) { famTake(f, g); packStats.famReused++; } else famFill(f, cfg, offset);
+     }
+     FAMS.delete(key);
+     FAMS.set(key, fams);
+     if (FAMS.size > FAMS_KEYS) FAMS.delete(FAMS.keys().next().value);
+     const pre = { order, faded, n: list.length };
+     m.set(key, pre);
+     return pre;
+ }
+ function sameFamily(g, f) {
+     if (!g.all || g.items.length !== f.items.length) return false;
+     for (let n = 0; n < f.items.length; n++) {
+         const a = f.items[n], b = g.items[n], o = a.o, e = b.e;
+         if (o !== b.o || a.fade !== b.fade || o.z !== b.z || o.editId !== b.editId || !!o.erase !== b.erase) return false;
+         if (!e || e.dropped || e.ver !== o._ver || e.src !== geomRef(o) || e.outline !== o._outline) return false;
+         const r = o.res || null, s = b.res;
+         if (r !== s && !(r && s && r[0] === s[0] && r[1] === s[1])) return false;
+     }
+     return true;
+ }
+ function famTake(f, g) {
+     for (let n = 0; n < f.items.length; n++) {
+         const m = f.items[n], b = g.items[n];
+         m.e = b.e; m.res = m.o.res || null; m.z = b.z; m.editId = b.editId; m.erase = b.erase;
+     }
+     f.all = []; f.rejected = g.rejected; f.over = g.over; f.union = g.union; f.unionSign = g.unionSign; f.mixed = g.mixed;
+     let n = 0;
+     for (const d of g.all) {
+         while (f.items[n].o !== d.o) n++;
+         const m = f.items[n];
+         f.all.push({ o: d.o, e: d.e, fade: d.fade, box: d.box, res: m.res, idx: m.idx });
+     }
+ }
+ function famFill(f, cfg, offset) {
+     f.all = []; f.rejected = 0; f.over = 0; f.union = null; f.unionSign = 0; f.mixed = 0;
+     for (const m of f.items) {
+         const e = entryFor(m.o, cfg);
+         m.e = e; m.res = m.o.res || null;
+         m.z = m.o.z; m.editId = m.o.editId; m.erase = !!m.o.erase;
+         if (e.bad) {
+             f.rejected++;
+             logOnce("a non-finite coordinate", "item id " + m.o.id + " (" + m.o.type + ") refused; it paints nothing");
+             continue;
+         }
+         if (e.maxAbs > COORD_BOUND) {
+             f.over++;
+             logOnce("a render-list coordinate past the bound", "item id " + m.o.id + " reaches " + e.maxAbs.toExponential(2)
+                 + " frame units, over §4.6's " + COORD_BOUND.toExponential(1) + "; the plate stays float64-exact to ~1.5e-8 px below it");
+         }
+         const own = m.res;
+         const res = offset ? [(own ? own[0] : 0) + offset[0], (own ? own[1] : 0) + offset[1]] : own;
+         f.all.push({ o: m.o, e, fade: m.fade, box: boxWithRes(e.box, res), res, idx: m.idx });
+     }
+ }
+ export function paintFamilies(list, q, Zq, dpr, cfg, live = null) {
+     const pre = prepassFor(list, q, Zq, dpr, cfg, live, null, null, null);
+     for (const f of pre.order) if (famStale(f)) { famFill(f, cfg, null); packStats.famRefills++; }
+     return pre.order;
+ }
+ function famStale(f) {
+     for (const m of f.items) {
+         const o = m.o, e = m.e;
+         if (e.dropped || e.ver !== o._ver || e.src !== geomRef(o) || e.outline !== o._outline || (o.res || null) !== m.res) return true;
+     }
+     return false;
+ }
+ export function packJob(list, opts) {
+     const { plates, q = 0, Zq, dpr = 1, cfg, paper = [255, 255, 255], pool = null, live = null, offset = null, only = null, omit = null, markDone = null, markZ = null } = opts;
+     const binner = new PlateBinner(plates, Zq);
+     const pre = prepassFor(list, q, Zq, dpr, cfg, live, only, omit, offset);
+     packStats.items += pre.n;
+     packStats.faded += pre.faded;
+     const kept = [];
+     for (const f of pre.order) {
+         if (famStale(f)) { famFill(f, cfg, offset); packStats.famRefills++; }
+         packStats.rejected += f.rejected; packStats.nonFinite += f.rejected; packStats.overBound += f.over;
+         const all = f.all;
+         if (!all.length) continue;
+         const drawn = [];
+         for (const d of all) {
+             if (!binner.add(d.idx, d.box)) { packStats.culled++; continue; }
+             drawn.push(d);
+         }
+         if (!drawn.length) continue;
+         if (f.union === null) { f.union = familyUnion(all); f.unionSign = unionSignOf(all, f, q, Zq, cfg); }
+         if (f.union) packStats.familyUnion++;
+         packStats.mixedWinding += f.mixed;
+         for (const d of drawn) { d.fam = f; kept.push(d); }
+     }
+     if (!kept.length) return emptyJob(opts, plates, paper, pool);
+     let nGeom = 0, nSubs = 0;
+     for (const d of kept) {
+         const g = geomOf(d, q, Zq, cfg);
+         d.skip = !g;
+         if (!g) { packStats.rejected++; continue; }
+         d.g = g;
+         nGeom += g.geom.length;
+         nSubs += g.subs.length;
+     }
+     const items = kept.filter((d) => !d.skip);
+     if (!items.length) return emptyJob(opts, plates, paper, pool);
+     const markOf = new Map();
+     for (const d of items) {
+         if (!d.o.erase) continue;
+         let M = markOf.get(d.fam);
+         if (!M) {
+             const z = markZ ? markZ(d.o.id) : null;
+             markOf.set(d.fam, (M = { k: Math.min(markOf.size, MASKS_MAX - 1), z: z != null ? z : d.fam.z, done: markDone ? markDone(d.o.id) : null, box: [Infinity, Infinity, -Infinity, -Infinity] }));
+         }
+         const b = d.g.box ? boxWithRes(d.g.box, d.res) : d.box;
+         if (b[0] < M.box[0]) M.box[0] = b[0];
+         if (b[1] < M.box[1]) M.box[1] = b[1];
+         if (b[2] > M.box[2]) M.box[2] = b[2];
+         if (b[3] > M.box[3]) M.box[3] = b[3];
+     }
+     const marks = [...markOf.values()];
+     const n = items.length;
+     const meta = alloc(pool, Uint32Array, n * META_WORDS);
+     const attr = alloc(pool, Float64Array, n * ATTR_DOUBLES);
+     const rgb = alloc(pool, Uint8Array, n * RGB_BYTES);
+     const subs = alloc(pool, Uint32Array, nSubs);
+     const geom = alloc(pool, Float64Array, nGeom);
+     const ids = alloc(pool, Int32Array, n);
+     let famIndex = -1, lastFam = null, gAt = 0, sAt = 0;
+     for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) {
+         const d = items[k], o = d.o, f = d.fam;
+         if (f !== lastFam) { famIndex++; lastFam = f; f.rgbv = parseColour(o.color); f.alpha = alphaOf(o, f, d.fade); }
+         const alpha = alphaOf(o, f, d.fade);
+         const col = parseColour(o.color);
+         if (alpha !== f.alpha || col[0] !== f.rgbv[0] || col[1] !== f.rgbv[1] || col[2] !== f.rgbv[2]) {
+             packStats.mixedFamily++;
+             logOnce("a family whose items disagree on colour or alpha",
+                 "family " + f.rid + ": the core paints the whole family with the first drawn item's, §3.3");
+         }
+         const m = k * META_WORDS;
+         meta[m] = d.g.kind;
+         meta[m + 1] = famIndex;
+         meta[m + 2] = gAt;
+         meta[m + 3] = d.g.geom.length;
+         meta[m + 4] = sAt;
+         meta[m + 5] = d.g.subs.length;
+         const isUnion = f.union || d.e.union;
+         const box = d.g.box ? boxWithRes(d.g.box, d.res) : d.box;
+         const M = o.erase ? markOf.get(f) : null;
+         let bits = 0;
+         if (!M) {
+             for (const K of marks) {
+                 if (f.z < K.z && !(K.done && K.done.has(o.id)) && box[0] <= K.box[2] && box[2] >= K.box[0] && box[1] <= K.box[3] && box[3] >= K.box[1]) bits |= 1 << K.k;
+             }
+         }
+         meta[m + 6] = (o.covers ? FLAG_COVERS : 0) | (o.erase ? FLAG_ERASE : 0) | (isUnion ? FLAG_UNION : 0) | (M ? FLAG_MASK : 0);
+         meta[m + 7] = M ? M.k : bits;
+         if (isUnion) packStats.unionItems++;
+         if (bits) packStats.masked++;
+         const a = k * ATTR_DOUBLES;
+         attr[a] = box[0]; attr[a + 1] = box[1]; attr[a + 2] = box[2]; attr[a + 3] = box[3];
+         attr[a + 4] = d.res ? d.res[0] : 0;
+         attr[a + 5] = d.res ? d.res[1] : 0;
+         attr[a + 6] = o.lwFrame || 0;
+         attr[a + 7] = alpha;
+         attr[a + 8] = isUnion ? f.unionSign : 0;
+         rgb[k * 3] = f.rgbv[0]; rgb[k * 3 + 1] = f.rgbv[1]; rgb[k * 3 + 2] = f.rgbv[2];
+         ids[k] = o.id | 0;
+         geom.set(d.g.geom, gAt);
+         for (let s = 0; s < d.g.subs.length; s++) subs[sAt + s] = d.g.subs[s];
+         gAt += d.g.geom.length;
+         sAt += d.g.subs.length;
+         packStats.packed++;
+     }
+     return {
+         jobId: opts.jobId != null ? opts.jobId : 0,
+         frameSeq: opts.frameSeq != null ? opts.frameSeq : 0,
+         postedAt: opts.postedAt != null ? opts.postedAt : 0,
+         epochs: opts.epochs ? Int32Array.from(opts.epochs) : new Int32Array(plates.length / 2),
+         q, Zq, plateW: PLATE_PX,
+         plates: Int32Array.from(plates),
+         paper: Uint8Array.from(paper),
+         nItems: n, meta, attr, rgb, subs, geom, ids,
+     };
+ }
+ function emptyJob(opts, plates, paper, pool) {
+     return {
+         jobId: opts.jobId != null ? opts.jobId : 0,
+         frameSeq: opts.frameSeq != null ? opts.frameSeq : 0,
+         postedAt: opts.postedAt != null ? opts.postedAt : 0,
+         epochs: opts.epochs ? Int32Array.from(opts.epochs) : new Int32Array(plates.length / 2),
+         q: opts.q || 0, Zq: opts.Zq, plateW: PLATE_PX,
+         plates: Int32Array.from(plates),
+         paper: Uint8Array.from(paper),
+         nItems: 0,
+         meta: alloc(pool, Uint32Array, 0), attr: alloc(pool, Float64Array, 0), rgb: alloc(pool, Uint8Array, 0),
+         subs: alloc(pool, Uint32Array, 0), geom: alloc(pool, Float64Array, 0), ids: alloc(pool, Int32Array, 0),
+     };
+ }
+ function alphaOf(o, f, fade) {
+     const op = o.opacity == null ? 1 : o.opacity;
+     return f.isFam ? op : op * fade;
+ }
+ const boxWithRes = (b, res) => (res ? Float64Array.from([b[0] + res[0], b[1] + res[1], b[2] + res[0], b[3] + res[1]]) : b);
+ function familyUnion(drawn) {
+     const boxes = [], signs = [], owner = [];
+     for (let k = 0; k < drawn.length; k++) {
+         const e = drawn[k].e;
+         if (!e.subBox) continue;
+         const res = drawn[k].res;
+         for (let s = 0; s < e.subSign.length; s++) {
+             boxes.push(res ? [e.subBox[s * 4] + res[0], e.subBox[s * 4 + 1] + res[1], e.subBox[s * 4 + 2] + res[0], e.subBox[s * 4 + 3] + res[1]]
+                 : [e.subBox[s * 4], e.subBox[s * 4 + 1], e.subBox[s * 4 + 2], e.subBox[s * 4 + 3]]);
+             signs.push(e.subSign[s]);
+             owner.push(k);
+         }
+     }
+     const flat = new Float64Array(boxes.length * 4);
+     for (let i = 0; i < boxes.length; i++) flat.set(boxes[i], i * 4);
+     const pos = [], neg = [];
+     for (let k = 0; k < boxes.length; k++) {
+         if (signs[k] >= 0) pos.push(k);
+         if (signs[k] <= 0) neg.push(k);
+     }
+     let tests = 0;
+     for (const i of pos) {
+         for (const j of neg) {
+             if (owner[i] === owner[j]) continue;
+             if (++tests > 20000) { packStats.overlapCapped++; return true; }
+             if (pairNeedsUnion(flat, i, signs[i], flat, j, signs[j])) return true;
+         }
+     }
+     return false;
+ }
+ function unionSignOf(all, f, q, Zq, cfg) {
+     let sum = 0, tot = 0, pos = 0, neg = 0;
+     for (const d of all) {
+         const a = unionAreaOf(d, q, Zq, cfg);
+         if (f.union || d.e.union) sum += a;
+         tot += a;
+         if (a > 0) pos++;
+         else if (a < 0) neg++;
+     }
+     f.mixed = 0;
+     if (pos && neg) {
+         const mixed = tot < 0 ? pos : neg;
+         f.mixed = mixed;
+         logOnce("a family whose items are wound against each other",
+             "family " + f.rid + ": " + mixed + " of " + (pos + neg) + " items are wound against its total; "
+             + "where two of them overlap the ink cancels to a hole, which no declared sign repairs (§4.2)");
+     }
+     return sum > 0 ? 1 : -1;
+ }
+ function unionAreaOf(d, q, Zq, cfg) {
+     if (!d.e.bands) return d.e.area;
+     const band = bandFor(d.o, q, Zq, cfg);
+     return band ? band.area : 0;
+ }
+ function geomOf(d, q, Zq, cfg) {
+     const e = d.e;
+     if (e.bands) {
+         const band = bandFor(d.o, q, Zq, cfg);
+         return band ? { kind: KIND_RINGS, geom: band.geom, subs: band.subs, box: band.box } : null;
+     }
+     if (!e.geomLen) return null;
+     flattenGeom(e, d.o);
+     return { kind: e.kind, geom: e.geom, subs: e.subs, box: null };
+ }
//// src/engine/raster/rasterPerf.js  (new file)
+ import { perfNow } from "../now";
+ export const ROWS = 400;
+ export const STRIDE = 40;
+ const TIMER_DEFS = [
+     ["eng.list", "ENGINE", "main"],
+     ["eng.settle", "ENGINE", "main"],
+     ["eng.cull", "ENGINE", "main"],
+     ["eng.band", "ENGINE", "main"],
+     ["eng.pack", "ENGINE", "main"],
+     ["eng.view", "ENGINE", "main"],
+     ["eng.lookup", "ENGINE", "main"],
+     ["eng.clip", "ENGINE", "worker"],
+     ["eng.local", "ENGINE", "worker"],
+     ["ras.edges", "RASTERISER", "worker"],
+     ["ras.union", "RASTERISER", "worker"],
+     ["ras.accum", "RASTERISER", "worker"],
+     ["ras.composite", "RASTERISER", "worker"],
+     ["xfer.out", "TRANSFER", "main"],
+     ["xfer.in", "TRANSFER", "main"],
+     ["xfer.serialise", "TRANSFER", "worker"],
+     ["br.bitmap", "BROWSER", "wall"],
+     ["br.blit", "BROWSER", "main"],
+     ["br.inferred", "BROWSER", "inferred"],
+     ["ovl.decide", "OVERLAY", "main"],
+     ["ovl.paint", "OVERLAY", "main"],
+     ["ovl.blit", "OVERLAY", "main"],
+     ["ovl.tail", "OVERLAY", "main"],
+     ["ovl.live", "OVERLAY", "main"],
+     ["queueWait", "TRANSFER", "wall"],
+     ["queueing", "TRANSFER", "wall"],
+     ["job.recvToStart", "RASTERISER", "wall"],
+     ["job.startToFinish", "RASTERISER", "wall"],
+     ["mainMs", "", "total"],
+     ["wallMs", "", "total"],
+     ["residualMain", "", "total"],
+     ["residualWall", "", "total"],
+     ["rafDelta", "", "total"],
+ ];
+ const COUNT_DEFS = [
+     "frameSeq", "kind", "mode", "q", "sMilli", "flags",
+     "platesWanted", "hits", "misses", "plateBlits",
+     "jobsPosted", "jobsArrived", "platesArrived", "bytesOut", "bytesIn", "drops",
+     "items", "itemsDrawn", "families", "unionItems", "edges", "chords", "rowsTouched", "coveredPx",
+     "antDashes", "antStamps", "antPixels", "overlayBlitPx", "overlayRepaints",
+     "liveMoves", "liveDirtyPx",
+ ];
+ export const T = {};
+ export const C = {};
+ export const TIMERS = TIMER_DEFS.map((d) => d[0]);
+ export const COUNTS = COUNT_DEFS.slice();
+ TIMER_DEFS.forEach((d, i) => { T[d[0]] = i; });
+ COUNT_DEFS.forEach((n, i) => { C[n] = i; });
+ if (TIMERS.length > STRIDE || COUNTS.length > STRIDE) {
+     throw new Error("rasterPerf: more names than slots");
+ }
+ const MAIN_PART = TIMER_DEFS.filter((d) => d[2] === "main").map((d) => T[d[0]]);
+ const SYNC_PART = TIMER_DEFS.filter((d) => d[2] === "worker").map((d) => T[d[0]]);
+ const WALL_PART = [T["queueWait"], T["queueing"], T["job.recvToStart"], T["job.startToFinish"],
+     T["xfer.serialise"], T["br.bitmap"], T["br.blit"]];
+ const PLATE_PART = [T["job.startToFinish"], T["eng.clip"], T["eng.local"], T["ras.edges"],
+     T["ras.union"], T["ras.accum"]];
+ const KINDS = ["render", "camera", "arrival", "crawl", "settle"];
+ const K_RENDER = KINDS.indexOf("render");
+ const K_ARRIVAL = KINDS.indexOf("arrival");
+ const K_CRAWL = KINDS.indexOf("crawl");
+ const MODES = ["unknown", "sync", "plates", "settle"];
+ const Q_SYNC = -1000, Q_SETTLE = -1001, Q_UNKNOWN = -1002;
+ const FLAG = {
+     inferredPending: 1,
+     rafKnown: 2,
+     shared: 4,
+     spansFrames: 8,
+     clamped: 16,
+     rasOnMain: 32,
+     truncated: 64,
+     hasJob: 128,
+ };
+ const PERF_FLOOR_MS = 8;
+ const MS_BUCKETS = [1, 2, 4, 8, 16, 33, 50, 100, 250, 500];
+ const JOBS_KEPT = 25;
+ const DROPS_KEPT = 8;
+ const DROP_WHYS_KEPT = 12;
+ const PENDING_MAX = 64;
+ let F = null;
+ let I = null;
+ let W = null;
+ let head = 0, used = 0, wraps = 0;
+ let open = -1, openT0 = 0, openQueuedAt = 0;
+ let crawlTicks = 0;
+ let src = {};
+ let clock = perfNow;
+ let wall = () => Date.now();
+ const totalsT = new Float64Array(STRIDE);
+ const totalsC = new Float64Array(STRIDE);
+ const workerT = new Float64Array(STRIDE);
+ const histMain = new Int32Array(MS_BUCKETS.length + 1);
+ const histWall = new Int32Array(MS_BUCKETS.length + 1);
+ const histPlate = new Int32Array(MS_BUCKETS.length + 1);
+ const kindRows = new Int32Array(KINDS.length);
+ const pending = new Int32Array(PENDING_MAX * 2);
+ let nPending = 0;
+ const SUBMITS = 64;
+ const subSeq = new Int32Array(SUBMITS);
+ const subT = new Float64Array(SUBMITS);
+ const subLeft = new Int32Array(SUBMITS);
+ let subAt = 0, nSub = 0;
+ let jobs = null, nJobs = 0;
+ let drops = null, nDrops = 0, dropAt = 0;
+ const dropWhy = new Map();
+ const stats = {
+     rows: 0, crawls: 0,
+     allocs: 0,
+     orphanAdds: 0,
+     orphanEnds: 0, unclosed: 0,
+     pendingLost: 0,
+     noQueuedAt: 0,
+     jobs: 0, staleDrops: 0, lastTickT: 0,
+     overlayBytes: 0,
+ };
+ const lastView = { mode: "", q: "", Zq: 0, s: 0, dpr: 0, rev: 0, frameId: "" };
+ function ensure() {
+     if (F) return;
+     F = new Float32Array(ROWS * STRIDE);
+     I = new Int32Array(ROWS * STRIDE);
+     W = new Float64Array(ROWS);
+     jobs = [];
+     for (let k = 0; k < JOBS_KEPT; k++) jobs.push(mkJobRec());
+     drops = [];
+     for (let k = 0; k < DROPS_KEPT; k++) drops.push(mkDropRec());
+     stats.allocs += 5;
+ }
+ function mkJobRec() {
+     return { t: 0, frameSeq: 0, jobId: 0, plates: 0, postedToArrivedMs: 0, queueWaitMs: 0, queueingMs: 0,
+         workerMs: 0, serialiseMs: 0, postMs: 0, recvMs: 0, bytesIn: 0, bytesOut: 0,
+         inline: false, stale: 0 };
+ }
+ function mkDropRec() {
+     return { t: 0, why: "", before: false, jobId: 0, frameSeq: 0, q: "", frameId: "", epoch: 0,
+         plates: 0, stale: 0 };
+ }
+ export function attach(s) {
+     src = s || {};
+     if (typeof src.now === "function") clock = src.now;
+     if (typeof src.wall === "function") wall = src.wall;
+ }
+ export function detach() { src = {}; }
+ export function beginFrame(frameSeq, kind) {
+     ensure();
+     if (open >= 0) { stats.unclosed++; closeRow(FLAG.truncated); }
+     open = head;
+     const base = open * STRIDE;
+     F.fill(0, base, base + STRIDE);
+     I.fill(0, base, base + STRIDE);
+     W[open] = wall();
+     openT0 = clock();
+     openQueuedAt = 0;
+     I[base + C.frameSeq] = frameSeq | 0;
+     I[base + C.kind] = kindCode(kind);
+     I[base + C.q] = Q_UNKNOWN;
+     return open;
+ }
+ export function endFrame() {
+     if (open < 0) { stats.orphanEnds++; return null; }
+     const base = open * STRIDE;
+     const end = clock();
+     const ms = end - openT0;
+     F[base + T.mainMs] = ms;
+     const t0 = openT0;
+     const row = closeRow(0, end);
+     if (src.notePerf && I[(row * STRIDE) + C.kind] === K_RENDER
+         && (ms >= PERF_FLOOR_MS || traceOn())) {
+         src.notePerf("rasterRender", t0, perfExtra(row));
+     }
+     return row;
+ }
+ export function add(slot, ms) {
+     if (open < 0) { stats.orphanAdds++; return; }
+     if (!(ms >= 0) || !isFinite(ms)) return;
+     F[(open * STRIDE) + slot] += ms;
+ }
+ export function bump(slot, n) {
+     if (open < 0) { stats.orphanAdds++; return; }
+     I[(open * STRIDE) + slot] += n | 0;
+ }
+ export function noteView(view) {
+     if (!view) return;
+     lastView.mode = String(view.mode || "");
+     lastView.q = view.q;
+     lastView.Zq = +view.Zq || 0;
+     lastView.s = +view.s || 0;
+     lastView.dpr = +view.dpr || 0;
+     lastView.rev = +view.rev || 0;
+     lastView.frameId = String(view.frameId == null ? "" : view.frameId);
+     if (open < 0) return;
+     const base = open * STRIDE;
+     I[base + C.q] = qCode(view.q);
+     I[base + C.mode] = MODES.indexOf(lastView.mode) < 0 ? 0 : MODES.indexOf(lastView.mode);
+     I[base + C.sMilli] = Math.round((+view.s || 0) * 1000);
+     if (lastView.mode === "sync") I[base + C.flags] |= FLAG.rasOnMain;
+ }
+ function closeRow(extraFlags, endAt) {
+     const row = open;
+     const base = row * STRIDE;
+     if (extraFlags) I[base + C.flags] |= extraFlags;
+     const needEnd = extraFlags === FLAG.truncated || (openQueuedAt > 0 && !F[base + T.wallMs]);
+     const end = needEnd && endAt == null ? clock() : endAt;
+     if (extraFlags === FLAG.truncated) F[base + T.mainMs] = end - openT0;
+     if (openQueuedAt > 0 && !F[base + T.wallMs]) F[base + T.wallMs] = end - openQueuedAt;
+     const sync = (I[base + C.flags] & FLAG.rasOnMain) !== 0;
+     let main = 0;
+     for (const s of MAIN_PART) main += F[base + s];
+     if (sync) for (const s of SYNC_PART) main += F[base + s];
+     F[base + T.residualMain] = F[base + T.mainMs] - main;
+     if (F[base + T.wallMs] > 0) {
+         let w = 0;
+         for (const s of WALL_PART) w += F[base + s];
+         F[base + T.residualWall] = F[base + T.wallMs] - w;
+     }
+     for (let s = 0; s < TIMERS.length; s++) totalsT[s] += F[base + s];
+     if (!sync) for (const s of PLATE_PART) workerT[s] += F[base + s];
+     for (let s = C.platesWanted; s < COUNTS.length; s++) totalsC[s] += I[base + s];
+     bucketOf(histMain, F[base + T.mainMs]);
+     if (F[base + T.wallMs] > 0) bucketOf(histWall, F[base + T.wallMs]);
+     kindRows[I[base + C.kind]]++;
+     stats.rows++;
+     if (nPending < PENDING_MAX) {
+         pending[nPending * 2] = row;
+         pending[nPending * 2 + 1] = I[base + C.frameSeq];
+         nPending++;
+         I[base + C.flags] |= FLAG.inferredPending;
+     } else {
+         stats.pendingLost++;
+     }
+     head = (head + 1) % ROWS;
+     if (head === 0) wraps++;
+     if (used < ROWS) used++;
+     open = -1;
+     return row;
+ }
+ function perfExtra(row) {
+     const base = row * STRIDE;
+     return { frameSeq: I[base + C.frameSeq], kind: KINDS[I[base + C.kind]],
+         mainMs: ms3(F[base + T.mainMs]), residualMs: ms3(F[base + T.residualMain]) };
+ }
+ export function noteSubmit(req, nPlates, nStrips) {
+     ensure();
+     const seq = req && req.frameSeq != null ? req.frameSeq | 0 : 0;
+     subSeq[subAt] = seq;
+     subT[subAt] = clock();
+     subLeft[subAt] = Math.max(1, nStrips | 0);
+     subAt = (subAt + 1) % SUBMITS;
+     if (nSub < SUBMITS) nSub++;
+     if (open < 0) return;
+     const base = open * STRIDE;
+     I[base + C.jobsPosted] += nStrips | 0;
+     I[base + C.misses] += nPlates | 0;
+     I[base + C.platesWanted] += nPlates | 0;
+ }
+ function queuedAtFor(seq, postedAt) {
+     let spent = -1;
+     for (let k = 0; k < nSub; k++) {
+         const at = (subAt - 1 - k + SUBMITS * 2) % SUBMITS;
+         if (subSeq[at] !== seq || subT[at] > postedAt) continue;
+         if (subLeft[at] > 0) { subLeft[at]--; return subT[at]; }
+         if (spent < 0) spent = at;
+     }
+     return spent < 0 ? -1 : subT[spent];
+ }
+ export function noteJob(info, res) {
+     ensure();
+     stats.jobs++;
+     if (open < 0) { stats.orphanAdds++; return; }
+     const base = open * STRIDE;
+     I[base + C.flags] |= FLAG.hasJob;
+     const q = queuedAtFor(info.frameSeq | 0, info.postedAt);
+     if (q < 0) stats.noQueuedAt++;
+     else {
+         if (info.postedAt > q) F[base + T.queueWait] += info.postedAt - q;
+         if (!openQueuedAt || q < openQueuedAt) openQueuedAt = q;
+     }
+     F[base + T.queueing] += +info.queueingMs || 0;
+     F[base + T["xfer.in"]] += +info.recvMs || 0;
+     F[base + T["xfer.serialise"]] += +(res && res.serialiseMs) || 0;
+     I[base + C.bytesOut] += info.bytesOut | 0;
+     I[base + C.bytesIn] += info.bytesIn | 0;
+     I[base + C.jobsArrived] += 1;
+     let workerMs = 0;
+     const nPlates = res && res.plates ? res.plates.length / 2 : 0;
+     if (res && res.timings && nPlates > 0) {
+         const st = res.timings.length / nPlates;
+         for (let p = 0; p < nPlates; p++) {
+             const o = p * st;
+             F[base + T["job.recvToStart"]] += res.timings[o];
+             F[base + T["job.startToFinish"]] += res.timings[o + 1];
+             F[base + T["eng.clip"]] += res.timings[o + 2];
+             F[base + T["eng.local"]] += res.timings[o + 3];
+             F[base + T["ras.edges"]] += res.timings[o + 4];
+             F[base + T["ras.union"]] += res.timings[o + 5];
+             F[base + T["ras.accum"]] += res.timings[o + 6];
+             workerMs += res.timings[o] + res.timings[o + 1];
+             bucketOf(histPlate, res.timings[o + 1]);
+         }
+     }
+     if (res && res.counts && nPlates > 0) {
+         const sc = res.counts.length / nPlates;
+         for (let p = 0; p < nPlates; p++) {
+             for (let k = 0; k < sc && k < 8; k++) I[base + C.items + k] += res.counts[p * sc + k];
+         }
+     }
+     I[base + C.platesArrived] += nPlates;
+     const staleN = info.stale && info.stale.stale ? info.stale.stale.length : 0;
+     takeJob(info, res, workerMs, staleN, q);
+     if (src.notePerf && ((+info.wallMs || 0) >= PERF_FLOOR_MS || traceOn())) {
+         src.notePerf("rasterJob", info.postedAt, {
+             frameSeq: info.frameSeq, plates: nPlates, wallMs: ms3(info.wallMs),
+             queueingMs: ms3(info.queueingMs), bytesIn: info.bytesIn | 0,
+         });
+     }
+ }
+ export function noteBitmap(ms) { add(T["br.bitmap"], ms); }
+ export function noteCompose(ms, plates, blits) {
+     add(T["br.blit"], ms);
+     if (open < 0) return;
+     const base = open * STRIDE;
+     I[base + C.plateBlits] += blits | 0;
+     if (I[base + C.kind] !== K_ARRIVAL) {
+         I[base + C.hits] += plates | 0;
+         I[base + C.platesWanted] += plates | 0;
+     }
+ }
+ export function noteDrop(req, d) {
+     ensure();
+     stats.staleDrops++;
+     if (open >= 0) I[(open * STRIDE) + C.drops] += 1;
+     const r = drops[dropAt];
+     r.t = wall();
+     r.why = String((d && d.why) || "");
+     tallyDrop(r.why);
+     r.before = !!(d && d.before);
+     r.jobId = (d && d.jobId) || 0;
+     r.frameSeq = pick(d, req, "frameSeq", 0);
+     r.q = String(pick(d, req, "q", ""));
+     r.frameId = String(pick(d, req, "frameId", ""));
+     r.epoch = pick(d, req, "epoch", 0);
+     r.plates = d && d.plates ? (d.plates.length / 2) | 0 : 0;
+     r.stale = d && d.stale ? d.stale.length : 0;
