+     dropAt = (dropAt + 1) % DROPS_KEPT;
+     if (nDrops < DROPS_KEPT) nDrops++;
+     if (src.notePerf) {
+         src.notePerf("rasterStale", clock(), { why: r.why, before: r.before, q: r.q,
+             frameSeq: r.frameSeq, plates: r.plates });
+     }
+ }
+ export function noteCrawl(rep) {
+     ensure();
+     if (!rep) return null;
+     if (open >= 0) { stats.unclosed++; closeRow(FLAG.truncated); }
+     crawlTicks++;
+     open = head;
+     const base = open * STRIDE;
+     F.fill(0, base, base + STRIDE);
+     I.fill(0, base, base + STRIDE);
+     W[open] = wall();
+     openT0 = clock();
+     openQueuedAt = 0;
+     I[base + C.frameSeq] = -crawlTicks;
+     I[base + C.kind] = K_CRAWL;
+     I[base + C.q] = qCode(lastView.q);
+     I[base + C.mode] = Math.max(0, MODES.indexOf(lastView.mode));
+     I[base + C.sMilli] = Math.round(lastView.s * 1000);
+     const decide = +rep.decideMs || 0;
+     const paint = rep.paintMs != null ? +rep.paintMs : (+rep.lastPhaseMs || 0);
+     const blit = +rep.blitMs || 0;
+     const live = +rep.liveMs || 0;
+     F[base + T["ovl.decide"]] = decide;
+     F[base + T["ovl.paint"]] = paint;
+     F[base + T["ovl.blit"]] = blit;
+     F[base + T["ovl.live"]] = live;
+     F[base + T.mainMs] = rep.mainMs != null ? +rep.mainMs : decide + paint + blit + live;
+     I[base + C.antDashes] = rep.dashes | 0;
+     I[base + C.antStamps] = rep.stamps | 0;
+     I[base + C.antPixels] = rep.pixels | 0;
+     I[base + C.overlayBlitPx] = rep.blitPx | 0;
+     I[base + C.overlayRepaints] = rep.repaints | 0;
+     I[base + C.liveMoves] = rep.liveMoves | 0;
+     I[base + C.liveDirtyPx] = rep.liveDirtyPx | 0;
+     if (rep.bytes != null) stats.overlayBytes = rep.bytes | 0;
+     stats.crawls = crawlTicks;
+     return closeRow(0);
+ }
+ export function onFrame(deltaMs, ts) {
+     if (!F || !nPending) return;
+     if (!(deltaMs >= 0) || !isFinite(deltaMs)) { nPending = 0; return; }
+     let sumMain = 0, n = 0;
+     for (let k = 0; k < nPending; k++) {
+         const row = pending[k * 2];
+         const base = row * STRIDE;
+         if (I[base + C.frameSeq] !== pending[k * 2 + 1]) continue;
+         sumMain += F[base + T.mainMs];
+         n++;
+     }
+     const infer = deltaMs - sumMain;
+     for (let k = 0; k < nPending; k++) {
+         const row = pending[k * 2];
+         const base = row * STRIDE;
+         if (I[base + C.frameSeq] !== pending[k * 2 + 1]) { stats.pendingLost++; continue; }
+         F[base + T.rafDelta] = deltaMs;
+         I[base + C.flags] |= FLAG.rafKnown;
+         I[base + C.flags] &= ~FLAG.inferredPending;
+         if (n > 1) I[base + C.flags] |= FLAG.shared;
+         if (F[base + T.mainMs] > deltaMs) I[base + C.flags] |= FLAG.spansFrames;
+         if (infer < 0) I[base + C.flags] |= FLAG.clamped;
+         if (k === nPending - 1) {
+             F[base + T["br.inferred"]] = infer > 0 ? infer : 0;
+             totalsT[T["br.inferred"]] += infer > 0 ? infer : 0;
+         }
+     }
+     totalsT[T.rafDelta] += deltaMs;
+     stats.lastTickT = ts != null ? ts : wall();
+     nPending = 0;
+ }
+ function takeJob(info, res, workerMs, staleN, q) {
+     const ms = +info.wallMs || 0;
+     let at = -1;
+     if (nJobs < JOBS_KEPT) { at = nJobs++; } else {
+         let worstAt = 0;
+         for (let k = 1; k < JOBS_KEPT; k++) {
+             if (jobs[k].postedToArrivedMs < jobs[worstAt].postedToArrivedMs) worstAt = k;
+         }
+         if (ms > jobs[worstAt].postedToArrivedMs) at = worstAt;
+     }
+     if (at < 0) return;
+     const r = jobs[at];
+     r.t = wall();
+     r.frameSeq = info.frameSeq | 0;
+     r.jobId = info.jobId | 0;
+     r.plates = res && res.plates ? res.plates.length / 2 : 0;
+     r.postedToArrivedMs = ms;
+     r.queueWaitMs = q >= 0 && info.postedAt > q ? info.postedAt - q : 0;
+     r.queueingMs = +info.queueingMs || 0;
+     r.workerMs = workerMs;
+     r.serialiseMs = +(res && res.serialiseMs) || 0;
+     r.postMs = +info.postMs || 0;
+     r.recvMs = +info.recvMs || 0;
+     r.bytesIn = info.bytesIn | 0;
+     r.bytesOut = info.bytesOut | 0;
+     r.inline = !!info.inline;
+     r.stale = staleN;
+ }
+ function tallyDrop(why) {
+     const k = dropWhy.has(why) || dropWhy.size < DROP_WHYS_KEPT ? why : "other";
+     dropWhy.set(k, (dropWhy.get(k) || 0) + 1);
+ }
+ function bucketOf(hist, ms) {
+     let i = 0;
+     while (i < MS_BUCKETS.length && ms > MS_BUCKETS[i]) i++;
+     hist[i]++;
+ }
+ function kindCode(kind) {
+     const k = KINDS.indexOf(String(kind));
+     return k < 0 ? 0 : k;
+ }
+ function qCode(q) {
+     if (q === "s") return Q_SYNC;
+     if (q === "e") return Q_SETTLE;
+     const n = +q;
+     return isFinite(n) && q !== "" && q != null ? Math.round(n) : Q_UNKNOWN;
+ }
+ function pick(d, req, key, dflt) {
+     if (d && d[key] != null) return d[key];
+     if (req && req[key] != null) return req[key];
+     return dflt;
+ }
+ function traceOn() { return typeof src.trace === "function" ? !!src.trace() : false; }
+ function ms3(ms) { return Math.round((+ms || 0) * 1000) / 1000; }
//// src/engine/raster/rasterWorkerClient.js  (new file)
+ import {
+     wireJob, unwireResult, resultStale, jobBytes, resultBytes, BufferPool,
+     GUARD_NAMES, COUNT_NAMES, GUARDS_PER_JOB, COUNTS_PER_PLATE, TIMINGS_PER_PLATE,
+ } from "./rasterJob";
+ import { runJob } from "../worker/rasterWorker";
+ import { PLATE_PX } from "./plate";
+ export const MAX_WORKERS = 4;
+ export const MAX_RESPAWNS = 3;
+ export const WATCHDOG_MS = 30000;
+ let workers = [];
+ let broken = false;
+ let answered = false;
+ let respawns = 0;
+ let watchdog = null;
+ let spawned = false;
+ let transport = null;
+ let seq = 0;
+ const queue = [];
+ const inFlight = new Map();
+ let heldNoSlot = 0;
+ const current = new Map();
+ const pool = new BufferPool(8 << 20);
+ const stats = {
+     jobs: 0, plates: 0, failed: 0,
+     dropsBefore: 0, dropsAfter: 0, staleOnArrival: 0,
+     bytesOut: 0, bytesIn: 0,
+     workerMs: 0, poolReadyMs: 0,
+     postMs: 0, recvMs: 0,
+     spawnedLate: 0,
+     respawns: 0, silent: 0,
+ };
+ const guardTotals = new Float64Array(GUARDS_PER_JOB);
+ const countTotals = new Float64Array(COUNTS_PER_PLATE);
+ const named = (names, a) => { const o = {}; names.forEach((n, i) => { o[n] = a[i]; }); return o; };
+ const now = () => (typeof performance !== "undefined" ? performance.now() : Date.now());
+ export function rasterWorkerCount() {
+     const cores = (typeof navigator !== "undefined" && navigator.hardwareConcurrency) || 1;
+     return Math.min(MAX_WORKERS, Math.max(1, cores - 1));
+ }
+ function workerUrl() {
+     const base = (typeof process !== "undefined" && process.env && process.env.PUBLIC_URL) || "";
+     return base + "/raster-worker.js";
+ }
+ export function ensureRasterPool() { return spawnPool(false); }
+ function spawnPool(late) {
+     if (transport || broken || spawned) return workers.length;
+     spawned = true;
+     if (late) stats.spawnedLate++;
+     if (typeof Worker === "undefined") { broken = true; return 0; }
+     const t0 = now();
+     const want = rasterWorkerCount();
+     for (let k = 0; k < want; k++) {
+         let w;
+         try {
+             w = new Worker(workerUrl());
+         } catch (err) {
+             broken = true;
+             terminateWorkers();
+             return 0;
+         }
+         const slot = { w, jobId: null };
+         w.onmessage = (e) => onMessage(slot, (e && e.data) || {});
+         w.onerror = (e) => onWorkerError(e);
+         workers.push(slot);
+     }
+     stats.poolReadyMs = now() - t0;
+     return workers.length;
+ }
+ function terminateWorkers() {
+     for (const s of workers) { try { s.w.terminate(); } catch (err) {  } }
+     workers = [];
+     spawned = false;
+ }
+ export function destroyRasterPool() {
+     if (watchdog != null) { clearTimeout(watchdog); watchdog = null; }
+     terminateWorkers();
+     abandonInFlight("destroyed");
+ }
+ const ABANDONED = -1;
+ export function abandonInFlight(why) {
+     const stranded = [...inFlight.values()];
+     inFlight.clear();
+     for (const f of stranded) {
+         if (f.slot) f.slot.jobId = ABANDONED;
+         else heldNoSlot++;
+         stats.failed++;
+         if (f.req.onDrop) f.req.onDrop({ jobId: f.jobId, plates: f.plates, why: why || "abandoned", before: false });
+     }
+     return stranded.length;
+ }
+ function releaseAbandoned(slot) {
+     if (slot) {
+         if (slot.jobId !== ABANDONED) return false;
+         slot.jobId = null;
+         return true;
+     }
+     if (heldNoSlot === 0) return false;
+     heldNoSlot--;
+     return true;
+ }
+ function onWorkerError(e) { failPool(e, answered); }
+ function failPool(e, respawn) {
+     const stranded = [...inFlight.values()];
+     inFlight.clear();
+     terminateWorkers();
+     for (const f of stranded) {
+         stats.failed++;
+         if (f.req.onDrop) f.req.onDrop({ jobId: f.jobId, plates: f.plates, why: "worker", before: false });
+     }
+     for (const f of stranded) queue.push({ req: f.req, plates: f.plates });
+     if (respawn && respawns < MAX_RESPAWNS) { respawns++; stats.respawns++; spawnPool(false); }
+     else broken = true;
+     if (e && e.preventDefault) e.preventDefault();
+     pump();
+ }
+ function armWatchdog() {
+     if (watchdog != null || transport || !workers.length) return;
+     watchdog = setTimeout(checkWatchdog, WATCHDOG_MS / 2);
+ }
+ function checkWatchdog() {
+     watchdog = null;
+     const t = now();
+     for (const rec of inFlight.values()) {
+         if (!rec.slot || t - rec.postedAt < WATCHDOG_MS) continue;
+         if (!rec.suspect) { rec.suspect = t; continue; }
+         stats.silent++;
+         failPool(null, true);
+         return;
+     }
+     for (const rec of inFlight.values()) if (rec.slot) { armWatchdog(); return; }
+ }
+ export const STRIPS_PER_WORKER = 2;
+ export function stripSizeFor(nPlates, workerCount) {
+     const n = workerCount != null ? workerCount : activeCount();
+     return Math.max(1, Math.ceil(nPlates / Math.max(1, n * STRIPS_PER_WORKER)));
+ }
+ function activeCount() {
+     if (transport) return rasterWorkerCount();
+     spawnPool(true);
+     return workers.length || 1;
+ }
+ export function submitPlates(req) {
+     const plates = req.plates instanceof Int32Array ? req.plates : Int32Array.from(req.plates || []);
+     dropStale(req.frameSeq, req.q);
+     current.set(req.q, req.frameSeq);
+     const per = stripSizeFor(plates.length / 2) * 2;
+     let n = 0;
+     for (let at = 0; at < plates.length; at += per) {
+         queue.push({ req, plates: plates.slice(at, at + per), prio: req.prio != null ? req.prio : 1 });
+         n++;
+     }
+     pump();
+     return n;
+ }
+ function dropStale(frameSeq, q) {
+     for (let k = queue.length - 1; k >= 0; k--) {
+         const e = queue[k];
+         if (e.req.q !== q || e.req.frameSeq === frameSeq) continue;
+         queue.splice(k, 1);
+         stats.dropsBefore++;
+         if (e.req.onDrop) e.req.onDrop({ jobId: null, plates: e.plates, why: "frame", before: true });
+     }
+     pruneCurrent();
+ }
+ function pruneCurrent() {
+     const live = new Set();
+     for (const e of queue) live.add(e.req.q);
+     for (const f of inFlight.values()) live.add(f.req.q);
+     for (const k of [...current.keys()]) if (!live.has(k)) current.delete(k);
+ }
+ export function dropQueued(why, q) {
+     let n = 0;
+     for (let k = queue.length - 1; k >= 0; k--) {
+         const e = queue[k];
+         if (q != null && e.req.q !== q) continue;
+         queue.splice(k, 1);
+         stats.dropsBefore++;
+         n++;
+         if (e.req.onDrop) e.req.onDrop({ jobId: null, plates: e.plates, why: why || "dropped", before: true });
+     }
+     pruneCurrent();
+     return n;
+ }
+ function pump() {
+     for (;;) {
+         if (!queue.length) return;
+         const slot = freeSlot();
+         if (slot === undefined) return;
+         const k = bestIndex();
+         if (k < 0) return;
+         const e = queue.splice(k, 1)[0];
+         if (current.get(e.req.q) !== e.req.frameSeq
+             || (e.req.stillWanted && !e.req.stillWanted(e.plates))) {
+             stats.dropsBefore++;
+             if (e.req.onDrop) e.req.onDrop({ jobId: null, plates: e.plates, why: "stale", before: true });
+             continue;
+         }
+         dispatch(e, slot);
+     }
+ }
+ const LOW_PRIO = 3;
+ function bestIndex() {
+     let low = 0;
+     for (const r of inFlight.values()) if ((r.req.prio != null ? r.req.prio : 1) >= LOW_PRIO) low++;
+     const lowCap = Math.max(1, rasterWorkerCount() - 1);
+     let best = -1, bestPrio = Infinity;
+     for (let k = 0; k < queue.length; k++) {
+         const p = queue[k].prio;
+         if (p >= bestPrio) continue;
+         if (p >= LOW_PRIO && low >= lowCap) continue;
+         best = k; bestPrio = p;
+         if (p === 0) break;
+     }
+     return best;
+ }
+ function freeSlot() {
+     if (transport) return inFlight.size + heldNoSlot < rasterWorkerCount() ? null : undefined;
+     if (!broken) spawnPool(true);
+     if (workers.length) {
+         for (const s of workers) if (s.jobId == null) return s;
+         return undefined;
+     }
+     return inFlight.size + heldNoSlot === 0 ? null : undefined;
+ }
+ function dispatch(entry, slot) {
+     const jobId = ++seq;
+     let job;
+     try {
+         job = entry.req.pack(entry.plates, jobId, pool);
+     } catch (err) {
+         stats.failed++;
+         if (entry.req.onDrop) entry.req.onDrop({ jobId, plates: entry.plates, why: "pack", before: true });
+         return;
+     }
+     if (!job) {
+         stats.dropsBefore++;
+         if (entry.req.onDrop) entry.req.onDrop({ jobId, plates: entry.plates, why: "empty", before: true });
+         return;
+     }
+     job.jobId = jobId;
+     if (job.frameSeq == null) job.frameSeq = entry.req.frameSeq;
+     const bytesOut = jobBytes(job);
+     const postedAt = now();
+     job.postedAt = postedAt;
+     const { msg, transfer } = wireJob(job);
+     const rec = { req: entry.req, plates: entry.plates, postedAt, slot, jobId, bytesOut, postMs: 0 };
+     inFlight.set(jobId, rec);
+     stats.jobs++;
+     stats.bytesOut += bytesOut;
+     if (slot) slot.jobId = jobId;
+     const donePost = () => { rec.postMs = now() - postedAt; stats.postMs += rec.postMs; };
+     if (transport) {
+         const p = transport(msg, transfer);
+         donePost();
+         p.then((out) => onMessage(slot, out || {}), (err) => onFailed(jobId, err, slot));
+         return;
+     }
+     if (slot) {
+         try {
+             slot.w.postMessage(msg, transfer);
+             donePost();
+             armWatchdog();
+         } catch (err) {
+             onFailed(jobId, err, slot);
+         }
+         return;
+     }
+     donePost();
+     Promise.resolve().then(() => runJob(msg, { receivedAt: now() }))
+         .then((out) => onMessage(null, out.msg), (err) => onFailed(jobId, err, null));
+ }
+ function onFailed(jobId, err, slot) {
+     const rec = inFlight.get(jobId);
+     if (!rec) {
+         if (releaseAbandoned(slot || null)) pump();
+         return;
+     }
+     inFlight.delete(jobId);
+     if (rec.slot) rec.slot.jobId = null;
+     stats.failed++;
+     if (rec.req.onDrop) {
+         rec.req.onDrop({ jobId, plates: rec.plates, why: String((err && err.message) || err || "failed"), before: false });
+     }
+     pump();
+ }
+ function onMessage(slot, data) {
+     const arrivedAt = now();
+     if (slot) answered = true;
+     if (data.kind === "raster-error") {
+         onFailed(data.jobId, new Error(data.err), slot);
+         return;
+     }
+     const res = unwireResult(data);
+     const rec = inFlight.get(res.jobId);
+     if (!rec) {
+         if (releaseAbandoned(slot)) pump();
+         return;
+     }
+     inFlight.delete(res.jobId);
+     if (rec.slot) rec.slot.jobId = null;
+     for (const b of res.recycled) pool.give(b);
+     res.recycled.length = 0;
+     const recvMs = now() - arrivedAt;
+     const nPlates = res.plates.length / 2;
+     let phases = res.serialiseMs || 0;
+     for (let p = 0; p < nPlates; p++) {
+         phases += res.timings[p * TIMINGS_PER_PLATE] + res.timings[p * TIMINGS_PER_PLATE + 1];
+     }
+     const wallMs = arrivedAt - rec.postedAt;
+     const info = {
+         jobId: res.jobId, frameSeq: res.frameSeq, plates: rec.plates,
+         postedAt: rec.postedAt, arrivedAt, wallMs,
+         queueingMs: wallMs - phases,
+         postMs: rec.postMs, recvMs,
+         bytesOut: rec.bytesOut, bytesIn: resultBytes(res),
+         stale: rec.req.epochOf ? resultStale(res, rec.req.epochOf) : null,
+         inline: !slot && !transport,
+     };
+     stats.plates += nPlates;
+     stats.bytesIn += info.bytesIn;
+     stats.workerMs += phases;
+     stats.recvMs += recvMs;
+     if (res.guards) for (let g = 0; g < GUARDS_PER_JOB; g++) guardTotals[g] += res.guards[g];
+     if (res.counts) for (let c = 0; c < res.counts.length; c++) countTotals[c % COUNTS_PER_PLATE] += res.counts[c];
+     if (info.stale) {
+         stats.dropsAfter++;
+         stats.staleOnArrival += info.stale.stale.length;
+         if (rec.req.onDrop) {
+             rec.req.onDrop({
+                 jobId: res.jobId, plates: rec.plates, stale: info.stale.stale,
+                 why: info.stale.why, before: false,
+             });
+         }
+     }
+     if (rec.req.onResult) rec.req.onResult(res, info);
+     pump();
+ }
+ export function plateIds(res, p) {
+     const end = p + 1 < res.idOff.length ? res.idOff[p + 1] : res.idSet.length;
+     return res.idSet.subarray(res.idOff[p], end);
+ }
+ export async function plateBitmap(px) {
+     if (!px || typeof createImageBitmap !== "function" || typeof ImageData !== "function") return null;
+     return createImageBitmap(new ImageData(px, PLATE_PX, PLATE_PX));
+ }
+ export function rasterWorkerReport() {
+     let held = heldNoSlot;
+     for (const s of workers) if (s.jobId === ABANDONED) held++;
+     return {
+         mode: transport ? "transport" : workers.length ? "workers" : "inline",
+         workers: workers.length, wanted: rasterWorkerCount(), broken, respawns: stats.respawns, silent: stats.silent,
+         queued: queue.length, inFlight: inFlight.size, heldSlots: held, liveRequests: current.size,
+         jobs: stats.jobs, plates: stats.plates, failed: stats.failed,
+         dropsBefore: stats.dropsBefore, dropsAfter: stats.dropsAfter, staleOnArrival: stats.staleOnArrival,
+         bytesOut: stats.bytesOut, bytesIn: stats.bytesIn,
+         workerMs: stats.workerMs, poolReadyMs: stats.poolReadyMs,
+         postMs: stats.postMs, recvMs: stats.recvMs, spawnedLate: stats.spawnedLate,
+         guards: named(GUARD_NAMES, guardTotals),
+         counts: named(COUNT_NAMES, countTotals),
+         pool: { takes: pool.takes, hits: pool.hits, allocBytes: pool.allocBytes, bytes: pool.bytes },
+     };
+ }
//// src/engine/report.js  (new file)
+ let yq = null;
+ export function macroYield() {
+     if (typeof MessageChannel !== "function") return new Promise((res) => setTimeout(res, 0));
+     if (!yq) {
+         const ch = new MessageChannel(), q = [];
+         ch.port1.onmessage = () => { const f = q.shift(); if (f) f(); };
+         if (ch.port1.unref) ch.port1.unref();
+         yq = { q, post: () => ch.port2.postMessage(0) };
+     }
+     return new Promise((res) => { yq.q.push(res); yq.post(); });
+ }
//// src/engine/sceneClient.js  (new file)
+ import { SceneIndex, CHUNK, MAX_CHUNKS, REACH } from "./sceneIndex";
+ import { segmentTerm, loopsBBox } from "./geometry/arcShape";
+ import { residual } from "./geometry/offsets";
+ import { Loop } from "./geometry/loop";
+ import { openSceneWorker } from "./sceneWorkerClient";
+ import { rasterWorkerReport } from "./raster/rasterWorkerClient";
+ const SLICE_MS = 8;
+ const MAX_WAIT_MS = 3000;
+ const SCENE_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS = 5000;
+ const SCENE_WORKER_RESPAWNS = 3;
+ const INLINE_AUTO_MAX = 5000;
+ const REC = { 0: 3, 1: 8, 2: 9, 3: 16 };
+ export const FOOT_MAX = 512;
+ export const FOOT_CHUNKS = 8192;
+ const ONE_LOOP = Object.freeze([0]);
+ const now = () => (typeof performance !== "undefined" ? performance.now() : Date.now());
+ function chunker(max) {
+     const out = [], starts = [];
+     let x0 = 0, y0 = 0, x1 = 0, y1 = 0, len = 0, open = false, px = 0, py = 0, have = false;
+     return {
+         out, starts,
+         put(x, y, jump) {
+             const dl = have && !jump ? Math.hypot(x - px, y - py) : 0;
+             if (open && (jump || Math.max(x1, x) - Math.min(x0, x) > max || Math.max(y1, y) - Math.min(y0, y) > max)) {
+                 out.push(x0, y0, x1, y1, len);
+                 open = false;
+                 if (!jump && have) { x0 = x1 = px; y0 = y1 = py; len = 0; open = true; }
+             }
+             if (!open) { if (jump) starts.push(out.length / 5); x0 = x1 = x; y0 = y1 = y; len = 0; open = true; }
+             if (x < x0) x0 = x; else if (x > x1) x1 = x;
+             if (y < y0) y0 = y; else if (y > y1) y1 = y;
+             len += dl;
+             px = x; py = y; have = true;
+         },
+         end() { if (open) out.push(x0, y0, x1, y1, len); open = false; },
+     };
+ }
+ function walkLoops(loops, max) {
+     const c = chunker(max), step = max / 2;
+     let area = 0, per = 0;
+     for (const lp of loops) {
+         const b = lp && lp.buf ? lp.buf : Loop.from(lp).buf;
+         if (!b || b.length < 5) continue;
+         let ax = b[0], ay = b[1];
+         const sx = ax, sy = ay;
+         let a = 0;
+         c.put(ax, ay, true);
+         for (let o = 2; o < b.length; o += REC[b[o]] || 8) {
+             const code = b[o];
+             let bx, by;
+             if (code === 0 || code === 2) {
+                 bx = code === 0 ? b[o + 1] : b[o + 7]; by = code === 0 ? b[o + 2] : b[o + 8];
+                 const L = Math.hypot(bx - ax, by - ay);
+                 per += L;
+                 const n = Math.ceil(L / step);
+                 for (let s = 1; s < n; s++) c.put(ax + ((bx - ax) * s) / n, ay + ((by - ay) * s) / n, false);
+             } else {
+                 const cx = b[o + 1], cy = b[o + 2], r = b[o + 3], a0 = b[o + 4], sw = b[o + 5];
+                 bx = code === 3 ? b[o + 14] : b[o + 6]; by = code === 3 ? b[o + 15] : b[o + 7];
+                 const L = Math.abs(r * sw);
+                 per += L;
+                 a += (r * r * segmentTerm(sw)) / 2;
+                 const n = Math.ceil(L / step);
+                 for (let s = 1; s < n; s++) { const th = a0 + (sw * s) / n; c.put(cx + r * Math.cos(th), cy + r * Math.sin(th), false); }
+             }
+             a += ((ax - sx) * (by - sy) - (bx - sx) * (ay - sy)) / 2;
+             c.put(bx, by, false);
+             ax = bx; ay = by;
+         }
+         area += a;
+     }
+     c.end();
+     return { out: c.out, loops: c.starts, area: Math.abs(area), per };
+ }
+ function walkPoly(rings, max, closed) {
+     const c = chunker(max), step = max / 2;
+     for (const pts of rings) {
+         if (!pts || !pts.length) continue;
+         let [ax, ay] = pts[0];
+         c.put(ax, ay, true);
+         const n = pts.length + (closed ? 1 : 0);
+         for (let i = 1; i < n; i++) {
+             const [bx, by] = pts[i % pts.length];
+             const L = Math.hypot(bx - ax, by - ay), k = Math.ceil(L / step);
+             for (let s = 1; s < k; s++) c.put(ax + ((bx - ax) * s) / k, ay + ((by - ay) * s) / k, false);
+             c.put(bx, by, false);
+             ax = bx; ay = by;
+         }
+     }
+     c.end();
+     return { out: c.out, loops: closed ? c.starts : null };
+ }
+ const boxSize = (out) => {
+     let x0 = Infinity, y0 = Infinity, x1 = -Infinity, y1 = -Infinity;
+     for (let i = 0; i < out.length; i += 5) { x0 = Math.min(x0, out[i]); y0 = Math.min(y0, out[i + 1]); x1 = Math.max(x1, out[i + 2]); y1 = Math.max(y1, out[i + 3]); }
+     return Number.isFinite(x0) ? Math.max(x1 - x0, y1 - y0) : 0;
+ };
+ export function sceneGeom(o) {
+     let w, walk = null;
+     for (let grow = 1; grow < 1e9; grow *= 2) {
+         if (o.type === "shape" && o.loops) {
+             if (w == null) {
+                 const first = walkLoops(o.loops, o.w > 0 ? CHUNK * o.w : Infinity);
+                 const mw = first.per > 0 ? (2 * first.area) / first.per : 0;
+                 w = o.w > 0 ? Math.max(Math.min(o.w, mw), o.w * 1e-6) : Math.max(boxSize(first.out), 1e-12) / CHUNK;
+                 if (o.w > 0 && (w >= o.w / 2 || first.out.length <= 5)) { walk = first; w = w || o.w; if (walk.out.length / 5 <= MAX_CHUNKS) break; }
+             }
+             walk = walkLoops(o.loops, CHUNK * w * grow);
+         } else if (o.type === "stroke" && o.pts) {
+             if (w == null) w = o.lwFrame > 0 ? o.lwFrame : 1e-9;
+             walk = walkPoly([o.pts], CHUNK * w * grow, false);
+         } else if (o.type === "fill" && o.polys) {
+             if (w == null) w = Math.max(boxSize(walkPoly(o.polys, Infinity, true).out), 1e-12) / CHUNK;
+             walk = walkPoly(o.polys, CHUNK * w * grow, true);
+         } else return null;
+         if (walk.out.length / 5 <= MAX_CHUNKS) break;
+     }
+     const out = walk && walk.out;
+     if (!out || !out.length) return null;
+     for (let i = 4; i < out.length; i += 5) if (!(out[i] > 0)) out[i] = w;
+     const lp = walk.loops;
+     return { w, raw: Float64Array.from(out), loops: !lp || !lp.length ? null : lp.length === 1 && lp[0] === 0 ? ONE_LOOP : lp.slice() };
+ }
+ const isInk = (doc, o) => !!o && !o.erase && !doc._pending.has(o) && (o.attachRect == null || orphanPatch(doc, o));
+ const orphanPatch = (doc, o) => {
+     for (const r of doc.editGroup(o)) if (r.obj !== o && !r.obj.erase && r.obj.attachRect == null) return false;
+     return true;
+ };
+ export class SceneClient {
+     constructor(E, { worker = true } = {}) {
+         this.E = E; this.doc = E.doc; this.lm = E.lm;
+         this.useWorker = worker;
+         this.dirty = new Set();
+         this.attr = new Set();
+         this.goneFrames = new Set();
+         this.patchFam = new Set();
+         this.famDirty = new Set();
+         this.hints = new Map();
+         this.foot = new Map();
+         this.footDirty = false;
+         this.footMax = FOOT_MAX; this.footChunks = FOOT_CHUNKS;
+         this.renames = [];
+         this.sent = new Map();
+         this.recW = new Float64Array(1024); this.recTX = new Float64Array(1024); this.recTY = new Float64Array(1024);
+         this.recHO = new Int32Array(1024); this.recTop = 0; this.recFree = [];
+         this.homes = new Map(); this.homeOf = [];
+         this.geom = new Set();
+         this.fsent = new Map();
+         this.markGeo = new Map();
+         this.frameKeys = new Map();
+         this.export = null;
+         this.inflight = null;
+         this.gen = 0;
+         this.frameEv = 0;
+         this.resync = null;
+         this.pending = null;
+         this.core = null; this.remote = null;
+         this.timer = null; this.idleId = null; this.waitSince = 0;
+         this.destroyed = false;
+         this.listeners = new Set();
+         this.stats = { batches: 0, items: 0, prepMs: 0, applyMs: 0, lastApplyMs: 0, lastPrepMs: 0, lastItems: 0, restores: 0 };
+         this._unsub = this.doc.subscribe((ev) => this._onEvent(ev));
+         this._unsubOps = this.doc.subscribeOps((ev) => this._onOp(ev));
+     }
+     destroy() {
+         this.destroyed = true;
+         this._cancel();
+         if (this._unsub) this._unsub();
+         if (this._unsubOps) this._unsubOps();
+         if (this.remote) this.remote.terminate();
+         this.remote = null; this.core = null;
+     }
+     onChange(fn) { this.listeners.add(fn); return () => this.listeners.delete(fn); }
+     _onEvent(ev) {
+         switch (ev.kind) {
+             case "reset":
+                 this._startResync(ev.load ? "load" : "reset");
+                 return;
+             case "add":
+                 if (ev.live) return;
+                 this.dirty.add(ev.id); this._family(ev.obj); break;
+             case "finalize": case "remove":
+                 if (ev.kind === "remove") this.markGeo.delete(ev.id);
+                 this.dirty.add(ev.id); this._family(ev.obj); break;
+             case "change":
+                 if (ev.attrOnly) { this.attr.add(ev.id); break; }
+                 if (!ev.offsetsOnly) { this.geom.add(ev.id); this.markGeo.delete(ev.id); }
+                 this.dirty.add(ev.id); break;
+             case "mergeFrame": case "splitFrame":
+                 this.frameEv++;
+                 for (const id of ev.ids || []) this.dirty.add(id);
+                 break;
+             case "removeFrame":
+                 this.frameEv++;
+                 this.goneFrames.add(ev.frame);
+                 break;
+             case "addFrame":
+                 this.frameEv++;
+                 for (const o of this.doc.list(ev.frame)) this.dirty.add(o.id);
+                 break;
+             case "refile": this.frameEv++; break;
+             default: break;
+         }
+         this._schedule();
+     }
+     _family(o) {
+         if (!o || !this.patchFam.size) return;
+         const k = this.doc.editKey(o);
+         if (this.patchFam.has(k)) this.famDirty.add(k);
+     }
+     _onOp(ev) {
+         if (ev.kind === "settle") { this.frameEv++; return; }
+         if (ev.kind === "op") {
+             for (const fp of this.foot.values()) if (fp.off && !fp.drop) { fp.drop = true; this.footDirty = true; }
+             if (this.footDirty) this._schedule();
+             return;
+         }
+         if ((ev.kind === "undo" || ev.kind === "redo") && ev.op && ev.op.strokeId != null) {
+             for (const fp of this.foot.values()) {
+                 if (fp.e !== ev.op.strokeId) continue;
+                 fp.off = ev.op.op === "eraseRevert"; this.footDirty = true;
+             }
+             if (this.footDirty) this._schedule();
+             return;
+         }
+         if (ev.kind !== "bake" || !ev.step || !ev.step.removed || !ev.step.pieces) return;
+         const src = ev.step.removed.obj && ev.step.removed.obj.id;
+         if (src == null) return;
+         for (const pc of ev.step.pieces) if (pc && pc.obj) this.hints.set(pc.obj.id, src);
+         if (ev.op && ev.op.strokeId != null) this._footprint(ev.op.strokeId, ev.step);
+     }
+     _footprint(markId, step) {
+         const mark = this.doc.getById(markId);
+         const src = step.removed.obj;
+         if (!mark || !src || src.erase || src.attachRect != null) return;
+         const rs = this.geom.has(src.id) ? undefined : this.sent.get(src.id);
+         const g = rs != null ? { w: this.recW[rs] } : sceneGeom(src);
+         let eg = this.markGeo.get(markId);
+         if (!eg) { eg = sceneGeom(mark.obj); if (eg) this.markGeo.set(markId, eg); }
+         if (!g || !eg) return;
+         const HO = String(step.removed.level);
+         const f = this.lm.frameFactor(mark.level, HO);
+         const pen = mark.obj.w > 0 ? mark.obj.w : mark.obj.lwFrame;
+         if (f == null || !(pen > 0) || !(pen * f < g.w)) return;
+         const host = step.pieces.find((pc) => pc && pc.obj && String(pc.level) === HO && !pc.obj.erase);
+         if (!host) return;
+         const box = src.loops ? loopsBBox(src.loops) : null;
+         if (!box) return;
+         const w = pen * f, reach = REACH * w;
+         const boxes = [];
+         const raw = eg.raw;
+         for (let i = 0; i < raw.length; i += 5) {
+             const r = this.lm.mapRectObj({ left: raw[i], top: raw[i + 1], right: raw[i + 2], bottom: raw[i + 3] }, mark.level, HO, host.obj.below, HO, true);
+             if (!r || r.left - box.x1 > reach || box.x0 - r.right > reach || r.top - box.y1 > reach || box.y0 - r.bottom > reach) continue;
+             boxes.push(r.left, r.top, r.right, r.bottom, raw[i + 4] * f);
+         }
+         if (!boxes.length) return;
+         const fr = this.lm.frame(HO);
+         this.foot.set(host.obj.id, { id: host.obj.id, e: markId, host: host.obj.id, key: this.doc.editKey(host.obj), home: HO, depth: fr ? fr.depth : null, w, boxes, off: false, raw: null, t: null });
+         this.footDirty = true;
+         this._capFoot();
+     }
+     _capFoot() {
+         let n = 0, c = 0;
+         for (const fp of this.foot.values()) if (!fp.drop) { n++; c += fp.boxes.length / 5; }
+         for (const fp of this.foot.values()) {
+             if (n <= this.footMax && c <= this.footChunks) break;
+             if (fp.drop) continue;
+             fp.drop = true; n--; c -= fp.boxes.length / 5; this.footDirty = true;
+         }
+     }
+     _footItems(items) {
+         for (const fp of [...this.foot.values()]) {
+             const id = -fp.id, s = this.fsent.get(id);
+             if (fp.drop) {
+                 if (s) { items.push({ id, gone: true }); this.fsent.delete(id); }
+                 this.foot.delete(fp.id);
+                 continue;
+             }
+             let h = !fp.off && this.doc.getById(fp.host);
+             if (h && String(h.level) !== fp.home) {
+                 const to = this.lm.frame(String(h.level));
+                 if (fp.depth == null) { const was = this.lm.frame(fp.home); fp.depth = was ? was.depth : null; }
+                 if (to && to.depth === fp.depth) fp.home = String(h.level);
+             }
+             if (!fp.off && (!h || String(h.level) !== fp.home)) {
+                 const t0 = h ? residual(h.obj.below, 0) : null;
+                 const heir = this.doc.editGroup(fp.key).find((r) => String(r.level) === fp.home && !r.obj.erase
+                     && String(residual(r.obj.below, 0)) === String(t0 || fp.t || null));
+                 if (heir) { fp.host = heir.obj.id; h = heir; } else h = null;
+             }
+             if (!h || !fp.boxes.length) {
+                 if (s) { items.push({ id, gone: true }); this.fsent.delete(id); }
+                 if (!fp.off && !h) this.foot.delete(fp.id);
+                 continue;
+             }
+             const t = residual(h.obj.below, 0);
+             fp.t = t ? [t[0], t[1]] : null;
+             const tx = t ? t[0] : 0, ty = t ? t[1] : 0;
+             if (!fp.raw) fp.raw = Float64Array.from(fp.boxes);
+             if (s && s.raw === fp.raw && s.home === fp.home && s.tx === tx && s.ty === ty) continue;
+             const rec = { id, home: fp.home, w: fp.w, t: t ? [tx, ty] : null };
+             if (!s || s.raw !== fp.raw) rec.raw = fp.raw;
+             items.push(rec);
+             this.fsent.set(id, { raw: fp.raw, home: fp.home, tx, ty });
+         }
+         this.footDirty = false;
+     }
+     _cancel() {
+         if (this.timer != null) { clearTimeout(this.timer); this.timer = null; }
+         if (this.idleId != null && typeof cancelIdleCallback === "function") cancelIdleCallback(this.idleId);
+         this.idleId = null;
+     }
+     _schedule(delay = 30) {
+         if (this.destroyed || this.timer != null || this.idleId != null) return;
+         if (typeof requestIdleCallback === "function") {
+             this.idleId = requestIdleCallback((dl) => {
+                 this.idleId = null;
+                 const t0 = now();
+                 this._tick(dl);
+                 if (this.E && this.E._perf) this.E._perf("sceneTick", t0, false);
+             }, { timeout: SCENE_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS });
+         } else {
+             this.timer = setTimeout(() => { this.timer = null; this._tick(null); }, delay);
+         }
+     }
+     _busy() {
+         const E = this.E;
+         if (E._drawing || E._dragSel || E._lasso || E._destroyed) return true;
+         if (E._panLast || (E._camBusy && E._camBusy())) return true;
+         if (this.waitSince && now() - this.waitSince > MAX_WAIT_MS) return false;
+         if (E._cutInflight) return true;
+         if (E.raster) {
+             try { const r = rasterWorkerReport(); if (r.queued || r.inFlight) return true; } catch (err) {  }
+         }
+         return false;
+     }
+     _tick(deadline) {
+         if (this.destroyed || this.inflight) return;
+         if (this._busy()) {
+             if (!this.waitSince) this.waitSince = now();
+             this.timer = setTimeout(() => { this.timer = null; this._schedule(); }, 150);
+             return;
+         }
+         this.waitSince = 0;
+         const budget = deadline && deadline.timeRemaining ? Math.max(4, Math.min(SLICE_MS, deadline.timeRemaining())) : SLICE_MS;
+         if (this.resync) {
+             if (!this._resyncWanted()) return;
+             if (!this._resyncStep(budget)) this._schedule();
+             return;
+         }
+         if (!this.pending) {
+             if (!this._hasWork()) return;
+             this._takeGone();
+             this.pending = { ids: [...this.dirty], i: 0, items: [], fev: this.frameEv };
+             this.dirty.clear();
+         }
+         if (!this._prepStep(this.pending, budget)) { this._schedule(); return; }
+         const p = this.pending;
+         this.pending = null;
+         this._sendPrepared(p);
+     }
+     _sendPrepared(p) {
+         if (p.fev !== this.frameEv) { this._takeGone(); if (this.dirty.size) this._fold(p); }
+         if (this.attr.size) {
+             const has = new Set(p.items.map((r) => r.id));
+             for (const id of this.attr) if (!has.has(id) && this.sent.has(id)) p.items.push({ id, attr: true });
+             this.attr.clear();
+         }
+         if (this.foot.size) this._footItems(p.items);
+         this._send({ frames: this._frameDiff(), items: p.items, renames: this.renames.splice(0) }, p.ids);
+     }
+     _hasWork() {
+         return !!(this.dirty.size || this.attr.size || this.goneFrames.size || this.famDirty.size || this.renames.length || this.footDirty || this._framesChanged());
+     }
+     _takeGone() {
+         if (this.goneFrames.size) {
+             const gone = new Set();
+             for (const f of this.goneFrames) { const h = this.homes.get(f); if (h != null) gone.add(h); }
+             if (gone.size) for (const [id, rs] of this.sent) if (gone.has(this.recHO[rs])) this.dirty.add(id);
+             this.goneFrames.clear();
+         }
+         for (const k of this.famDirty) for (const r of this.doc.editGroup(k)) if (r.obj.attachRect != null) this.dirty.add(r.obj.id);
+         this.famDirty.clear();
+     }
+     _fold(st) {
+         const f = { ids: [...this.dirty], i: 0, items: [] };
+         this.dirty.clear();
+         this._prepStep(f, Infinity);
+         const at = new Map();
+         st.items.forEach((r, i) => at.set(r.id, i));
+         for (const r of f.items) {
+             const i = at.get(r.id);
+             if (i == null) { at.set(r.id, st.items.length); st.items.push(r); continue; }
+             const was = st.items[i], m = { ...r };
+             if (!r.gone && !was.gone) {
+                 if (!m.raw && was.raw) { m.raw = was.raw; if (was.loops) m.loops = was.loops; }
+                 if (m.hint == null && was.hint != null) m.hint = was.hint;
+             }
+             st.items[i] = m;
+         }
+         for (const id of f.ids) st.ids.push(id);
+     }
+     _resyncWanted() {
+         if (this._forced) return true;
+         if (this._mode() === "worker") return true;
+         let n = 0;
+         for (const L of this.doc.levels()) n += this.doc.count(L);
+         return n <= INLINE_AUTO_MAX;
+     }
+     _mode() {
+         if (this.remote) return "worker";
+         if (this.core) return "inline";
+         if (this.useWorker) { this.remote = openSceneWorker(); if (this.remote) return "worker"; }
+         this.core = new SceneIndex();
+         return "inline";
+     }
+     _prepStep(st, budget) {
+         const t0 = now();
+         const doc = this.doc;
+         while (st.i < st.ids.length) {
+             const id = st.ids[st.i++];
+             const rec = doc.getById(id);
+             const o = rec && rec.obj;
+             const s = this.sent.get(id);
+             if (o && o.attachRect != null && !o.erase) this.patchFam.add(doc.editKey(o));
+             const ink = isInk(doc, o), fresh = s === undefined || this.geom.has(id);
+             const g = ink && fresh ? sceneGeom(o) : null;
+             this.geom.delete(id);
+             if (!ink || (fresh && !g)) {
+                 if (s !== undefined) { st.items.push({ id, gone: true }); this.sent.delete(id); this.recFree.push(s); }
+                 this.hints.delete(id);
+             } else {
+                 const t = residual(o.below, 0);
+                 const tx = t ? t[0] : 0, ty = t ? t[1] : 0, home = o._home, ho = this._homeIx(home);
+                 const w = g ? g.w : this.recW[s];
+                 if (fresh || this.recW[s] !== w || this.recHO[s] !== ho || this.recTX[s] !== tx || this.recTY[s] !== ty) {
+                     const r = { id, home, w, t: t ? [tx, ty] : null };
+                     if (fresh) { r.raw = g.raw; if (g.loops) r.loops = g.loops; }
+                     const h = this.hints.get(id);
+                     if (s === undefined && h != null) r.hint = h;
+                     st.items.push(r);
+                     const k = s !== undefined ? s : this._recSlot();
+                     this.recW[k] = w; this.recTX[k] = tx; this.recTY[k] = ty; this.recHO[k] = ho;
+                     if (s === undefined) this.sent.set(id, k);
+                 }
+                 this.hints.delete(id);
+             }
+             if ((st.i & 63) === 0 && now() - t0 > budget) break;
+         }
+         this.stats.prepMs += now() - t0;
+         this.stats.lastPrepMs = (st.prepMs = (st.prepMs || 0) + (now() - t0));
+         return st.i >= st.ids.length;
+     }
+     _recSlot() {
+         if (this.recFree.length) return this.recFree.pop();
+         const k = this.recTop++;
+         if (k >= this.recW.length) {
+             const n = this.recW.length * 2, grow = (A) => { const B = new A.constructor(n); B.set(A); return B; };
+             this.recW = grow(this.recW); this.recTX = grow(this.recTX); this.recTY = grow(this.recTY); this.recHO = grow(this.recHO);
+         }
+         return k;
+     }
+     _homeIx(home) {
+         let h = this.homes.get(home);
+         if (h == null) { h = this.homeOf.length; this.homes.set(home, h); this.homeOf.push(home); }
+         return h;
+     }
+     _frameRec(f) {
+         return { id: f.id, parent: f.parent == null ? null : f.parent, depth: f.depth,
+             cx: f.centre ? f.centre.x : 0, cy: f.centre ? f.centre.y : 0,
+             sx: f.shift ? f.shift[0] : 0, sy: f.shift ? f.shift[1] : 0 };
+     }
+     _sameFrame(r, f) {
+         return !!r && r.parent === (f.parent == null ? null : f.parent) && r.depth === f.depth
+             && r.cx === (f.centre ? f.centre.x : 0) && r.cy === (f.centre ? f.centre.y : 0)
+             && r.sx === (f.shift ? f.shift[0] : 0) && r.sy === (f.shift ? f.shift[1] : 0);
+     }
+     _framesChanged() {
+         if (this.frameKeys.size !== this.lm.frames.size) return true;
+         for (const f of this.lm.frames.values()) if (!this._sameFrame(this.frameKeys.get(f.id), f)) return true;
+         return false;
+     }
+     _frameDiff() {
+         const out = [];
+         for (const f of this.lm.frames.values()) {
+             if (this._sameFrame(this.frameKeys.get(f.id), f)) continue;
+             const r = this._frameRec(f);
+             out.push(r); this.frameKeys.set(f.id, { ...r });
+         }
+         for (const id of [...this.frameKeys.keys()]) if (!this.lm.frames.has(id)) { out.push({ id, gone: true }); this.frameKeys.delete(id); }
+         return out;
+     }
+     _startResync(kind, state) {
+         this._cancel();
+         this.gen++;
+         this.inflight = null;
+         this.pending = null;
+         this.dirty.clear(); this.attr.clear(); this.goneFrames.clear(); this.famDirty.clear(); this.patchFam.clear();
+         this.hints.clear(); this.frameKeys.clear();
+         this.sent.clear(); this.recTop = 0; this.recFree = []; this.geom.clear(); this.fsent.clear(); this.markGeo.clear();
+         this.resync = { kind, ids: null, i: 0, items: [], fev: this.frameEv, state: kind === "reset" ? (state !== undefined ? state : this.export) : undefined };
+         if (kind === "load") { this.export = null; this.foot = new Map(); }
+         this._schedule();
+     }
+     _loadFoot(list) {
+         this.foot = new Map();
+         for (const e of Array.isArray(list) ? list : []) {
+             if (!e || !Number.isInteger(e.id) || !Array.isArray(e.raw) || e.raw.length % 5 || !(e.w > 0)) continue;
+             const fr = this.lm.frame(String(e.home));
+             this.foot.set(e.id, { id: e.id, e: e.e, host: e.host, key: e.key, home: String(e.home), depth: fr ? fr.depth : null, w: e.w, boxes: e.raw.slice(), off: !!e.off, raw: null, t: null });
+         }
+         this._capFoot();
+         this.footDirty = this.foot.size > 0;
+     }
+     _resyncStep(budget) {
+         const r = this.resync;
+         if (r.kind === "load" && !r.footRead) {
+             r.footRead = true;
+             const saved = this.E.docMeta && this.E.docMeta.sceneIndex;
+             this._loadFoot(saved && saved.erase);
+         }
+         if (!r.ids) {
+             r.ids = [];
+             for (const L of this.doc.levels()) for (const o of this.doc.list(L)) r.ids.push(o.id);
+             this.dirty.clear();
+         }
+         if (!this._prepStep(r, budget)) return false;
+         if (r.fev !== this.frameEv && this.dirty.size) this._fold(r);
+         this.resync = null;
+         const restore = {};
+         const meta = this.E.docMeta || {};
+         const saved = r.kind === "load" ? meta.sceneIndex : r.state || meta.sceneIndex;
+         if (saved && saved.v === 3 && Array.isArray(saved.scenes)) restore.state = saved;
+         else {
+             const old = (meta.scenes || []).filter((s) => !s.kind && /^s\d+$/.test(String(s.id))).map((s) => {
+                 const f = this.lm.frameFor(s.level);
+                 const seq = parseInt(String(s.id).replace(/^s/, ""), 10);
+                 return f && s.rect ? { id: s.id, seq: Number.isFinite(seq) ? seq : 0, name: s.name, named: !!s.pinned, anchor: f.id, rect: { ...s.rect } } : null;
+             }).filter(Boolean);
+             restore.legacy = old;
+             restore.seq = Math.max(meta.sceneSeq || 1, ...old.map((s) => s.seq + 1), 1);
+         }
+         this.frameKeys.clear();
+         const frames = this._frameDiff();
+         if (this.foot.size) this._footItems(r.items);
+         this.stats.restores++;
+         this._send({ restore, frames, items: r.items, renames: this.renames.splice(0) }, r.ids);
+         return true;
+     }
+     _send(batch, ids) {
+         const mode = this._mode();
+         this.stats.batches++;
+         this.stats.lastItems = batch.items.length;
+         this.stats.items += batch.items.length;
+         if (mode === "inline") {
+             let out;
+             try { out = this.core.apply(batch); } catch (err) { this._failed(err, batch, ids); return; }
+             this._landed(out);
+             return;
+         }
+         const gen = this.gen;
+         this.inflight = { ids, gen, restore: !!batch.restore, state: batch.restore ? batch.restore.state || null : null, t0: now() };
+         this.remote.post(batch).then((out) => {
+             if (this.destroyed || gen !== this.gen) return;
+             this.inflight = null;
+             this._landed(out);
+         }, (err) => {
+             if (this.destroyed || gen !== this.gen) return;
+             this.inflight = null;
+             try { if (this.remote) this.remote.terminate(); } catch (e) {  }
+             this.remote = null;
+             this.workerFails = (this.workerFails || 0) + 1;
+             if (this.workerFails > SCENE_WORKER_RESPAWNS) this.useWorker = false;
+             this._failed(err, batch, ids);
+         });
+     }
+     _failed(err, batch, ids) {
+         try { console.warn("scene index failed; rebuilding", err); } catch (e) {  }
+         this.core = null;
+         if (batch && batch.restore) { this._startResync("reset", batch.restore.state || null); return; }
+         const base = this.export || (this.E.docMeta && this.E.docMeta.sceneIndex) || null;
+         const again = new Set(base && base.dirty ? base.dirty : []);
+         for (const id of ids || []) again.add(id);
+         for (const id of this.dirty) again.add(id);
+         if (this.pending) for (const id of this.pending.ids) again.add(id);
+         const state = base ? { ...base, dirty: [...again].filter((id) => Number.isInteger(id) && id >= 0) } : null;
+         this._startResync("reset", state);
+     }
+     _landed(out) {
+         if (!out) return;
+         this.export = out.export;
+         this.stats.lastApplyMs = out.ms || 0;
+         this.stats.applyMs += out.ms || 0;
+         this.stats.lastStats = out.stats;
+         this.E.docMeta = { ...this.E.docMeta, scenes: out.scenes };
+         for (const fn of this.listeners) { try { fn(out); } catch (err) {  } }
+         if (this._owed()) this._schedule();
+     }
+     _owed() {
+         return !!(this.resync || this.pending || this._hasWork());
+     }
+     flushNow() {
+         if (this.destroyed) return false;
+         const E = this.E;
+         if (E._drawing || E._dragSel || E._lasso) { this._schedule(); return false; }
+         this._forced = true;
+         try {
+             if (this._mode() !== "inline") { this._cancel(); this._schedule(0); return false; }
+             for (let guard = 0; guard < 1000; guard++) {
+                 if (this.resync) { this._resyncStep(Infinity); continue; }
+                 if (this.pending) {
+                     this._prepStep(this.pending, Infinity);
+                     const p = this.pending; this.pending = null;
+                     this._sendPrepared(p);
+                     continue;
+                 }
+                 if (this._hasWork()) {
+                     this._takeGone();
+                     const st = { ids: [...this.dirty], i: 0, items: [], fev: this.frameEv };
+                     this.dirty.clear();
+                     this._prepStep(st, Infinity);
+                     this._sendPrepared(st);
+                     continue;
+                 }
+                 break;
+             }
+             return true;
+         } finally { this._forced = false; }
+     }
+     rename(id, name) {
+         const list = this.E.docMeta.scenes || [];
+         const s = list.find((x) => x.id === id);
+         if (!s || typeof name !== "string" || !name.trim()) return false;
+         const nm = name.trim().slice(0, 120);
+         this.E.docMeta = { ...this.E.docMeta, scenes: list.map((x) => (x.id === id ? { ...x, name: nm, pinned: true, auto: false } : x)) };
+         this.renames.push([id, nm]);
+         this._schedule(0);
+         return true;
+     }
+     exportForSave() {
+         const base = this.export || (this.E.docMeta && this.E.docMeta.sceneIndex) || null;
+         if (!base) return null;
+         const dirty = new Set(base.dirty || []);
+         for (const id of this.dirty) dirty.add(id);
+         if (this.pending) for (const id of this.pending.ids) dirty.add(id);
+         if (this.inflight && !this.inflight.restore) for (const id of this.inflight.ids || []) dirty.add(id);
+         const rs = this.resync ? this.resync.state : this.inflight && this.inflight.restore ? this.inflight.state : null;
+         if (rs && rs.dirty) for (const id of rs.dirty) dirty.add(id);
+         let scenes = base.scenes;
+         if (this.renames.length) {
+             const names = new Map(this.renames);
+             scenes = scenes.map((s) => (names.has(s.id) ? { ...s, name: names.get(s.id), named: true } : s));
+         }
+         const erase = [...this.foot.values()].filter((fp) => !fp.drop && fp.boxes.length).map((fp) => {
+             const e = { id: fp.id, e: fp.e, host: fp.host, key: fp.key, home: fp.home, w: fp.w, raw: fp.boxes.slice() };
+             if (fp.off) e.off = true;
+             return e;
+         });
+         return { ...base, scenes, dirty: [...dirty].filter((id) => Number.isInteger(id) && id >= 0), erase };
+     }
+ }
//// src/engine/sceneIndex.js  (new file)
+ import { R } from "./frameLattice";
+ export const REF_STROKE_PX = 2.5;
+ export const JOIN_PX = 2250;
+ export const ZOOM_SPREAD = 16;
+ export const CHUNK_PX = 1500;
+ export const STRAY_RUN_PX = 3000;
+ export const MAX_SPAN_PX = 4500;
+ export const MAX_PARTS = 12;
+ export const MAX_SIBLINGS = 20;
+ export const FRAME_PAD = 0.1;
+ export const MATCH_OVERLAP = 0.5;
+ export const ECHO_ZOOM = 3;
+ export const ECHO_UP = 8;
+ export const REACH = JOIN_PX / REF_STROKE_PX;
+ export const CHUNK = CHUNK_PX / REF_STROKE_PX;
+ export const STRAY_CHUNKS = Math.round(STRAY_RUN_PX / CHUNK_PX);
+ export const SPREAD_OCT = Math.log2(ZOOM_SPREAD);
+ export const MAX_CHUNKS = 2048;
+ export const TOMB_MAX = 256;
+ const LOG_R = Math.log2(R);
+ const BANDS = Math.ceil(SPREAD_OCT) + 1;
+ const EPS_L = 1e-9;
+ const XTX = 0, XTY = 1, XW = 2, XL = 3, XMASS = 4, XFMASS = 5, XFL = 6, XT = 7;
+ const ECHO_LOG = Math.log2(ECHO_ZOOM);
+ const frameLog = (f, depth) => Math.log2(Math.max(f.w, f.h)) - LOG_R * depth;
+ const SEEDS_MAX = 32;
+ const OFF = 2 ** 25, KEYM = 2 ** 26;
+ const cellIx = (v) => { const i = Math.floor(v); return i < -OFF ? -OFF : i >= OFF ? OFF - 1 : i; };
+ export const fitsSpread = (lo, hi) => hi - lo <= SPREAD_OCT + EPS_L;
+ const now = () => (typeof performance !== "undefined" ? performance.now() : Date.now());
+ export class FrameTable {
+     constructor() { this.cur = new Map(); this.prev = null; this._maps = new Map(); this._old = new Map(); }
+     get(id) { return this.cur.get(id); }
+     depth(id) { const f = this.cur.get(id); return f ? f.depth : null; }
+     update(recs) {
+         const moved = new Set(), shifted = new Set(), gone = new Set();
+         this.prev = null;
+         if (!recs || !recs.length) return { moved, shifted, gone };
+         this.prev = new Map();
+         for (const r of recs) {
+             const was = this.cur.get(r.id) || null;
+             if (r.gone) { if (was) { this.prev.set(r.id, was); this.cur.delete(r.id); gone.add(r.id); } continue; }
+             const f = { id: r.id, parent: r.parent == null ? null : r.parent, depth: r.depth,
+                 cx: r.cx || 0, cy: r.cy || 0, sx: r.sx || 0, sy: r.sy || 0 };
+             if (was && was.parent === f.parent && was.depth === f.depth && was.cx === f.cx && was.cy === f.cy) {
+                 if (was.sx !== f.sx || was.sy !== f.sy) { shifted.add(f.id); this.prev.set(f.id, was); this.cur.set(f.id, f); }
+                 continue;
+             }
+             this.prev.set(r.id, was);
+             this.cur.set(r.id, f);
+             if (was) moved.add(r.id);
+         }
+         this._maps.clear(); this._old.clear();
+         return { moved, shifted, gone };
+     }
+     settle() { this.prev = null; this._old.clear(); }
+     _rec(id, old) {
+         if (old && this.prev && this.prev.has(id)) return this.prev.get(id);
+         return this.cur.get(id);
+     }
+     parentOf(id, old = false) { const f = this._rec(id, old); return f ? f.parent : null; }
+     under(id, roots) {
+         if (!roots || !roots.size) return false;
+         let f = this.cur.get(id);
+         for (let g = 0; f && g < 4096; g++) { if (roots.has(f.id)) return true; f = f.parent != null ? this.cur.get(f.parent) : null; }
+         return false;
+     }
+     _up(id, depth, old) {
+         let f = this._rec(id, old), s = 1, ox = 0, oy = 0;
+         while (f && f.depth > depth) {
+             if (f.parent == null) return null;
+             s /= R; ox = ox / R + f.cx; oy = oy / R + f.cy;
+             f = this._rec(f.parent, old);
+         }
+         return f && f.depth === depth ? { f, s, ox, oy } : null;
+     }
+     _across(a, b, old) {
+         const ca = [], cb = [];
+         let fa = a, fb = b;
+         for (let g = 0; fa !== fb; g++) {
+             if (!fa || !fb || fa.parent == null || fb.parent == null || g > 4096) return null;
+             ca.push(fa); cb.push(fb);
+             fa = this._rec(fa.parent, old); fb = this._rec(fb.parent, old);
+         }
+         let ox = 0, oy = 0;
+         for (let i = ca.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) { ox = (ox + ca[i].cx - cb[i].cx) * R; oy = (oy + ca[i].cy - cb[i].cy) * R; }
+         return [ox, oy];
+     }
+     map(from, to, old = false) {
+         const useOld = !!(old && this.prev && this.prev.size);
+         const cache = useOld ? this._old : this._maps;
+         const key = from + "\u0001" + to;
+         const hit = cache.get(key);
+         if (hit !== undefined) return hit;
+         let out = null;
+         const a = this._rec(from, useOld), b = this._rec(to, useOld);
+         if (a && b) {
+             const d = Math.min(a.depth, b.depth);
+             const A = this._up(from, d, useOld), B = this._up(to, d, useOld);
+             const off = A && B ? this._across(A.f, B.f, useOld) : null;
+             if (off) out = { s: A.s / B.s, ox: (A.ox + off[0] - B.ox) / B.s, oy: (A.oy + off[1] - B.oy) / B.s };
+         }
+         cache.set(key, out);
+         return out;
+     }
+ }
+ export function encodeIds(ids) {
+     const s = [...ids].sort((a, b) => a - b);
+     const out = [];
+     let prev = 0;
+     for (let i = 0; i < s.length;) {
+         let j = i;
+         while (j + 1 < s.length && s[j + 1] === s[j] + 1) j++;
+         const gap = (s[i] - prev).toString(36);
+         out.push(j > i ? gap + "." + (j - i).toString(36) : gap);
+         prev = s[j];
+         i = j + 1;
+     }
+     return out.join(",");
+ }
+ export function decodeIds(str, max = MAX_IDS) {
+     const out = [];
+     if (!str) return out;
+     let prev = 0;
+     for (const tok of String(str).split(",")) {
+         const [g, e] = tok.split(".");
+         const a = prev + parseInt(g, 36);
+         const n = e ? parseInt(e, 36) : 0;
+         if (!Number.isFinite(a) || !Number.isFinite(n) || n < 0 || out.length + n + 1 > max) return out;
+         for (let k = 0; k <= n; k++) out.push(a + k);
+         prev = a + n;
+     }
+     return out;
+ }
+ export const rectCover = (t, r) => {
+     const ix = Math.max(0, Math.min(t.x + t.w, r.x + r.w) - Math.max(t.x, r.x));
+     const iy = Math.max(0, Math.min(t.y + t.h, r.y + r.h) - Math.max(t.y, r.y));
+     const a = t.w * t.h;
+     return a > 0 ? (ix * iy) / a : 0;
+ };
+ const grow = (bb, P, o0, n) => {
+     for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) {
+         const o = o0 + 4 * k;
+         if (P[o] < bb[0]) bb[0] = P[o]; if (P[o + 1] < bb[1]) bb[1] = P[o + 1];
+         if (P[o + 2] > bb[2]) bb[2] = P[o + 2]; if (P[o + 3] > bb[3]) bb[3] = P[o + 3];
+     }
+ };
+ const EMPTY_BB = () => [Infinity, Infinity, -Infinity, -Infinity];
+ export const MAX_IDS = 1e7;
+ const newTag = () => Math.floor(Math.random() * 2176782336).toString(36);
+ const keyOf = (S) => (S.vtag ? S.vtag + "-" : "") + S.version.toString(36);
+ const rowKey = (R) => (R.members.length === 1 ? keyOf(R.S) : "e" + fnv(R.members.map(keyOf).join(".") + "|" + R.shown.idn.id));
+ const fnv = (str) => { let h = 0x811c9dc5; for (let i = 0; i < str.length; i++) { h ^= str.charCodeAt(i); h = Math.imul(h, 0x01000193) >>> 0; } return h.toString(36); };
+ const newGrid = () => ({ all: new Map(), own: new Map(), P: new Uint32Array(64), top: 0, waste: 0, live: 0 });
+ class CellTab {
+     constructor(cap = 8) { this.keys = new Float64Array(cap).fill(-1); this.segs = new Int32Array(cap); this.mask = cap - 1; this.n = 0; }
+     _h(key) { const hi = Math.floor(key / KEYM), lo = key - hi * KEYM; return (Math.imul(hi, 0x9E3779B1) ^ Math.imul(lo, 0x85EBCA77)) & this.mask; }
+     find(key) {
+         const K = this.keys;
+         for (let i = this._h(key); ; i = (i + 1) & this.mask) { const k = K[i]; if (k === key) return this.segs[i]; if (k < 0) return -1; }
+     }
+     set(key, seg) {
+         if ((this.n + 1) * 2 > this.mask + 1) this._grow();
+         const K = this.keys;
+         let i = this._h(key);
+         while (K[i] >= 0 && K[i] !== key) i = (i + 1) & this.mask;
+         if (K[i] < 0) { this.n++; K[i] = key; }
+         this.segs[i] = seg;
+     }
+     _grow() {
+         const K = this.keys, V = this.segs;
+         const t = new CellTab((this.mask + 1) * 2);
+         for (let i = 0; i < K.length; i++) if (K[i] >= 0) t.set(K[i], V[i]);
+         this.keys = t.keys; this.segs = t.segs; this.mask = t.mask; this.n = t.n;
+     }
+ }
+ function segAlloc(G, cap) {
+     const need = G.top + 2 + cap;
+     if (need > G.P.length) { const P = new Uint32Array(Math.max(need, G.P.length * 2)); P.set(G.P.subarray(0, G.top)); G.P = P; }
+     const s = G.top;
+     G.P[s] = cap; G.P[s + 1] = 0; G.top = need;
+     return s;
+ }
+ const roomFor = (len) => len + (len >> 2) + 1;
+ function gridCompact(G) {
+     let need = 0;
+     const tabs = [...G.all.values(), ...G.own.values()];
+     for (const t of tabs) for (let i = 0; i < t.keys.length; i++) if (t.keys[i] >= 0) { const len = G.P[t.segs[i] + 1]; if (len) need += 2 + roomFor(len); }
+     const P = new Uint32Array(Math.max(64, need));
+     let top = 0;
+     const redo = (bands) => {
+         for (const [b, t] of bands) {
+             let n = 0;
+             for (let i = 0; i < t.keys.length; i++) if (t.keys[i] >= 0 && G.P[t.segs[i] + 1]) n++;
+             let cap = 8; while (cap < 2 * n) cap *= 2;
+             const u = new CellTab(cap);
+             for (let i = 0; i < t.keys.length; i++) {
+                 if (t.keys[i] < 0) continue;
+                 const s = t.segs[i], len = G.P[s + 1];
+                 if (!len) continue;
+                 const cap = roomFor(len);
+                 P[top] = cap; P[top + 1] = len;
+                 P.set(G.P.subarray(s + 2, s + 2 + len), top + 2);
+                 u.set(t.keys[i], top);
+                 top += 2 + cap;
+             }
+             if (u.n) bands.set(b, u); else bands.delete(b);
+         }
+     };
+     const old = G.P;
+     redo(G.all); redo(G.own);
+     void old;
+     let live = 0;
+     for (const t of [...G.all.values(), ...G.own.values()]) for (let i = 0; i < t.keys.length; i++) if (t.keys[i] >= 0) live += 2 + P[t.segs[i] + 1];
+     G.P = P; G.top = top; G.waste = 0; G.live = live;
+ }
+ const padInk = (r, wmin) => {
+     let x = r[0], y = r[1], w = r[2] - r[0], h = r[3] - r[1];
+     if (w < wmin) { x -= (wmin - w) / 2; w = wmin; }
+     if (h < wmin) { y -= (wmin - h) / 2; h = wmin; }
+     const pad = FRAME_PAD * Math.max(w, h);
+     return { x: x - pad, y: y - pad, w: w + 2 * pad, h: h + 2 * pad };
+ };
+ export function cutParts(pts, limit) {
+     const box = (ps) => { const b = EMPTY_BB(); for (const q of ps) { if (q.x0 < b[0]) b[0] = q.x0; if (q.y0 < b[1]) b[1] = q.y0; if (q.x1 > b[2]) b[2] = q.x1; if (q.y1 > b[3]) b[3] = q.y1; } return b; };
+     const side = (b) => Math.max(b[2] - b[0], b[3] - b[1]);
+     const done = [], open = [{ ps: pts, b: box(pts), depth: 0, path: "" }];
+     let n = 1;
+     while (open.length) {
+         let bi = 0;
+         for (let i = 1; i < open.length; i++) if (side(open[i].b) > side(open[bi].b)) bi = i;
+         const P = open.splice(bi, 1)[0], b = P.b, w = b[2] - b[0], h = b[3] - b[1];
+         if (Math.max(w, h) <= limit || P.depth >= 12 || P.ps.length < 2 || n >= MAX_PARTS) { done.push(P); continue; }
+         const ax = w >= h ? 0 : 1, lo = b[ax] + 0.2 * (ax ? h : w), span = 0.6 * (ax ? h : w);
+         const BINS = 32, bins = new Float64Array(BINS);
+         for (const q of P.ps) {
+             const c = ax ? (q.y0 + q.y1) / 2 : (q.x0 + q.x1) / 2;
+             const i = Math.floor(((c - lo) / span) * BINS);
+             if (i >= 0 && i < BINS) bins[i] += q.mass;
+         }
+         let at = 0;
+         for (let i = 1; i < BINS; i++) if (bins[i] < bins[at] || (bins[i] === bins[at] && Math.abs(i - BINS / 2) < Math.abs(at - BINS / 2))) at = i;
+         const cutAt = lo + ((at + 0.5) / BINS) * span;
+         const A = [], B = [];
+         for (const q of P.ps) ((ax ? (q.y0 + q.y1) / 2 : (q.x0 + q.x1) / 2) < cutAt ? A : B).push(q);
+         if (!A.length || !B.length) { done.push(P); continue; }
+         open.push({ ps: A, b: box(A), depth: P.depth + 1, path: P.path + "0" }, { ps: B, b: box(B), depth: P.depth + 1, path: P.path + "1" });
+         n++;
+     }
+     return done.sort((a, b) => (a.path < b.path ? -1 : a.path > b.path ? 1 : 0)).map((P) => P.b);
+ }
+ const ONE_LOOP = Object.freeze([0]);
+ const loopStarts = (loops, n) => {
+     if (!Array.isArray(loops) || !loops.length) return null;
+     if (loops.length === 1 && loops[0] === 0) return ONE_LOOP;
+     const v = loops.filter((a) => a >= 0 && a < n);
+     return v.length ? v : null;
+ };
+ export function chunkRuns(n, loops, f) {
+     const out = [];
+     const segs = loops && loops.length ? loops.map((a, i) => [a, i + 1 < loops.length ? loops[i + 1] : n]) : null;
+     if (!segs) {
+         let cur = null;
+         for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) { if (f(k)) { if (!cur) { cur = []; out.push(cur); } cur.push(k); } else cur = null; }
+         return out;
+     }
+     for (const [a, b] of segs) {
+         const len = b - a;
+         if (len <= 0) continue;
+         let p = -1;
+         for (let k = a; k < b; k++) if (!f(k)) { p = k; break; }
+         if (p < 0) { const all = []; for (let k = a; k < b; k++) all.push(k); out.push(all); continue; }
+         let cur = null;
+         for (let t = 1; t <= len; t++) {
+             const k = a + ((p - a + t) % len);
+             if (f(k)) { if (!cur) { cur = []; out.push(cur); } cur.push(k); } else cur = null;
+         }
+     }
+     return out;
+ }
+ function fileIn(G, bands, b, i0, i1, j0, j1, e, add) {
+     let t = bands.get(b);
+     if (!t) { if (!add) return; t = new CellTab(); bands.set(b, t); }
+     for (let i = i0; i <= i1; i++) {
+         for (let j = j0; j <= j1; j++) {
+             const key = (i + OFF) * KEYM + (j + OFF);
+             let s = t.find(key);
+             if (add) {
+                 if (s < 0) { s = segAlloc(G, 2); t.set(key, s); G.live += 2; }
+                 else if (G.P[s + 1] === G.P[s]) {
+                     const len = G.P[s + 1], s2 = segAlloc(G, 2 * len);
+                     G.P.copyWithin(s2 + 2, s + 2, s + 2 + len); G.P[s2 + 1] = len;
+                     G.waste += 2 + G.P[s];
+                     t.set(key, s2); s = s2;
+                 }
+                 const P = G.P;
+                 P[s + 2 + P[s + 1]] = e; P[s + 1]++; G.live++;
+             } else if (s >= 0) {
+                 const P = G.P, end = s + 2 + P[s + 1];
+                 for (let q = s + 2; q < end; q++) if (P[q] === e) { P.copyWithin(q, q + 1, end); P[s + 1]--; G.live--; break; }
+             }
+         }
+     }
+ }
+ export class SceneIndex {
+     constructor() { this._reset(); this.seq = 1; this.ver = 1; this.pseq = 1; this.rseq = 1; this.batchNo = 0; this.seedsMax = SEEDS_MAX; this.vtag = newTag(); this.autoCompact = true; }
+     compact() { this._compact(); }
+     _reset() {
+         this.frames = new FrameTable();
+         this.items = new Map();
+         this.scenes = new Set();
+         this.byId = new Map();
+         this.buckets = new Map();
+         this.slots = [];
+         this._free = [];
+         this.RP = new Float64Array(1024); this.rTop = 0; this.rW = 0;
+         this.LP = new Float64Array(1024); this.lTop = 0; this.lW = 0;
+         this.VP = new Uint8Array(1024); this.vTop = 0; this.vW = 0;
+         this.XD = new Float64Array(8 * 1024);
+         this.tombs = [];
+         this._tombClock = 0;
+         this._stamp = 0;
+         this.regions = new Map();
+         this.regionOf = new Map();
+         this.rows = [];
+         this.rowOf = new Map();
+         this.stats = { queries: 0, cells: 0, entries: 0 };
+     }
+     _depth(id) { const d = this.frames.depth(id); return d == null ? 0 : d; }
+     _reserve(items) {
+         let floats = 0, fresh = 0;
+         for (const r of items || []) if (r.raw && !r.gone && !this.items.has(r.id)) { floats += 5 * Math.min((r.raw.length / 5) | 0, MAX_CHUNKS); fresh++; }
+         if (!fresh) return;
+         if (this.rTop + floats > this.RP.length) { const Q = new Float64Array(this.rTop + floats + (this.rTop >> 3)); Q.set(this.RP.subarray(0, this.rTop)); this.RP = Q; }
+         const slots = 8 * (this.slots.length + fresh);
+         if (slots > this.XD.length) { const D = new Float64Array(slots + (slots >> 3)); D.set(this.XD); this.XD = D; }
+     }
+     _alloc(which, n) {
+         const P = which === "R" ? this.RP : which === "L" ? this.LP : this.VP;
+         const top = which === "R" ? this.rTop : which === "L" ? this.lTop : this.vTop;
+         if (top + n > P.length) {
+             const Q = new P.constructor(Math.max(top + n, P.length * 2));
+             Q.set(P.subarray(0, top));
+             if (which === "R") this.RP = Q; else if (which === "L") this.LP = Q; else this.VP = Q;
+         }
+         if (which === "R") this.rTop = top + n; else if (which === "L") this.lTop = top + n; else this.vTop = top + n;
+         return top;
+     }
+     _setGeom(x, r) {
+         if (r.raw) {
+             const n = Math.min((r.raw.length / 5) | 0, MAX_CHUNKS);
+             if (x.ro < 0 || n !== x.n) { if (x.ro >= 0) this.rW += 5 * x.n; x.ro = this._alloc("R", 5 * n); }
+             x.n = n;
+             this.RP.set(r.raw.subarray ? r.raw.subarray(0, 5 * n) : r.raw.slice(0, 5 * n), x.ro);
+         }
+         this.XD[x.slot * 8 + XW] = r.w;
+         x.loops = loopStarts(r.loops, x.n);
+         this.XD[x.slot * 8 + XL] = Math.log2(r.w) - LOG_R * this._depth(x.home);
+         const RP = this.RP;
+         let len = 0;
+         for (let k = 0; k < x.n; k++) len += RP[x.ro + 5 * k + 4];
+         this.XD[x.slot * 8 + XMASS] = Math.max(len, r.w) / r.w;
+     }
+     _mkItem(r) {
+         const x = { id: r.id, slot: 0, home: r.home, ro: -1, n: 0, scene: null, lo: -1, fn: 0, band: 0, vo: -1, vn: 0,
+             validOk: false, next: null, loops: null, gone: false };
+         x.slot = this._free.length ? this._free.pop() : this.slots.length;
+         this.slots[x.slot] = x;
+         if (8 * (x.slot + 1) > this.XD.length) { const D = new Float64Array(Math.max(8 * (x.slot + 1), 2 * this.XD.length)); D.set(this.XD); this.XD = D; }
+         this.XD.fill(0, 8 * x.slot, 8 * x.slot + 8);
+         this.XD[8 * x.slot + XW] = 1; this.XD[8 * x.slot + XT] = 1;
+         if (r.t) { this.XD[x.slot * 8 + XTX] = r.t[0]; this.XD[x.slot * 8 + XTY] = r.t[1]; }
+         this._setGeom(x, r);
+         this.items.set(x.id, x);
+         return x;
+     }
+     _dropItem(x) {
+         if (this.items.get(x.id) === x) this.items.delete(x.id);
+         this.slots[x.slot] = null; this._free.push(x.slot);
+         this.rW += 5 * x.n; if (x.lo >= 0) this.lW += 4 * x.fn; if (x.vo >= 0) this.vW += x.vn;
+     }
+     _locFor(x) {
+         if (x.lo < 0 || x.fn !== x.n) { if (x.lo >= 0) this.lW += 4 * x.fn; x.lo = this._alloc("L", 4 * x.n); }
+         return x.lo;
+     }
+     _boxesIn(x, anchor, out, oo, ox, oy) {
+         const M = this.frames.map(x.home, anchor);
+         const f = this.frames.get(x.home);
+         if (!M || !f) return null;
+         const dx = this.XD[x.slot * 8 + XTX] + f.sx, dy = this.XD[x.slot * 8 + XTY] + f.sy, raw = this.RP, r0 = x.ro;
+         for (let k = 0; k < x.n; k++) {
+             const i = r0 + 5 * k, o = oo + 4 * k;
+             out[o] = (raw[i] + dx) * M.s + M.ox - ox; out[o + 1] = (raw[i + 1] + dy) * M.s + M.oy - oy;
+             out[o + 2] = (raw[i + 2] + dx) * M.s + M.ox - ox; out[o + 3] = (raw[i + 3] + dy) * M.s + M.oy - oy;
+         }
+         return M;
+     }
+     _homeBox(x) {
+         const f = this.frames.get(x.home);
+         const dx = this.XD[x.slot * 8 + XTX] + (f ? f.sx : 0), dy = this.XD[x.slot * 8 + XTY] + (f ? f.sy : 0), raw = this.RP;
+         let x0 = Infinity, y0 = Infinity, x1 = -Infinity, y1 = -Infinity;
+         for (let k = 0; k < x.n; k++) {
+             const i = x.ro + 5 * k;
+             if (raw[i] < x0) x0 = raw[i]; if (raw[i + 1] < y0) y0 = raw[i + 1];
+             if (raw[i + 2] > x1) x1 = raw[i + 2]; if (raw[i + 3] > y1) y1 = raw[i + 3];
+         }
+         return [x0 + dx, y0 + dy, x1 + dx, y1 + dy];
+     }
+     _compact(force = false) {
+         const live = (top, w) => top - w;
+         const pools = [["R", this.rTop, this.rW], ["L", this.lTop, this.lW], ["V", this.vTop, this.vW]];
+         for (const [which, top, w] of pools) {
+             const P = which === "R" ? this.RP : which === "L" ? this.LP : this.VP;
+             const used = live(top, w);
+             if (!force && !(w > used || (P.length > 4096 && P.length > 1.5 * used))) continue;
+             const Q = new P.constructor(Math.max(1024, Math.ceil(used * 1.125)));
+             let at = 0;
+             for (const x of this.slots) {
+                 if (!x) continue;
+                 if (which === "R") { Q.set(P.subarray(x.ro, x.ro + 5 * x.n), at); x.ro = at; at += 5 * x.n; }
+                 else if (which === "L") { if (x.lo >= 0) { Q.set(P.subarray(x.lo, x.lo + 4 * x.fn), at); x.lo = at; at += 4 * x.fn; } }
+                 else if (x.vo >= 0) { Q.set(P.subarray(x.vo, x.vo + x.vn), at); x.vo = at; at += x.vn; }
+             }
+             if (which === "R") { this.RP = Q; this.rTop = at; this.rW = 0; }
+             else if (which === "L") { this.LP = Q; this.lTop = at; this.lW = 0; }
+             else { this.VP = Q; this.vTop = at; this.vW = 0; }
+         }
+         for (const S of this.scenes) for (const G of [S.grid, S.mgrid]) if (force || (G.P.length > 256 && G.P.length > 2 * G.live)) gridCompact(G);
+         const want = 8 * this.slots.length;
+         if (force || (this.XD.length > 8192 && this.XD.length > 1.5 * want)) { const D = new Float64Array(Math.ceil(want * 1.125)); D.set(this.XD.subarray(0, want)); this.XD = D; }
+     }
+     _cells(S, x, add) {
+         this._cellsIn(S.grid, x, add);
+         if (x.fn >= 3) this._cellsIn(S.mgrid, x, add);
+     }
+     _cellsOut(G, xs) {
+         if (!xs.length) return;
+         if (!this._out || this._out.length < this.slots.length) this._out = new Uint8Array(this.slots.length * 2);
+         const out = this._out, leavers = xs;
+         for (const x of xs) out[x.slot] = 1;
+         const L = this.LP, hit = new Set();
+         let work = 0;
+         for (const x of xs) work += x.fn * (BANDS + 2);
+         if (work * 4 > G.live) {
+             for (const t of [...G.all.values(), ...G.own.values()]) for (let i = 0; i < t.keys.length; i++) if (t.keys[i] >= 0) hit.add(t.segs[i]);
+             xs = [];
+         }
+         const note = (t, i0, i1, j0, j1) => {
+             if (!t) return;
+             for (let i = i0; i <= i1; i++) for (let j = j0; j <= j1; j++) { const s = t.find((i + OFF) * KEYM + (j + OFF)); if (s >= 0) hit.add(s); }
+         };
+         for (const x of xs) {
+             for (let k = 0; k < x.fn; k++) {
+                 const o = x.lo + 4 * k;
+                 for (let b = x.band; b <= x.band + BANDS; b++) {
+                     const inv = 1 / 2 ** (b + 1);
+                     const i0 = cellIx(L[o] * inv), i1 = cellIx(L[o + 2] * inv), j0 = cellIx(L[o + 1] * inv), j1 = cellIx(L[o + 3] * inv);
+                     note(G.all.get(b), i0, i1, j0, j1);
+                     if (b === x.band) note(G.own.get(b), i0, i1, j0, j1);
+                 }
+             }
+         }
+         const P = G.P;
+         for (const s of hit) {
+             const end = s + 2 + P[s + 1];
+             let w = s + 2;
+             for (let q = s + 2; q < end; q++) { const e = P[q]; if (!out[(e / MAX_CHUNKS) | 0]) P[w++] = e; }
+             G.live -= end - w; P[s + 1] = w - s - 2;
+         }
+         for (const x of leavers) out[x.slot] = 0;
+     }
+     _cellsIn(G, x, add) {
+         const loc = this.LP, l0 = x.lo;
+         for (let k = 0; k < x.fn; k++) {
+             const o = l0 + 4 * k, e = x.slot * MAX_CHUNKS + k;
+             for (let b = x.band; b <= x.band + BANDS; b++) {
+                 const inv = 1 / 2 ** (b + 1);
+                 const i0 = cellIx(loc[o] * inv), i1 = cellIx(loc[o + 2] * inv), j0 = cellIx(loc[o + 1] * inv), j1 = cellIx(loc[o + 3] * inv);
+                 fileIn(G, G.all, b, i0, i1, j0, j1, e, add);
+                 if (b === x.band) fileIn(G, G.own, b, i0, i1, j0, j1, e, add);
+             }
+         }
+     }
+     _near(S, x0, y0, x1, y1, T, b, fn, G = S.grid, full = null) {
+         this.stats.queries++;
+         if (this._scan(G, G.all.get(b), b, x0, y0, x1, y1, T, fn, full && full.all.get(b))) return true;
+         for (let bb = b + 1; bb <= b + BANDS; bb++) if (this._scan(G, G.own.get(bb), bb, x0, y0, x1, y1, T, fn, full && full.own.get(bb))) return true;
+         return false;
+     }
+     _scan(G, t, bb, x0, y0, x1, y1, T, fn, seen) {
+         if (!t || !t.n) return false;
+         const slots = this.slots, L = this.LP, P = G.P, XD = this.XD;
+         const side = 2 ** (bb + 1), inv = 1 / side;
+         const i0 = cellIx((x0 - side) * inv), i1 = cellIx((x1 + side) * inv);
+         const j0 = cellIx((y0 - side) * inv), j1 = cellIx((y1 + side) * inv);
+         for (let i = i0; i <= i1; i++) {
+             for (let j = j0; j <= j1; j++) {
+                 this.stats.cells++;
+                 const key = (i + OFF) * KEYM + (j + OFF);
+                 const s = t.find(key);
+                 if (s < 0) continue;
+                 const len = P[s + 1];
+                 if (!len || (seen && seen.get(key) >= len)) continue;
+                 for (let q = s + 2, end = s + 2 + len; q < end; q++) {
+                     const e = P[q], sl = Math.floor(e / MAX_CHUNKS);
+                     const m = slots[sl];
+                     this.stats.entries++;
+                     const o = m.lo + 4 * (e % MAX_CHUNKS), mT = XD[sl * 8 + XT];
+                     const TT = mT > T ? mT : T;
+                     if (L[o] - x1 > TT || x0 - L[o + 2] > TT || L[o + 1] - y1 > TT || y0 - L[o + 3] > TT) continue;
+                     if (fn(m, e % MAX_CHUNKS)) return true;
+                 }
+             }
+         }
+         return false;
+     }
+     _valid(S, m, k) {
+         if (m.fn < 3 || S.members.size < 2) return true;
+         if (!m.validOk) this._computeValid(S, m);
+         return this.VP[m.vo + k] === 1;
+     }
+     _computeValid(S, m) {
+         const n = m.fn, lonely = new Uint8Array(n), loc = this.LP;
+         const other = (q) => q !== m;
+         for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) {
+             const o = m.lo + 4 * k;
+             lonely[k] = this._near(S, loc[o], loc[o + 1], loc[o + 2], loc[o + 3], this.XD[m.slot * 8 + XT], m.band, other) ? 0 : 1;
+         }
+         if (m.vo < 0 || m.vn !== n) { if (m.vo >= 0) this.vW += m.vn; m.vo = this._alloc("V", n); m.vn = n; }
+         const V = this.VP, v0 = m.vo;
+         V.fill(1, v0, v0 + n);
+         for (const run of chunkRuns(n, m.loops, (k) => lonely[k] === 1)) if (run.length >= STRAY_CHUNKS) for (const q of run) V[v0 + q] = 0;
+         m.validOk = true;
+     }
+     _invalidateNear(S, x) {
+         if (!S.multi) return;
+         const loc = this.LP;
+         const drop = (m) => { m.validOk = false; return false; };
+         for (let k = 0; k < x.fn; k++) {
+             const o = x.lo + 4 * k;
+             this._near(S, loc[o], loc[o + 1], loc[o + 2], loc[o + 3], this.XD[x.slot * 8 + XT], x.band, drop, S.mgrid);
+         }
+     }
